7. Asked the cult's purpose, he gives the line they have heard before — the glory of Tiamat. Asked why Greenest, he says it was the largest city closest to the camp and the raid has been an absolute success. Asked the next target, he claims that is above his rank.
8. Asked where the cult was before Greenest, the cultist refuses. He offers a deal: release him, let him reach a safe distance, and he will shout the answer back across the rocks.
9. Sinsong refuses outright — let him go and he will run straight to the camp. Mialee laughs; Eguzkilore stays out of it. Nyx insists she gave the man her word and will not break it. Nuberroca defers to her — little one, what is your call. She nods. Nuberroca lifts the man bodily, walks him to a clear stretch of rock, and puts him down gently.
10. The cultist runs. Sinsong reaches with the staff for a non-lethal blow as the man passes; the strike misses. The hunter Dashes a hundred and twenty feet across open ground, turns, cups his hands to his mouth and shouts back — *from the east!*
11. Sinsong's bolt of fire travels the full hundred and twenty feet and catches him. The hunter falls and does not get up. Mialee says, flat, that what he just did was *wrong*. Sinsong shrugs — they are capable of tracking us.

## 9 - Back to Greenest
_~11:00, 24 Marpenoth 1489 DR_

1. The party gathers what gear they need. Forty rations of butchered venison go on Nuberroca's back; the goliath gives Sinsong ten and keeps the rest, complaining about the weight more than the pain.
2. Nyx takes the hunters' arrows — the half that survived the volleys — back into her quiver. Eguzkilore takes a few. Nuberroca tries to claim a crossbow and is talked out of it. He keeps four hand-axes off the dead drakes' handlers and a small leather pouch of mundane junk.
3. Nyx covers their tracks again on the way out, then leads them north along the rim and west around the bowl, keeping the cult camp well off their north shoulder. They cross the foothills back into farmland by mid-morning.
4. They reach Greenest at about half past three. The town is still in its post-assault state — fewer wounded out in the streets, the cremations long since done, the soldiers visibly less ragged.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 5}}
\page

## 10 - Report and reward
_~15:30, 24 Marpenoth 1489 DR_

1. The party stops at the keep's modest armoury before reporting in. Nuberroca trades one of his old hand-axes back, claims a proper shield and accepts that an old pickaxe sells for a single piece of gold. Eguzkilore replenishes arrows.
2. Three healing potions sit on the keep's small market table at fifty gold a piece — a discounted hero's price. Amidamaru sets aside ten gold for incense; he intends to bind a familiar soon. The rest of the purchases wait on **Tarbaw**'s reward.
3. Tarbaw receives them in the side room. Mialee opens the report — camp twelve miles south, rough strength two hundred to three hundred, a handful of leaders. Nyx fills in: Langdedrosa Cyanwrath, Rezmir the black half-dragon as chief, a red-robed mage they could not name and Frulam Mondath named under interrogation. Cultists of Tiamat. Treasure piled up at a heavily-guarded cave at the camp's east end. Eight prisoners cleaning latrines; Leosin Erlanthar found and freed; his fate after that unknown.
4. Asked the cult's intent, Nyx repeats the line she has been given: glory to Tiamat. Greenest as the largest city closest to the camp and a confirmed successful raid. She offers her own guess that the line is what the rank-and-file believe and that the leaders may want something else; she also offers her guess at the next target — based on the cultist's last shout, eastward in origin and possibly westward in plan, with Candlekeep and Baldur's Gate as the obvious large-city candidates. Tarbaw does not commit to a route but says he will warn the city watch.
5. Tarbaw counts out **two hundred pieces of gold for each of them** out of the keep coffers. Mialee sets aside a hundred of hers and pushes it back across the table — for the town. Nuberroca produces twenty rations of venison and adds them to the same pile. The governor praises their honour.
6. The party splits the remaining potions: one to Amidamaru, one to Sinsong, one to Nyx. Amidamaru sits in the keep's outer yard with the incense and works the binding ritual. An hour later, a small owl familiar perches on his shell.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 5}}

\page

# Session 6 {{float:"right" 2025-03-09}}
{wide}

## 1 - Evening at the keep
_~17:00, 24 Marpenoth 1489 DR_

1. Back at the keep after the camp recon, the party debriefs again with **Tarbaw** in the common room. Sinsong asks whether the captured cultist Miguel brought back from the southern excursion has talked.
2. Tarbaw answers heavily — neither prisoner survived the interrogation. **Escobert** worked both of them; one yielded the camp's location before dying, the other gave nothing. Tarbaw says drily that Escobert is *exceptionally good at extracting information*.
3. Tarbaw remarks that with both prisoners dead the cult's most valuable witness is **Leosin Erlanthar**, and the party did not bring him back. Nyx answers flatly that they lost sight of him; Mialee adds that the monk did not come on his own. Tarbaw waves it off — they have done enough already.
4. Amidamaru asks whether the town has anyone left to rescue. Tarbaw confirms several prisoners were taken to the camp; the party admits they did spot the eight at the latrines but were detected and forced to flee.
5. The party settles in. Mialee asks after a pearl in town — Tarbaw shakes his head, the looters cleaned out anything of value. Nyx orders a drink; Sinsong joins her. The party spends the next two hours drinking in the keep's common room.
6. Sinsong asks Tarbaw before bed what the keep needs of them tomorrow. Tarbaw answers that recovery is the priority — the guards are still tending the wounded; help with that is welcome.
7. The party calls an early night and retreats to the barracks, sleeping soundly until the next morning.

## 2 - Morning briefing
_~08:00, 25 Marpenoth 1489 DR_

1. The party comes down to the keep's hall and finds **Nesim Waladra** waiting for them with another figure. The second man wears traveller's robes and a monk's bearing; the party recognises him from the valley. It is **Leosin Erlanthar**.
2. Leosin greets them. He looks bruised and gaunt but visibly recovered; the cuts from the stake are scarred over. He thanks them for their help with a wry remark that they *could have helped a bit more*, but the healing on the rim was what mattered.
3. Asked how he escaped, Leosin says the distraction fire drew the cultists toward the eastern tents and the prisoner stakes; the middle of the bowl emptied long enough for him to slip into the tents, knock out two guards along the way, and climb out by the north face.
4. Amidamaru gives him medical advice to help the morning's recovery. Sinsong approaches, shakes his hand, and wishes him a swift recovery — the monk's colour comes up, reinvigorated.
5. Leosin admits why he is really here. He must travel north to Elturel to report to his order; he cannot stay. He asks the party to return to the camp and bring back what they can on the heavily-guarded cave at the east end — the cult are calling it a *hatchery*, and that word alone is what he most wants verified. He offers them a reward: **one hundred and fifty gold pieces per head**.
6. Asked what the cave might hold, Leosin spreads his hands — kobolds, dragons, something else; that is what he wants the party to find out. Asked whether he saw treasure carried out, he confirms steady movement of cultists and pack-drakes hauling crates into it. They are using it as a stockpile at the least.
7. Nyx asks Leosin about his order. He answers that he is just a monk — his order is secular rather than religious; he works in concert with the Council of Lords and the Order of the Gauntlet, gathering intelligence to head off further raids like Greenest. He is from Berdusk; Elturel is one stop on his northward route.
8. If the party arrives at Elturel and Leosin has already moved on, they should ask for **Ontharr Frume** of the Order of the Gauntlet.
9. Leosin recommends a proper rest before they go back to the valley. The party agrees and spends the morning resupplying — rations, arrows, rope. Sinsong complains about the weight of all the gear he is carrying; Eguzkilore leaves most of his pack in a chest at the keep.
10. They set out before noon, retracing their route south at an easy pace. First open ground, then the foothills. At the chokepoint where the defile ambush hit them two days earlier they slow to a crawl — and find it *empty*, no scouts, no signs of fresh movement.
11. After four hours walking, they finally approach the bowl. The lower gate that bristled with crossbowmen on the previous run is *unmanned*.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 6}}
\page

## 3 - The camp emptied out
_~13:30, 25 Marpenoth 1489 DR_

1. From the rim they take in the change. The kobolds' shanty quarter at the camp's lower end is *burned to the ground* — the lean-tos torched along their entire run, only the central tower and the better-built shelters still standing. There is no smoke now; the burn is hours old.
2. A circuit of the perimeter shows what happened. Many small parties have broken from the camp through the main gate, each then turning off in a different direction once clear. The heavier-loaded tracks — including a few cart-trails — all bear **west**. The lighter parties scatter every which way.
3. Sinsong puts on the cultist robe again and the party walks in. Four armed humans at a small tent on the upper terrace see them coming; the men are sitting at their bows and traps, calm. One looks up, points casually at the cave, and tells Sinsong — without bothering to lower his voice — that *the friend in the bushes does not need to hide; they are not staying*. The four mount waiting horses and gallop off.
4. With the camp clear the party works the tents room by room. The hunters' shelter holds bedrolls for sixteen and nothing of value. The robed cultists' tents have been stripped down to broken pottery and household scraps. The well-decorated central tent — where **Rezmir** and the others had emerged the day before — is empty too.
5. The household debris is worthless to the party but useful to the people of Greenest. Nuberroca packs about seventy-five kilos of plates, cutlery, and pots into an improvised bundle to carry on the way out, complaining as he does so about losing his stealth. Nyx sweeps the central tent for anything of value and finds ten gold pieces scattered between bedding.
6. With the camp checked, they turn for the eastern cave.

\column

## 4 - The cave mouth
_~14:30, 25 Marpenoth 1489 DR_

1. Sinsong listens at the entrance — running water, the natural acoustics of a deep cave, nothing else. Nuberroca crouches to read the prints at the threshold: drake tracks, days fresh, plus human boot-prints layered over them. Past the threshold the dark is broken by a *soft purple light* from further in.
2. The dwarf casts magical light on a small plate from the tent loot and gives it to Amidamaru for illumination. Amidamaru, who lived three decades underground, returns it with a polite thanks.
3. Mialee scouts first, stepping past a column at the bend. Two attackers waiting in cover behind the column move on her in one motion — coordinated, fast, each striking twice. Before the rest can react **Mialee is on the ground, bleeding out**.
4. Nyx hears her cries from the bend. Nuberroca, seeing Mia drop, *rages* — he had not meant to, but the small one going down decides it for him. He feels the surge run through him, increasing his vitality, and pushes a portion of it across to Eguzkilore.
5. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca closes and brings his greataxe down on the first attacker — a clean swing, the man dead before he can lift his weapon again. Eguzkilore's bow drops the second.
6. Sinsong reaches Mia, stabilises her with his magic, then closes her worst wound with a healing — the elf opens her eyes, alive and on her feet again. Mialee, light-headed, finds a *vial of green liquid* on the body next to her and pockets it.
7. The cleric lays a magical bond on Amidamaru, Mialee, and Nuberroca — they feel their skills honed for the combat.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 6}}
\page

## 5 - The fungi chamber

1. Past the column the cave opens into a wider hall lit purple. Stone stairs lead down into it. Nuberroca takes the lead; partway down one of the treads sinks under him and the staircase *folds flat into a ramp* — he tumbles to the floor in a heap of plates and gear.
2. He picks himself up to find the chamber's floor carpeted with fungi. Several are knee-high; *four are taller than a person*, glowing softly purple, swaying without wind.
3. Nyx asks whether any of them are edible. Nuberroca, who has seen mountain fungi like them, answers that these are *dangerous* — to eat, and worse to touch.
4. The large fungi prove him right at once. They lurch toward him in the same swaying motion; on contact they secrete a putrid slick that eats at his skin. The first strike opens his arm; a faint blue glow runs across the wound and closes it partially. The second slick gets past the heal, the substance visibly melting through his hide where it touches.
5. Mialee shoots from cover. Eguzkilore puts another arrow in the damaged fungus. Nyx marks the wounded fungus and lands a third arrow.
6. Amidamaru, on the steps above, throws a bolt of fire down at the wounded fungus. The shot burns it badly but the thing holds together; the others keep coming.
7. The fungi keep grinding at Nuberroca, the rot biting deep. He drops the closest one with a greataxe sweep, then the next, then the third. The last falls to a combined volley from the steps.
8. Nuberroca turns to Sinsong with a gash along one arm still bleeding the brown slick of the rot. Sinsong sighs at him, calls down a healing, and the wound closes. Nuberroca claps the dwarf on the shoulder and tells him that in his tribe, when someone saves your life *you become blood brothers* — and Sinsong is now his blood brother. Sinsong submits to it with a flat look, mutters about needing to be more careful, and turns away — at which Nuberroca, grinning, adds, *"Gracias, me encanta cómo te luce la calva cuando me curas."*
9. From the chamber they spot a corridor behind them, with a side passage to the left and a broader chamber at the end. Eguzkilore sees barrels stacked at the back.
10. The party sweeps the broken-down storeroom on the right. Sinsong digs through abandoned chests and turns up sixteen gold pieces — nothing else of value, only smashed pottery and ash.
11. On the floor of the same room they find a cultist — sprawled, *blackout drunk*. Nuberroca disarms him, ties him with rope, then slaps him hard enough to draw a groan and nothing more. The bundle of plates and the unconscious cultist together would weigh over a hundred and eighty kilos, so the barbarian leaves the cultist there.

## 6 - The corridor fight

1. The party leaves the storeroom toward the passage at the middle of the corridor. It narrows to a doorway; the chamber beyond is shadowed and silent.
2. ***[EPIC]*** Combat opens before Nuberroca clears the threshold. A cultist runs into the doorway; Nyx's first shot punches through his face and *drops him on the spot*. The body falls and the doorway is briefly clear.
3. More cultists pile in behind him. Two press up to the door at speed; a third hangs back beside two crossbow guards along the far wall. The cultists try to crowd the doorway against Amidamaru — the tortle plants himself in the gap, dodging and shouting back into the corridor for the others to stay clear and to leave one of the cultists alive for questioning.
4. Sinsong, behind Amidamaru, conjures a sword of *pale spirit-light* beside the doorway and it cuts the first cultist in a hard arc.
5. Amidamaru, seeing three cultists line up cleanly behind the second wave, lifts the alchemical mask at his throat and fans a cone of fire down the corridor. Two of the three duck in time behind their cover; the third takes the full burst and goes down moments later.
6. From a side chamber a robed woman strides out, calm. Mialee names her aloud — **Frulam Mondath**. With a small gesture she conjures a sword of pale spirit-light beside her, and sends it skimming forward toward the doorway; the blade tracks Nyx first along the corridor wall.
7. On her next beat Frulam fixes on Amidamaru in the gap and speaks a simple command: *"Tortuga, al suelo."* Amidamaru's knees fold under him before he can argue. The conjured blade pares him from above as he tries to recover, and Amidamaru cannot rise without making it worse.
8. Mialee shoots out of cover and lands a hit on Frulam, but the priestess manages to keep her concentration on the sword.
9. Nyx's second arrow finds another cultist in the chest. Eguzkilore's bow tracks the line — patient shots, mostly into the crossbow guards behind the cover at the far wall.
10. Frulam turns from Amidamaru to Nuberroca, who has just shouldered into the doorway; she lets the spirit-sword over Amidamaru dissolve and speaks a holding-word. Nuberroca freezes in place, *paralysed*, the rage still on him but his limbs locked.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 6}}
\page

11. Sinsong, seeing his opening, throws a sleep-spell at Frulam — it clips her: for a beat she stands frozen, unable to act, but does not go down. The effect is enough to break her concentration and Nuberroca can move again. Two berserkers push into the corridor behind her.
12. Amidamaru fans an acid spray down a thirty-foot lane — half the chamber catches some of it; one berserker takes the full hit, the other and Frulam herself manage to avoid the worst of it.
13. The priestess, recovered from the stupor thanks to the acid spray, drives her halberd into Nuberroca and leaves the goliath with *only a sliver of life*.
14. The combat reshapes in a single beat. Two more barbarian berserkers in cult colours push in from the back chamber — axes already swinging — and behind them comes **Langdedrosa Cyanwrath** himself.
15. ***[EPIC]*** Langdedrosa pushes through into the corridor and stands between the party and Frulam, shielding her. He fixes Nuberroca across the doorway and speaks in a voice the goliath knows: *"Tú. Veo que sobreviviste a tus heridas, no seré tan generoso esta vez."*
16. Sinsong throws another sleep-spell, catching Frulam and one of the berserkers — both fold where they stand.
17. A barbarian swings on Amidamaru, but a flash of light covers the tortle and the axe slides aside, doing no damage.
18. ***[EPIC]*** Mialee breaks cover for a clean shot at one of the new arrivals. The arrow strikes home and the berserker bends his knee, visibly wounded.
19. Langdedrosa stoops over Frulam and shakes her awake; Nuberroca, with a swift cleave of his axe, finishes off the wounded berserker.
20. Another berserker badly hits Amidamaru — the axe should have killed him outright, but a wash of *pale blue light* moves across his shell where the steel bit, preventing some of the damage. The tortle drops to a sliver and stays upright, then disengages and runs back down the corridor.
21. Frulam, now awake, fires off another spirit-sword and sends it at Sinsong. The conjured blade catches him cleanly — a *critical strike to the chest*. The dwarf is hurled into the wall by the impact, his old neck tattoo flaring; he slides down the stones bleeding but standing.
22. Enraged, Frulam calls a beam of light down on the dwarf, finally dropping him. The sleeping berserker awakens.
23. Mialee shoots Frulam — the arrow barely grazes her but the hit is enough to break her concentration. The spirit-sword over Sinsong dissolves.
24. Langdedrosa, hemmed in, opens his jaws and breathes lightning down the corridor — a *clean white line* straight through the doorway. Nuberroca half-evades behind the entrance; Eguzkilore is in the open and takes the full force, gear smoking; Sinsong, lying flat, is missed by inches. The line leaves everyone in the corridor *barely standing* — Nuberroca and Eguzkilore staggered, Sinsong still out, Mialee and Nyx down to a sliver.
25. The line breaks. Nuberroca disengages and pulls back out of the doorway. Eguzkilore moves up to where Sinsong lies, hauls the dwarf over his shoulder, and starts the run back the way they came. Nyx covers their retreat from the far end of the corridor with her bow.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 6}}

\page

# Session 7 {{float:"right" 2025-03-16}}
{wide}

## 1 - Lightning returns
_~15:00, 25 Marpenoth 1489 DR_

1. The corridor fight continues. **Frulam Mondath** stands in the chamber beyond with **Langdedrosa Cyanwrath** and berserkers behind her, while at the doorway Nuberroca is still in his battle-fury with a sliver of life left in him. At the back of the corridor Eguzkilore carries the unconscious Sinsong, who is still bleeding out, and Amidamaru — having pulled back from the line — has turned back into the tunnel.
2. Nuberroca dissolves into a wisp of cloud and reforms at the back of the corridor, reaches for his pouch of bandages to staunch Sinsong's bleeding, then realises he can pour a healing potion directly down the dwarf's throat instead. The potion brings Sinsong back to consciousness, still in Eguzkilore's arms.
3. Nyx steps back from the doorway, murmurs some strange words, and pulls a tangle of vines up from the corridor stones — wide enough to take in all the cult fighters at the front. Every one of them tears clear as the creepers reach, but the floor is now a wrestling mat of green underfoot, and the cultists will have difficulty moving.
4. With three cultists pressing forward and the rest of the party at his back, Amidamaru plants himself in the corridor mouth, braced for any blow that comes: the tortle cannot run without leaving everyone else exposed.
5. **Frulam conjures a spectral blade beside her**; the blade pares at Mialee and misses, and the priestess advances toward the doorway.
6. Mialee leans out and looses an arrow at Frulam, then slips back along the wall — the priestess does not see her go.
7. Langdedrosa, lightning gathered in his throat once more, fixes the corridor again and advances towards the heroes.
8. The half-dragon breathes a clean white line down the passage, catching everyone in the corridor in single file. Sinsong, shielded by the rocks, avoids the direct hit; the rest of the party all twist clear of the worst, but the line still rakes them — Nuberroca and Mialee (still hidden along the wall) weather it, while **Eguzkilore, Nyx, Amidamaru and Sinsong all collapse to the stones, unconscious**.
9. From the chamber beyond, Mia hears a low roar: *"No sé dónde estás, pero te buscaré y te cazaré."*
10. ***[EPIC]*** Eguzkilore, lying on his back bleeding out, fights death off with a sudden surge; his eyes flutter open and he pushes himself up. Between the unconscious Sinsong and the unconscious Nyx beside him he picks Nyx — smaller and easier to carry — gathers the halfling under one arm, and sprints for the cave mouth.

## 2 - Hauling the wounded

1. Turning to cover the retreat, Eguzkilore digs deep for a second burst of effort and looses a bow shot at the nearest pursuing kobold — the arrow goes wide — and patches himself up moments later by sheer force of will.
2. The cultists push down the corridor regardless. The tangle of vines still bites: a berserker forces through the green and only just clears the corridor; **Frulam Mondath** follows hard on his heels.
3. Inside the cave Nuberroca moves to recover the unconscious Sinsong, dropping the bundle of plates and household debris he had hauled out of the camp earlier that afternoon to lighten his load before lifting the cleric.
4. He considers the cave wall — could a goliath swing collapse the corridor on top of the cult? Nuberroca judges the task too far beyond him and lets it go.
5. The goliath throws a hand axe at the nearest kobold instead: the axe takes the creature in the chest and drops it on the stone. A second axe from his other hand clips another kobold — the blow lands but the creature stays standing.
6. Behind him a berserker reaches the unconscious **Amidamaru** in the corridor and pins him to the stone. The tortle is held in place, not finished.
7. From her hiding spot Mialee fires twice at a kobold and both shots go wide; she slips back along the wall, lost from sight again.
8. Langdedrosa sprints the length of the corridor in one motion, his eyes on Nuberroca: *"tú eres mío."*
9. At the doorway two kobolds catch Eguzkilore as he runs through with Nyx in his arms — both blows land, one with crushing force. The borrowed vitality Nuberroca had poured into the elf moments before holds him on his feet where the hit should have dropped him.
10. In Nuberroca's arms Sinsong stirs back to consciousness. The goliath presses a finger to the dwarf's lips and tells him *"tranquilo, Cabezabombilla"*; on contact the bald head *flares with a soft glow*.
11. Sinsong, half-awake, weaves a protective binding on the goliath who is carrying him — a tether that shares part of Nuberroca's wounds with the dwarf.
12. Nuberroca looks **Langdedrosa Cyanwrath** in the eye across the corridor and answers him with the line the half-dragon left him with the day before: *"Acabaré contigo, pero no hoy. Todavía no soy lo suficientemente fuerte."*
13. The goliath then grips the pendant at his throat, invokes the world tree — *"ayúdanos, gran árbol del mundo"* — and burns the last of his resolve so the wisp of cloud takes Sinsong with him.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 7}}
\page

14. He lands further down the corridor and brings his greataxe down on a kobold in a wild, open swing, cleaving the creature apart in a single stroke. At the same moment a surge of borrowed vitality pours across the corridor from Nuberroca into Eguzkilore at the doorway.
15. As they push into the entrance cave, **Langdedrosa Cyanwrath** emerges from the corridor mouth behind them — visibly furious, lightning crackling at his throat once more. The half-dragon is winding up a second breath but has not yet released it.
16. From Nuberroca's shoulder Sinsong whispers a word of healing over Nyx; the halfling, still in Eguzkilore's arms, comes awake at the doorway.
17. They flee in earnest. Nuberroca catches a parting blow on his way out — his battle-fury blunting it, a faint blue glow knitting the gash half-closed as he moves — then sprints through the cave mouth with Sinsong over his shoulder.
18. Outside the cave the five still on their feet are clear of the corridor. Still pinned by the berserker, **Amidamaru is left behind**.

## 3 - Fog cloud and flight

1. The party debates direction between the tents as it moves. Eguzkilore argues for breaking out through the camp's main exit; Sinsong calls for north; the group commits to north and falls in behind him.
2. The cave's kobold guards stagger out into the daylight, blink at the sun, and turn straight back inside — but the berserkers and **Langdedrosa Cyanwrath** push out after the party, closing to about sixty feet behind.
3. Nyx turns mid-stride, fixes her will on the casting, and drops a fog cloud between them and the pursuers. The berserkers run straight into the wall of vapour and lose the trail — the cloud buys the party the gap they need. Through the murk they hear voices searching in tongues they do not understand.
4. The party threads behind the abandoned tents toward the rim instead of through the gate, the white cliff-face above them visible from the camp.
5. With the chase broken for the moment Sinsong calls them in close and binds three of the party in a shared blessing — a steady channel that lends each a small magical hand for the next ten minutes.
6. After a while the party watches from cover as the berserkers come back through the fog, glance about, and turn back toward the cave. **Langdedrosa Cyanwrath** and the two berserkers retreat — Amidamaru still inside — and the party is loose in the bowl with nobody on their trail.

\column

## 4 - Rest and return

1. The group debates whether to rest or push back in at once. Eguzkilore worries the longer they wait the worse Amidamaru's odds; Sinsong wants to wait until dawn; Nyx points out the kobolds will not change their habits whether the party rests or not.
2. They settle on leaving the bowl entirely. Mialee remembers caves in the slope above the camp from the rim recon two days back; the party climbs up out of the valley basin and finds a hide that keeps the cave entrance in view.
3. Nyx uses her last spell of the day to conjure berries for the party's rations; Sinsong sits down to keep copying the spell — the new scroll work he had started in Greenest.
4. **Watches are split.** Eguzkilore takes the first four hours; Mia takes the second four. The other four sleep eight hours straight.
5. Sinsong, after the watch is set, says aloud what he wants everyone to remember: *"me gustaría que apreciaseis que os he salvado a todos."* Mialee answers him: *"un minuto de silencio por los que no han podido salir de la cueva."*
6. The long rest passes without incident. The party wakes after the eight hours; the camp below them is still dark — Nyx wondering aloud whether the kobolds keep an inverted cycle and might be the ones asleep now.
7. After some arguments about the approach, the party settles on a plan: Nyx and Mialee will move ahead as a stealthy vanguard, while Sinsong, Eguzkilore and Nuberroca follow some distance behind in case they are needed.
8. They cross the camp under cover of the tents. The cave entrance is empty — no Langdedrosa, no Frulam, no guards.
9. Nyx sends a magical message in the direction they last saw Amidamaru — *"Amidamaru, are you there?"* After a long beat the answer comes back: he is in the room where they captured the drunkard; he is hurt but only a little; he is unarmed and stripped of his gear. Three kobolds are around him; he does not know where Langdedrosa or Frulam are.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 7}}
\page

## 5 - The cave skirmish
_~00:00, 26 Marpenoth 1489 DR_

1. Some kobolds at the entrance spot them as they advance. Eguzkilore readies a bow shot at the kobold he can see; Mialee drops the first kobold with a single arrow, then steadies her aim for a measured follow-up. Sinsong steps out and hurls a bolt of fire at a second kobold across the chamber, dropping it noisily on the stone.
2. Nyx readies a shot for the first enemy in range, and Nuberroca readies a swing for any attacker that closes on him. The cave is dim, and the corridor turns create blind spots.
3. A kobold creeps into Nyx's line of sight; she lands a clean shot and drops it dead at her feet. Mialee conjures an illusory double of Nyx at a column further forward as a decoy for the kobolds to track, and Sinsong's second bolt of fire punches straight through its centre and into the kobold past it; the illusion shimmers and reforms behind the bolt.
4. A berserker rounds the column from the bend and fumbles into Nuberroca's space, axe still in hand — and trips flat before he can swing. Nuberroca lifts his greataxe over the sprawled man and brings it down with crushing force, splitting the berserker's axe in half and leaving him trembling on the stone, still alive. Mialee notes the barbarian has been disarmed.
5. The downed berserker hauls himself up, slashes at Nuberroca with what is left of his weapon in a reckless lunge, then shouts the party's positions back into the cave — three on the right, two on the left, spread out.
6. Moments later they clear the first wave. As the tripped berserker tries to break away, Nuberroca's axe catches him mid-pivot and drops him. Eguzkilore and Nyx pick off the last kobolds at the bend while Sinsong refreshes the shared blessing over three of the party. Sounds of fresh reinforcements coming down the passage carry from the dark. The five push to the next column and reform behind it; Mialee slips into hiding, leaving her illusory double of Nyx still standing at the original angle as a static decoy. Nuberroca scoops a fallen crossbow off the stone and readies a shot for whatever comes next.
7. ***[EPIC]*** Mialee repositions around the column to flank — and the move takes her right past **Langdedrosa Cyanwrath**, hidden behind it. She had been moving unseen until that step, but it puts her squarely in his line of sight; the half-dragon steps out and catches her twice in the same beat with the sword. She had not searched the chamber and never saw him coming.
8. The party throws everything at Langdedrosa at once. Mialee slips back, repositions, and snaps off a bow shot of her own — the arrow lands, but the column blunts the shot. Sinsong conjures a sword of pale spirit-light beside the half-dragon; he cannot see Langdedrosa directly, but the blade strikes on his behalf and lands a clean opening hit. Sinsong follows with a bolt of fire that bites through the column's cover with searing force. The half-dragon is visibly bloodied before he can counter-attack.
9. Langdedrosa's lightning breath catches the back of the chamber; Mialee, Nyx and Sinsong ride out the worst of it. Sinsong, struck hard, lets his focus slip — the spirit-blade dissolves in the air.
10. Nyx fires back from cover and her arrow finds him — a clean, hard shot — *"Toma, hijo de puta."* The half-dragon is visibly down to a sliver. Mialee uncaps a healing potion and drinks it all down, the brew settling in deep, then ducks behind a column.
11. Langdedrosa's breath gathers again at his throat. He squares up and lines a second white line down the chamber, voice carrying ahead of it: *"no vais a salir ni uno con vida de aquí."* **Eguzkilore** is caught full, **Nuberroca** partially — a wash of pale blue light running over the scorched line on the goliath's side as the worst of the breath bleeds off; two kobolds in the path of the breath drop dead.

## 6 - Cyanwrath falls

1. With Eguzkilore reeling from the breath and Nuberroca bleeding from his own share of it, a fresh berserker steps out from behind the columns and drives his blade into the goliath's back — a faint blue glow chasing the steel and pulling the wound shut behind it. The kobolds press in around Nuberroca; only his rage is keeping him on his feet.
2. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca calls **Langdedrosa Cyanwrath** out by name: *"Langdedrosa, ¿así es como pelea un guerrero, escondiéndose detrás de rayos y atacando a los caídos? Si tienes honor, deja de jugar con presas fáciles y enfréntate a un verdadero titán. Ven a por mí, demuestra que no eres un lagarto cobarde."* The goliath dissolves into a cloud and reforms at the half-dragon's flank, bringing the greataxe down twice from behind. Both blows land squarely, and the half-dragon visibly buckles under them.
3. Eguzkilore tries to follow up with a shot, drawing on his last reserves of clarity and grit to stay upright at the wall. Mialee ducks back into cover behind the column.
4. Sinsong steps forward and calls a beam of celestial light down on Langdedrosa — but with companions in the way the beam cannot be aimed safely. It catches Eguzkilore in the side as the elf is gripping his scimitar, mid-swing at Langdedrosa, leaving him visibly wrecked and staring back at the cleric.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 7}}
\page

5. **Langdedrosa Cyanwrath** recovers and rounds on Nuberroca with a sneer: *"¿Cómo puedes atreverte a retarme a un duelo cuando sois cinco atacándome a mi?"* He lines another lightning breath squarely on the goliath against the wall and lets it go; the breath rakes him, but a flicker of pale blue runs over the worst of the burn before it can sink in.
6. Nyx, with the bow at full reach, fires the killing shot. The arrow takes Langdedrosa as he stands and he goes down on one knee, then to the stone. He looks across at Nuberroca and answers him — *"no eres más que un maldito cobarde que necesita de muchos compañeros para abatirme"* — and falls. Nuberroca answers back as the half-dragon hits the stone: *"Muere bastardo rodeado de tus kobolds, perro."*
7. With the half-dragon down, Eguzkilore turns to the remaining berserker — the one that had been hitting Nuberroca from behind — and tries to talk him out of the fight: *"esta no es tu guerra, márchate y no te haremos nada."* The words land well and the berserker visibly hesitates, but the kobolds are still shooting and Nyx is hitting him from a distance; he ultimately decides to keep fighting, drives Eguzkilore down to a knee, and is then cut down by the elf himself seconds later.
8. **Langdedrosa is alive.** Sinsong reaches the half-dragon where he fell and finds him still breathing — unconscious, but his pulse holds where many others would have stopped. Nuberroca produces his rope and binds him fast before he can recover.
9. **The party loots the bodies.** Langdedrosa carried a spear, a longsword and a handful of curios — a glowing worm, a ball of grease, a piece of tree bark — plus a small purse of silver and copper. Nuberroca remarks on the glowing worm and the dwarf's bald head together. Mialee pulls a ring off one of the cult bodies, and Nuberroca claims a map off one of the kobolds.
10. Amidamaru, hearing Nyx's voice up the passage, **pulls free of his bindings**, finds his gear back in the cell, cuts past two kobolds with his claws on the way out, and meets the halfling in the corridor with word that his stretch is clear and the party can move through. Nyx hugs him in the dark; Sinsong checks him over for damage.
11. With **Amidamaru recovered** and the cave quiet, Nuberroca turns back to the half-dragon — but when he checks for breath there is none. **Langdedrosa has died** where he lay, the wounds too deep for recovery.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 7}}

\page

# Session 8 {{float:"right" 2025-03-23}}
{wide}

## 1 - Past the barracks
_~00:30, 26 Marpenoth 1489 DR_

1. Leaving **Langdedrosa Cyanwrath**'s corpse behind in the columned chamber where he fell, the party crosses back to the barracks where they fought **Frulam Mondath** the day before.
2. From the corridor at the back of the barracks they catch the sound of something coming up. Eguzkilore readies an arrow on his bow, waiting on whatever clears the corridor mouth.
3. A *guard drake* bursts from the corridor and charges Eguzkilore. The elf's held arrow drives into the creature, but the drake covers the distance anyway and reaches him in melee.
4. Now with the drake at sword's reach, Eguzkilore lets the bow fall and draws his scimitar, then brings the blade down on the creature in one full swing — the cut runs as deep as the steel can carry.
5. Nuberroca charges in, weaving through the others to get to the drake — a few paces shy of the cleaner flanking angle, but he engages from where he can with **Langdedrosa**'s greatsword. The blow lands cleanly.
6. Nyx steps to a fresh angle between two of the room's beds and lines a clean bow shot into the drake.
7. The drake turns on Nuberroca with a bite and a tail-strike — both fail utterly; from across the chamber Sinsong answers with a *bolt of fire* at the drake.
8. Nuberroca, swinging recklessly, brings the greatsword up and down in a single arc and severs the drake's head clean from its body.
9. Nyx listens for what else is in the cave. Beyond the constant water-drip and cave noise, she picks out the soft beat of *wings* from the corridor the party came down — faint but distinct, larger than a bat.

\column

## 2 - Frulam's quarters

1. The party advances cautiously into the chamber beyond the doorway. Nyx slips into the room with stealth; Eguzkilore moves up with her; Sinsong follows behind. Nuberroca, badly hurt from the previous fight, hangs back and asks aloud to be patched up.
2. Past the doorway lie two adjoining chambers, both *elegantly appointed* by the standards of the cult camp. The main room holds a writing table laid with several notes and a map, and a bookshelf along the wall. The bedroom beyond has a large bed and two chests — the clothing scattered around the bed reads as a woman's. Both rooms are empty of cultists.
3. Sinsong, scanning the floor, gets fixated on a small *yellow rug* tucked along the wall that does not match any of the other rug-and-furniture pairings in the chamber.
4. Sinsong steps onto the yellow rug to take a closer look. The rug gives way under his weight; he plunges through a concealed hole in the floor. At the last moment he catches hold of a rope hanging in the shaft and stops his fall.
5. Sinsong drags himself back up the rope into the chamber. He tells the others what he saw at the bottom of the shaft: a *gigantic statue*, an altar before it, and at least one drake with kobolds around it. Based on his mental map, he thinks the chamber down there can also be reached around from another way.
6. Nyx investigates the bookshelf and pulls out a *book on dragons* the cultists had been keeping. She slips it into her pack to read in her own time. After that, the halfling gathers up the loose notes from the table and pockets the map as well.
7. While the others search, Sinsong asks Nuberroca to push the drake's corpse to block the corridor, then Nuberroca shoves the bookshelf across the bedroom door as a rough barricade. From across the room Nyx watches *winged kobolds* pour out of the shaft and mutters in resignation — *"la barricada más inútil del mundo"*.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 8}}
\page

## 3 - The winged kobolds

1. A kobold throws a net at Eguzkilore while he stands over the hole. The throw catches him *cleanly* — he is tangled in the netting, unable to move.
2. A second kobold flutters over the elf and hurls a rock straight at the restrained Eguzkilore. The stone lands hard.
3. Another stone catches the elf archer square in the head; **Eguzkilore collapses unconscious, still tangled in the net**. Another rock follows — Sinsong is the target this time; the throw goes wide.
4. Nyx steps to the kobold pressing through the shaft and runs it through with her sword; it falls dead at her feet.
5. Sinsong calls a *spectral sword* into the air at his side, and the blade strikes the kobold pressing next to him from a flanking angle. He then crosses to Eguzkilore, lays a hand on him and steadies the elf's bleeding with a magic word, before falling back behind the writing table into cover.
6. Three kobolds press in around Nyx, two flanking her for a coordinated strike — one strike gets through and lands hard, drawing blood. She strikes the kobold on her right, killing it. Another disengages and slips towards the shaft; Nyx hacks it as it goes, but the kobold survives the hit and disappears through the hole.
7. Sinsong sends the *spectral sword* across at the last kobold pressing Nyx — the blade strikes home and obliterates the creature. He then crosses to Eguzkilore and casts a healing spell on the elf — barely enough to bring him back, but the elf cracks his eyes open.
8. Nuberroca plants himself with one foot either side of the shaft and *rips the net* off Eguzkilore with his bare hands. He hauls the elf up: *"venga, Eguzkilore, espabila, que todavía queda trabajo por hacer"*.
9. Nyx, remembering she had not done it the day before, hands one of her *magical berries* to each of them — one bite is enough to revive a teammate at the *threshold of life*.
10. Nyx asks aloud whether they should block the trapdoor or go down to the lower chamber; Sinsong suggests they barricade it. Nuberroca drops the heavy table over the shaft mouth to seal it for the moment.
11. The barbarian moves to the two chests in the corner: he checks the first for traps and opens it — empty. Nyx tries the second herself and finds only **women's clothing**. Eguzkilore walks the kobold bodies on the floor and pockets the meagre valuables they have.

\column

## 4 - Down the rope

1. With the upper room cleared and the trapdoor barricade shoved aside, Sinsong sends his *spectral sword* down the shaft and swings it blindly in the dark — the sword has no senses of its own and finds nothing. The shaft is a *thirty feet* drop.
2. Sinsong lowers himself down the rope and ends his descent at about ten feet above the floor, where a drake below jumps to snap at him — *both bites fail*. He tries to climb back up, but Nuberroca, who has come down behind him into the shaft mouth, is jammed shoulder-to-shoulder across the tunnel above him — Sinsong cannot climb past the goliath.
3. Nyx descends the rope until she reaches Nuberroca, lets go of it, and steps up onto the goliath's shoulders so her hands are free for the bow. From that unstable perch she draws on the drake, the shot harder for the footing.
4. ***[EPIC]*** Nyx's arrow threads the narrow gap between Nuberroca's shoulder and the wall of the shaft. Exactly as the drake lifts a claw at Sinsong below, the arrow drives into the drake's paw — the kind of shot nobody quite expects to land.
5. Sinsong slips past the drake's reach, repositions on the floor, and the spectral sword strikes the drake from a flanking angle.
6. With the shaft below clear, Nuberroca drops the rest of the way to the floor, takes up the flanking position, and brings the greatsword down on the drake. Nyx comes off the rope behind him and her arrow lands too.
7. Sinsong sends the *spectral sword* in again and follows with a *bolt of fire* for a small burn. The drake answers with one last bite at Nuberroca and slips wide. Nuberroca grips Sinsong's shoulder and a measure of his fury passes through, hardening him against the next blow; then he brings the greatsword down on the drake between them — the killing blow. The tunnel to the chamber is clear.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 8}}
\page

## 5 - Tiamat's chamber

1. With the drake down, the four take in the room properly. Three of the four walls and the floor are *tooled smooth* and run with damp. Bas-relief *dragons* are carved across the smooth walls, their tails knotting around one another in long looping patterns; the ceiling is left in its raw cave form.
2. The chamber's centrepiece is a vast statue of a *five-headed dragon*, each head a different make, the creature climbing out of an erupting volcano; smaller dragons in the carving fly around its flanks. Sinsong, with the right schooling, recognises **Tiamat** at once.
3. A small *wooden chest*, well-made and decorated, sits on the floor in the corner of the chamber, in front of the statue.
4. Sinsong, looking longer at the walls than the others, notices something off about the dragon carvings — there are *more black dragons than any other breed*, perhaps one in three. Nyx wonders aloud what elemental type they are but nobody at the table has the lore, so she tries to find the info on her new book.
5. Sinsong sends the *spectral sword* across to strike the chest. The blow drives the chest a hand's-breadth sideways, and the statue takes a small chip in the same arc. *Click.*
6. From the chamber rises a long *hissing* — like *the tongue of a serpent, but a hundred at once* — and then, from the mouths of every black dragon carved into the walls, a pale liquid spits out, *eating the stone wherever it touches.* The whole chamber is sprayed with it.
7. As the acid pours, the party throws themselves clear. Nuberroca's instincts pull him out faster than the others, and the party fares well — only a brief burn on each of them, and they shake the worst of it off. The burn breaks Sinsong's hold on the *spectral sword*, however, and the blade dissolves into the air.
8. The acrid *vapour* created is the worse half of the trap. They hold their breath against it and ride it out — Eguzkilore catches the worst of the cloud. After a brief, agonising moment, the acid disperses.
9. Nyx's *dragon book* is ruined by the spray, as the halfling was checking it when the trap triggered. Half its pages are eaten away. She tucks what is left back into her pack.
10. Nuberroca checks the chest for traps and decides the lock has some sort of *spring* mechanism more elaborate than a chest of this kind warrants. He sets his shield, takes the scimitar, and brings the pommel down hard on the lock. The lock breaks — but nothing further fires.
11. Nyx focuses past the cave-noise and catches two distinct sounds: *kobolds* down one corridor, *drakes* down another. She points each direction out for the others.

\column

## 6 - The hatchery

1. From the *darkness* to the east a single *kobold* emerges, lifts the key off a hook on the wall and turns it in a *barred iron gate* on the right side of the chamber. Beyond the bars Nyx makes out shapes that look like *eggs* — larger than a halfling.
2. As the kobold turns the key in the gate, two *drakes* answer it with a long roar from the deeper passage. Another kobold follows on its heels.
3. Eguzkilore advances and puts an arrow into the first drake. Sinsong's bolt of fire takes a kobold cleanly across the floor.
4. Nuberroca plants himself in the corridor mouth and brings the greatsword down with full force on one of the kobolds — the blow lands true and deep.
5. The drakes shift to pin Nuberroca from two sides. The first drake catches him with a bite and a tail-strike; a *blue glow* runs across each fresh cut and closes it partway. Nuberroca is still badly hurt, but his fury holds the worst of it back.
6. From the bend, Nyx puts a *clean hard arrow* into the first drake and drops it dead — the arrow is left lodged in its head. Nuberroca lets out a clearly audible breath.
7. The second drake repositions to flank Nuberroca and presses again — bite and tail. The blows land, but his fury holds.
8. Sinsong, from cover, hurls a *bolt of fire* at the surviving drake — a long, roaring stream that arcs past Nuberroca's cheek and tears clean into the creature. *"Os he vuelto a salvar"*.
9. Nuberroca brings the greatsword down on the wounded drake. The east passage is clear.
10. Nyx walks the bodies afterward and recovers two of her arrows from the kobolds. Eguzkilore recovers one of his own. Sinsong gathers the four of them in close and weaves a *bonded blessing* over them.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 8}}
\page

## 7 - Behind the iron gate

1. The party walks up to the *iron gate* the kobold had opened. The key is hanging on a hook nearby. Through the bars Nyx makes out three *eggs*, roughly a metre tall each, in straw, and *blood-stains* on the floor that *do not look fresh*. The chamber beyond stretches out past the reach of their light.
2. Nyx asks the group whether they want to go in and investigate. Eguzkilore wants in; Nuberroca cautions them to enter with care, in stealth and in the shadows.
3. Eguzkilore eases through the gate and down the few steps into the egg chamber, treading carefully so as not to make a sound.
4. Sinsong calls for action: he raises a *bolt of fire* at one of the eggs and the **straw under it catches**. The egg itself stays intact in the flame.
5. Nuberroca strides over to the burning egg and brings the greatsword down two-handed. The shell splits and a *half-formed dragon hatchling* crawls out, manages a single rasping attempt at breath, and *dies* on the floor. He looks at the others, tears a small wing from the body and tries to *cook it over the burning straw*.
6. Nyx, watching the goliath cook a half-formed dragon, walks back to the iron gate and quietly takes the key off the hook on the wall, slipping it into her pocket. In case something goes wrong, she explains, they can run out and close the gate. Nuberroca approves.
7. Eguzkilore drifts over to join Nuberroca and asks if there is anything else worth taking off the hatchling — a scale, a claw. There is not. He sits down with the goliath and the two of them set about cooking a slice of half-formed dragon over the burning straw.
8. While the two cook, Nyx puts the question to the group plainly: should they *destroy* the remaining eggs so they cannot grow up and cause harm later, or leave them? Nuberroca floats *taking one with them* instead.

\column

## 8 - The living column

1. To the east of the chamber one of the cave columns *begins to move*. A long *tentacle* unfurls from it, snakes across the floor to the broken egg, hooks the dead hatchling and drags it back — *"comida"*.
2. The party reads it for what it is — **a Roper** — a column-shaped predator, frankly large and, at least for the moment, only interested in eating.
3. Eguzkilore breaks for the gate at a dash, calling for Nyx to ready the key. Sinsong, behind cover, lobs another *bolt of fire* at a different egg as bait — the straw beneath it catches too.
4. Nuberroca cracks open the recently ignited egg with the greatsword; another half-formed hatchling, like the first, fails its first breath and dies on the floor. He gestures at the Roper and points to the body — *"comida"*.
5. The Roper crawls slowly toward the gate, hooks the second hatchling on a tentacle and eats it on the move.
6. Sinsong slips outside the gate, swings it shut behind him, and through the bars hurls a final *bolt of fire* at the Roper. The creature shudders under the flame; from its bulk dozens of fresh tentacles boil up, *writhing*.
7. Tentacles whip out across the chamber. The first volley mostly fails, but a second wave catches Nuberroca cleanly — and where it touches, tiny *needles* drive into the skin and *drain the strength* out of him. Later strikes catch Eguzkilore as well and pull him hard against the iron gate; the gate strains but holds.
8. The Roper *climbs the ceiling* and crosses through the gap above the iron gate, stretching its body over the bars. From the new vantage it reaches tentacles to both sides — biting Nuberroca on the way through, a heavy blow, even with his fury blunting the worst of it. A *blue glow* flickers across the bite as it opens, but the wound outpaces it. The goliath is staying upright on fury alone. Another tentacle grapples Nyx.
9. Nuberroca *dissolves into a wisp of cloud* out of the monster's grip and reforms behind Nyx. He plants himself and tears the tentacle off her by main strength.
10. Nyx hurls the last two *rations* in her pack at the Roper as bait. A clean, vicious tentacle catches Nyx in the same moment, but the Roper's hunger overrides the rest of its attacks — it drags the food in with two tentacles and eats them in two bites.
11. Eguzkilore cuts free of the tentacle that has him and moves toward the shrine's chamber, but the path is jammed with Sinsong at the doorway and he can advance no further.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 8}}
\page

## 9 - A chance to scape

1. ***[EPIC]*** Sinsong, with the situation about to get past the party, *weaves a binding of sleep* over the Roper. The creature's tentacles slacken; it slumps where it stands — stupor deep enough to hold for a while, as long as no one strikes it.
2. With the Roper slumped from Sinsong's binding, Eguzkilore breaks past him and dashes the rest of the way out.
3. Nuberroca takes the moment Sinsong's *sleep* has bought and crosses back to the wooden chest. The thing is heavy — *sixty pounds* — but he gathers it under one arm and falls back toward the rope, his stride halved by the load.
4. Nyx still has the key in her pocket, but the gate was already shut from when Sinsong slipped out, and with the Roper slumped across the gap on the ceiling, locking it would change nothing. She turns away from the bars and falls in with the rest of the retreat.
5. The four of them work their way back to the shaft and start the climb up — Nyx leads, Eguzkilore and Sinsong behind her, Nuberroca with the chest. The Roper, behind them, stays where it slumped.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 8}}

\page

# Session 9 {{float:"right" 2025-04-06}}
{wide}

## 1 - The cave mouth
_~01:00, 26 Marpenoth 1489 DR_

1. At the cave's outer chamber, Amidamaru and Mialee face *three kobolds* who just jumped on them from deeper in the cave.
2. Below, the party who descended into the Tiamat-statue chamber haul themselves up the shaft — Nuberroca carrying the heavy wooden chest from the shrine up with him, *slow* under its weight — leaving the Roper *asleep* behind.
3. Nyx, remembering that the monster's bulk *deforms* enough to squeeze through narrow places, asks Nuberroca to help her tip the chamber's *bookshelf* across the shaft mouth. Between the two of them, the heavy shelf goes down and the hole is sealed.
4. Eguzkilore plants himself between the two doorways and *holds his shot for whatever moves first*.
5. Mialee opens with a clean arrow at the kobold closing on Amidamaru, the kobold drops and she hides back.
6. With the shelf in place, and unaware of her friends' struggle, Nyx walks across to Frulam Mondath's abandoned bed and lies down on it for a moment.
7. The remaining kobolds swarm Amidamaru — three blows in quick succession, the third lands hard. **Amidamaru collapses unconscious** at the cave mouth.
8. Nuberroca, hearing nothing from the bedroom, returns down the corridor. Eguzkilore is jammed in the doorway as a stopper — Nuberroca breaks into a run and squeezes past him through the narrow gap.
9. Mialee, from cover, threads another shot into a second kobold — the creature's eyes are on the downed tortle, not on her, and the arrow finds a vital spot. The third kobold remains.
10. Nuberroca, arriving at the scene, *hurls his greataxe* at the surviving kobold — the throw goes wide. *The kobold flinches as the spinning blade whirls over its head and embeds itself in a stone column behind it*. The stone cracks, but the kobold lives.
11. Mialee crosses to where Amidamaru is bleeding out and presses one of Nyx's *berries* into his mouth. The tortle comes back at the *threshold of life*. From cover behind a rock, Nyx threads a perfect shot into the last kobold and the chamber is silent again.

\column

## 2 - The watch-cave

1. Nuberroca demands a long rest — *non-negotiable*. Amidamaru, *tortured during his captivity*, has not slept and presses for the same. Nyx is mostly fresh and offers to keep watch. They pull back to the small cave on the white cliffs — the one they had used to surveil the hideout the day before.
2. On the way out, Nuberroca pauses by the body of **Langdedrosa Cyanwrath** that the party left behind a few hours ago. He confirms the half-dragon is dead, then dislodges the greataxe and **decapitates him**. He fastens the head to his belt by a strap, *hanging visible*.
3. Inside the watch-cave, Nyx prepares a fresh batch of *berries* — ten in all, one for each teammate and five for herself. She tells Nuberroca that one berry sustains a person for the day and *heals a little*.
4. While the others bed down, Nyx asks Amidamaru what the cultists *wanted* from him during his captivity. He prefers not to discuss what they did to him, but volunteers that they wanted to know about **Leosin** *the monk*, *what we had done* with him and *where he was*. Nyx files the thread away — they will have to go looking for him once they are out of here.
5. As the last task before the watches begin, Mialee turns to the **wooden chest** Nuberroca had carried out — its lock already broken open by the goliath's scimitar pommel in the shrine chamber. Even so, the party stays cautious of whatever else the cult might have layered into it: Nuberroca sets the chest deep in the cave and steps well away, and from a safe distance Mialee conjures an unseen hand of force and works the lid open through it. A small *click* as the lid gives — nothing further.
6. The chest holds *pearls*, several *gems* of various sizes, a *fine ring* that takes no magical aura under Mialee's touch, a *broken-diamond fragment*, and a *necklace*, also non-magical. Mialee thinks the lot may belong to families in Greenest — they could return them.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 9}}
\page

## 3 - First light
_~09:30, 26 Marpenoth 1489 DR_

1. The night watches pass without incident — eight hours of rest, no one approaching the cave. As the sun rises, a great flock of bats — *thousands, probably tens of thousands* — pours into the cave.
2. By full morning the party debates next steps. Nyx wants to push *north* into the cave system, but leaving the last dragon egg intact would be a problem. Eguzkilore and Mia report seeing no movement out of the cave through the night.
3. The party approaches the outer entrance and finds the whole place *strewn* with loose coins, scraps of cloth — *the kobolds' loincloths* — and assorted small junk. The Roper has *escaped*: Nyx reads the spread as the creature crawling out to feast on what was left of the kobold corpses.
4. Mialee, who never met the Roper, asks what they are about to face. Nyx describes it: *column-shaped*, *tentacled*, *massive*; it had hooked her by the chest yesterday before someone — Nuberroca, or maybe Eguzkilore — tore the tentacle off. *"Tortuga, cuidado, que como te coja el Roper…"*.

## 4 - Gold on the floor
_~10:00, 26 Marpenoth 1489 DR_

1. Amidamaru — drawn by the *gold scattered on the floor* — strides ahead of the rest, scooping the most valuable pieces into his pack.
2. As he rounds one of the cave columns, *tentacles unfurl from it* — what he took for a pillar is the Roper itself, its bulk indistinguishable from the stone columns around it. In a single beat its tentacles snake out and seize *both* Amidamaru and Nuberroca — two tentacles each. *Sticky, needled* — pulling them in.
3. Nyx raises a binding of *coloured light* over the creature — its outline lights up across the cave, making the creature easier to target.
4. Amidamaru, gripped by the tentacles around his shell, throws his hands out in a *cone of fire* — straight at the Roper and the tentacles holding them. The creature twists away from the worst of the flare, protecting its body from most of the searing heat.
5. Eguzkilore advances and lines a shot at the lit-up creature, missing badly despite the clear angle. Nuberroca, restrained, brings the greatsword down on the tentacle around Amidamaru — the blow lands cleanly, but the tentacle holds.

\column

6. The Roper draws its bound captives in 25 feet at a single pull, bites Nuberroca when close enough, and lashes fresh tentacles around Nyx and Eguzkilore as well — *the needles drive in and the strength runs out of them*.
7. Eguzkilore, still tangled, threads a perfect shot into the creature; the Roper *bleeds slightly*, but stays standing. The body is too large to feel a single arrow.
8. ***[EPIC]*** Amidamaru fires a second *cone of fire* across the Roper. The creature cannot twist clear this time; **every tentacle burns off**, freeing the grappled party in the same breath. *"No necesitamos a Sinsong, me tienen a mí"*.
9. Eguzkilore, pulling back to safer ground, takes a *bite* on the way out — heavy damage but he stays up. Mialee threads a shot into the Roper's flank with one of her last arrows. Nuberroca shares a little of his fury with Nyx while she stands behind him, *saving her life* with a thin shield of borrowed strength.
10. The Roper *grows six new tentacles in a single moment* and rises full again. Out of the new surge it lunges, opens its jaw, and bites Amidamaru — *the shell crunches* under the bite, blood streams from the tortle's flank, *one arm hangs useless* with the pain.
11. Nyx puts her full strength into a sword-swing — the blade, seething with dark energy, bites deeper than expected and the creature reels.
12. Amidamaru throws his third *cone of fire* — the Roper cannot twist clear again, every tentacle catches once more. The room fills with the stench of burning flesh.
13. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca pushes Amidamaru away from the monster and brings the greatsword down two-handed on the column's body. The Roper *gives* — the column begins to deform under the blow, *half-digested kobold flesh* sloughs out from underneath, and the whole creature collapses on the floor in an *unpleasant* wet heap. *"¡Espera que voy a echar la pota!"* — Nyx, vomiting berries.
14. A lone *kobold* who had stayed clear of the fight breaks for the inner cave; Eguzkilore levels his bow and *takes him down* with a single shot.
15. The party patches up afterward. Amidamaru hands Nuberroca a handful of pebbles he has *tinkered* to give off dim light at 5–10 feet — torches that don't need oil.

{{note,quote
*¡Espera que voy a echar la pota!*

{{attribution Nyx, *vomiting berries.*}}
}}

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 9}}
\page

## 5 - The barracks

1. The party heads back inward, going again towards the explored chambers. Mialee summons **Echo** — her *bat familiar* — and sends it ahead through the corridor to see what waits.
2. Echo, hanging from the ceiling, returns the count: **five kobolds and one drake** roosting on the old barracks beds. They are *making the place their own* — settling in.
3. Nuberroca decides to charge for the surprise. The kobold at the door sees him coming as he runs in — *no real surprise* — and the goliath's opening blow misses.
4. The kobolds fall back in coordinated pairs, working together. A net flies across the corridor and catches Eguzkilore, leaving him restrained, his bow useless.
5. Mialee — too far to cut him free in person — calls up an unseen hand of force, lifts a dagger from her own belt in mid-air, and slashes at the net with it. The net is weakened and Eguzkilore tears through the rest with a pull.
6. Nuberroca rakes through the kobold line with the greatsword, swinging recklessly. He takes hits — kobold *darts* and the drake's bite-and-tail-strike — but his fury holds the worst of it back, a faint *blue glow* knitting each fresh cut closed as it opens.
7. Nyx pulls a clean shot into the drake from her cover — she has had it tracked since the start of the fight, making the shot more damaging. By then Eguzkilore drops the last kobold standing in the corridor with a precise arrow.
8. With only the drake left in the room, Mialee threads a follow-up shot into it from her flank. The drake totters but doesn't fall.
9. Nuberroca squares with the drake from a flanking angle and brings the greatsword down in a lethal arc. The drake takes the blow and dies on the floor.

\column

## 6 - Down the rope

1. With the barracks clear, Nuberroca walks over to Amidamaru with his *arm bleeding and badly cut up* and asks for help. Amidamaru, deadpan — *I can help you, but that's where my help ends*. Nuberroca calls on the cleric — *"Sinsong, cúrame"* — and Sinsong weaves a powerful healing over the goliath; Nuberroca is brought back nearly whole.
2. The party walks back to Frulam's quarters. Nyx confirms her suspicion — the *bookshelf barricade* across the shaft is *broken through*, the area beyond wrecked. The Roper had to smash through the shelf to get past — it had forced its way out before dawn.
3. Nyx volunteers to scout the lower chamber first — *quiet, alone, then signal up*. She slides down the rope and lands behind cover. From her position she messages her partners: *clear*.
4. The party drops down the rope into the south-west corner of the shrine chamber, but before they can push out from the landing Nyx signals them — *kobolds, many*.

## 7 - The shrine
_~10:30, 26 Marpenoth 1489 DR_

1. As soon as the party pushes into the chamber, the kobolds — *prepared and waiting* — open with a hail of *sling-stones* from cover. Others dart in close, daggers out, less to wound than to *keep the heroes hemmed in* — and with the party hard-pressed at arm's reach, the slingers' stones find their marks almost every time.
2. Mialee, who had held still to steady her aim, finally lands a clean shot — the arrow slips past a kobold's guard; Nyx ribs her that it's her first all morning.
3. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca hauls **Langdedrosa Cyanwrath's severed head** off his belt and *brandishes it* at the kobolds. His fury and the trophy together make the threat work; three kobolds *break and run, frightened*.
4. Nyx, badly bloodied, pulls back behind a table for cover. The remaining kobolds press into the corridor mouth.
5. With the halfling safe, the party closes the wave down — Amidamaru slays a kobold at the corridor mouth, Nuberroca *pulps* another outright, a *bolt of fire* takes a third, and Eguzkilore's bow drops one more. The last of the ambushers — those not already cut down or routed — break and flee into the dark.
6. With the first wave broken and a brief lull, Nyx points out the dragon egg the previous night had left intact. Mialee asks what to do: break it or carry it. Nuberroca, from across the chamber, settles it — *"¡Destruid el huevo!"*.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 9}}
\page

7. Mia slips through the iron gate into the **hatchery** and strikes the egg with a sharp blow — the shell holds. She then pours the last of her oil across the straw of the nest and lights it as she retreats back toward the shrine.
8. The fire is barely settling when a fresh wave of kobolds — slipped in unseen through the side tunnels while the party's eyes were on the egg — falls on them.
9. The kobold pack focuses on Eguzkilore — crowding in close so their blows land thick and fast; he **goes down** on the chamber floor, breath shallow, and the press of kobolds walls him off — none of his partners can cut through to reach him.
10. The kobolds then turn on Amidamaru — stones and a dagger driven home as the pack closes around him — and **the tortle drops to the ground**, slumping unconscious at the tunnel mouth he had been watching. His breath shallows alongside the elf's.
11. Meanwhile Nyx, half-broken behind her table and *out of options*, weaves an illusion of a kobold over herself — *as close to one of theirs as she can manage*. She steps out and orders the kobolds, in Common, *not to kill the others — the mistress wants them alive, especially the elf*. Her accent slips — Common is not the kobolds' tongue — but the survivors begin to *repeat orders back to those behind them* in Draconic that the party cannot understand.
12. ***[EPIC]*** Eguzkilore's body lurches once more against the dark — a last shudder runs the length of the elf, his hand scrabbling weakly at the cold stone for a bow that is no longer in reach. Blood spreads black beneath him in the half-light; the breath rattles slow out of his chest and does not draw back. **Eguzkilore *dies* on the floor of the Tiamat chamber**, under the five-headed statue and the guttering flame of the candles — beyond any saving.
13. The kobolds, *not killing for sport* and now seemingly under the disguised orders, send one of their own across to attempt to *stabilise* the elf, but Eguzkilore is already gone. The kobolds begin to *drag the body out* toward the cave entrance. Amidamaru stabilises quietly in the background as the fight winds down.

\column

## 8 - The bat-cave

1. While the fight in the Tiamat chamber was raging, Nuberroca had broken away down the tunnel he'd been guarding, set on learning how the kobold reinforcements were reaching them so fast. The way was *near-black*, but he could make out their tracks and **a flight of stairs they had descended**. As he cleared the stairs, *the ceiling caved in* — not on top of him, but sealing the corridor at his back.
2. Unable to see, he took out one of the *glowing pebbles* Amidamaru had given him and pressed on by its dim light. A stretch of floor *gave way* under his weight, and *poisoned needles hidden underneath* drove into his foot — he shook off the worst, but the poison left him *light-headed*, not quite drugged.
3. The cave opened around him: the floor thick with *bat guano*, scattered with small *bat corpses* drained dry — *husks, desiccated*. Far overhead, past the reach of his light, the ceiling was *alive with thousands of bats* — heard more than seen.
4. Nuberroca worked his way along, *hugging the wall* on the broken ground, hunting for anything useful — *potions, weapons*. By a *pile of old bones* in the north he caught a *glint* of something down in the refuse and moved to take it.
5. The *stench* hit him first. *Four troglodytes* — reptilian and foul — uncoiled from cracks in the wall, already almost on him; in the dark his sight reached barely ten feet. They pressed him from every side with tooth and claw; his *shield* turned most of it, but one raking blow got through. *Cornered*, the barbarian knew the fight here was not winnable in numbers.
6. Nuberroca *winks into a swirl of cloud* and re-forms thirty feet clear of the pack, the troglodytes letting him go. The way he came in is sealed by the cave-in behind him, so he strikes out by another route — and catches the *soft purple glow* of the fungi chamber the party had pushed through on its first descent into the cave. He makes for the light and works his way back toward the *entry chamber*.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 9}}
\page

## 9 - Mialee's gambit

1. Mialee, taking Nyx's disguise as the model, weaves an illusion of a kobold over herself — *taller than most but small enough to pass for the largest of theirs*. She steps out among the wreckage and tells the kobolds in front of her, in Common, *that there is nothing here*.
2. Mia then remembers the *trap plate* Nyx had warned the party about — the floor plate under where the chest had sat. She calls up her unseen hand of force and presses the plate from above.
3. ***[EPIC]*** The *hissing* rises in the chamber again. *Acid pours from every dragon mouth* in the walls; the spraying is followed by the *vapour cloud*. **Most of the kobolds in the chamber die**, choking in the spray or the gas; *four* survive and bolt. Mialee, caught by the spray, takes a hit of acid burn herself.
4. **Amidamaru**, unconscious on the floor, catches the spray and the gas with everyone else. The first lash of acid strips away the fragile hold that had kept him steady; the choking cloud behind it drives the next breath wrong in his chest. He is no longer merely down — he is *dying*.

## 10 - The long way out

1. Nyx, in her kobold disguise, walks out alongside the *kobold dragging Eguzkilore's body* — taking up the second arm to help carry the elf toward the entrance. Along the corridor she cuts the helper kobold's throat.
2. Nuberroca, returning at a dash from the bat-cave, meets a *kobold* in the corridor that calls out *"Soy Nyx"* in his face. He picks the creature up by the head, finds *the skull does not match the kobold* it should be under the illusion, and sets her down again. Together with Eguzkilore's body between them, they make for the cave entrance.
3. Mialee, *still in the kobold-illusion*, crosses the wreckage of the Tiamat chamber to **Amidamaru**, drops a *berry* into his mouth, and tells him to run — *back the way we came in, the path is clear*.
4. At the cave entrance, Sinsong receives Eguzkilore's body. He weaves a magical ward over the corpse to *halt decay* — ten days before the chance of resurrection closes. The dwarf declines to spend his remaining power on healing the others while there is still a body to keep.
5. The kobolds returning to the chamber, alerted by Mialee and Amidamaru's run, charge to intercept. Stones rain down from several angles at once; one catches Amidamaru on the run and **the tortle drops again**. Mia finds herself overtaken by the survivors closing on her position.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 9}}

\page

# Session 10 {{float:"right" 2025-04-13}}
{wide}

## 1 - Prayer or blood
_~11:00, 26 Marpenoth 1489 DR_

1. In the cave entrance the party has Eguzkilore's body laid down — Nyx, still disguised as a kobold, struggles to say it plainly: *he is gone*. Inside the temple of Tiamat, Amidamaru is sprawled out cold, barely breathing. Mialee, in kobold form, is nearby.
2. Nuberroca won't leave Eguzkilore lying there and bundles the body across his back with rope, slung like a pack.
3. The barbarian, *bleeding*, then asks Sinsong to heal him before he runs back inside. Sinsong demands prayer first: *"levanta las manos y grita 'Sinsong'"*. Nuberroca grabs him by the head and presses back — *"¿Por qué te tengo que rezar? ¿Quién eres tú? ¿Y por qué tienes esas marcas en la cabeza?"*. Sinsong's pitch: *"Soy una persona que cree que se pueden hacer las cosas bien, pero para eso necesito amigos que puedan acompañarme en este viaje"*.
4. Nyx breaks off, out of patience — *"estáis hablando mucho y no estáis haciendo nada"*. When Sinsong finally moves, he heals Nyx instead — *the one within easy reach* — leaving Nuberroca unhealed.
5. Sinsong folds an *emboldening bond* across the party. Nyx dashes into the cave to reach Amidamaru and Mialee; Sinsong sets off last and, with the others already out of sight, loses the trail in the dark.
6. Inside, Mialee slips Amidamaru a *healing potion* from his own belt-pouch; the tortle stirs, *back from the threshold*. She then turns and runs a clean blade through the kobold beside her — dropping all pretense. The wounded kobold can't square it — *a kobold striking a kobold* is *anathema* to their mentality — and the pack closes on her at once, blades finding the gaps from every side.
7. Amidamaru, on his feet again, brings his *infused* blade through the nearest kobold — *the strike kills, and green fire skips off the corpse to scorch the kobold beside it*. One down, one burning.
8. The kobold pack focuses on Amidamaru — blows landing from every angle as they close in, every swing finding home. **The tortle drops again**. Mialee, alone now against the remaining kobolds, slips behind a column to hide, but a kobold tracks her, *finds her*, and lands a clean hit. **She goes down.**

\column

## 2 - Gruesome rescue

1. Nuberroca reaches the shaft that drops to the Tiamat chamber and takes the descent the fast way — he steps off the edge without doubt, twists in mid-air, and catches enough rope to soften the landing. The fall stings — rope-burns across his palms, a scrape down one shin — then *a soft blue glow flickers across his skin and the scratches close over*, and the blood-fury already roaring through him swallows the rest.
2. The goliath dissolves into *a swirl of cloud* and re-forms at Amidamaru's shoulder thirty feet across the chamber. Then he recognizes Mialee too — *choking on her own blood, gasping for breath*. He has barely a step left to reach her, but there is a kobold in his way.
3. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca *hurls Eguzkilore's body at the kobold pressing Mialee* to clear his path — the off-balance throw goes wide and the body slams into the stone floor. *A sickening crack as an arm folds back in an unnatural way; teeth snap loose against the stone; a slow line of blood draws across the floor from the elf's slack mouth.*
4. Ignoring the kobold, the barbarian takes another step, pulls the bandages and salves from his belt-pouch and works on Mialee until her breathing steadies.
5. Nyx kneels beside Amidamaru and presses one of her *berries* into his mouth in a single quick motion. The tortle's eyes open again.
6. The last kobold breaks for the door. Nuberroca's greataxe meets it at the edge of the chamber — *the kobold's pieces tumble on the floor*. The fight is over.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 10}}
\page

## 3 - The lone explorer
_~11:30, 26 Marpenoth 1489 DR_

1.
