# Session 27 {{float:"right" 2025-11-02}}
{wide}

## 1 - The doppelganger unmasked
_~09:00?, 25 Nightal 1489 DR?_

1. The combat is already running — someone (**Mialee**, by what the group later confirms) had already thrown a whisper across the camp to the real Nyx, and the halfling in front of them had not been the one who answered [Td:261-272]. Amidamaru casts his own whisper now, north-east towards the family wagon, and the real Nyx answers from somewhere inside [Td:3-16]. He dashes into that wagon, slips behind the family's trunks [Td:17-21], and prises the biggest chest open: **Darla** is curled inside, unresponsive, immobile [Td:51-56].
2. The fake Nyx will not drop the act. She shouts at the party that *"vuestra falsa compañera no sobrevivirá si me hacéis daño"* [Td:164-167] and turns her bow on Nuberroca, who is out of rage and takes two shots clean [Td:85-89, Td:183-186]. Eguzkilore crosses the camp, catches her bodily and drops her speed to zero, then punches her in the face: *"¿dónde estáis?"* [Td:107-137, Td:144-146].
3. Amidamaru goes to her with a healing hand on the forehead to keep her upright [Td:170-174], then — eyes closed — presses the muzzle of his pistol against her leg and fires [Td:176-177]. She holds the lie: *"yo soy Nyx, hay una Nyx falsa que está intentando suplantarme, que no os diré dónde está, porque si no me dejáis escapar"* [Td:179-180]. Behind her, Sinsong holds the spirits he had conjured around himself, and the fake Nyx walks through them and tears open again [Td:237-240].
4. Mialee works at the second big chest with her unseen hand — the lock gives quietly — and finds the real **Nyx** inside: curled, half-dressed, barely reacting [Td:298-325]. Amidamaru gives her a sip of water; she stirs but does not come round [Td:314-321].
5. Nuberroca swings on the fake Nyx recklessly [Td:337-343], then flares across the camp in a step of cloud to come down beside the real Nyx and strike at her double again [Td:344-345]. The doppelganger carries a brutal counter-blow back into him — a long tear across his shoulder — and he spends the cultist strapped to his chest as a shield; the man's head opens, brains and blood on Nuberroca's own front [Td:373-398]. The fake Nyx, still bleeding, pleads: *"dejadme en paz, malditos bastardos, yo solo quiero vivir"* [Td:429].
6. Kaelthar reaches out with his will and the fake Nyx goes rigid, paralysed where she stands [Td:434-453]. Amidamaru closes the gap, puts the pistol to her forehead, and fires at point-blank [Td:460-469].
7. ***[EPIC]*** Her head bursts [Td:492-493]. The halfling shape loses hold and swells into a loose, human-sized form, blue-skinned and dark-blue, features unformed, the clothes straining at the seams [Td:494-509].

## 2 - Two drugged women

1. Amidamaru lifts the real Nyx out of the chest and carries her outside into the air [Td:600-601]. Eguzkilore picks Darla up on his wolf and takes her out separately, bound against mistaken identity [Td:617-623]. Neither woman has any visible wound [Td:536-539]; both read as drugged, not poisoned [Td:316, Td:553-556].
2. Kaelthar lays hands on Nyx but at his level the gift only lifts poison, which is not what she has [Td:549-556]. Sinsong offers a prayer that *might* reach her — he needs the faith of the whole group — and asks that the party accept his stones and his cause before he tries [Td:596-598, Td:712-715]. The party will not; the offer dies there [Td:720-726].
3. Amidamaru stands over Nyx and feels for any magic held on her — nothing [Td:749-750]. He whispers across to her mind and asks: she tells him she noted a prick, could not move after that, and that whoever held her has been injecting her every day [Td:739-745]. He names the drug a daily dose at about twenty-four hours and tells the others the only cure he can see is waiting it out [Td:747-751].
4. Darla is clamped in shackles [Td:762-768]. Amidamaru wipes the dead doppelganger clean with a flick of will [Td:633-634 ?ord], pockets a short sword, a longbow (Eguzkilore's old one, back from her), armour and clothes, and gives the lot to Nyx when she is awake enough to take it [Td:645-651].
5. The camp is shaken. Nobody is cooking — people are gathered around the body of the doppelganger — and the merchants have run out of questions once they see it [Td:768-782]. The caravan sends dinner and beer across to the party in thanks, more open-handed than usual [Td:784-787], and **Baird** stands them to the drinks himself [Td:875]. The watches are posted [Td:807-813]; the night passes without further event and the party take a long rest at dawn [Td:874-883]. Nyx is still deep in the drug's hold — exhausted and only slowly coming back; each night will peel one layer off [Td:886-890].

## 3 - A knife in Carlon's back
_~08:00?, 26 Nightal 1489 DR_

Murder most foul -> This should happen the 27th

1. By morning Darla's shackles are empty on the ground — not forced, simply slipped — the group has clamped a shape-changer in an iron that opens for any shape she likes [Td:896-901]. And by the chest wagon lies **Carlon** the harpist, dead on the ground, a single short-sword puncture in the back from behind [Td:902-905]. The doppelganger worked with its fists [Td:904-905]; this is someone else.
2. **Elfking** comes over and examines the body — the man was leaning into the treasure wagon when it was done — then turns his attention openly to Mialee and the short sword hanging from her belt [Td:947-957]. Miguel and Fenwir are already saying it aloud to the other guards: these adventurers are not going to hand the loot over, they killed Carlon to keep it [Td:974-979, Td:997-1007]. Amidamaru works the crowd and reads the murmur as unled — nobody is directing it [Td:1041-1043].
3. Nuberroca, Eguzkilore, and Kaelthar take the wolf and go to track the doppelganger. Beside where Darla's shackles lay Nuberroca picks up tiny prints — gnome- or halfling-sized — that four or five paces further become full human prints heading for the trees [Td:926-937 ?ord]. They follow into the wood but Nuberroca cannot hold the trail and they lose it [Td:1047-1051].
4. Nuberroca comes back furious. He confronts Miguel first, finds he has the wrong man, apologises, and goes to **Fenwir** instead — fists in his face, demanding what rumours he is spreading [Td:1064-1082]. Nothing shifts.
5. The hooded man who travels alone — until this moment silent — speaks up for the first time: if there are no witnesses, let the gods take vengeance on the guilty [Td:1088-1092]. The guards drop it and resume their posts [Td:1101-1104]. On the way back to his wagon Eguzkilore passes the two tied captives — **Checo** and **Mane** — and the wolf's tail swats them in the face as he goes [Td:1116-1121].
6. Nuberroca puts the whole thing to rest from the box of his cart: *"vuestro intelecto y vuestras palabras no sirven para resolver los problemas"* [Td:1105]; they will keep moving to Waterdeep [Td:1106].

## 4 - Jamna the Harper
_~07:00?, 27 Nightal 1489 DR_

1. The day on the road passes without incident; by next morning the caravan has Waterdeep in sight on the horizon, about two days from the gates [Td:1136-1138]. On the road, Amidamaru goes forward to the small gnome woman from Daggerford — the Harper the party will come to know as **Jamna** — and introduces himself over breakfast [Td:1213-1218]. He apologises for Sinsong's suspicion the day before [Td:1217-1219]. She accepts the apology — awkwardly, she does not know him — and asks how she can help [Td:1221-1224].
2. She lays it out: *"tenemos diferentes jefes pero mismos objetivos"* [Td:1225-1227]. She was in the caravan to pick out the Cult of the Dragon operators and neutralise them, and the party has already done that work for her [Td:1227-1230].
3. The party confirms Castle Naerytar as the destination Amidamaru overheard the cultist soldiers name [Td:1245-1246]. Jamna has heard of it too — she believes it is in the Marismas, the Mere of Dead Men, from an older reference tying it to the Order of the Astrologers' holdings there [Td:1235-1250]. She knows the party belong to the Order of the Gauntlet — all except Mialee and Nyx, who name themselves as Harpers [Td:1256-1261]. She is a Harper herself; different cells of the same organisation [Td:1266-1278].
4. She confirms the handler: *"Si no me confundo, **Leosin** será el que ha hablado"* [Td:1278-1281]. Amidamaru confirms Leosin sent them after this caravan [Td:1282].
5. The plan: when the caravan reaches Waterdeep in two days, Jamna will take the chests on to Leosin and the Order of the Gauntlet, with a positive report of the party [Td:1286-1289]. The party should go straight for the north gate and head on to the **Posada de Carnath** — a roadhouse on the road to Neverwinter which Jamna believes is the cultists' handover point and which is seeing new foot traffic because *Lord Neverember* is rebuilding it after a catastrophe over the Winter [Td:1291-1306]. She has not been able to confirm it directly; her last cultist caravan — brought in by one *Bael* — was lost before it reached the point [Td:1312-1315]. Beyond Carnath they always lose the thread [Td:1316-1320].

## 5 - The heart in the mage's pocket

1. The party's attention lands on the other hooded figure in the caravan — the one who had spoken for them that morning. Nyx walks the caravan apologising to the merchants about her strange behaviour the previous days — *"no era yo, ¿sabéis?"* — using the crowd as cover [Td:1502-1511], while Mialee works an unseen hand into the hooded man's pocket from the far side [Td:1512, Td:1521-1528]. The pocket holds an insane weight of coins, more than fits in a jacket [Td:1532-1535].
2. Below the coins she finds something fist-sized, soft, that gives under her fingers [Td:1538-1540]; a small pouch with pellets inside [Td:1540-1542]; and two folded scraps of parchment, inked in a script none of them read, more sigil than letter [Td:1583-1587]. She lifts one of the parchments on the hand up over the crowd [Td:1581-1583]. The man is beginning to notice [Td:1580].
3. Mialee cleans the hand off when it comes back [Td:1546-1548]. They whisper across to Nyx about the fist-sized thing: it is a heart — too big for a halfling, perhaps human, perhaps pig [Td:1552-1559].
4. They go to Jamna with what they have. She confirms it: Red Wizards of Thay are usually bald and tattooed, and the daggers they carry leave wounds no magic will close [Td:1644-1646]. She cannot place the heart [Td:1649]. If this really is **Azbara Jos** [Td:1676] — a name the party does not catch cleanly — then he is one of the most powerful mages alive, and the party is to be very careful of him [Td:1687-1689]. Nuberroca presses the group for a reason to attack if they are not sure he is the villain; the party does not commit [Td:1691-1694].

## 6 - What the blue dwarf left in Nuberroca

1. Later on the road Nuberroca pulls Nyx, Mialee, and Eguzkilore aside, out of Jamna's earshot [Td:1733-1735], and tells them a secret. Since the blue dwarf hurt his head and showed him the vision of the undead walking into his village, something has been changing in him [Td:1737-1746].
2. ***[EPIC]*** He opens his fist. The veins across his hand turn from blue to black [Td:1741]; a dark halo of smoke peels off the knuckles [Td:1742-1743]. He bends and brushes a wildflower by the roadside with the back of his hand and the flower blackens, withers, and crumbles [Td:1797-1798].
3. He tells them he does not know what it is. Something guides his anger now, and when Sinsong once put a note on his back something inside him pushed out and tried to tear it off [Td:1763-1766].
4. Amidamaru pats him on the shoulder and feels him for any magic held or lingering — nothing [Td:1771-1773]. Eguzkilore tells Nuberroca that when they reach Waterdeep he should have someone look at it [Td:1782-1783]. Nuberroca agrees but names the darkness in himself as something he does not like [Td:1784-1785].

## 7 - Torch in the fog
_~22:00?, 28 Nightal 1489 DR_

1. The GM tells the party they have one more day of travel ahead and will reach Waterdeep by the morning of the 29th [Td:1714-1716]. The day passes without major events [Td:1842-1846]. During the night Nyx and Eguzkilore have the watch when a torch comes toward the camp through a cold, thick fog [Td:1847-1868]. Eguzkilore reads the weather — the coming days are to be clear, which makes the fog around them stranger [Td:1866-1874]. He steps out of the arc of light and calls: *"¿quién va ahí por última vez?"* [Td:1849]. He cannot see anything beyond the torch itself [Td:1851-1855].
2. Nyx takes a ray of cold in the back from the direction of the torch and sees roughly where it came from [Td:1890-1900]. Eguzkilore wheels and runs for the sleeping camp — a long, hard dash to the sleepers and a longer one to shake them awake [Td:1909-1915]. Nyx throws down a shimmer of fey light into the fog as a marker and knows now where the mage stands [Td:1930-1940].
3. A black-mantled mage walks out of the fog and throws three ribbons of fire at Nyx — she takes the whole set and nearly goes [Td:1988-1990, Td:2082-2085]. Amidamaru shoves her up onto Nuberroca's shoulder [Td:2095-2097]. Nyx fires back — her shot would have hit, but the arrow rebounds off a wall of light at the mage's chest [Td:2100-2102]. Nuberroca steps out from behind the cart, flashes across the camp in a step of cloud to land in front of him [Td:2115-2123], and his first swing meets a magical barrier and slides off [Td:2125-2134].
4. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca gives the strike over to the dark aura. The blue lines along the shaft of the lance darken; black smoke runs down the wood; the point drives in and the mage grasps at his own chest, burning [Td:2140-2145]. The mage holds himself upright, meets Nuberroca's eye, and says: *"Sois más fuertes de lo que pensaba. Pero no estáis solos, tenéis más enemigos de los que creéis. Rezmir escuchará de esto. Nos vemos más adelante."* [Td:2155-2158]. A plane-cut opens between them like a strip of millimetre film, closes a breath later, and the mage is gone [Td:2165-2170].

## 8 - Anton vanishes with the horses
_~06:00?, 29 Nightal 1489 DR_

1. Dawn comes while they sort themselves out [Td:2185-2186]. The two guards on watch have been knifed from behind in positions a long step from where they bedded down — they had been moved before they were killed [Td:2190-2191]. The two prisoners are dead in their bindings [Td:2000-2002]. One of their own wagons is gone; the horses have all been cut loose and run off [Td:2193-2194]. **Anton** — the human the caravan had called friend, the man Nuberroca had been protecting on his own cart — is gone with the wagon [Td:2196-2197].
2. Nuberroca comes awake into it furious, pisses on the tent frame to clear his head [Td:2050], and then the whole caravan — guards, merchants, party — goes out together to run down the horses [Td:2228-2229]. They have most of them back by nine-twenty, the stragglers through the morning [Td:2216-2222].
3. The lost wagon has stayed on the road, forward or back the party cannot tell [Td:2236-2237]. They are four hours out from Waterdeep's gates and the day is already open; they call the session here and will plan the city at the table next time [Td:2258-2263].

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 27}}

\page

# Session 28 {{float:"right" 2025-11-09}}
{wide}

## 1 - Dawn on the road
_~09:30?, 29 Nightal 1489 DR_

1. The caravan picks itself up on the road to Waterdeep [Td:1-10]. **Anton** has gone with their wagon and the horses have only just been brought in; the two prisoners are dead in their bindings; the merchants are intact and want to move. The bodies will be left on the pile the party has already been dragging behind them [Td:21-24]. Mialee levels a long glare at **Miguel** and **Fenwir**, the two Greenest guards who had been talking the day before about her stealing the cart; neither answers it [Td:13-18].
2. Nobody has horses to chase the stolen wagon with [Td:50]. Amidamaru sends his familiar **Munin** forward along the road to find where the wagon goes [Td:73-82]; when the gates are in sight later the bird is nowhere above the carts queued at the arch [Td:589-590].
3. On the move again, the party rakes over the plan — deliver the chests to the Harpers in Waterdeep, press north to the roadhouse at **Carnath** [Td:140-165]. Mialee fumbles trying to recover the handler's name — *"Eloísi... Erosin"* — the elf monk Jamna had named in S27 [Td:181-186].

## 2 - A quiet word with Nyx

1. Half-way to the gates Amidamaru pulls Nyx aside from the carts for a moment [Td:308-311]. He asks after her; she waves it off; he lays a hand on her and heals the last of the beating she took in the chest [Td:315-316]. She protests — he has already cast [Td:317-321].
2. He pours the whole thing out to her — *"¿Qué hacemos siguiendo esta caravana? ... Teníamos una misión anterior muy tranquila, en paz, y venimos a meternos en algo que secuestran, nos torturan, estamos a punto de morir día tras día"* [Td:332-336]. He calls the rest of them *"una manga de inadaptados sociales"* — *"uno se cree Maradona, el otro un pitufo con esteroides, el otro un T-Rex que sabe hablar"* [Td:337-338]. His brothers are off looking for a place safe from the dragons; he could be with them, not here [Td:342-346].
3. Nyx holds her ground quietly. They have helped many; a barbarian village was saved; the road has their blood on it but it is still the right road [Td:348-358]. She will not rest easy knowing the cult is still at the party's back [Td:371-374]. She hands him an inspiration for the honesty of the ask [Td:370].

## 3 - A hooded rider ahead of the gates
_~11:30?_

1. The gates of Waterdeep come up a mile and a half out — four or five carts queued at the arch, guards on the walls and movement along the battlements [Td:383-385]. A single lone rider on a big black destrier waits a few hundred paces short of the arch, perpendicular to the road, watching the caravan come [Td:385-386, Td:420].
2. Sinsong picks the shape out at distance by something he recognises in it — *"Nuberroca, es que no vamos a ser capaces. Ni a tontazos."* [Td:449-456]. As the carts close in, the others make him out: hooded, red-cloaked, flame-sigils and other arcane marks on the cloth that none of them can place [Td:654-659].
3. Sinsong asks to be hidden before the elf's eye lands on him. Amidamaru lays a hand on his shoulder and the dwarf disappears — the spell will hold about an hour [Td:456-467]. Sinsong climbs down into the family cart and pulls the blanket over himself [Td:466-467, Td:615-616]; Amidamaru whispers across his mind — *"no sólo quédate callado, sino quédate quieto"* [Td:526-529].
4. Nuberroca, who is told only that they are hiding Sinsong, loops a cord round his own chest and takes Amidamaru up onto his back as if the tortle were his next *bebé*, replacing the dead cultist from the night before [Td:555-562]. The merchants look once and look away — the fools' legend has grown that much [Td:563-568].
5. **Baird** calls the guard-fees out along the road while they walk — Kaelthar five, Nyx ten, Eguzkilore eight, Mialee fifteen, Amidamaru ten; Nuberroca gratis, a joke the barbarian lets stand [Td:596-603]. Kaelthar's wolf is hooded under cloth and his own hood pulled up — the civil quarter does not love a dragonborn or a loose beast [Td:625-644].

## 4 - Elodin's tale
_~12:00?_

1. The rider lets them close. He pulls the hood back — an elf; eyes injected red to the whites; froth at the corner of his mouth. *"A vosotros os conozco. ¿Dónde está Sinsong? Llevo esperando. En Waterdeep nadie le ha visto. Ni dijisteis que estaba aquí."* [Td:671-679]. Mialee buys them a day — the dwarf will meet them tomorrow in the plaza [Td:680-687].
2. He falls in alongside the carts: *"Vale, iré con vosotros"* [Td:688]. Pressed on why — *"Tengo que matarle. Es un ser vago, manipulador. Él condenó a Blandea y muchos de ellos están petrificados por su culpa."* [Td:706-713].
3. He gives his tale as they walk. A dark stone turned up in a mine in the Far Hills; the villagers who touched it sickened and slowly turned to stone [Td:719-730]. By his telling a madman convinced Sinsong to use the stone, and Sinsong turned nearly the whole of his village into stone himself [Td:731-734]. Elodin was turned to stone when he came after; a *patrona* he met in the dark cut him a deal and brought him back — the price is Sinsong's blood for the people's lives [Td:739-744]. *"Se ganó mi confianza y fingió ser mi amigo y cuando bajé la guardia me intentó asesinar."* [Td:742-743]. If this ends in a sword he will take the party with him: *"vosotros seréis los que pagáis sus errores."* [Td:755-757].
4. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca enters rage and levels the long polearm at the elf's face [Td:758-760]. The elf's sword lights with a wrapped black light and takes the shaft in two across the middle [Td:762-764]. Elodin keeps the point against him: *"si me buscáis, me encontráis"* [Td:765]. Nuberroca grips the broken shaft back: *"con amenazas no funcionamos aquí, elfo"* [Td:766].
5. Mialee reaches for whatever magic hangs on him — a mage's armour and the sword itself magical [Td:770-786]. The party catches breath on the move, enough that Nuberroca pulls a few points back from the earlier bruising [Td:792-798].

## 5 - A spoken command
_~12:30?_

1. Elodin spends a spell on Nuberroca at a glance. *"Cálmate, escúchame y acompáñame a la plaza hasta que aparezca Sinsong."* [Td:873-879, Td:893]. Nuberroca cannot shake the pull of it; the rage goes out of him and he walks on meekly, a half-zombie led by the elf's command [Td:884-905].
2. Mialee is the only one at the carts who sees the spell cast [Td:902-905]. The others see only that the barbarian stopped in his tracks and walks beside the elf like a servant [Td:905-907].
3. Mialee reads it and does not force the fight — the elf plainly has more than they could trade blows for in the open [Td:946-953]. She tells Nyx the plan: let Nuberroca walk with the elf to the plaza and bring him the beer up there, keep eyes on him the whole way [Td:949-957].

## 6 - Through the arch

1. Guards at the arch stop the first merchants and ask their business [Td:971-984]. Mialee gives them **Nyx**'s name — *"Soy Nyx, somos una caravana de comerciantes desde Baldur's Gate"* [Td:977-981]. Guards count the carts, do not press, wave them in [Td:994-998].
2. Eguzkilore stops at Darla's cart on the way through for the two large empty chests that had held her and the real Nyx [Td:1003-1025]. A guard asks what is in them; Eguzkilore gives the only honest answer available: *"no, están vacíos"* [Td:1022-1023]. The guard half-helps, half-shrugs, and waves him through [Td:1030-1033].
3. Inside the arch Jamna presses up to Mialee one more time — the gold on the cart is for the Harpers and she does not trust Mialee to hand it off herself; she will walk with the cart to see the drop made [Td:1035-1053]. Mialee agrees to witnesses.
4. The caravan dissolves at the gate — each merchant collects what is owed and goes [Td:1054-1056].

## 7 - A knife on the orphanage road
_~13:30?_

1. ***[EPIC]*** On the way out of the plaza with the cart Mialee feels a long tear across the ribs — a short-sword in from behind [Td:1290-1292]. A second swing follows and misses [Td:1293]. **Jamna** has the blade: *"este oro es para los arpistas, y no confío en ti lo más mínimo"* — the gold is for the Harpers, and the party will not be the ones delivering it [Td:1298-1299].
2. Mialee pulls her reserved turn out of her sleeve. *"Has cometido un error"* — and she blinks away in a step to a different position on the street [Td:1537-1540]. On her next pass she comes back on Jamna with sneak and dagger, non-lethal; the gnome drops at the cart's wheel [Td:1655-1683 ?ord].
3. Mialee strips her: a short sword and eight hidden daggers [Td:1848-1849 ?ord]; a few gold pieces in a purse; and tucked away in a bag, several of the cult coins that had been in the chests the caravan was escorting [Td:1839-1842 ?ord]. The Harper story was a cover of some kind. Mialee binds her, gags her, and shuts her into one of the empty chests Darla had just handed over; moves the cart on toward the plaza [Td:1824-1853 ?ord].

## 8 - The elf finds Sinsong
_~13:45?_

1. In the central plaza — a wide square ringed with gilded taverns and a fountain the party will not stop quoting [Td:1146-1152] — Nyx picks the Compañía del Jabalí paladin out of a tavern window, the one Amidamaru calls *pececito dorado* for the fungi-field performance in S20 [Td:1176-1178, Td:1211-1212]. They note him and keep walking.
2. Nuberroca, still under command, is paraded through the square with Elodin at his shoulder [Td:1399-1403]. Sinsong tries to whisper to Nuberroca from his hiding-corner; the macro misfires twice before the message lands [Td:1368-1395]. Elodin's eye lands on him and he screams the name across the plaza, drawing the great sword [Td:1413-1419].
3. ***[EPIC]*** Two orbs of purple energy fire from the blade's edge [Td:1420-1429]. They burst against the corner-stones where Sinsong has taken cover; a bystander catches shrapnel. The square empties in a breath [Td:1429-1431].

## 9 - A guard is dissolved

1. The fight spills into the market-streets. Eguzkilore shoots first; the arrow slips off a thin halo of light at the elf's chest and does not draw blood [Td:1462-1483]. Nyx throws a bank of fog across the junction to break line of sight [Td:1554-1567] and Sinsong wraps himself in a protective ward [Td:1778]. When the net begins to close Sinsong grows wings from his back and climbs to a rooftop, out of reach [Td:1976-1991].
2. Kaelthar's steed takes shape at his side [Td:1591]. Nuberroca pulls up close to the elf, the broken long polearm swapped for a one-handed spear and shield now [Td:1613-1619], and tries to talk him down: *"pero relájate, hombre. La violencia no te va a solucionar los problemas."* [Td:1600-1620, Td:1746-1748].
3. ***[EPIC]*** Six guards led by a sergeant come into the square from the east [Td:1937-1944]. The elf picks the first out of their line and points a bare hand at him. A ribbon of green light leaves it and the man starts to melt from inside with poison running through him; he goes down in a pile and dies within seconds [Td:1993-2001].
4. Nyx tries to grapple him on the way through and misses by a hand [Td:2089-2098]; a guard's blade clips him across the back on the opportunity [Td:2100-2117]. Elodin shouts across the empty square — *"¡Sinsong, cobarde! ¡Te encontraré!"* [Td:2123-2125].
5. Kaelthar comes down the street at full stride, vow called on the elf, and lands a crushing strike on the first swing [Td:1903 ?ord, Td:2211-2265]. Eguzkilore sends two more arrows after it in the same breath, the second one clean through [Td:2340-2349]. The elf falls limp in the dust at the sergeant's feet [Td:2350-2351].

## 10 - The elf slips the net

1. The sergeant cuffs Elodin, drops a net over him, and props him enough to stabilise but no further [Td:2317-2320]. Mialee walks the elf's great sword out of the arrest circle with an unseen hand along the ground [Td:2373-2383].
2. The elf comes round long enough to curse Sinsong and mutter a prayer to a name none of them catch, in a tongue none of them know, asking help in finishing the dwarf [Td:2362-2367].
3. ***[EPIC]*** He speaks three more words — *"Kal Ort Por"* — a thin white line opens in the air before him, swallows him whole, and closes behind him [Td:2487-2494]. The sergeant stares at the empty net on the ground [Td:2500-2502]. The party has seen that exit once already — the Red Wizard **Azbara Jos** had used it to leave the camp on the Daggerford road [Td:2496-2499].
4. The sergeant turns on them. A man has been killed in his square today, and the elf is the one he wants for it [Td:2559-2588]. Nuberroca accepts the question and tells him what he saw of Elodin — an elf, strong, hunting the dwarf by his cart; said he was his patron's man and would have the dwarf's blood [Td:2559-2574]. The sergeant takes the sword and takes the dead man and goes [Td:2581-2588].

## 11 - Far Hills

1. The guards leave the plaza. The party gathers in a side-street. Sinsong, down from the roof at last, opens the cloth bundle at his belt and unwraps a small dark stone — enough that the others can see, no further; nobody is to touch it. Fifteen days to turn to stone, no cure known. Half his village is as it was when he left [Td:2650-2663].
2. He gives the long version at last. The stone was turned up in a mine in the **Far Hills**. His master went to find a cure and touched the stone himself. Sinsong rode to **Candlekeep** to read; he rode to the elf hold at **Myth Drannor** and took the hospitality of a wizard called **Elodin**; between them they found a plant said to lift the curse. Elodin touched it experimenting and began to petrify himself. The next night Sinsong saw a figure in the dark cutting a deal with Elodin for his life. Elodin woke the next day cured — eyes red to the whites — and lit the room with a crack of purple energy as Sinsong fled. The brand on Sinsong's bald scalp dates from that night [Td:2664-2673].
3. Nyx admits she had searched his pack months ago, seen the stone, and so believed him. She did not touch it [Td:2675-2690].
4. Nuberroca takes a pinch of grit from his belt — the *arenilla* of Sinsong's own stone, the one he had destroyed under earlier watch — and lets it fall through his fingers like salt. *"Lo importante es conservar los amigos, Sinsong."* [Td:2706-2712]. Sinsong hands Kaelthar a platinum for the save in the fight and holds the thanks back from Nuberroca [Td:2602-2614 ?ord].

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 28}}

\page

# Session 29 {{float:"right" 2025-11-30}}
{wide}

## 1 - The tortle leaves the road
_~14:30?, 29 Nightal 1489 DR_

1. Still in the Waterdeep square where **Elodin** cut the air and vanished, the party takes stock. The combat ended with the elf immobilised and gone through a thin white line, a spell none of them placed save that the word *"Kal Ort Por"* carried through it — the same phrase **Azbara** had used on the Daggerford road [Td:3-9].
2. **Amidamaru** calls it there. The tortle has family on the road looking for a place safe from the dragons, three months out at least, maybe four, and he will go to them; sending-stone word can reach him if the party has urgent magic to ask of him [Td:11-24]. He walks away from the square; the party's mage is gone.
3. The square is thick with guards now. The dead man Elodin melted into the stones in the fight is a Waterdeep uniform, and the patrols along the walls have doubled — not pressing the party, just walking [Td:27-37].

## 2 - A rogue in the tavern

1. Mialee makes for *the tavern of the boar* on a hunch; the Compañía del Jabalí told them weeks ago it was their place [Td:39-44].
2. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca does not wait. He clouds a step across the common room and lands at the back of a man he picks out at a crowded table, one hand on the man's shoulder, the other across the throat. The blue arms go round the neck before the man can turn [Td:56-73]. *"Piensa bien tus próximas acciones, pequeña Renata"* [Td:76].
3. The man is **Jorath Vexundar**, the Compañía rogue who had sold them out at the fungal fields — the fiesta boys the party have not forgotten. He goes white, surrenders his weight, and begins at once to spin — the big legend from Greenest, the heroes who closed up the dragons, the dear friends he has been talking up all week to the merchants gathered round him for a Baldur's Gate contract [Td:77-102, Td:160-181].
4. Nyx watches it from the door and reads the shape of the con. She crosses to the barman and lays a gold piece on the counter — *"estoy buscando a este y a sus amigos, ¿los conoces?"* [Td:198-199]. The barman takes the coin and gives the straight answer: Jorath has been there a few days, two companions in and out — one in armour, one in elegant clothes; the big-eater legend has been Jorath's steady pitch [Td:203-211].
5. A guard pushes in on the murmur; Sinsong crosses, speaks the small blessing of a spell under his breath, and talks the guard into a nothing — a minor scene, friends greeting friends, nobody being held against his will [Td:329-347]. The guard shrugs it off and goes.

## 3 - Nyx tells Nuberroca to put him down

1. In a side table Nyx takes Nuberroca aside — the barbarian still carries Jorath by the arm [Td:296-320]. The plan is not a tavern brawl. Let the rogue finish his pitch, let the merchants put the gold on the table, and take the purse off him when he leaves the room; return it to the merchants or keep what feels right as the price of the trouble [Td:306-336].
2. Nuberroca accepts and lets Jorath go on his word; the barbarian plants himself at a wall with a beer and watches [Td:402-411]. Jorath goes back to his table and resumes the show, the price now floated at 200 gold for both companies to ride the Baldur's Gate run that very night [Td:370-378].

## 4 - A window and a chase
_~15:30?_

1. Jorath, thirsty, calls for the barman's attention without paying and climbs the stairs toward the toilets, saying his throat is dry [Td:549-561]. Nyx goes up behind him; Kaelthar takes the stairs; Nuberroca stays below [Td:562-576].
2. From the landing Nyx hears a shutter bang open and no footstep after; the door is locked. She breaks it with her shoulder on the second try and finds the window swinging and the man gone over the sill, climbing down the back wall [Td:609-635, Td:657-662]. She throws herself after him [Td:670].
3. Down in the common room Nuberroca, confused on the chain of the thing, picks up a nearby beggar off a bench like a sack of potatoes and carries him toward the door — *comida, comida* — the barman draws a sword from under the bar and jumps across to intercept, taking a moment to blink at the goliath holding a confused man under one arm. Sinsong grapples the barman, the beggar is set down with an apology, and nobody loses a limb to the misunderstanding [Td:634-669, Td:689-702].
4. On the street Nyx takes the pursuit; Kaelthar runs up on his wolf at double her stride; Sinsong follows after setting the barman down; Nuberroca stays at the tavern with his beer and the confused beggar; Mialee is still at the orphanage [Td:691-702, Td:772-786, Td:836-838, Td:859-868]. Jorath limps from the drop and does not get far [Td:671-674]; Nyx overhauls him and lays hands on him [Td:789-801].
5. He draws a short sword and gashes her forearm when she takes his wrist — the strike is for release, not kill [Td:802-808]. She holds. The others close in.

## 5 - A guard is called

1. Rifling the man's pockets Sinsong lifts a smooth sphere he does not recognise and a purse heavier than a thief's should be; the Compañía insignia catches a thumb, loose coppers [Td:870-881, Td:903-910]. Jorath bargains hard — twenty gold if they will let him walk — *"nunca os he hecho daño, nunca os he atacado, por favor, dejadme en paz"* [Td:886-892]; Nyx will not stake her name on the deal [Td:924-933].
2. Sinsong closes it with a trick: a Waterdeep guard insignia from Elodin's earlier work in the square passes from Sinsong's hand to Nyx [Td:516-517 ?ord, Td:882]; the dwarf drops a word of sleep over Jorath and the rogue folds where he stands, the insignia slipped into his pocket while he goes under [Td:874, Td:920, Td:928-942]. *"Yo estoy buscando un guardia"* [Td:943] — Nyx walks the first patrol down the lane to the body on the stones [Td:946]. Sinsong steps in with the story — the man took a Waterdeep insignia off a dead guard, he saw it himself [Td:954, Td:957-958]. The guard searches, finds the planted insignia, cuffs the limp rogue, and marches him off [Td:960-968].
3. The barman, still back at the tavern, leans out of the broken window and bellows at the empty street about his door; nobody answers him and he gives up on it [Td:972-978]. Nyx permits herself a quiet satisfaction [Td:978].

## 6 - Rooftop watchers
_~16:30?_

1. The party trickles together on the street — Nuberroca walking back from his beer with his head tilted at the eaves, convinced an *amigo* in the city has warned him to watch the roofs; Mialee drifting in from the north with word from her people [Td:987-1000, Td:1011].
2. Mialee carries it: her little birds at the orphanage put a watcher on a rooftop during the fight with Elodin earlier that day, and the word on the street is that someone is paying good coin to put the party in the ground. No name, no face. Watch the roofs [Td:1017-1030].
3. Nuberroca mentions he is short a weapon — the elf cut his lance in two in the square. Mialee produces the great sword she walked out of the arrest circle in the aftermath of that fight, no longer carrying the black light but still a well-made length of steel, and hands it to him; the goliath takes it and sets it on his shoulder [Td:1037-1055].

## 7 - At the Plaza del Pueblo
_~17:00?_

1. The party moves to the market square to spend what little coin they have — herbalist, potions, armoury, jeweller, all under one plaza [Td:1112-1116]. Nuberroca sells Elodin's sword for 25 gold and buys a lance for 2, keeping the balance; the pike would have been eighteen pounds and the breastplate he eyes is sixty gold he does not have [Td:1193-1270].
2. Mialee buys one greater and two basic healing potions for 300 gold [Td:1332-1334]; Kaelthar counts his purse and buys nothing — *"pobres como ratas, sois aquí todos"* [Td:1375-1377]. The talk turns to a diamond for revivify, 300 gold the minimum and the jeweller only carries 100-piece and 1000-piece cuts — split a thousand and perhaps three of three hundred come out, perhaps not [Td:1382-1456].
3. Mialee demonstrates her honest trade in open air. She closes her palm, murmurs a small working, and two pieces of platinum lift out of Sinsong's pouch and settle into Nuberroca's — *redistribuyo la riqueza*, she says, straight-faced, while the jeweller looks on [Td:1510-1527]. Nobody but the party sees the hand that carried them [Td:1515-1521].
4. Nuberroca lays a Cult-of-the-Dragon medallion on the counter and reads the jeweller's face when the dragon figure crosses it — nothing comes back, no spark of knowledge; the elegant piece is worth 25 gold to the man, the worn one worthless [Td:1633-1640]. Sinsong tries his own black stone from the Far Hills on the counter; the jeweller turns it over, calls it nothing he knows, and hands it back [Td:1619-1622]. Mialee reaches for a word of suggestion to put a crack in the man and the jeweller catches the shape of the spell forming — *"por favor, en mi tienda de magia no"* — and asks them to leave [Td:1591-1602].

## 8 - Three errands at dusk
_~18:00?_

1. The party splits for the two hours of daylight remaining [Td:1699-1711]. Mialee and Sinsong to the tavern tables for dice; Nuberroca and Kaelthar to the fighting-pits for prize-fights; Nyx and Eguzkilore to the north quarter to look for the cult.
2. At the tables Mialee plays the first game straight and wins twenty gold off a guard; the second she wins again, the third she loses to a veteran who sits down with fifty gold in front of him. She clears a touch over fifty, more than she brought [Td:1757-1795, Td:1860-1893].
3. At the ring Kaelthar steps into a strength contest against a thin, wiry elf and takes the loss, his supporters down ten gold apiece [Td:1865-1893]. Nuberroca goes next, strikes true, and leaves the elf on the straw with a broken rib [Td:1896-1927]; the house pays him twenty-five gold for the fight and five for the manner of it. Mialee, Sinsong, and Kaelthar each take seventy-five back on the bet [Td:1927-1937].
4. North of the plaza Nyx and Eguzkilore walk the Neverwinter-caravan quarter — Lord Neverember's crest on half the wagons, the long rebuild of the road to Neverwinter underway and crews hiring from every corner [Td:1972-1990]. Nyx catches sight of the stolen wagon in a fenced private lot off the main road, well-watched; Eguzkilore stays to keep an eye on it while Nyx goes back to pull the rest in [Td:2000-2007, Td:2092-2104].

## 9 - A diamond, cut hard

1. The group pools everything at the jeweller's before closing — the twelve-hundred of mixed coin, Nuberroca's lapis lazuli, Mialee's small gems, a 25-gold bottle of fine wine Mialee drops on the counter when her gems come up short [Td:2032-2048]. The jeweller takes the thousand and two hundred for the labour and promises the diamond cut by morning [Td:2051-2057].
2. The split comes out at two diamonds of 400 gold apiece and 200 gold worth of diamond dust — no third stone of 300, the labour did what the labour could [Td:2059-2068]. Sinsong takes custody; the diamonds live in his belt-pouch, since he is the only cleric at the table who can call them to work [Td:2062-2064, Td:2134-2143].

## 10 - A bolt from the dark
_~22:00?_

1. ***[EPIC]*** The five of them — Eguzkilore still at the wagon-watch — are back on the street talking out where to sleep when the first bolt comes out of the dark. It takes Nuberroca through the shoulder and the burn in it is not steel — an unfamiliar venom that reaches deep [Td:2178-2185, Td:2412]. A second bolt follows at the throat; the goliath drops in the street without seeing his attacker [Td:2199-2203]. A third skids off and plants in Kaelthar's wolf, which is standing beside him [Td:2215-2219].
2. The shooter is on a rooftop eighty feet west in the lightless lane, and nobody at ground level can place him [Td:2206-2219, Td:2264-2267]. Kaelthar closes the ground to Nuberroca, lays his hands on the chest wound, and brings him back [Td:2224-2232]. Sinsong moves forward and casts a compulsion across the dark — *approach* — the dark figure resists it once, folds the second time, and begins to come down [Td:2334-2343].
3. The attacker drops to the street and crosses in a half-crouch; Mialee waits in cover with her short bow strung, the arrowhead smeared in her kit's own poison, and takes him from behind as he passes under her lane — the venom catches at the wound but he fights off the worst of it and keeps moving [Td:2359-2378].
4. Kaelthar meets him in close with a thrown javelin that bites and a second javelin that goes wide [Td:2386-2408]; Nyx looses twice from the corner — one wide, one in — and slips back into cover [Td:2413-2419]. Mialee comes at him from the opposite side; the second arrow lands clean through the ribs and the follow-up stab from the shadow opens him badly across the chest [Td:2420-2440].
5. He gathers himself, breaks off, and makes it back up onto the roof the way he came — out of reach before the next arrow could meet him [Td:2487-2497]. Kaelthar lifts into the air after him on draconic wings and finds the pursuit heavy, barely clearing twenty feet [Td:2501-2529].
6. ***[EPIC]*** Mialee crosses the ground on the short blink her elven lineage affords her at level five, climbs the near wall at a run, and looses a last arrow across the gap in the dark — the shaft takes him where he crouches and finishes him [Td:2551-2601]. Nuberroca drops the small javelin and shield, takes up his new lance, and clouds himself the thirty feet to the rooftop in a step [Td:2603-2621]. He drags the body down and lays it in the street. The dead man's gear is a cheap crossbow, broken in the fall, and a short sword glistening with the green viscous poison that put Nuberroca down [Td:2632-2635]. Mialee brushes the blade bare-handed and takes a bad dose of it herself — the shaking of a bad fever before she can wipe the hand clean [Td:2636-2642].

## 11 - The guard takes the body

1. Nuberroca walks the corpse to a passing guard and lays it at his feet [Td:2659-2670]. The guard crouches, reads the venom on the blade, pockets it as evidence of the price of such work, and blows a short on his whistle; two more patrols close in and the three of them haul the body off between them [Td:2681-2699].
2. Nuberroca presses — *si por lo que sea encontráis más información acerca de asesinos* [Td:2700] — and the guard offers nothing he does not already have. *Si supiésemos dónde contratarlos estarían todos arrestados* [Td:2712]. The dead man's purse carried thirty-seven gold and no paper [Td:2719-2727].
3. The gold pays the bed. Three gold a night each at the tavern and the party takes six rooms; Mialee rides the first watch at the wagon in the north quarter rather than sleep [Td:2738-2771]. The night passes without further knock on the door [Td:2774].

{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 29}}
