# Session 34 {{float:"right" 2026-01-18}}
{wide}

## 1 - Two stones on a cairn
_~06:30, 10 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The party wakes early at the roadhouse [Td:7-8]. Over the first bowl of the morning they trade what they remember of yesterday's work; Eguzkilore and Mialee between them earn the day's small favours for recalling it best [Td:4-57].
2. Eguzkilore wanders out past the yard and his eye catches on a small cairn of walker-stones. On top where there should be one stone there are two, very alike. He touches them and feels at once that they are magical, though not what they do [Td:70-85].
3. He walks the pair back inside and offers them to Sinsong. Sinsong pockets them without ceremony: *"Pesas un poco, Sinsong."* *"Pues las putas piedras estas."* [Td:88-109].

## 2 - Breakfast and the rats that were not rats
_~07:00?, 10 Hammer_

1. Over the morning's thin fare Nuberroca brings the newly-arrived Kaelthar up on the previous day's run — the owlbear, the carnomorphs that boiled up out of the ground, the ration-haul they brought back and the civilian whose leg was savaged while they were trying to sleep. He mentions in passing that between him and Sinsong they stabilised the wounded man before turning in, and allows that he was already asleep for most of it [Td:124-141].
2. The common room is heavier than the day before. **Eoman**, the worker Sinsong healed, sits hunched over his bowl with the look of a man newly reminded of his own skin [Td:233-238].
3. Nuberroca approaches **Bog Luck**, who breaks off his conversation the moment the goliath sits down. Bog Luck calls the night *"una especie de animal, algunos animales, especie de ratas extrañas o algún monstruo de la marisma"* — the sentries, he says, handled it. He is grateful for Eoman's care and will have the man off work for the day [Td:252-262].
4. The barman grumbles for his part — the rats were worse last night than any night before, hammering the walls, as though in heat. Nuberroca and Sinsong press him in jest: Sinsong insists his extermination was professional. The barman shrugs and keeps wiping his glasses [Td:316-336].
5. Clark arrives, reads out the day's work-roster, and names the party's six as the escort: Kaelthar, Nyx, Nuberroca, Mialee, Sinsong, Eguzkilore. Half an hour, then march. The night-rest was supposed to buy them another day off, but *"hay que agilizar"* — some of the workers mean to leave in a day or two [Td:338-355].

## 3 - At the marsh-edge
_~09:00?, 10 Hammer_

1. An hour and a half's walk brings them to firm ground within smell of the marsh [Td:412-420]. The workers scatter to begin surveying the roadbed [Td:422-424]; the party take the escort [Td:426]. Nyx and Kaelthar peel off to the far side out of sight [Td:437-438]. Eight hours ahead of them, with nothing yet moving [Td:427-429].
2. Each of them picks a station. Mialee patrols; Eguzkilore keeps a crossbow across his knees under cover; Sinsong readies a fire-spell for the first ten minutes and then gives it up to flick pebbles [Td:430-436].
3. One of the workers — **Dillian** — keeps looking at Nuberroca. Eguzkilore loops the long way through the camp to come alongside the goliath without drawing eyes and whispers that the man is watching him too long [Td:481-502].

## 4 - A warning shot
_~10:30?, 10 Hammer_

1. Nuberroca drifts within line of Dillian. The man's two companions evaporate the moment he arrives, leaving Dillian pretending to measure a trunk [Td:503-517].
2. Later Nuberroca puts a crossbow bolt into the tree inches from the man's head. Dillian flinches and goes back to work with a visibly steadier hand [Td:524-530].
3. Sinsong crosses to Nuberroca when the next lull comes. *"Nuestro objetivo es que aguanten con vida y lanzándole virotes no suelen aguantar con vida."* Nuberroca shrugs — *"me ha salido de dentro"* — and promises not to shoot allies. For now [Td:538-549].

## 5 - A spine from the bush
_~15:00, 10 Hammer_

1. The afternoon stretches. One of the small bone-things erupts from the brush at Dillian's elbow; Nuberroca is on it in a step with the halberd and pins the creature to the ground through its tangle of broken limbs [Td:626-635].
2. Nuberroca uses the moment. He puts a hand on Dillian's shoulder — *"¿por qué me miras tanto?"* — and asks under his breath who told the man to watch him [Td:641-660]. Dillian stammers that he was told the goliath was dangerous and needed watching [Td:788-791]. Two of his co-workers arrive at the sound of his earlier scream [Td:667-670]; Nuberroca waves off their concern with the dead thing on the point of his spear, and walks Dillian off alone *"para ayudarle a relajarse"* [Td:671-704].
3. Pressed harder [Td:732-738], Dillian names the man who gave the order. Nuberroca leans close for the whisper: *"Sarek"* [Td:739-740]. From the edge of his eye Nuberroca marks that Dillian's two friends are watching his every step [Td:741-743], and that something in the standing water nearby is moving [Td:744-747].

## 6 - The ambush in the shallows
_~15:30?, 10 Hammer_

1. More of the small carnomorphs — Boneleechers in their new skins — burst from the ground around them. Nuberroca cuts the first down as it closes [Td:750-761].
2. From the pools behind them a larger thing hauls itself up: four legs under a mass of muscle and tendon with no proper skin on it, antennae and extra ears and eyes scattered down its back, and eight eyes in a face like an insect's. A smaller sibling scuttles clear of the water beside it. Something larger comes up a moment behind: armoured in bone-plates like an armadillo crossed with a beetle, trailing fluid that may be marsh-water or may be its own [Td:810-836].
3. Dillian is catatonic in Nuberroca's grip [Td:708-713 ?ord]. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca gathers what he has left — his once-a-day cloud-step and a hoarded piece of inspiration — and burns both at once [Td:904-926] to vanish into mist with the man held hard against him and reappear thirty feet closer to the party [Td:934-956]. Dillian, dropped on his feet beside the civilians, vomits once and sprints for his companions [Td:957-959].
4. Sinsong's bald head lights up and a ring of radiant angels forms around him; he names the civilians, the workers, and the party as under its protection, and the swamp-things that step inside are torn by the light [Td:1000-1021]. Mialee ducks into the body of the surveying crew and makes herself invisible among them [Td:1037-1046].
5. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca sets on the eye-covered Metagorger with the halberd and lands two full-force strikes in succession, cutting antennae and splitting half its eyes before it has a chance to answer. Blood runs out of it from every seam, but the thing is still standing [Td:966-989]. Eguzkilore puts an arrow between the armoured one's bone-plates for the first good hit of the fight [Td:884-897 ?ord].
6. The armoured one bounds across the ground and tries to reach the party. Eguzkilore disengages, binds a wound closed with the breath he still has, and gets clear [Td:1142-1163]. One of the Metagorgers pivots in mid-charge, snatches up the body of a fallen Boneleecher and swallows it whole; its wounds close as it eats [Td:1300-1310].
7. Mialee's shot drops a leg off the eye-covered one and leaves it staggering [Td:1240-1247]. A small one springs at Nuberroca and impales itself on his spear [Td:1256-1261]. Nuberroca's next pass takes down a small one, carries through on the backswing into another, and the third stroke lands on the armoured one [Td:1360-1373]. The last big one folds under the halberd, armour-plates and all [Td:1430-1433]. He turns to call the scattering civilians back around him [Td:1434-1437].

## 7 - Dillian is dead
_~16:30, 10 Hammer_

1. In the lull after the fight a voice screams — Dillian's, cut short [Td:1302]. By the time the party gets to him he is dead [Td:1391-1392].
2. Mialee searches the body and recovers a small purse [Td:1466]. She later hands it off at five pieces of gold and twenty coppers [Td:1519].
3. Sinsong goes to Dillian's two friends, **Jyn** and **Xod**, and asks what they would have done with the body. Jyn speaks for both: they barely knew him, and the risk was understood when they signed on. If he does not rise again, that will do [Td:1489-1495].
4. Nuberroca cuts in with the fact that Dillian spoke to him before the end, and that he knows what Dillian said [Td:1496-1500]. Jyn's face shuts — *"no creo que la incompetencia de tus compañeros sea culpa nuestra"* [Td:1550] — and he announces that he will not keep working for the pittance they are paid, and goes [Td:1551-1553].
5. Mialee hands Jyn Dillian's purse, as from the dead man's own kin [Td:1519]. Jyn takes it [Td:1550].
6. Sinsong, having worked out that the man who sent Dillian is of a piece with somebody from an older session, bolts in a panic — *"es el Rodri, me está siguiendo. El Odir viene a por nosotros, hay que huir de aquí"* — and makes for the trees. Mialee, Nuberroca and Eguzkilore chase him down and talk him back [Td:1531-1548].
7. By the time the party collects itself the civilians have scattered. About half, under Jyn, are walking back toward the roadhouse without them. The rest cluster around the party [Td:1575-1602].
8. An hour on, Mialee goes back to look at the body on her way out. Dillian is gone. Nothing drags away through the mud — it has simply been taken [Td:1627-1643].

## 8 - Back under the eaves
_~18:00, 10 Hammer_

1. The party returns to Carnath with the workers an hour behind the first group. The common room looks at them as they come in [Td:1657-1662].
2. Nuberroca is loud about the day's work from the door. **Isaiash**, one of the gate-guards, snaps back from behind the counter: *"ahí está la carretera para Waterdeep. No sé qué hacéis aquí."* Nuberroca invites him to perform an obscene favour; Isaiash answers in kind, lowering his voice when he realises the room is thin [Td:1670-1687].
3. Sinsong sits down to beers in the near-empty bar [Td:1697-1703] and ends up at the counter with **Jonan**, one of the escort, grousing about workers who do not work [Td:1713-1727].
4. Mialee sees Jyn and the returning workers go straight to **Ardred**, the caravan's registrar, who hears them out and immediately pulls out his book to record it [Td:1727-1730].
5. Nuberroca sits in on a knot of workers — Jarth, Runo, Reurig — and the man he has come looking for, **Sarek** [Td:1948-1958]. Prodded, Sarek names himself a soldier who follows orders [Td:1990-1992]. Nuberroca pushes him to show the insignia of his cult [Td:1994-1996]; Sarek's companion goes for his weapon and Sarek stops him with a flat *"mañana, aún no"* [Td:2003-2004].
6. ***[EPIC]*** Sarek turns on Nuberroca and warns him in a voice that reaches no further than the two of them: *"andaos con ojo porque estáis creando muchos enemigos, y no sois tan fuertes como creéis."* And walks off [Td:2004-2009].
7. Nuberroca takes the warning to Mialee first [Td:2060-2062] and then straight to Bog Luck [Td:2083-2088]. Bog Luck professes surprise — Sarek is not the sort — and promises to speak to him [Td:2091-2094].

## 9 - A ransacked room, a locked door
_~19:00?, 10 Hammer_

1. Upstairs the party finds the room turned over. Nothing is missing; they carry everything on them. Someone was looking for something and left no mark of what [Td:2108-2127].
2. Sinsong, hidden in a corner of the common hallway [Td:2071-2080 ?ord], watches **Anton** — the cultist who stole the party's cart on the road from Waterdeep — slip out of Bog Luck's locked office and back in again [Td:2298-2317]. He brings the news to the party in their room [Td:2298-2302].
3. Nuberroca lays out Sarek's threat [Td:2322-2330]; Sinsong lays out Anton going in and out of a door only Bog Luck should hold the key to [Td:2301-2304]. The two pieces fit — Bog Luck is in on it, or he is being used [Td:2308-2317].
4. Sinsong palms one of the stones Eguzkilore gave him in the morning and speaks into it [Td:2384-2385]; Mialee's answering voice comes through the mate stone in her hand [Td:2386-2390]. The two stones are a pair of **Sending Stones**, good for one exchange a day [Td:2400-2408].
5. The plan writes itself. Mialee will pick the lock on Bog Luck's office under cover of a distraction; Nuberroca will keep Bog Luck himself occupied over supper; Sinsong will plant himself in the yard with his view of the office door [Td:2367-2379 ?ord].

## 10 - What the ledger said
_~20:00?, 10 Hammer_

1. Mialee reaches the door with her floating invisible hand on it, picks the lock on the first try, and slips in. Light inside gives her away for a heartbeat and she pulls the door almost closed behind her [Td:2454-2466].
2. The room is spartan and immaculate: a desk with an empty top but for an inkwell and a pouch of sand, a bookshelf of philosophy and natural history worn with use, and under the bed a trunk whose lid is not locked. Inside the trunk is a Waterdeep guard-officer's uniform with full regalia, and black clothing that could be a cultist's and could as easily not be [Td:2491-2501, Td:2674-2686].
3. On the shelf is a ledger. Entries of valuables kept there carry two ticks apiece — except the most recent, which carry only one. Beside each entry is a name and a small gilt mark of a dwarf smoking a pipe. Among the one-tick entries sit *"dos esferas de detención"* — the pair she lifted from the inner storeroom chest the night of her S33 scouting — and she adds the second tick to the entry with the inkwell, folding her own theft back into the ledger. A twelve-platinum bag plus a cheque under the name *Ardred* takes a tick as well, and she blots the ink. The trunk, the uniform, and the shelf go back exactly as they stood [Td:2505-2534, Td:2686-2688].
4. In the yard Sinsong is lifting his voice — *"¡socorro! ¡socorro ya!"* — to put eyes on him. Isaiash and **Jarth** come at a half-run; Jarth takes the pantomime for what it is and goes straight for the office door. Sinsong has roped Anton to the ground with Command and cords between the two shouts; Isaiash, when he arrives, cuts the ropes without a word [Td:2466-2478 ?ord, Td:2546-2583].
5. Anton snaps upright and puts a rapier through Sinsong's ribs [Td:2649-2650] — the blade goes in between the bones *"sin demasiada complicación"* [Td:2669-2669] — and Sinsong drops [Td:2667-2673].

## 11 - The brawl in the yard
_~20:30?, 10 Hammer_

1. Sarek closes on Eguzkilore and tries to pin him; Eguzkilore slips free [Td:2628-2638]. Jarth comes at him from the other side and gets the hold [Td:2641-2646]. Eguzkilore wrestles free of Jarth on his own next move and catches him in a counter-hold [Td:3012-3024].
2. Nuberroca, down from Bog Luck's table on the first shout, grapples Jarth back and tears him away from Eguzkilore [Td:2777-2821]. He opens with a full-force halberd stroke under the chin and a cleaving follow-through that catches Sarek on the backswing [Td:2949-2961].
3. Mialee seals the office door shut from inside using her floating hand on the lock-plate, bolts out past the melee, and comes around behind Jarth with the shortsword in reverse grip — a sneaking strike where he cannot see her coming [Td:2831-2843, Td:3108-3123].
4. ***[EPIC]*** Jarth, bleeding, drops his sword long enough to pull a talisman from his collar and chant a line Nuberroca does not recognise. The goliath's limbs lock on him; the halberd falls out of his grip and his fury snuffs out at once [Td:2965-2985]. The next sword-stroke that lands on him lands clean through the open guard, and takes the breath out of him [Td:2987-2993].
5. Isaiash shelves his loyalty to the house and switches sides, dragging Sinsong's body clear while shouting that the party started it, then setting arrows against Eguzkilore and Mialee from the near rail [Td:3075-3081].
6. Mialee drops Jarth on her next pass with the shortsword in both hands — non-lethal to the end, barely a flicker of breath left in him [Td:3108-3124]. Eguzkilore puts his bow aside for a scimitar and takes two careful cuts at Sarek, slowing him but not dropping him [Td:3140-3158].
7. **Bog Luck** fills the office doorway with his semi-orc bulk — *"¿qué cojones está pasando aquí?"* — and reads the yard in a glance: Sinsong bleeding on the stones, Isaiash tending him, three of his guards and three of the escort upright with weapons out. He steps in between with hands open, not even drawing [Td:3067-3075 ?ord, Td:3159-3171].

## 12 - Under watch
_~21:00, 10 Hammer_

1. On Bog Luck's order every weapon in the yard lowers at the same time. The guards collect themselves; the party collects itself [Td:3203-3209].
2. Bog Luck sends the party upstairs under watch. *"Son las 9 de la noche en ambos sitios"* — too late to sort it tonight; tomorrow he will have answers from all of them [Td:3209-3214].
3. On the way up Nuberroca makes the one offering he can — he warns Bog Luck that his guests have been in the room and that Sarek has already told the goliath he will not be alive to see noon. Bog Luck's face goes still at the word *"revuelto"*, and he says nothing [Td:3216-3221].
4. The night passes without further disturbance. The long rest comes with it, though not the full of its usual benefit — Sinsong wakes the goliath mid-rest to half his vitality and no more, and the escort lies down knowing exactly what the morning will bring [Td:3255-3265].

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 34}}

\page

# Session 35 {{float:"right" 2026-01-25}}
{wide}

## 1 - The morning quiz
_~07:00, 11 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Before breakfast the party talks over yesterday's wreckage — what the small bone-things are called, what their mission is, who they suspect [Td:1-30]. Eguzkilore pins down **Metagorger** as the big one; a second answer places **carnomorphs** as the family [Td:9-19]. Suspects: **Anton**, the cultist who stole the cart and knifed Sinsong in the ribs; **Bog Luck**; the guards drawn around him [Td:47-54]. Bog Luck has been complaining about rats in the storeroom for months; the carnomorphs are only days old; the party ties the two to the meteor shower out loud for the first time [Td:88-96].
2. The night before, the party had been ransacked; while straightening the room Nuberroca turns up with new marks on his sheet — things the recent days have left on him [Td:106-110]. Sinsong, lifting a mattress, pulls out **nine pieces of copper** — pathetic for a bribe or a reward [Td:111-116].

## 2 - Nyx's dream
_~07:10?, 11 Hammer_

1. Nyx wakes agitated, breath fast, visibly rattled in her bed [Td:124-144]. She tells a dream she cannot shake: she was somebody like Nuberroca — a goliath, axe in hand, hammer-wielders beside her, marching into a fight with orcs. They lost and she died. She lay dead a while, and then rose hungry and started walking without quite knowing how [Td:148-166].
2. Walking in that half-state she saw **lizardfolk** come up out of the earth, carrying boxes to a castle — not golden, not above a river of piss, a plain castle [Td:168-174]. Then a marsh, two eyes in the dark, a mouth closed on her, and she was flown off into the night [Td:175-177].
3. Sinsong, who studies these things, chews on *"los enanos del Fallow"* a moment — the name sounds familiar but will not place for him [Td:200-217]. He presses Nyx on whether she has ever truly known hunger; she answers that she has, in the lean stretches between settlements, and the party moves on without decoding any of it [Td:181-193].

## 3 - Faith narrowed, half the caravan leaves
_~07:30?, 11 Hammer_

1. Sinsong announces his guardians will from here on protect only his faithful, and polls [Td:252-284]. Nyx is with him in friendship but has no faith. Nuberroca is recovering his confidence. Eguzkilore: *"ninguno cree en Sinsong"* — Nuberroca was mauled by orcs and ogres three days ago and no cure came [Td:281-285]. Kaelthar and Mialee leave the question for later.
2. Downstairs, three or four workers and a guard are harnessing horses to carts [Td:297-299]. A worker tells Nuberroca plainly: *nos vamos*. One has died, the pay does not cover this; some are staying, most are not [Td:301-307].
3. Nuberroca steps where **Bog Luck** cannot see him and gives **Sarek** a two-handed obscenity — a wave with the open hand, then grabs his crotch with the other [Td:339-343]. Nyx translates sotto voce that in local manners this reads loud [Td:343-344]. Sarek stares him down: *"Hoy no tengo que contenerme. No me busques las vueltas. Porque igual yo me marcho, pero tú no llegas a mañana"* [Td:349-352]. And then, turning back to his cart: *"No se nos ha perdido nada en este Cenagal lleno de escoria como vosotros"* [Td:355-358].
4. Three carts leave in short order — three civilians and a guard each, Sarek among them [Td:433-434, Td:959-962].

## 4 - The empty carts and the quarrel
_~08:00?, 11 Hammer_

1. Nyx throws a trace-less veil over herself and slips out behind the carts [Td:429-432]. She closes on one — none of them trying to be quiet — and sees the bed empty of the cult's cargo, only four or five bags of personal clothes [Td:449-456]. Mialee goes to the next cart in turn and finds the same [Td:486-493]: all three are empty.
2. From the yard the party watches **Bog Luck** confront Sarek on the way out. The exchange runs loud enough to catch: Bog Luck says this is not how it is done, Sarek's work ended when the workers left; Sarek shoots back his work was to deliver the cargo, it has been delivered, and if he stays he will end up killing someone — better to go on time [Td:467-478]. Bog Luck leaves it with a disgusted snort and stalks off; the party catches the sour look on the way past [Td:476-478].

## 5 - The idol in the strongroom
_~08:30?, 11 Hammer_

1. Nyx asks Bog Luck about the locked chamber at the back of the house. He walks her through it: in winter the bedroom-door locks freeze, so he removed them and in exchange keeps all valuables himself in the strongroom, with a register, and only he has the key [Td:575-582]. The departing workers took only what they had deposited. Nyx offers to drop off a piece of her own and he will not let her in: *"soy perro viejo, ya he visto muchos trucos"* [Td:588-589]. Nothing is being asked back of unusual urgency, he says — only that it is strange so many are leaving so early [Td:600-607].
2. Sinsong steps into the warehouse and reaches with a working to locate cult idols [Td:539-542 ?ord]. He gets one — about sixty feet to the south, inside the strongroom [Td:555-560]. Only the one, and the spell cannot say whether it is cult-stolen or some panoli's household icon [Td:965-969].

## 6 - The pack
_~08:45?, 11 Hammer_

1. A guard — **Craith** — walks up loud, accusing Nuberroca of saying his face stinks and dropping the rest: before the half of the caravan rode out, they had been telling everyone the party were thieves [Td:722-739]. Craith walks off indignant [Td:740]; the people in the yard now eye the party like they will grab their purses when the party comes near [Td:741].
2. Nuberroca walks over to **Bog Luck** to have it out: *"¿Qué les pasa a tus guardias que me están insultando todo el rato?"* [Td:748-752]. Bog Luck answers with the fuller version — **Isaías** and **Bael**, yesterday, while the party was out on the road: provisions stolen from fellow travelers, a purse of gold lifted, and the lot denied when confronted [Td:756-760].
3. Nuberroca opens the pack to show he has nothing he could have stolen. Bog Luck reaches in. He pulls out a **silver ingot** — *"¿cómo has conseguido esto honradamente?"* [Td:787-793] — and from Nuberroca's deeper inventory, the two **cult talismans** the party took in Greenest off the dead [Td:810-812].
4. Bog Luck's verdict: *"Veo que es cierto que sois ladrones"* [Td:801]. He closes the pack and hands it back [Td:803-805]. The talismans don't read as cultist loot — Nuberroca has been too vocal about standing against the cult for anyone to buy that — so they read as plain theft [Td:816-820].

## 7 - The arson gambit
_~09:00?, 11 Hammer_

1. Sinsong, quiet to Nyx and Nuberroca: he has sent a floating hand into the warehouse and set a lantern of fire on the ceiling, roped to drop when the door moves [Td:830-842]. The idea is to force whoever can open the strongroom to come out and act [Td:841-846]. Nuberroca steps back from the door so he is not standing over it when it starts.
2. Smoke begins to seep from the warehouse — a thin ribbon through the roof-boards [Td:905-909]. Nyx, loud and innocent, calls to Bog Luck from where she stands: *"Voxlack, huele a quemado aquí, mira a ver qué pasa"* and holds her ground so he can see it was not her [Td:910-911]. Bog Luck opens up; smoke and dancing light inside; the yard rushes in with buckets from the well [Td:912-918]. Nuberroca draws water and gives it to **Konnor**; Konnor takes it with a strange look [Td:924-925].
3. Ten minutes and the fire is out [Td:926]. Some tools are calcinated; the roof needs repair; nothing structural [Td:926, Td:986-987]. Bog Luck catches Sinsong on the way past and lays the bill out: fifteen or twenty pieces of gold, more if the beams need replacing, charged against the construction company; and the party is not to set foot in the warehouse again [Td:926-989]. A guard plants himself at the broken door to make sure [Td:989].
4. Nuberroca, probing: *"¿Te suena el nombre de Langdedrosa?"* [Td:1153]. Insight comes back empty — Bog Luck shows no flicker [Td:1159-1162]. Nuberroca, aside to Mialee: *"este está en el ajo"* [Td:1163] and walks off. The party eventually hands Bog Luck six pieces of gold — *"para que acabes con las ratas... que molestan al cocinero"* [Td:1178-1189]. Bog Luck pockets them, thanks him, and asks the party once more to take their things and leave [Td:1188-1191].

## 8 - The faithful counted
_~09:15?, 11 Hammer_

1. Second poll before anyone draws steel: do you believe in Sinsong? Kaelthar: yes, draws out his little stone — *"haz tu magia"* [Td:1256-1258]. Mialee: she believes in the magic, not that it will save her, but holds the stone up anyway [Td:1260-1266]. **Eoman**, from across the yard, says yes [Td:1280-1281]. Nyx, Nuberroca, Eguzkilore: no [Td:1273-1281]. **Sufe**: uncertain [Td:1282]. Two in by firm count, three or four out [Td:1275-1278].

## 9 - The door breached
_~09:20?, 11 Hammer_

1. Nuberroca tells Konnor to step aside and tries to force the door — a grapple, a shove — Konnor is pinned to it and will not move [Td:1227-1297]. Nuberroca, turning: *"La diplomacia no funciona"* [Td:1242-1244].
2. ***[EPIC]*** Kaelthar lines up a charge and barrels straight at Konnor to put him through the door and clear it. Two grapples catch at him as he passes — one of them Bog Luck's — and neither takes hold [Td:1430-1441]. Kaelthar's shoulder hits Konnor; Konnor flies **fifteen feet** back and lands flat, and the door bursts open behind him [Td:1450-1455].
3. Bog Luck, livid: *"¡Me cago en vuestra puñetera estampa! Ahí van diez piezas más de oro"* [Td:1456-1457]. Nuberroca, flat: *"Yo no pago más"* [Td:1458-1459].

## 10 - The yard opens
_~09:25?, 11 Hammer_

1. From her perch behind a cart Nyx puts a critical shot into Bog Luck's **right leg** — the half-orc jerks as if a jolt has gone through him; the leg takes a lasting wound [Td:1588-1599].
2. Sinsong plants himself and reaches to call down his radiant guardians — gestures, words, and the glow on his bald head does not come; nothing happens; the slot stands [Td:1515-1530 ?ord]. It is the first time any of the party have seen him flub a spell [Td:1735-1738].
3. Mialee, from cover, puts an arrow into Craith as he draws his lance on Kaelthar — a sneak-blow slipped in on the opening [Td:1577-1583]. Nuberroca drops the halberd into **Wex**, cleaves through the stroke to the man beside him with the follow-through, and brings the halberd back around for the kill — Wex folds to the ground, unconscious [Td:1695-1715].
4. On Sinsong's second attempt the guardians come at last — a ring of light that spares the ones Sinsong names (the party and Eoman, the **Heroes of Marsken**) and catches the rest. Wex on the ground takes it full and dies where he lies; the ring brushes Craith, Konnor, and Runo clustered around [Td:1721-1760].

## 11 - Eoman turns
_~09:30?, 11 Hammer_

1. Mialee's second shot catches **Eoman** with the sliver of poison on her blade — a sneak shot from behind the cart [Td:2033-2056]. He makes the save; nothing seems to come of it [Td:2049-2053]. Then he starts shaking.
2. Eoman's scimitar falls from his hand; the hand itself begins to pull apart — tendons cracking, bone resetting under the skin, and an outsized clawed thing extends from the stump where the hand had been [Td:2060-2061]. The arm grows out of all proportion. He turns on Mialee and begins to attack with it [Td:2061-2062].
3. One word rises out of the gibberish he is saying: **Tiamat** [Td:2077]. The claw hits Mialee hard; she rolls half the damage off with the thief's reflex, but the second blow lays her on the ground, unconscious [Td:2078-2082]. Eoman stands over her, still swinging, and the blow would have been enough — two failed death-strokes banked against her tally before the next hand pulls her back [Td:2087-2092]. Then Eoman stops, stares at his arm as it comes slowly apart back into a hand, and sits down on the dust, catatonic [Td:2093-2095].

## 12 - Bog Luck's rampage
_~09:35?, 11 Hammer_

1. ***[EPIC]*** Bog Luck comes off the door with the longsword out and does the work in one turn — three strokes in a single pass, crowd and position working for him: Eguzkilore drops; Kaelthar drops; Nuberroca drops [Td:1858-1860]. All three on the ground in a breath. He turns back and bellows: *"¡Aguantad la línea, joder!"* [Td:2149-2150] and the guards straighten up.
2. **Runo**, who has been standing with the civilians, pivots and crits Sinsong with a scimitar — the blade has something on it, and when it hits, the right arm underneath takes a burn that eats the nerves and the leather beneath [Td:1876-1888]. Sinsong's armour will carry three fewer points for the next heartbeat. He hits far too well for a civilian, Sinsong sees [Td:1875-1878].
3. Sinsong, head alight again, reaches out across the yard and pulls Kaelthar back from the edge — the little word of healing, and the dragonborn opens his eyes [Td:1988-1999].
4. Runo's blows bring Sinsong down — the old tattoo on his back flares and holds him at one breath of life instead of none; a second strike puts him under again and the tattoo answers a second time [Td:2224-2237]. Only then does a different voice come out of Runo — words in **draconic** none of them can read, except Kaelthar on the ground who catches *"por la gloria de los dragones, alabada sea Tiamat"* [Td:2242-2248]. Sinsong keeps his guardians going and the thread of life with it.
5. Nuberroca, up from the floor and furious, puts the halberd through Runo on a reckless stroke; Runo folds and does not come back [Td:2266-2269].

## 13 - The reversal and the last stand
_~09:45?, 11 Hammer_

1. Bog Luck draws something off his belt — a small blue sphere — and flings it at Nuberroca; the goliath's will holds and the orb's hold slides off him [Td:2443-2460]. Nuberroca, delighted: *"¡A tu culo! ¡Alegrito a Bob Black! ¡Soy más listo de lo que piensas!"* [Td:2461-2463].
2. Earlier in the exchange Nyx had closed on Mialee's unconscious body and tipped one of her berries into her mouth; Mialee came back on one point of life [Td:2117-2130 ?ord]. Ardred, who had been pinning Eguzkilore, finds her and crits her on the ground — the scimitar's whatever-it-was burns through her left arm [Td:2370-2390]. Nyx comes in with a second berry; Mialee comes back again [Td:2416-2423].
3. Eguzkilore cuts himself loose from Ardred, catches his breath, and surges through to a second shot — a crit into Ardred's back; the man goes face-first into the dust [Td:2478-2522]. Kaelthar levers himself up off the stones and tries to put a hold on Bog Luck across the room — the half-orc's will is strong enough; the spell slides [Td:2541-2563].
4. Nuberroca shoves across the yard to Bog Luck. *"Ríndete."* Bog Luck looks at his fallen crew and the party still standing, and settles: *"¡Jamás!"* [Td:2587-2591]. Nuberroca lands the halberd on him twice and knocks the half-orc's concentration loose [Td:2592-2628].
5. Sinsong reaches out and lays the second-level word on Ardred and Bog Luck both — *"acercaos a mí"* [Td:2634-2643]. The compulsion does not take; Ardred, hauled upright and spitting, goes back to Mialee and crits her again — this time her ankle shatters [Td:2660-2676]. From her perch Nyx answers it: a shot into Ardred that lands sharp enough to finish him, and the man falls dead across Mialee's legs [Td:2704-2731].
6. Bog Luck pulls his second blue sphere and hurls it at Nuberroca; this time the goliath's save slides and he locks in place, paralysed [Td:2733-2740].
7. ***[EPIC]*** Eguzkilore nocks one more, steadies the shot with everything he has left, and puts the arrow through Bog Luck's back. The half-orc falls [Td:2749-2762].

## 14 - The flight and the slaughter
_~09:55?, 11 Hammer_

1. The surviving civilians — the ones who had been hiding in the rooms — crack and run. **Dem** stands rooted a moment staring at the ring of guardians Sinsong has standing around him; then his legs thicken, warp, and he jumps clean over Sinsong's shoulder-high head and lands beyond the ring, running [Td:2857-2862].
2. Nuberroca sprints after Dem — ninety feet to Dem's seventy, and catches him on open ground. The boy is still on the mutated legs; Nuberroca puts the halberd through him non-lethal and drops him unconscious into the dust, hauls him back to the roadhouse [Td:2866-2900]. Kaelthar grabs the other prisoner — **Lathar** — and hauls him in gagged [Td:2902-2951].
3. ***[EPIC]*** Sinsong, inside the ring, lets the ring do the work. The small-sized civilians who try to flee past him die as the radiance brushes them — automatically, no save for a hit that would have finished them anyway [Td:2838-2843 ?ord]. Mialee, flat, as the rest run: *"Carne de carnomorfo. Nadie hace más"* [Td:2875-2877].
4. Nuberroca later, hands open to Sinsong: *"voy a empezar a pensar que eres fuerte en la batalla, pero no creo que matar criaturas inocentes o que no te están atacando solucione nada"* [Td:2933-2934]. Sinsong, convinced some of the fleeing were already turning: *"¿Tú has visto cómo se han convertido? Todos ellos están en siajo"* [Td:2935-2937]. The two of them leave the argument where it stands.

## 15 - The strongroom and the trapdoor
_~10:10?, 11 Hammer_

1. The door to the strongroom is slammed shut again and barred from inside. Sinsong throws bolts of fire at it until the wood gives [Td:2797-2980]. Mialee reaches for traps before anyone steps in [Td:2981-2982]; the room is nearly empty — a few old crates against one wall, dust on the floor, and the clear shape of where many more crates used to stand [Td:3002-3009]. The carts' cargo is gone from here.
2. Among the loose crates Mialee cracks a statuette out of a nest of straw — an elegant **Chauntea** figure, two or three hundred pieces of gold to the right buyer [Td:3027-3041]. The rest of the loose crates hold tools, old papers, two bottles of a strong spirit expensive enough to warrant the chamber [Td:3038-3039].
3. In the corner one crate will not move. Sinsong tries a bolt of fire on it; the box takes a slow burn and Mialee smothers it fast [Td:3053-3059]. Where the wood gives there is nothing inside — the crate is empty and nailed to the floor. Levered against its own bottom, it tips on a hidden hinge: the crate is the lid, and under the lid is a trapdoor and the start of a tunnel going down [Td:3061-3064].
4. Nuberroca pulls them back from the opening — Lathar and Dem first, questions first, the tunnel after. The two prisoners are chained in the chamber; the carnomorph bodies piled aside; the party takes the short rest they can afford before descending [Td:3067-3100].

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 35}}

\page

# Session 36 {{float:"right" 2026-02-01}}
{wide}

## 1 - The interrogation that gave nothing
_~11:00?, 11 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. In the strongroom, over the hour and a half since the yard quieted, Nuberroca has worked the two prisoners without a hand laid on them — what they were doing at the roadhouse, why the boy's legs twisted mid-flight, why they follow the Cult of the Dragon, how they knew **Bog Luck**, what was in the strongroom, why they were spying, and who **Sarek** is to them [Td:28-37]. The two give nothing — *"no dijeron una puta mierda"* [Td:39]. He notes they wear no cult tattoos, no pendants, no cult signs; only a small mark of **Chauntea** that **Lathar** carries [Td:41, Td:685-686].
2. Sinsong asks for a moment alone with each prisoner [Td:43]. He kindles a small flame in his hand and shows it to Lathar; Lathar looks at the Chauntea mark and answers that he will not change his faith *"aunque me vaya la vida en ello"* [Td:687-693]. **Dem** by this time is crying, catatonic, pleading only to be let go [Td:684 ?ord, Td:693-694].
3. Mialee comes in on Sinsong's wordless signal, two blades out. Sinsong says low: *"corta las piernas"* [Td:699-707]. Dem goes to pieces [Td:709]. Sinsong eases off — *"parece que son civiles, de verdad"* [Td:710]. Nyx agrees: *"tiene toda la pinta de que son civiles, inocentes"* [Td:718-720].

## 2 - The yard turns

1. An hour and a half later, scraping noises outside [Td:46-48]. Eguzkilore slips to the doorway: one of the carnomorphs is in the yard, hunched over one of the piled bodies — rat-sized, a knot of broken bone and fur that cracks on the stones with every step [Td:62-71, Td:268-276]. He puts an arrow into it and a second as it convulses and dies [Td:138-152]. More come over the fence — three, four of the small ones scrambling under a cart [Td:170-193, Td:287-294].
2. Pottery breaks on the roof overhead [Td:345-352]. A larger one — dwarf-sized, winged, built of the same stitched bone — plummets from above and lands flat on Mialee, pinning her [Td:430-443]. She twists her blades up to catch its jaws and keeps the teeth off her [Td:443-446].
3. Mialee cuts loose and backs off, calling for cover [Td:450-456]. The wings tear after Eguzkilore and the claw tears open a seam in his chest that barely bleeds — but something inside it begins to grind, a dull inner fury that drops him into a sudden, dragging weariness and will try to swell after every fight he lives through [Td:497-515].
4. Sinsong throws a bolt of fire at the big one and misses, calls Mialee into the strongroom, drops a ward on Eguzkilore [Td:462, Td:544-554, Td:581-589]. Nyx puts her mark on the Metagorger and the next arrow goes clean through — the thing drops like a sack of broken bone, three shafts standing out of its back [Td:594-603]. Eguzkilore steadies himself long enough to clamp down on the bleed inside and holds [Td:604-606].

## 3 - The haul

1. The yard quiet, the party splits. Eguzkilore turns out to have had Bog Luck's keys on him since yesterday without saying so; Nuberroca takes the ring, works through two until the right one turns, and goes into Bog Luck's room with Nyx [Td:744-765]. Sinsong, Eguzkilore and Kaelthar stay in the strongroom and warehouse and start taking everything on the shelves at once [Td:862-905].
2. In Bog Luck's room, the account books and ledgers look clean — names, wages, deposit-and-return columns, the inventory of the strongroom [Td:769-775, Td:921-934]. The books on the shelf are a surprise: philosophy and natural history, out of place for a half-orc innkeeper [Td:770-771]. Nuberroca takes two of the natural-history volumes [Td:789-794]. Nyx and Nuberroca take the room apart — mattresses, drawers, false bottoms, walls, floor, ceiling — and come away with nothing [Td:852-963]. Sinsong, drifting between rooms during the search, pockets a stash of forty gold without announcing it [Td:842 ?ord, Td:3168-3174].
3. In the strongroom, Sinsong lifts a chest and triggers a trap — the second click after the first, a green cloud boiling out — and the three in the chamber (Sinsong, Mialee, Kaelthar) all catch a lungful [Td:874-895]. Eguzkilore had stepped outside for air before the lid came up [Td:897-900]. All three cough through; the chamber reeks of acid, and Sinsong's old burn-tattoo on his back flares at the heat of it [Td:886-895].
4. Someone realises the two prisoners were left with no one minding them for an hour or more. Dem is gone. Lathar is still chained, still there [Td:981-995]. The warehouse's bags and backpacks come up next; the party pile the lot [Td:1013-1016].

## 4 - What Lathar knows

1. Nyx, piecing together what Lathar and Dem have — and haven't — said against the visions she and Mialee had the night before, says it out loud: the **lizardfolk** of the swamp are not merely passing through this country, they *collaborate* with the Cult — they are the ones carrying the cargo the party has been chasing [Td:1003-1010].

## 5 - Dem walks

1. Nyx unties the boy. Nuberroca asks where he lives — Waterdeep, he answers, and he will wait for the next caravan [Td:1058-1081]. Nuberroca gives him food from the cult packs and ten pieces of silver; sees the boy's eyes and adds another ten [Td:1097-1108]. He offers Dem a place as his squire for a wage — Dem hesitates, then shakes his head: *"me dan ustedes mucho miedo"* [Td:1110-1117]. He takes the coin and runs for the stables [Td:1136-1148, Td:1166-1192].

## 6 - The yard dismissed

1. Nuberroca puts his shoulder to the upstairs door, which was wedged shut from inside, and finds the rest of the roadhouse's people in a corner with kitchen knives — the cook **Grisel** out front with a carving blade, a makeshift barricade of chairs and benches [Td:1159-1165]. Kaelthar steps in open-handed; the party tells them Bog Luck's crew were the thieves, the proof is in the room they were guarding [Td:1176-1180, Td:1211-1214].
2. Nuberroca, blunt, offers Grisel first blood — walks up unarmed and lets the cook stab him to see what a carving fork can do; the point lands clean at the throat and does not scratch him [Td:1225-1234]. He walks it back and warns them to defend themselves not from the party but from what else is out there [Td:1230-1232], and leaves them with a line he turns over for a moment: *"si mis dioses me hubiesen dado el don de la palabra seguiría hablando con vosotros, pero como no es así, os dejamos vivos"* [Td:1232-1235]. The party passes a hat around: a piece of gold each toward getting the civilians somewhere else [Td:1251-1263].

## 7 - The rope goes down
_~15:00, 11 Hammer_

1. Mialee suggests a long rest, back in the yard — the tunnel can wait for the morning [Td:1284-1298]. Nyx heads to the trapdoor to run a rope while the rest settle. The shaft drops in a steep ramp, pitch-black beyond ten feet, carved rough and winding north-west [Td:1265-1281 ?ord, Td:3165-3166].
2. The galloping comes before anyone is laid out — hooves at speed on the road, fists on the main gate, five or six voices on the other side [Td:1319-1337]. The party moves for the tunnel. Nyx is still on the rope [Td:1343-1347].

## 8 - Sarek breaks the door

1. ***[EPIC]*** The main door explodes inward and **Sarek** is through, snarling: *"os teníamos que haber matado cuando tuvimos ocasión, sabíamos que erais unos putos asesinos"* [Td:1349-1357]. Sinsong, poised and waiting, throws a bolt of fire square into his face — the hit takes him off his feet, and as he tries to rise his ankle folds under him and will not hold [Td:1358-1372].
2. Mialee drops a sheet of fog across the doorway behind the first wave, dense enough to swallow Kaelthar whole, and shouts the party into the tunnel [Td:1424-1442]. One of the cultist guards rises on a working of flight, drifting ten feet above the mist to spot Nuberroca over the inner rail — Nuberroca and Nyx both open on him as he clears the cover and leave him reeling, twenty-one points of shot and halberd between them [Td:1518-1566].

## 9 - The tide at the door

1. The yard fills — guards and spellcasters, Sarek still on the ground. Nuberroca wades in with the halberd, guard down and striking without care for the opening it gives, and cuts down **Bael** and **Xod** on the first pass; **Reurig** takes the follow-up as he closes [Td:1584-1649].
2. Mialee lays a poisoned sliver in behind Sarek's ribs — the sickness takes [Td:1687-1704] — and sends a floating, unseen hand at his throat to pluck the amulet off him mid-chant [Td:1712-1735]. He catches the cord in his palm before she can snap it, and the hand slides away [Td:1731-1735].

## 10 - Who believes in Sinsong

1. Sinsong stops in the middle of the yard, thinking of the ring of guardians he had been about to call down, and remembers to put the question he had forgotten at the start: *"¿quiénes creéis en Sinsong?"* [Td:1900-1915]. Nuberroca raises a hand — *"tengo una lucecita adentro"* — more guilt than conviction [Td:1923-1933]. Kaelthar follows: *"ésta es mi última vez que te doy mi aprobación"* [Td:1921-1922]. Nyx last — potential, not divinity [Td:1937-1949]. Three. Sinsong tallies and decides it is not enough — *"o funciona entero o no funciona"* [Td:1957] — holds the slot and steps away from the ring unmade, out of reach [Td:1952-1970].

## 11 - The oil and the guardians

1. Rounds pass without the ring. The cultist on the ceiling sings down a line of fire that catches Nuberroca across the shoulder, a searing stripe the length of his back [Td:1972-1991]. **Ibis** opens Nuberroca's side with a long sword and the goliath is wearing wounds [Td:2015-2021].
2. Sinsong tries the invocation a second time and this time the ring blazes around his head and reaches out — Sarek shakes it off, two of the others crumple inside the brightness [Td:2210-2240].
3. ***[EPIC]*** Kaelthar hefts a canister of lamp oil out of his pack, slings it the length of the yard, and bursts it over Sarek's shoulders — Mialee calls out the moment and both halves of the throw land clean [Td:2378-2410]. Sarek is soaked [Td:2401-2410]; from here on any flame that touches him will cost him more than the hit itself [Td:2420-2424].
4. Mialee's arrow finishes **Zephur** in the same breath [Td:2491-2502]. She sends an unseen hand across the yard to pull the strongroom door shut behind the party, then drops into the shaft and takes the first stretch of rope at a run [Td:2505-2510].

## 12 - The halberd clears

1. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca comes off the fog line with the halberd horizontal and shears into **Nefion** — the hit lands and the divine fire in him flares black at the strike, the follow-through taking **Isaiash** at his side. The second swing kills Nefion where he stands and the floating blade hanging over him winks out [Td:2602-2648].

## 13 - The pile at the trapdoor

1. The shaft bottlenecks. Nyx and Sinsong go down first and land one on top of the other at the bottom [Td:2529-2534]. Kaelthar steps on the mist and reappears at the far doorway, a hard charge carrying him the rest of the way through, and the trapdoor comes down behind him [Td:2109-2130 ?ord, Td:2755-2757].
2. Earlier in the pass Nefion had hit Nuberroca and set a floating blade on him in the same breath — the conjured sword took the goliath hard across the shoulder, the nerves in his left arm burning out and his readiness to answer blows with it [Td:2559-2572]. Now a cultist with reach drops through the trapdoor onto Kaelthar — a crushing landing with the sword, and Kaelthar's left arm goes the same way Nuberroca's did [Td:2787-2800].
3. The trapdoor opens again from above and **Jyn**'s conjured sword reaches down the shaft to take the fingers of Kaelthar's left hand apart — Kaelthar drops what he holds, folds unconscious, and slides the rest of the way down the rope to the tunnel floor [Td:2864-2879].

## 14 - The wall of fire and the exit

1. Nuberroca scoops Kaelthar up off the stones and runs — thirty feet, then another thirty on the dash, the dragonborn on his shoulder [Td:2893-2910]. He sets Kaelthar down long enough to work a healer's kit over him and stop the bleeding before the ground takes him, then shoulders him again [Td:3066-3079].
2. ***[EPIC]*** Nyx turns on the stairs and puts a shot clean through one of the pursuers who had come down the rope — the arrow goes in to the fletching and he drops where he hung on the line [Td:3002-3005]. Sarek has taken enough to fall by then; Sinsong throws a bolt of fire onto his oil-soaked body in the tunnel mouth, the oil catches full, and a five-foot wall of flame drops across the entrance, cooking anyone who tries to cross [Td:3028-3036]. **Isaiash** from the lip of the trapdoor roars after them: *"os encontraremos y no pararemos hasta daros caza, malditos asesinos"* [Td:3118-3125].

## 15 - The dark and the speech
_~16:15?, 11 Hammer_

1. The pursuit falls off. The tunnel runs crude and serpentine, cut by rough tools and claws, no light at the far end though an hour has passed and daylight should have reached a mouth by now [Td:3162-3167]. Sinsong wakes Kaelthar with a word of healing and the dragonborn relights the ward of faith around himself for a lamp, then settles on a simple glow laid against his talisman instead to spare the stronger working [Td:3143-3160 ?ord].
2. Sinsong gathers the party in the dark and lays out what he had held back: forty pieces of gold he had turned up in Bog Luck's room, eight for each of them [Td:3168-3174]. *"Me gustaría que empezaseis a confiar en mí"* [Td:3170]. Nuberroca puts a hand on his shoulder: *"Cabezabombilla, estos días has luchado bien. Creo que tienes madera de campeón"* [Td:3178-3181]. Sinsong, quieter: *"Gracias, Nuberroca, sé que eso significa mucho para ti"* [Td:3181]. The party moves on into the dark, Nuberroca carrying Kaelthar, no sound of pursuit behind [Td:3184-3188].

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 36}}

\page

# Session 37 {{float:"right" 2026-02-08}}
{wide}

## 1 - Rest in the tunnel
_~16:30, 11 Hammer? 1490 DR_

1. The party sits roughly 240 ft into the tunnel, bruised and spent [Td:282], with pursuers somewhere behind them [Td:181]. In the dark Nyx's foot catches something metallic — a short pipe-shaped object, no tobacco smell, no clear purpose yet [Td:177-180].
2. Nuberroca hands Nyx a spare length of rope to replace the one Nyx lost on the way in [Td:182-188].
3. The party decides to camp in the tunnel rather than press on wounded [Td:207-213]. Mialee argues for pushing forward [Td:260-263], but Nuberroca insists on resting — half the party is heavily hurt and there is no proper healer in the group with Sinsong away [Td:275, 279].
4. Nyx places her warding spell behind the party, audible, triggering if anyone comes within sixty feet [Td:287-290, 296-298, 302-305].
5. Mialee breathes incense and summons Echo [Td:293-299]. She sends the bat eighty feet ahead down the tunnel to watch, with instructions to come running if it sees anything [Td:317-331].
6. Nyx distributes a palm of her berries to the party — four to Mialee to shave one day from a lingering grave wound, one to Eguzkilore for the last of his bruises [Td:426-430].
7. Nuberroca works a day off one of his own grave wounds through the long rest with his Healer's Kit [Td:463-468, 500-515].
8. Over the rest Nyx fiddles with the strange pipe. Up close it has no tobacco at all, and when she sets it to her ear she can hear noticeably farther than her own ear reaches — a listening horn, not a pipe [Td:476-482].
9. Eight hours pass. Neither the alarm nor Echo stirs; no one comes down the tunnel [Td:457-460 ?ord].

## 2 - Through the tunnel to the marsh
_~00:30?, 12 Hammer? 1490 DR_

1. Nuberroca argues against open flame in the enclosed tunnel [Td:540-541], but there is no other way for the party to see together, and Mialee suggests Sinsong's bald head would have done nicely were he here [Td:536-547]. Nuberroca ends up walking point with a lit torch while the others ring him at a distance [Td:590-600].
2. Over the hours the walls shift under Nuberroca's torchlight — carved stone softens into packed earth, then earth braced with woven willow holding the roof and walls together [Td:549-551]. The air thickens into damp and some stretches of wall drip [Td:552-553].
3. After roughly three hours there is still no light ahead; after four the party sees an arch of pale, steady light at the tunnel's end — not the flicker of torches, something constant [Td:555-559].
4. Mialee sends Echo out through the arch to listen and look. The bat reports the marsh beyond — a boggy flat with scattered trees, no one in sight on the near ground [Td:560-575].
5. The party emerges into the **Mere of Dead Men** [Td:571].

## 3 - Following the webbed tracks
_~05:00?, 12 Hammer? 1490 DR_

1. Half an hour out of the tunnel the dawn is coming on grey [Td:681]. A path winds out ahead of them, clearly marked with hatchets on trees and strips of cloth tied to branches [Td:602-603].
2. In the muddy stretches above the water the party can see fresh webbed footprints [Td:603]. Nuberroca and Nyx study them — unfamiliar but, from the tunnel's context, likely **lizardfolk** [Td:608-609, 644].
3. Nyx reminds Nuberroca that she and Mialee both saw lizardfolk in their visions — these are the same tracks their dreams showed [Td:646-648].
4. The marsh is foul and slow going — bugs rising from every pool, mud dragging at every step [Td:682-685]. The party follows the trail cautiously.
5. Nyx's ear catches something ahead: splashing, several bodies, moving as quietly as they know how [Td:694-697]. She signs the others to halt in a soldier's gesture and slips forward alone to scout [Td:698-700].

## 4 - The lizardfolk ambush
_~05:15?, 12 Hammer? 1490 DR_

1. Nyx tries to hide in a thicket. The lie of her hiding is one of the worst she can remember, and she knows at once that at least one of them has seen her [Td:716-724].
2. Looking back from the thicket she counts around seventeen — most about human height but a touch shorter; two or three the size of a tall man; one of them very large indeed, nearly Nuberroca's size, and it is the big one that has her [Td:744-749, 951-953].
3. Behind her Nuberroca steps apart from the others, into the torchlight alone, and starts to laugh like a madman at nothing — bait for the ambush [Td:701-705 ?ord].
4. ***[EPIC]*** Nyx draws, breathes, and sends an arrow into the big one's shoulder from cover — a hard hit, enough that the party can see him flinch and set [Td:808, 826-827].
5. Nuberroca strikes the flat of his torch against his shield and calls out in the dark: *"No nos acechéis criaturas. Si queréis pelear, salid a por ella. Si no, hablad."* [Td:752-753 ?ord].
6. Nyx whispers a word to Nuberroca across the gap — the biggest one is now his to meet [Td:751 ?ord].
7. The lizardfolk carry clubs set with small stones and a brace of javelins each; none has thrown yet [Td:884-886, 894].
8. Nuberroca produces a strip of travel-ration and a gold piece and holds them out to the nearest advancing lizardfolk [Td:909-917]. The lizardfolk hesitates, lowers its javelin a fraction, and approaches with its shield-arm forward [Td:915-920]. The others watch and seem to be weighing their choices [Td:916].

## 5 - Battle in the marsh
_~05:20?, 12 Hammer? 1490 DR_

1. One of the lizardfolk breaks the quiet and hurls a javelin at Nyx [Td:1013-1015]. She twists, draws her bow in the same motion and sends an arrow back along its path — a critical shot into the thrower's belly, a near-killing wound [Td:1024-1041]. The lizardfolk crumples, dragging himself behind the trunk and cursing Nyx in his own tongue: *matadla, que me ha dejado de la mierda* — only the big one and Kaelthar catch his words [Td:1339-1341].
2. Kaelthar enters the fight behind Nuberroca's cover [Td:1047], a shimmer of warding turning the first javelins thrown at him [Td:1211-1215, 1297-1301].
3. One among the lizardfolk raises brambles out of the ground — thorny vines that whip across the party's footing, blood for every step they try through [Td:1225-1230]. The marsh they stand on becomes a trap.
4. ***[EPIC]*** A javelin crits into Nyx's left arm; blood goes fast and the shock leaves her stunned where she stands [Td:1458-1472]. 
5. Mialee throws a bank of thick fog across her own ground, then steps into the cloud herself and sees her targets through Echo's blind-sight [Td:1543-1573].
6. A critical hit lands on Nuberroca's right leg — a burn across the flesh that will leave him more vulnerable to fire from now on [Td:1735-1738].
7. Nuberroca drops his shield and spear, pulls free his **halberd**, and sets to work with its longer reach [Td:1761-1764].
8. The lizardfolk caster, seeing Mialee's fog still standing, rains **Hail of Stone** down through the whole column — a pillar of falling stone sixty feet tall [Td:1842-1853]. Everyone inside takes it hard: Nyx drops to the ground [Td:1853, 1873-1874]; Mialee survives with slivers [Td:1864-1865]; Echo is pulverised in the air [Td:1866-1870].
9. Mialee, through the fog, drives a scimitar into a lizardfolk's flank — a stab in from his blind side [Td:1914-1917]. 
10. ***[EPIC]*** Mialee uses her *mage hand* to tip a healing potion down Nyx's throat — Nyx stirs on the ground, half conscious but seeing [Td:1921-1931, 1965-1967].
11. ***[EPIC]*** The chief draws a steel sword — the only worked metal among the band [Td:1611-1612 ?ord] — and takes two shots at Kaelthar's back. The second lands critical and drops Kaelthar unconscious; the chief pulls the kill at the last moment and the dragonborn stays alive [Td:1978-1996, 2015-2022].
12. The chief shouts an order only Kaelthar half-catches, and the next blows against the downed PCs come softer — the lizardfolk pull their strikes to *daño no letal*, binding the fallen instead of killing them [Td:2041-2056, 2089].
13. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca invokes his cloud-stride and vanishes from the ring — he reappears outside the lizardfolk circle, free of the surround [Td:2103-2105]. The lizardfolk flinch at the magic [Td:2105].
14. Nuberroca lowers his halberd and holds out his hands: *"Efectivamente somos buenos guerreros y todavía tenemos más trucos, pero este combate lo habéis ganado vosotros."* [Td:2106-2109].
15. The caster speaks and the brambles melt back into the ground — the fight is called [Td:2113-2114].

## 6 - The chief's bargain
_~05:30, 12 Hammer? 1490 DR_

1. The chief steps forward, keeping a careful distance from Nuberroca's reach, and asks whether he is the party's leader [Td:2121-2122]. Nuberroca answers that he is not — but that he often ends up speaking for them [Td:2124].
2. Nuberroca offers five gold and then another thirty silver as reparation. The chief accepts the coin and tells Nuberroca he looks a reasonable, intelligent man [Td:2142-2153].
3. The bound party is brought water that steadies them for talking [Td:2162]. Nuberroca explains the party had come up through a tunnel after a fight at a roadhouse on the far side — and later asks the chief straight whether they themselves run with the Cult of the Dragon [Td:2157-2201].
4. The chief answers in broken Common: *"A veces hay gente que merece morir. El culto hace lo que cree correcto. Aunque a veces tenga aliados malos."* [Td:2205-2209]. He plants the cult as neighbour, not kin.
5. The chief explains why he wants the party's blades: another kin of the marsh — the **bullywugs** — killed his lizardfolk leader by a stab in the back, and the bullywugs outnumber his band [Td:2290-2302].
6. His offer: kill the bullywugs' leader for us. Safe passage through the marsh can be talked about when the thing is done [Td:2303-2305]. Nuberroca accepts the terms [Td:2306-2308]; Nyx nods the same from where she sits [Td:2309].
7. The chief adds that the bullywugs are more numerous than his own band — the party will have to be *más inteligentes* than they were against lizardfolk if they expect to walk out of the bullywug nest [Td:2314-2317].
8. Kaelthar, awake and bound, pushes the chief in what Draconic he can muster — a kick to the leg and then a boot in the ribs for his trouble [Td:2311-2321 ?ord].
9. The lizardfolk mark Mialee for a magic-user by her fog and leave her bound; the same goes for Sinsong-in-absentia, by Nuberroca's accounting of the party [Td:2366-2370].
10. The chief sends the bulk of his band off to patrol the marsh and keeps a handful with him to guard the party [Td:2373], then at last introduces himself: **Snapjaw** [Td:2383]. Draconic comes to him naturally; his Common is broken — *"yo Snapjaw, vosotros fuertes, matad bullybooks"* [Td:2385].
11. Snapjaw commits to travelling the next leg beside the party, his group to follow when summoned [Td:2409-2412].
12. The sky over the mere is just beginning to pale when Snapjaw calls the day's work done — ~05:30 by now — information on the bullywugs' nest and leader is to be exchanged while the party rests the rest of the morning out [Td:2358 ?ord, 2413-2419].

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 37}}

\page

# Session 38 {{float:"right" 2026-02-15}}
{wide}

## 1 - The deal takes hold
_~06:00?, 12 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nuberroca holds **Mialee** in his lap, cord round her wrists, and tells **Snapjaw** they have surrendered and only want rest and to do as asked [Td:16]; Mialee tries to work the ropes free, Nuberroca grips her tighter [Td:132-141].
2. Mialee presses Snapjaw on why the lizardfolk serve the Cult; the answer: *"La gloria es de Tiamat, pero nosotros no rezamos a los dragones"* [Td:27-31], and the bullywugs — not the dragons — are what they want dead [Td:33-35].
3. The lizardfolk name their lord of the castle: *"Dralmorrer"*, *"El castillo en el lago"*, where *"el agua es verde"* [Td:42-52]; Nuberroca mocks the exchange by pissing and showing *"agua amarilla"* while Mialee is still in his lap [Td:72-77].
4. ***[EPIC]*** One lizardfolk takes a shine to **Eguzkilore**'s short sword and offers four silver, a dried bird, and two beads of its own make [Td:112-121]; Sinsong pushes Eguzkilore to accept and quietly pays five gold for the bird [Td:142-150], the lizardfolk around them erupting into a frenzy of dried-bird and crude-collar offers once they see the gold change hands [Td:161-173].
5. Mialee learns from Snapjaw that the *señora dragona* commands the dragonborn chief and that gold flows up to the dragonborn [Td:183-194].
6. Nuberroca trades his pickaxe to one of the big lizardfolk for a pair of "magic" collars; on handling them he realises they are not magical, just village-stall trinkets [Td:195-215].
7. Snapjaw announces the deal is sealed — the lizardfolk patrol will carry on, and **Tompu** will guide the party to the castle where they must kill **Pharblex** [Td:225-230]; *"Pharblex es peligroso, él mató a nuestro líder"* [Td:231]; the lizardfolk return the party's weapons and withdraw [Td:225, Td:243-247], and Nuberroca releases Mialee from his lap with the warning not to pull more tricks in front of these people [Td:249-250].

## 2 - Waking the dragon
_~06:30?, 12 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Sinsong lays his hands on **Kaelthar** — his bald head flaring — and heals him back to his senses [Td:251, 266].
2. Kaelthar comes to raging about his missing weapons and starts for the lizardfolk shouting *"hay que matarlos a todos"* [Td:270-273]; Sinsong drops him with a Sleep spell before he can land a blow [Td:276-284].
3. Nuberroca kneels beside him as he wakes and explains: *"Sinsong te acaba de lanzar un hechizo, como me hacía a mí"* [Td:291], and that the party has agreed to kill the bullywug leader in exchange for release [Td:297-299].
4. The party asks Tompu through Kaelthar for a short rest; Kaelthar reports that Tompu is calling them cowards but will defer to them [Td:308-315].

## 3 - Short rest, long leaving
_~07:00?, 12 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Eguzkilore hands the lizardfolk scimitar to Kaelthar so he has something to swing [Td:339-348], then asks whether he can swap his weapon mastery to the unidentified magical longsword Mialee is carrying before combat [Td:352-375].
2. Nuberroca spends time trying to lash the unidentified magical longsword onto the shaft of a spear for a one-handed polearm, then thinks better of it — the lash-up would shatter at the first thrust and the plan is quietly shelved [Td:390-487], the sword earmarked for Kaelthar instead [Td:489-491].
3. Nuberroca sets out to re-stock his medicine kit by foraging through the marsh with Tompu's help, book in hand; the plants are the right plants but he fumbles the processing — a *natural 1* on his medicine throw — and comes back empty-handed [Td:498-553].

## 4 - Marsh road to Naerytar
_~07:00?, 12 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The party asks Tompu where to the castle; he answers in words they do not understand except *"Naerytar"*, which Kaelthar translates as the bullywug castle [Td:524-528].
2. Mialee rides on **Nuberroca**'s shoulders — his pace suits her better than her own [Td:531-534]; formation: Eguzkilore on point with Tompu, Kaelthar in the centre [Td:558-566].
3. Tompu stops them more than once with a hand on Eguzkilore's shoulder — far ahead a crocodile the size of a cart surfaces; the guide carries them wide of it without incident [Td:567-574].
4. After roughly two hours of marsh the party stumbles on a far larger lizardfolk patrol; whether these serve the Cult too the party cannot say and has not asked [Td:575-578].

## 5 - The second patrol
_~09:00?, 12 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Tompu goes ahead and signals the strangers with bird-calls and gutturals the party can barely parse [Td:580-586]; the lizardfolk respond in kind and the leader of the patrol — a bead-draped magic-user with limited common — steps forward [Td:603-608].
2. The leader mutters over one of his trinkets, his eyes light briefly [Td:620-623], and when it fades he addresses them in words they understand: *"vais a matar a los Wollybugs, dice Tompu"* [Td:623-624].
3. He pledges four of his own warriors — **Atzi** the big one and three others — to join the escort [Td:636-640], and names the party's deadline: *"una luna y un sol en llegar. En dos lunas estaremos allí"* [Td:655-659].
4. Mialee buys their loyalty with three daggers to the smaller lizardfolk and a short sword to Atzi [Td:665-685]; the warriors look at her *"admirándote"* in reply [Td:685-686].
5. ***[EPIC]*** Mialee doubles down — she lifts one of **Sinsong**'s painted *piedras* with an unseen hand and lets it drift into the big one's palm with the words *"necesitaréis este objeto mágico"* [Td:686-694]; Nuberroca passes his own stone, then Sinsong hands over three more, promising *"en los descansos cortos voy haciendo más"* [Td:697-702].
6. Sinsong admits in passing that his *piedras* are painted river-pebbles — the first batch was his transformed villagers, these are plain stones with eyes drawn on [Td:704-710].
7. Tompu, bypassed in the gift-giving, pads up with his hand out and sad eyes; Mialee hands him her last scimitar and he lights up [Td:711-719].
8. From here on the party moves at the centre of a lizardfolk escort, Mialee crowning herself *"la señora de los lagartos"* [Td:720-724]; Nuberroca grins *"vamos como los porteadores"* [Td:725-726], the lizardfolk closing ranks round the march.

## 6 - Bullywug patrol
_~11:00?, 12 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. One hour on, Tompu and the escort stop in their tracks, sniffing the air; the word they share needs no translation — *"Bullywugs"* [Td:800-813], and the stench of rot catches the party next [Td:810-812].
2. A patrol of mud-caked frog-men is sweeping the ground straight toward them; the lizardfolk, who go to lengths to keep themselves clean, visibly recoil [Td:816-824].
3. Sinsong draws a steadying bond between Mialee, Eguzkilore and himself to carry them through the bluff [Td:836-862]; Mialee tells Tompu through Kaelthar they are not fighting this patrol — they will pretend to be cultists bound for the castle with the lizardfolk as escort [Td:863-867].
4. Tompu stares and Atzi's lizard-face falls — *"Ibais a matar Bullywugs"* [Td:868-869]; Mialee answers that they will, but Pharblex first [Td:870-874].
5. The bullywugs pass without stopping, pointing at the lizardfolk and laughing something close to it [Td:884-889]; the lizardfolk duck their heads and let it ride [Td:889-890].
6. Kaelthar overhears the lizardfolk muttering afterwards — the party ducking a patrol has them whispering whether this *"libertadores"* are truly going to free them or are just cowards [Td:900].

## 7 - The reed embarcadero
_~late evening, 12 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Darkness closes in and the lizardfolk, who have no night-vision, start struggling with the going; Sinsong takes Tompu's spear, kindles a Light on the blade and gives it back — Tompu drops it in panic the first time until Kaelthar explains it is friendly magic [Td:932-940].
2. By spear-light they push on another two and a half hours and reach a stone platform rising out of the water — three dugout canoes rest on reed decks, with fire-blackened cobbles at the centre [Td:941-947].
3. The lizardfolk head straight for two reed baskets, dig out dried birds and crushed rats and settle to eat; the four new escorts try to barter their meal for coin from Sinsong, who shows them his roast chicken and waves them off [Td:951-957].
4. Sinsong talks the group through a wholesale healing session: with hope kindled over them he moves scar-closing prayer by prayer between Nuberroca, Mialee, Eguzkilore and himself [Td:1006, Td:1019-1072, Td:1098-1105].
5. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca pulls out his medicine kit and Sinsong steadies his hands — between them they close two of Nuberroca's lingering wounds in a single pass [Td:1120-1156].

## 8 - The hunt for mounts
_~morning, 13 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The night-camp mud shows heavy tracks a wide arc round the camp — lizard-feet much broader than Tompu's; the guide names them *"caballos lagarto"* — mounts of a kind, if the party could tame them [Td:1223-1235].
2. Nuberroca scouts for herbs again under Tompu's guidance; this time he returns with a plant that smells right and Sinsong, bald head aglow, works the paste in and closes another grave wound [Td:1267-1292].
3. The party sets out east into the marsh after the tracks; roughly two hours in Tompu points out four lizards *"bastante más grandes que un caballo"* half-hidden in the reeds, feeding [Td:1482-1491].
4. Sinsong edges close with a guiding whisper on his lips, quiet as he can manage, then lets a sleeping prayer roll through the reeds — three of the four lizards topple on the spot [Td:1534-1591, Td:1663]; one, far larger than the rest, stays wide awake and locks on him [Td:1591-1601].
5. The big lizard dashes him down and goes for a bite that falls short as he bolts [Td:1601-1613]; Nuberroca steps in with his spear held crossways, no cut, blocking the beast off while shouting at Eguzkilore to help [Td:1619-1623].
6. Mialee tosses rations wide of the sleeping ones to draw a quiet one toward her, then floats a rope round its neck with an unseen hand and steps up onto its back [Td:1673-1703]; Nuberroca turns to Atzi for help, and with the big one's steadying hand on his shoulder his next approach comes smoother [Td:1627].
7. ***[EPIC]*** The big lizard rips a gash across Eguzkilore's face — the wound soaks him in blood and venom-slick skin — and Eguzkilore is blinded for as long as the wound takes to heal [Td:1843-1858]; Nuberroca kneels beside him with wrapping and marsh herbs trying to steady the damage, but his fingers slip and the quick-fix fails [Td:1894-1906].

## 9 - The bullywug ambush
_~midday, 13 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Croaking rolls up out of the reeds and every lizardfolk whips to the southeast; a swarm of giant toads leaps bodily out of the undergrowth onto **Tayana**, one of the escort lizardfolk, and in seconds she is dead [Td:1913-1917].
2. Behind the leaping shapes a bullywug in feather-collars — their shaman — strides forward, a slick-coated blade already in hand [Td:1920-1921, Td:2006, Td:2012-2016].
3. Tompu bolts [Td:1926-1927]; Mialee presses on with the taming, Sinsong keeps the sleep on the fallen lizards, and Nuberroca swings up onto the lizard he has broken, polearm couched [Td:2093-2098].
4. The bullywug shaman closes on Nuberroca with a venom-slicked blade — the sting of the cut flashes on each hit, but the venom does not take hold [Td:2007-2016].
5. Sinsong calls down a protecting swarm of light — picking friends out, sparing the lizardfolk and the half-tamed mounts; the first toads die under it [Td:2019-2033].
6. ***[EPIC]*** The shaman answers with a wide green blast of his own that catches the lot of them [Td:2127-2144]; Sinsong is caught full in it, loses the swarm, and goes down right there in the mud [Td:2138, Td:2147-2156].
7. Nuberroca, nearly dropped himself but still mounted, slams a bone-crush through the shaman's hand — the staff flies loose and the shaman is forced onto the back foot [Td:2101-2122 ?ord].

## 10 - Berry and bite
_~midday, 13 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Eguzkilore gropes his way through the dark to Sinsong — he cannot see but he can hear him fall — and feeds him one of Nyx's berries; Sinsong comes to [Td:2227-2240].
2. Atzi vaults onto a toad and crushes its skull with his mace, then tears the throat out of a second with a bite [Td:2315-2321]; the surviving toads break and bound for the open water [Td:2322-2329].
3. Nuberroca swings down from his lizard, drops his pack and chases the retreating shaman — three spears thrown, the first catching the shaman between the shoulders and dropping him face-first in the mud [Td:2353-2389]; a second thrown spear catches another frog as the swarm scatters [Td:2389-2399].
4. The lizardfolk gather Tayana's body, strip her collar of feathers, and mark the spot with sticks and stones [Td:2402-2404]; Tompu offers the party a bitter blessing — *"Hemos perdido a un hermano, pero hemos matado muchos bullywugs y tenéis vuestros caballos"* [Td:2405-2407].
5. Nuberroca names his mount **Juancho** and Mialee names hers **Atascaburras** [Td:2423-2426, Td:2517]; the mounts stay nervous but will carry them.
6. Mialee rolls out the mage's blade and corpses — nothing of value except a bag of spell-components useless to a Harper, and a residue on the blade that she pieces together as the mage's own natural venom, sweating off its own skin, dissipating in minutes [Td:2527-2555]; she searches for empty vials to collect it and finds she has none [Td:2554-2578].
7. Sinsong unfolds the sealed message he pulled from **Bog Luck**'s warehouse and tells the group *"creo que era para este momento"* — the content stays close [Td:2585-2593].

## 11 - By canoe to the next camp
_~afternoon, 13 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Big heal between combat and travel: Sinsong lays a wave of peace over the party, rounds the healing off on himself with his bald-head glow again, and the party burns through most of Nyx's remaining berries [Td:2461-2486].
2. The three canoes allow two mochila-carrying passengers each, with the lizardfolk and the tamed mounts swimming alongside; after eight hours on the water the lizardfolk call a halt at a dry island [Td:2650-2721].

## 12 - The pendant of Sinsong's warrior
_~night, 13 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Guard rotation set for the night — Mialee and Eguzkilore pull the watches since elves rest the shortest; Eguzkilore insists his blindness only makes him the best watch they have [Td:2724-2731].
2. More healing before sleep — Sinsong moves through the party closing lingering wounds on Nuberroca, Mialee and Kaelthar; Nuberroca lets his rage carry a healing of its own and closes a wound off his own fury [Td:2743-2800].
3. ***[EPIC]*** Sinsong draws Nuberroca aside and offers him the recipe he found at Bog Luck's warehouse — a cruel rite to validate Nuberroca's faith in Sinsong and bind him as his warrior; Nuberroca presses on what it protects against — mages, red mages, necromancers, undead — and agrees [Td:2866-2889].
4. Sinsong binds Nuberroca's own blood into the stone with three bell-tolls of pain; by the third chime fresh wounds have opened across Nuberroca's body and the stone is reforged — Sinsong lays the newly-minted pendant round his neck, promising a will of iron against mind-magic and a steadiness no mage could shake [Td:2890-2935].
5. Nuberroca prays his thanks — *"Sinsong, soy más fuerte de lo que crees"* — flexes his pectorals and spends the night curled by Juancho getting to know him better [Td:2938-2947, Td:2993].

{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 38}}
