# Session 41 {{float:"right" 2026-03-15}}
{wide}

## 1 - Round ten on the drum
_~11:00, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nyx looses a last arrow into the bullywug follower who had been chasing them up the stairs about the true faith, and finishes him on the stones [Td:249-250].
2. Eguzkilore pulls on the longbow, holds a breath, and takes a jumping bullywug clean through the eye at the top of its leap over Nuberroca; the body folds against the parapet [Td:252-273].
3. Mialee leans over the stone lip and shouts in the broken common she has — _"los Bullywugs han revelado contra el culto y contra Tiamat"_ — and tries to pass it off as a lizardfolk truth. The lizards below hear enough to suspect her, no more [Td:280-290].
4. She draws the stones back around her and lays a fog across the drum platform; the drum and the party vanish into the cloud [Td:287-293].

## 2 - Nuberroca at the stairhead

1. Three bullywug lizard-shamans in feathered gear gesture up from the ground; the stone around the platform lifts and ripples. A wave of grinding earth catches Harley, Paco the skeleton and Eguzkilore. Harley drops under the hit; Paco goes down with her and stays down; Eguzkilore holds his footing on the stones [Td:425-464].
2. Nuberroca comes in reckless, polearm swinging, and splits a leaping bullywug champion in two with the first swing; both halves drop behind him and he's bathed in it [Td:362-365 ?ord, Td:489-494].
3. The second swing could kill the lizard beside him. Instead the goliath drives the polearm into the wall, strikes his own chest, and roars _"¡Snapjaw!"_ at the lizard before swinging back and killing the frog beside it instead [Td:493-500]. The spared lizard, unknown to the party, is a Snapjaw loyalist [Td:510-512].
4. ***[EPIC]*** Sinsong, in the ring of harrying light that turns around him on the drum, hurls a bolt of flame into the drum skin from an open hand; the hide catches and begins to smoulder from the edge [Td:513-515].

## 3 - The lizardfolk hold the body

1. More bullywug champions reach the platform. Three champions and a mage press Nuberroca at once, all swinging with advantage because he is surrounded; every attack lands and he falls under the weight of them [Td:781-803, Td:811-814].
2. One of the bigger lizardfolk sprawls across the goliath's chest, keeping the bullywugs off him; the lizards want him alive [Td:816-818, Td:1428-1430].
3. Sinsong, still in melee contact with the goliath, lays a curing touch on him on his reaction; Nuberroca gasps up to a single point of life and does not rise [Td:875-883].
4. The spared Snapjaw-loyal lizard does not make it through the next round — he lies dead on the stones nearby, a quiet casualty of the room [Td:1167-1169].

## 4 - Kaelthar on the wing
_~11:15?, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Kaelthar arrives late to the fight through the front gate. The lizardfolk pressing on Tompu are not pressing hard but will not join; Tompu is taking shoves and open hands and not an outright beating [Td:928-933].
2. Kaelthar steps between them and speaks to the lizards directly, in Draconic — his first language and theirs — a short piece on common enemies and refuges Tompu is being refused [Td:950-971]. The words land further than they should, and the lizards shift on their feet, hesitant, half-dubious [Td:1007-1015].
3. ***[EPIC]*** On the back half of his first turn he unfurls dragonborn wings, launches off the ground, and climbs straight at the second-floor tower window — _"a tomar por culo"_. The giant lizards can scale walls; they will not catch him on the climb and know it [Td:1046-1072].
4. Below him the lizardfolk guard begins to drift away from Tompu toward the tower door, drawn by the fight [Td:1076].

## 5 - The rope above the fog

1. Nyx, invisible inside Mialee's drifting fog, cuts a ten-foot length from her sixty-foot rope with the short sword and raises a Rope Trick straight up through the cloud. She climbs through the slit on the last of her turn and the opening closes behind her [Td:1188-1224].
2. Mialee's fog loses its anchor the moment Nyx passes into the dimensional pocket — concentration cannot cross the border, and the cloud rolls off the platform as it fades [Td:1308-1313].
3. Nuberroca comes to and finds himself free to shift in the breath of the recovery; he uses his species jaunt and reappears beside the tower window [Td:1131-1145 ?ord].
4. The first thing he does from the parapet is roar — _"¡Snapjaw!"_ — out across the castle grounds [Td:1163-1169].

## 6 - The twin acid spheres
_~11:30?, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. A new flight of bullywug mages reaches the tower. The first throws a sphere of green acid over Nuberroca and Kaelthar; the acid rolls over both of them and both are caught in the full of it. Nuberroca drops where he stands [Td:1364-1380].
2. The second mage plants the same spell in the same place. Kaelthar pushes a second-chance moment and shrugs most of the acid off; Nuberroca, unconscious on the stones, takes the dose again at full and his life flickers nearer the edge [Td:1381-1416].
3. A third mage reaches the platform with a running jump, swings a bogged staff into Kaelthar for a heavy blow, and drops the dragonborn to the floor; the lizardfolk around them keep hitting without killing — the order from below is still _"vivos"_ [Td:1422-1430].
4. From her Rope Trick slit, Nyx drops back down the rope, slides across the smoke, and feeds one of her berries to Nuberroca where he lies. He breathes again [Td:1443-1449].

## 7 - Up the rope

1. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca comes to with Nyx on top of him. He pulls her up, runs to the rope with her and drops her at the base so she can climb back into the pocket [Td:1753-1760]. Then he comes back across the stones for Kaelthar — grips him by the tail and drags him to the rope [derived:Td:1795-1818].
2. Nuberroca stamps a foot and shrinks himself to Medium so the carrying weight stops slowing him [Td:1680-1693 ?ord], then climbs the rope with Kaelthar in tow; both reach the pocket at the top [Td:1829-1838].
3. Sinsong does not make it. The lizards press him from all sides inside his still-turning ring of light and begin to drag him off the drum by the hips. Mialee, climbing, calls down to him by his nickname — _"Cabezabombilla"_ — before anyone else notices he is being taken [Td:1864-1876].
4. Mialee pulls the rope up behind her last of all and the slit closes [Td:1875-1883].

## 8 - The elf and the bald man

1. From the Rope Trick slit the party watches the surviving defenders stack the bullywug and lizard corpses on the still-smouldering drum. The elegant elf Dralmorrer gives orders in the courtyard, chewing out the bullywugs for the carnage; a bullywug mage finishes the dissolution with a last acid sphere over the pile [Td:1912-1917, Td:1957-1961].
2. Harley sits silently beside them and cries as her undead burn down to grease on the drum [Td:1963-1967].
3. Beside Dralmorrer a bald man in red robes with four hooded bodyguards steps into the courtyard — pale faces, half-visible tattoos under the hoods. Mialee recognises him from the caravan on the road [Td:1952-1977 ?ord].
4. The bald man looks straight up at the Rope Trick slit, meets eyes with the party, and gives a thin smile; a cultist runs off at his word. Below him more cultists are quietly forming a circle under the slit; they know where the party is and are reading for the blast [Td:1991-2022].

## 9 - The bag of holding

1. Harley opens her Bag of Holding and tells the group to climb in. Mialee, Nyx and Eguzkilore fit — Eguzkilore just — and vanish into the pocket. Nuberroca at eight feet is too big by far, and Kaelthar is only almost small enough [Td:2032-2074].
2. Harley puts invisibility on both Nuberroca and Kaelthar at once with a single casting, then on herself; the Bag goes into Nuberroca's hand [Td:2092-2109].
3. Harley climbs into the Bag with the others, carrying it with her; the three outside are left invisible and holding the pocket [Td:2102-2109].
4. Kaelthar, still invisible, lays a curing touch on the goliath and gives him enough to stand; then takes the rest of his lay-on-hands pool back into his own wounds [Td:2165-2186].

## 10 - The dimension collapses

1. The Rope Trick hour runs out. The floor of the dimension vanishes under their feet; everyone inside drops the ten feet to the stones below — a bruising fall but no worse [Td:2218-2219].
2. Nuberroca cushions his landing with his species jaunt and reappears at the tower window, still invisible, before the mages can pick him out of the air [Td:2199-2203 ?ord, Td:2219-2222].
3. Kaelthar flashes to the same spot in a plume of mist and lands beside the goliath. The casting gives him away — his invisibility snaps the moment the spell forms, where Nuberroca's species jaunt had kept the goliath hidden, and the cultists on the ramparts see him at once [Td:2203-2223].
4. The dragonclaw and his bodyguards swing on the newly-visible dragonborn and land the first hit from the ramparts [Td:2224-2225].

## 11 - Command, chain, and the forest

1. One of the hooded bodyguards speaks a word of command at Kaelthar; the first word slides off him. A second bodyguard follows with the same word, and this one takes. Kaelthar begins to walk toward them against his will [Td:2256-2275].
2. He gets as far as the melee ring and takes the opening swings; the first blow lands cleanly with him pinned on all sides, the next drops him to a single point and he falls unconscious. The cultists seize him before Nuberroca can turn [Td:2371-2402].
3. Nuberroca, still invisible, drops over the parapet for the fall and lands near Juancho and Atascaburras, who have come back up the path for him. He runs ninety feet a turn until the trees cover him and the castle is out of sight behind him [Td:2297-2307 ?ord, Td:2406-2410].
4. In a clearing beyond the forest edge he tips the Bag of Holding out onto the ground like a sack of marbles; the three inside spill out gasping, cold and half-asphyxiated — barely a minute of air left between them [Td:2410-2411, Td:2353-2365].

## 12 - The smithy

1. In the castle, concurrent with the forest escape above, Sinsong wakes to being slapped awake by cultists. His armour, weapon and staff are stripped and piled a few feet away, under guard; Kaelthar's gear is in the same pile. Across the room the dragonborn is unconscious and almost naked, gagged, bleeding but stable — someone has attended his wounds [Tdi:3-6].
2. The room is an old smithy, poorly kept, with many piles of half-finished weapons at the walls. Dralmorrer stands elegant in the middle, keeping his distance so the blood won't reach him; three lizardfolk guards and two cultists hold the two prisoners; near the door stand Azbara Jos — bald, tattooed, red-robed — and two pale hooded men in red with armour and tattoos showing under the hoods [Tdi:7-16].
3. Dralmorrer tells Sinsong not to try to lie; their friend by the door will know. He wants names, abilities, spells, and objectives [Tdi:16-18].
4. Sinsong tells him he is Sinsong, from a mountain dwarf village half of which has been turned to stone, that he has come looking for a cure, and that the dragonborn — _"Kaelthar o algo así"_ — is a travelling companion he barely knows [Tdi:23-31]. The lie-check against the pale guard fails; Sinsong has not sold it [Tdi:33-36].

## 13 - The Red Wizard's questions

1. Azbara Jos crosses the room without quite touching Sinsong and demands to know which spells he uses, how he flew above the drum, how he summoned the creatures that attacked for him [Tdi:97-101].
2. Sinsong tries the gambit from the drum. Flying, he explains, is a procedure of discipline — lie face down and shout _"Mía, ataca"_ at the right moment [Tdi:99-105]. Azbara does not laugh — _"parece que piensas que esto es un juego"_ [Tdi:106-107]. Sinsong admits to the ring of harrying light and the fire-bolt by name, and admits he could not re-raise them now without his focus [Tdi:111-129].
3. Dralmorrer presses him on the lizardfolk ally. Sinsong gives up Tompu's name. Dralmorrer turns and speaks a short phrase in a language Sinsong doesn't know to the lizardfolk guard beside him; the single word _Tompu_ comes through [Tdi:151-157]. The guard confirms Tompu is one of their own fort's lizardfolk — a traitor [Tdi:158-162]. Dralmorrer drives a knife into Sinsong's hand for the lie — Tompu had told the party only that the lizardfolk here were being pressed by the bullywugs [Tdi:163-169].
4. Pressed on their objectives, Sinsong tells him they're looking for friends; one of Dralmorrer's bodyguards dislocates Sinsong's elbow for the evasiveness [Tdi:193-204]. Pressed again on who sent them — the Harpers? the Order of the Gauntlet? — Sinsong points at the gagged Kaelthar: _"el dragón... el Lao Gigante"_ [Tdi:233-249].

## 14 - Pharblex demands blood

1. Pharblex steps into the smithy — a bullywug mage half again the size of any the party has seen, in a crude mitre with necklaces of bones, feathers and dead bugs. He is flanked by two mages and three champions and comes straight at Sinsong with a dagger [Tdi:283-288].
2. Dralmorrer stops him by the wrist. The argument begins at a shout: the party have killed something like thirty-three of Pharblex's bullywugs across the morning, and he wants both their heads. Dralmorrer refuses, and Azbara watches disappointed from the back of the room, as if the whole exchange is beneath him [Tdi:289-294].
3. While they argue, Dralmorrer gives an order to the cultist holding Sinsong — mark them. The cultist takes a dragon-shaped pendant from his neck, picks it up with the smithy's own tongs, and lays it in the forge coals to heat [Tdi:294-296].
4. The argument runs some ten minutes. In the middle of it a cultist arrives with a quiet word; Dralmorrer orders Pharblex out, and the bullywug chief, surprised to be overruled, leaves [Tdi:300-304].

## 15 - Anton of the road

1. Anton — the man the party last saw with the caravan on the road — walks in and sees Sinsong tied. He explodes immediately — _"¡Cabrón! ¡Chicas puñaladas me pegaste!"_ — and lunges, until the Thayan guard puts a knife to his throat [Tdi:309-314].
2. Anton has a short private word with Dralmorrer. He tells him the party killed all his companions on the road. He will nonetheless speak for them: once the interrogation is done, Dralmorrer should let them go [Tdi:321-325].
3. On his way out, Anton turns and smiles at Azbara — _"Salvo que los necesites para tus armas"_ [Tdi:326-327]. Sinsong catches the word — weapons — and starts to ask; the next cultist's palm closes his mouth hard; here the questions come from one side only [Tdi:328-336].

## 16 - Tompu's blood on the blade

1. The smithy door opens again and two cultists drag a bound lizardfolk inside. Dralmorrer looks across to Sinsong: _"¿Este es el Tompu del que hablabas?"_ Sinsong confirms him [Tdi:337-343].
2. Sinsong plays friendly, hoping Tompu's half-connections can save them — _"menos mal que estás aquí"_ — but Tompu is terrified and will not meet his eyes [Tdi:343-348].
3. Dralmorrer walks over to Tompu and tells him, with the elvish quiet that doesn't need volume, how deeply he is disappointed in him — _"con todos los esfuerzos que he hecho para que vosotros recobréis vuestra antigua gloria"_ — then turns to one of the Thayan guards and says only _"todo tuyo"_ [Tdi:353-355].
4. ***[EPIC]*** The guard takes a half-finished blade from a nearby pile — one of the better-made among the bad ones, still unquenched — and sinks it into Tompu's chest. The blade drinks the blood as it enters. Tompu's scream travels through the smithy walls — the same scream the scouts outside will hear [Tdi:356-365]. The guard pulls the blade clear only when the drink is done, then takes Tompu's head and the body is dragged away [Tdi:363-365].

## 17 - The mark of Tiamat

1. ***[EPIC]*** The cultist with the forge tongs lifts the pendant out of the coals — the dragon shape red-hot in the fire — and presses it hard into Sinsong's forehead. The brand holds for a long second before he lifts it off. The mark of Tiamat sits on the Peace cleric's brow [Tdi:366-368].
2. He crosses the room and does the same to Kaelthar; the brand wakes the dragonborn mid-press, screaming into his gag [Tdi:369-371].
3. Sinsong goes under from the pain of the burn — the interrogation continues with Kaelthar alone for another twenty minutes which Sinsong will not remember [Tdi:371-385].

## 18 - A hut in the trees
_~13:00, 15 Hammer 1490 DR?_

1. In a clearing well south of the castle Harley kneels in the dirt, lays down a small crystal orb and a careful ring of stones, and casts her own fuller version of a shelter-dome over them. A ten-minute ritual leaves a transparent semi-sphere around the five of them, opaque from outside, moved as she moves. She tints it to vanish into the forest green [Td:2546-2585].
2. Eguzkilore has no proficiency to craft arrows; Mialee has the Scribe's hands for it. In the hour of the short rest she pulls wood from the forest and makes him sixteen improvised arrows — poor, blunt, but shootable [Td:2619-2680].
3. Nuberroca lies down and sleeps where he stops; Mialee and Nyx argue it is still morning and he will not be moved [Td:2615-2650 ?ord].

## 19 - The scouts and the breach

1. Nyx and Mialee move out together along the forest edge toward the castle. The scream from the castle, when it comes, is a lizardfolk voice — Eguzkilore makes the species through the trees [Td:3002-3003]. Nuberroca immediately guesses — Tompu [Td:3003].
2. On the north-east side of the castle wall Mialee finds a breach in the stones, a possible back way in, though the moat is still in the way of it. They mark it and keep moving [Td:2861-2882 ?ord].
3. From a line of sight on the primary tower, Mialee throws a sending-whisper at the window she can see and reaches first Kaelthar, then Sinsong. Both answer. They are on the first floor; they are watched; they can speak only if they can hide their lips [Td:2758-2810 ?ord].
4. Kaelthar reports what he sees in the smithy: the bald man, two to four Thayan-looking clerics, three lizardfolk guards, Dralmorrer and two bodyguards behind him, and someone else by a door he can't fully see [Td:2792-2799].

## 20 - The council in the trees

1. The scouts rejoin the hut and tell what they have. There is a breach but there is also the moat; the back door is not a free door [Td:2932-2938]. From the trees they can see smoke rising from the first tower — someone, now, is setting fires inside the castle [Td:3030-3037]. Mialee tries another whisper at Kaelthar and Sinsong — _"¿sois vosotros?"_ — and gets no answer back; whoever is lighting fires is not either of them [Td:3039-3049].
2. Nyx offers the opening they have been circling: she will disguise herself as a bullywug and burn one of their huts in open sight of the lizardfolk — enough to light the quarrel between the two camps, and then she will run [Td:2976-2985]. If the castle empties to put the fire down, the back breach may be theirs for the taking.
3. The patrols below are no longer bullywugs or lizardfolk alone — mixed groups now, with a cultist at the head of each. The lesson from the drum has been learned [Td:2525-2534 ?ord].
4. The party decides to wait for dark. Nothing more they can do by daylight — Mialee's recommendation stands [Td:2938-2951]. The session ends with the five of them at the edge of the trees watching the smoke go up from the first tower, and the rest of the plan held for after sundown [Td:3050-3063].

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 41}}

\page

# Session 42 - BACKTRACKED {{float:"right" 2026-03-22}}
{wide}

## 1 - Two screams from the castle
_~12:20, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. From the forest hide inside Harley's cabin — invisible to the outside, where the party took the short rest after their escape from the tower [Td:144-162] — they hear two screams of agony, voices they know well: Sinsong, then Kaelthar [Td:166-171]. A single lizardman's death-scream from the castle came through 5 to 10 minutes earlier, with a small flash and a thin ribbon of smoke on top of the tower a few minutes after that [Td:163-165].
2. Nyx and Eguzkilore are the only ones fit to move in. Harley cannot leave the cabin or it dissolves on the spot [Td:218]; Mialee will hold back near the breach to help from outside [Td:869]. Nuberroca will join from the cabin only — _"yo no voy con el sigilo a ningún lado"_ [Td:838-839, Td:888-889].

## 2 - Across the moat
_~12:30?, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The cleft in the outer wall Nyx found before is wide enough even for Nuberroca [Td:419-420]; the moat beneath holds at least two or three crocodiles seen through the gap [Td:325-326, Td:507-509]. Mialee drifts a ration into the water with her invisible mage hand to pull the crocodiles off their line [Td:343-345, Td:369-371]. A larger beast surges out of the water, swallows the ration, and scares the smaller crocodiles clear [Td:410-411].
2. Before stepping in, Eguzkilore calls on the woods — a quiet weave over the three of them that softens every step and smothers the ripples [Td:382-401]. The party crosses dry, unnoticed [Td:410].

## 3 - Through the gate as locals
_~12:50?, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nyx shifts her shape into a lizardman with _Disguise Self_ [Td:494, Td:781-782], caking herself in mud so the smell carries lizard rather than halfling [Td:517-519]. Eguzkilore pulls on cultist robes [Td:526, Td:537-540]; Mialee has no robe and stays back at the breach [Td:540].
2. **Azbara Jos** passes with two bodyguards on the walk in. He eyes the lizard-shaped Nyx, grunts _"límpiate,"_ and keeps moving [Td:569-570]. The pair cross into the central yard without being challenged [Td:596-598].
3. The yard is a working camp — lizardfolk running the big mounts through drills under **Atzi** [Td:582-586], mixed patrols of bullywugs, lizardfolk and a cultist walking the perimeter [Td:583]. Further in they recognise **Dralmorrer**, now in the half-plate the cult took off Sinsong — plainly too large on him, with the voluminous padding showing underneath [Td:588-595].

## 4 - Ejected from the hall
_~13:00?, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nyx walks on toward the hall at a normal pace; bullywugs inside the doorway grab her [Td:603-608], feel wrong texture under the illusion [Td:609-613], and shove the pair of them straight back out [Td:619-621] — _"aquí no aceptamos a los de vuestra calaña"_ [Td:621-622]. They drop into cover among crates by the yard [Td:623-624].
2. From there they hear it — four or five muffled punches, something cracking, a short pained groan unmistakably Kaelthar's, then silence [Td:669-677].

## 5 - The forge
_~13:10?, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. ***[EPIC]*** They slip into the forge chamber. Three lizardmen are hammering indifferent work at the anvils; the beat Nyx and Eguzkilore came for is in the middle of the room, where Dralmorrer stands watching cultists finish beating Kaelthar bloody [Td:686-694]. Kaelthar's right arm is bent wrong at the elbow, his body stripped to clothes and welted all over, the Tiamat five-head seared raw into his forehead; his left arm is gone, cut off at the shoulder — and lying on the floor near his feet [Td:688-698].
2. Kaelthar slumps unconscious under the last blow [Td:699-700]. The cultist in finer robes picks up the severed arm, carries it over to the forge-fire, and throws it in [Td:713]. Dralmorrer orders Kaelthar taken to Azbara [Td:708-712]. Two others heft him between them [Td:713-717].
3. Nyx steps up to help carry — the cultist assigned alongside hangs back a step, more interested in counting coins than keeping pace [Td:716-718]. On the walk Nyx bends close, slips one of Nyx's berries into Kaelthar's mouth as unobtrusively as she can [Td:722]. He breathes but does not come around [Td:729].

## 6 - The handover
_~13:20?, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The leader turns on both of them before they reach the room — _"¿qué haces?"_ at Nyx [Td:751-753], then _"deja de ser tan puto flojo y haz tu trabajo"_ at the counting cultist [Td:754] — and the two of them haul Kaelthar onward without her [Td:755-756]. Nyx eases out of the carry and messages Mialee — _estamos aquí al lado_ [Td:757] — then falls back to join Eguzkilore [Td:773-776].
2. Mialee pulls up to the gate with Eguzkilore and cracks the door open to peek: Kaelthar laid out on a table inside, a single guard standing watch — bald, every inch of the pate tattooed, red robes under his armour, armed [Td:912-921]. A Red Wizard of Thay [Td:1225].

## 7 - The guard on the table
_~13:30?, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nuberroca has only just arrived back from the forest — half an hour late, no help in getting to the gate; he will fight from the cabin if he can and no further [Td:815, Td:875-877, Td:884-889].
2. Nyx recaps the state for the ones watching — Kaelthar one-armed and dying, Eguzkilore in cultist robes, Mialee hidden at the breach, only one guard in the room [Td:865-871]. Mialee carries a hold-charm she can lob through her invisible mage hand — the same bind the cult has thrown at Nuberroca before [Td:979-984, Td:1003-1004]. The plan: open the door a crack, drift the charm in from above the guard, close [Td:1011, Td:1037-1046].
3. The charm lands and the guard freezes mid-breath [Td:1044-1046]. Mialee's first swing glances off the armour [Td:1065-1073]; her off-hand follow-up slips a sneak-cut home [Td:1077-1083]. Eguzkilore closes behind him with the dagger and the throat opens on the first stroke [Td:1088-1102], then on the second [Td:1105-1109]. Nyx aims for the knockout and glances off [Td:1110-1115]. One more exchange — Mialee drives the blade home on the held body [Td:1130-1143] and Eguzkilore finishes him [Td:1144-1150].

## 8 - The second wizard
_~13:40?, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nyx checks Kaelthar on the table — he reads of blood loss and nothing magical she can see [Td:1162-1176]. She tries a healing potion down him and it washes in and does nothing [Td:1227-1233]. _"Le acabo de echar como 500 de oro en la boca"_ [Td:1233]. He breathes [Td:1236].
2. Mialee closes and padlocks the door — a move that will prove regrettable in a few beats [Td:1189-1207]. From above, something takes the stairs down fast; a second Red Wizard sweeps into the room, picks up a sword, barks a single word that sounds very much like _alarm_, and charges Eguzkilore [Td:1242-1247].
3. ***[EPIC]*** Mialee drives a poisoned stroke into the second wizard and the cut takes — he staggers under the venom [Td:1381-1395]. The downed first wizard begins to breathe again at Mialee's feet — the Red Wizards do not stay down [Td:1527-1533]. Nyx catches the second wizard on the way past with a lucky cut from behind [Td:1553-1561]. Mialee pins him with another hold-charm [Td:1589-1599] and finishes him on the scimitar follow-up [Td:1600-1611]. Then they take time with the bodies to stop another revival — a head comes off, arms come off, and Eguzkilore walks away with a six-pound greatsword [Td:1627-1641, Td:1655-1670].

## 9 - Dragging Kaelthar out
_~14:10?, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Eguzkilore can only shuffle a step at a time with Kaelthar in his arms; the body is close to two hundred pounds even without armour and now minus one arm [Td:1442-1456]. Mialee and Nyx take turns under the weight, and Eguzkilore digs deeper to carry him further than he had in him [Td:1459-1465, Td:1682-1684]. The trained mounts in the yard do not raise a bark; nobody looks their way [Td:1691-1693].
2. Eguzkilore's quiet-weave still rides them on the way out [Td:1700]. At the breach they repeat the ration-and-crocodile trick and cross back where the water narrows [Td:1718-1739]. Nyx tries to message Nuberroca at the cabin — too far by half, with a castle between — and gives up [Td:1723-1731].
3. Kaelthar comes around on the walk back, broken ribs grinding at every breath, hand smashed, elbow dislocated, left arm missing [Td:1753-1762]. _"¿Puedes andar? Me puedo arrastrar"_ [Td:1763-1766].

## 10 - Back in the cabin
_~15:00?, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nuberroca presses Sinsong's necklace — the one Sinsong gave him long ago — against Kaelthar's face and the stump at the shoulder, hoping something takes. Nothing visibly moves [Td:1800-1805].
2. Mialee walks the castle wall for an hour trying every window with _message_ for Sinsong, and gets no answer anywhere she tries [Td:1869-1876, Td:1920-1941]. She burns the one-a-day sending-stone call Sinsong gave her — _¿Estás bien?_ — and that is silent too [Td:1959-1978]. They settle for returning to the cabin [Td:1987-1988].
3. Someone finally registers Sinsong — he has been standing in the cabin for half an hour, unnoticed [Td:2009-2017].

## 11 - Prove it
_~16:00?, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nuberroca lifts Sinsong clean off the ground by the neck [Td:2028, Td:2040-2041]. _"Demuéstrame que eres Sinsong"_ [Td:2031]. Sinsong has wounds, a dislocated arm, and the Tiamat five-head seared into his forehead the same as Kaelthar's [Td:2027, Td:2047-2048]. He lights the bald-head glow; Nuberroca puts him down and hugs him: _"es Sinsong"_ [Td:2064-2067].
2. Sinsong — _"he notado cómo ha intentado manipular mis pensamientos. De la única forma que él sabe"_ — accepts the test as being recognised [Td:2068-2070]. He wants his gear back. Nuberroca counters: _"un dios no puede ser materialista"_ [Td:2072-2077].

## 12 - The curse
_~16:10?, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Sinsong puts the fact plain — both of them are cursed, no healing takes on either, and he needs sleep before he can draw _Remove Curse_ into his prayers [Td:2212-2215]. Nyx: _"te he tirado una poción carísima a la basura entonces"_ [Td:2216]. Sinsong lays hands and prayer on Nuberroca and this one lands clean [Td:2192-2199]. Kaelthar tries a blessing on himself, Sinsong and Mialee — it takes on Mialee alone and glances off both of the branded, confirming the pattern [Td:2211-2213, Td:2383].
2. On how he escaped at all, Sinsong serves only a shrug — he let himself be captured for the information, got little of use, _"son más inteligentes de lo que esperaba"_ [Td:2224-2227]. The transit from the far tower to the cabin he does not account for.
3. Nyx shows round the papers she picked up at the castle [Td:1239-1240 ?ord, Td:2152]. The cult has been running carnomorph experiments — mostly on a rat and on some sort of quadruped, likely the one the lizardfolk fought the party over earlier [Td:2157-2160]. The margin note: _burn the corpses, then watch the ashes_ [Td:2160-2164].

## 13 - Waiting out the hours
_~16:30?, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nuberroca forages the woodline for the second time, pulls enough herbs to recharge the healer's kit — he and Sinsong tend wounds with it between them, Sinsong lending advantage on the medicine rolls [Td:1779-1792, Td:2231-2256, Td:2288-2298]. Mialee strips branches and crafts eight rough arrows for Eguzkilore [Td:2264-2306].
2. The curse keeps them awake even as the exhaustion stacks — tired without being tired enough to sleep, like coming off a long training run dead on your feet but wide-eyed [Td:2127-2133]. Sinsong tells Nuberroca straight — _"tenemos que esperar a las doce para podernos ir a dormir"_ [Td:2122-2124].
3. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca sets out a plan for the night watch: _"¿y si durante ocho horas matamos a todas las criaturas que hay en este bosque y luego nos vamos a dormir mientras se transforman en carnomorfos?"_ [Td:2166-2168]. Nobody takes him up on it. They move south under the cabin's cover [Td:2173-2178] and spend the hours instead on herbs, arrows, and the identification of a pendant Eguzkilore has been carrying — it reads as a protection charm, _"como si tuvieses el navegador en modo incógnito"_ [Td:2322-2325], needing attunement, which he does not take [Td:2328-2329].

## 14 - Second cabin and the long rest
_~00:00?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. At midnight Harley calls the cabin out of the air for a second time; this one costs her nothing beyond the time to chant it [Td:2420-2423]. They settle in for a long and uninterrupted ten-hour sleep [Td:2454-2463].
2. Sinsong wakes with the right prayers prepared and lifts the curse off both of them — _"todas las maldiciones se quitan"_ — at the cost of several of his strongest slots [Td:2537-2543]. The Tiamat brands stay seared into their foreheads; the healing block is gone.
3. Kaelthar wakes to find his left arm has not come back — the rest heals wounds, not limbs [Td:2493-2497 ?ord]. He has nothing to fight with — _"tienes una zanahoria, literalmente una zanahoria, con eso puedes pegar"_ [Td:2548]. Kaelthar still has the Sinsong stone equipped as his magic focus [Td:2581-2588]. Sinsong lays out that the staff was where his healing power lived — the whistle alone was decoration — and the cult still has it [Td:2626-2634].

## 15 - Snapjaw returns alone
_~10:30?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. From the treeline they see **Snapjaw** come up the castle road with only three others left of the twenty lizardfolk he set out with — he is walking, but wounded [Td:2740-2745]. The lizardfolk camp does not come out to meet him; some watch him pass and spit at the ground [Td:2747-2750, Td:2812]. The castle takes him in [Td:2751].
2. Every patrol they see since the tower fight now runs mixed — cultists, bullywugs and lizardfolk together, never a single one of the three alone [Td:2748-2750]. In the whole night nobody on watch sighted a fire or torch anywhere in the castle [Td:2778-2789].
3. Kaelthar tells the party the inside of his captivity for the first time [Td:2679-2710 ?ord]. He woke bound with Sinsong beside him, the red mage over them with two warriors in matching robes [Td:2681]. Then a bullywug larger than any they had met — a mage with a papal-cut hat and plain fury in the eyes — came in and argued with the red mage: the mage wanted them alive, _"quiere forjar un arma y quiere sacar información,"_ the bullywug wanted them dead [Td:2682-2688]. The mage won on the strength of the brand, _"ya estaba marcado y no deberíamos de ser ningún tipo de problema"_ [Td:2689-2690].
4. Then two cultists dragged **Tompu** into the forge, and the red warriors drew a strangely-worked sword from the fire and ran it through his chest; the blood on the blade did not run off, the warrior smiled, and they took the body away [Td:2691-2695]. Kaelthar's own interrogation was for the names and abilities of all ten, how they found the castle, what they knew of the cult, who they had told, every magic item they carried and where they got it, the undead and who sent them [Td:2698-2699]. He stirred the answers to pass as confused rather than lie outright; Dralmorrer kept twisting at his arm and fingers through it [Td:2700-2706]. _"Me costó el brazo y las costillas y prácticamente la vida"_ [Td:2705]. _"Te lo han quemado y lo tiraron a la chimenea,"_ Nyx tells him about the arm; he takes it [Td:2724-2729].

## 16 - Ketchup
_~11:00?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The next lizardman out of camp walks within hearing. Sinsong puts an order on him in that voice that cannot be refused — _"yo te protegeré si salgo"_ — and the lizardman freezes mid-stride [Td:2860-2870]. Nuberroca steps up, catches him by the throat and tries to drag him into the cabin; the cabin's unseen wall refuses him and the lizardman cannot pass through [Td:2872-2884]. Nuberroca settles for pinning him against that wall until he stops kicking [Td:2881].
2. Kaelthar translates. The lizardman gives his name: **Ketchup** [Td:2897-2902]. Kaelthar asks why the lizardfolk never came up to the second floor in the tower fight [Td:2904, Td:2927]. Ketchup's answer: the party were the liars — the lizardfolk watched from below and saw cultists fighting the party and their own dead burned beside the enemy [Td:2917-2920, Td:2928-2932]. He is _"leal a Tiamat,"_ as the cult is, and the two are on the same team [Td:2937-2939]. **Snapjaw** is a traitor [Td:2948]; Tompu was a fool who followed Snapjaw and now has his head on a pike at the castle entrance [Td:2949-2950].
3. Who runs the camp: Ketchup serves the cult, the cult hands the orders through Dralmorrer, and when Dralmorrer says to work with the bullywugs they work with the bullywugs [Td:3050-3053]. Three names threaten the party in order of ascending power — _"la señora Aragona,"_ Dralmorrer, and then a lizardfolk great-master expected but not yet arrived, **Boragandar** (also rendered _Waragandar_), who _"os matará a todos y os devorará"_ [Td:3041-3043, Td:2958-2960].

## 17 - The devourers
_~11:10?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nuberroca asks about the carnomorphs. Ketchup names them _"los devoradores"_ — kill them on sight, and if they bite you _"es mejor morir que ver cómo progresa"_ [Td:3087-3094]. Cultist standing order for the bitten: deliver them to the red mage for study [Td:3115].
2. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca lands the harder claim — _"llevamos varias jornadas matando carnomorfos por doquier e incluso a Coatl"_ [Td:3100]. Ketchup flinches on the word Coatl, calls it _imposible_, then recovers: _"no es el ser más poderoso del pantano. Ni siquiera es uno único, hay varios"_ [Td:3101-3114].
3. Nuberroca asks if the lizardman will raise the alarm on being released. Ketchup swears he will not [Td:3122-3127]. The party reads him and splits; Kaelthar lands on _mintiendo_ [Td:3128-3146]. Mialee argues the non-lethal route; Nuberroca holds for the kill [Td:3151-3158]. _"Este muerto y le tiramos por aquí por la espesura para que no encuentren el cadáver fácil"_ [Td:3159].

## 18 - Cliffhanger on the treeline
_~11:20?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nuberroca calls out the next lizardman from the camp, ready to try again [Td:3168-3171]. The GM warns them that the pool of ears willing to listen gets smaller every time one does not come back [Td:3172-3175]. Nuberroca counters: _"también está el factor miedo. Si han muerto 60 de los sapos a los que les tiran acojonados, pues también..."_ [Td:3176-3178]. Session cuts with the next lizardman still inbound [Td:3184-3187].

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 42}}

\page

# Session 43 {{float:"right" 2026-03-29}}
{wide}

## 1 - The morning hut
_~08:00?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR?_

1. The party wakes inside **Harley**'s dome-shaped hut in the swamp outside **Castle Naerytar**, where they bedded down after the previous night's rescue of **Sinsong** and **Kaelthar** [Td:5-10]. **Mialee** sleeps on through the morning conversation in a corner of the hut [Td:255-258]. Harley opens the day with the cult greeting — *"Alabado sea nuestro Dios"* — and parts of the party answer in kind [Td:5-8].
2. Sinsong announces he spent the night awake while the others slept, running his identification ritual on everything the party is carrying; he reels off the hours worked and declares every item on them identified [Td:21-23], [Td:268-272]. Over the course of the morning he walks each PC's inventory in turn until the pass is clean [Td:349-352].
3. The lizardfolk prisoner **Ketsu**, taken the night before and still held by **Nuberroca**, has to be dealt with before anything else [Td:51], [Td:65]. The party pauses to read his intent — Harley and Sinsong come down firmly against killing him; Nyx and Eguzkilore think it is a bad idea but will defer to the group [Td:51-57]. The ethics volley — *"¿No somos asesinos?"* — tips the vote away from a clean execution [Td:69-70].
4. The compromise is to knock Ketsu out. Nuberroca first clamps a hand over the lizardfolk's mouth to muffle him [Td:65-67], then — on Sinsong's say-so — strikes him on the head and leaves him unconscious in the grass [Td:91-96], [Td:121-122]. Sinsong moves in close, strips the weapon and shield so Ketsu cannot ambush them when he comes to [Td:146-147], and ends up with a pair of small gems from the lizardfolk's belt in his own pocket [Td:148-151].
5. Sinsong turns to **Harley** and resumes his campaign to recruit her to his cult, pressing one of his small pendant stones into her palm and telling her that so long as she wears it she is his friend [Td:164-170], [Td:198-213]. The ritual stumbles on her literal mind: she holds the stone up and asks what faith is and what the stone is for, tries to barter (more stones means more friends), and finally reaches out to rub his bald head affectionately when he turns away in frustration [Td:217-227], [Td:229-232]. He gives up and crosses to Nyx, who confirms with a hand on her chest that she wears his stone equipped [Td:237-242].
6. Through the hut's one-way wall the party can see the swamp outside: Snapjaw's lizardfolk have already moved deeper into the castle; the figures still visible are a patrol group, separate from his [Td:263-265]. Eguzkilore and Nyx take stock of their quivers — five arrows between them [Td:312-316], [Td:672-678]. Harley re-casts her hut so they will have another eight hours of cover [Td:306-311].

## 2 - The strike from inside the hut

1. Harley gets bored and asks who has an idea [Td:362]. Before anyone answers, Sinsong turns to her and says he will attack — *"Me giro y empiezo a hacer un fireball en la mano"* — asking only where to aim [Td:364-365]. Harley waves vaguely north and tells him he is intelligent enough to find a target [Td:366-369].
2. ***[EPIC]*** Sinsong steps to the edge of the dome, points, and a sphere of green light leaves his hand and strikes a lizardfolk sentry out in the swamp [derived:Td:364+Td:374+Td:388]. The attack is a magical ability rather than a spell, so it carries straight through the hut's wall — stones ricochet back into the dome and everyone inside takes a hit of the backlash [Td:389-393], [Td:423-430], [Td:434-436].
3. The dome is now a bright green half-sphere plain against the swamp — *"gracias a esa gilipollez, ahora todo el mundo sabe dónde está vuestra cabaña"* [Td:417].
4. **Mialee** rouses to the chaos [Td:255-258 ?ord], [Td:440]. She slips to the hut's edge, lets fly on a hidden shot, poisons the lizard she catches, and ducks back into cover before anyone can spot her [Td:478-480], [Td:641-651]. Nyx shoots another of the sentries down [Td:482-493]. Harley points a finger at a third and sends out a sound like a struck bell [Td:505-511]. Eguzkilore sends an arrow at the one still standing [Td:515-516].
5. At the alarm the whole patrol surges [Td:517-519]. Within the breath that follows, the castle's **drum** begins to beat again across the water, raising the alarm [Td:731-735]. Sinsong's hope that they had burned the drum last night falls flat — *"han hecho otro"*, they built a new one [Td:736-742].
6. Harley cries out at the pattern — *"Hay que reconocer cómo en seis segundos se moviliza todo el puto castillo"* [Td:745].

## 3 - The flight

1. Sinsong calls out from inside the hut: do they stand and fight, or run? [Td:602]. Eguzkilore votes to run at once [Td:605-606]; Harley agrees that frontal fighting is suicide given the leaders they have not even seen yet [Td:296-298].
2. **Kaelthar** — missing an arm from his earlier captivity, barely able to lift a weapon — is slow to react and finds the fight already on when he turns to it [Td:544-548], [Td:759-764]. He steps outside the hut and summons his wolf steed **Fenrir** (the steed cannot enter the dome) [Td:580-595], ducks back inside and settles for a brief shimmer of protection on himself before stepping out again [Td:565-568].
3. The party scatters. Sinsong dashes south out of the hut [Td:609-614] and Nuberroca falls in behind him [Td:622-624]; Mialee breaks the other way to draw heat [Td:632-633]; Eguzkilore and Nyx push east through the undergrowth [Td:627-629].
4. The lizardfolk clear the fence in a single bound and come after them [Td:518-519 ?ord]; two of them rush the hut and discover they cannot cross its wall, but the rest flow around it [Td:712-715].
5. Realising he can do nothing on the ground, Kaelthar unfurls his draconic wings and lifts straight up into the air, leaving **Fenrir** on the ground as his decoy [Td:1143-1159]. He climbs until he is a vertical line above the swamp — ninety feet up, beyond javelin reach, nothing to swing at [Td:1444-1456].
6. **Mialee** throws down a bank of fog across the chase line and vanishes into it [Td:1181-1183]. **Nyx**, bitten by a leaping lizard [Td:1211], drops into a protective ward Sinsong casts on her at a distance [Td:1113-1127 ?ord] and keeps moving on halved speed through the swamp mud [Td:1198-1200].
7. Harley weaves her sorcery into a cover of invisibility that reaches over both herself and Eguzkilore — one point of her inner spark spent to carry the pair [Td:869-872 ?ord]. The two of them fade from view [Td:874]; the lizards' pursuit slides past them into empty swamp [Td:911-916].

## 4 - Sinsong falls, Mialee taken

1. ***[EPIC]*** A lizardfolk **geomancer** closes the distance on Sinsong, lifts both hands, and fires a bolt of green sickness at the airborne cleric; the magic rolls over Sinsong and he pitches off balance [Td:1411-1416]. He drops the thirty feet he had cleared through the air, hits the swamp ground unconscious, and the fall itself leaves him one slip closer to death [Td:1422-1430].
2. Two lizards pack around the downed cleric, grapple him, and begin to drag him back toward the castle — restrained, unconscious, taken [Td:1619-1625].
3. Mialee, from her hidden fog pocket, reaches an unseen hand into Nyx's belt pouch and fishes a berry out; she slips it between the halfling's teeth and Nyx comes back to one point of life with three berries left [Td:1860-1865].
4. Nyx uses the moment to shape herself into a small lizardfolk, walks forward out of the fog, and begins to pantomime helping the pack hunt the party down [Td:1891-1897], [Td:2001-2013].
5. The pack keeps groping blindly for Mialee without finding her, but at point-blank range even blind strikes land by sheer saturation [Td:1641-1655 ?ord]; she takes several hits and finally calls it — *"Me rindo, no hace falta que me peguéis más"* — and lays down her weapons [Td:1972-1979]. The lizards haul her up between them and carry her back toward the castle gates with the other prisoners [Td:1977-1980], [Td:2092-2094].

## 5 - Nuberroca lost, Kaelthar away

1. Nuberroca stays with the retreat only long enough to drop a leading lizard with his polearm, then another; he keeps the pursuit on himself while the others thin out across the swamp [Td:1735-1745], [Td:1808-1817].
2. ***[EPIC]*** In his last gesture visible to the party, he hurls Sinsong's small faith stone away from himself into the swamp — *"con mi último aliento de vida cojo la piedra de Sinsong y la tiro a toma por culo"* — refusing to let it be taken with him [Td:1830-1831].
3. Nuberroca falls. Four or five of the lizards drop around him before the circle closes; the geomancer-mages hang back and he never gets his axe into them [Td:2173-2181].
4. Kaelthar, still aloft, drifts west with the remaining minutes of his wings until the enemy below stops looking up and the air is empty [Td:1836-1839]. Harley teleports twice more at full invisible-dash to put the chase behind her [Td:1538-1548 ?ord], [Td:2109-2113].

## 6 - Regrouping in the swamp

1. The pursuit scatters. Eguzkilore and Kaelthar spend half an hour making sure they are no longer followed before they circle back toward where they lost each other [Td:2214-2217], [Td:2244-2246]. On their way, the ground is churned with the tracks of every creature from the fight; the only readable prints they can pick out are a lizardfolk's and a woman's, heading east [Td:2297-2300].
2. Nyx, slipping free of the enemy once the search spreads out, picks up Harley's trail and finds her first [Td:2158-2167 ?ord], [Td:2199-2201]. Harley re-raises her hut on the ground where the two women meet so they can sit and breathe and plan [Td:2251-2254].
3. Eguzkilore uses his **Sending Stone** to call Mialee's name across the swamp [Td:2218-2222]. The voice that answers is not Mialee's — it is a calm, educated voice they have never heard before: *"Mialee está en nuestro poder. Si os entregáis ahora, no morirán"* [Td:2222-2227]. He has no words left to answer; the message runs out [Td:2228].
4. The four survivors eventually find the glow of Harley's hut and reconvene — Eguzkilore and Kaelthar guide in on the half-green dome they recognise from the morning [Td:2316-2320]. Kaelthar lays hands on Nyx, and the party takes a short rest before they decide anything [Td:2343-2349], [Td:2375-2378].

## 7 - The plan, and the father

1. Harley tells the others she is going in through the front gate — alone, as a cultist, as the "lost daughter" of a Red Wizard she claims is her father; she describes him as bald, pale-skinned, with a red cloak like her own [Td:2480-2482], [Td:2557-2558]. Sinsong — speaking in Harley's ear by a thread of magic — warns her: *"Harley, yo que tú, no me haría muchas ilusiones con tu padre. Puede que no sea tan majo como esperas que sea"* [Td:2572-2573].
2. Nyx offers the alternative. If Harley hides her from sight and hands her **Mialee's Bag of Holding**, she can creep into the keep, bundle Mialee and Sinsong one at a time into the bag, and throw the bag out of a window to Eguzkilore waiting below — the bag is a pocket dimension, the people inside it should land soft [Td:2515-2528], [Td:2566-2571]. The plan carries two risks only: that she will give herself away by sound, and that the captives will be chained to a wall [Td:2535-2538].
3. The plan is set, and they add Harley's gambit as a decoy. Harley fades out of sight, re-shapes her cloak to match the cultists' robes, and walks for the front gate [Td:2190-2192 ?ord]; Nyx enters through the back, hidden and leaving no trail [Td:2614-2619], [Td:2647-2650].
4. ***[EPIC]*** Harley reaches the gate and begins shouting at the top of her lungs — *"¡Papá! ¡Papá! ¡He venido a casa!"* [Td:2683-2686]. Cultists spill from the keep, surround her at swordpoint, and demand her weapons; she insists she is unarmed and submits [Td:2700-2708], [Td:2772-2782].
5. She picks a human in a red cloak out of the receiving party — a Thayan — and runs at him screaming *"¡Papá!"*, trying to throw her arms around his neck [Td:2765-2771]. He pivots away: *"Yo no tengo hijos"* [Td:2784-2786]. The guards gag her, bind her arms, and set her against the wall with the other captives [Td:2787], [Td:2818-2822].
6. Thrown with the others, Harley sees the state of the room — Mialee and Nuberroca are already here, with dried blood on their hands, and the cultists are already working on Mialee; Sinsong is not in sight [Td:2721-2724 ?ord], [Td:2762-2764].

## 8 - The elf in the kitchen

1. **Nyx**, hidden and leaving no trail, drifts through the keep's back halls silent and unseen [Td:2647-2658]. She finds the kitchen one floor down from the dining room and watches a tiefling and an elf working at a table while a dwarf oversees them [Td:2862-2874].
2. She writes a note in charcoal on the back of a surveillance report she had picked up on an earlier run — *soy una arpista que está intentando rescatar a sus amigos; si me puedes ayudar, nos vemos* — and drops it crumpled beside the elf's chopping board, where it blinks visible as it leaves her unseen hand [Td:2908-2917], [Td:2928-2938]. The elf twitches, feigns a near-miss with his knife — *"casi me corto"* — pockets the note under his apron, and tells the dwarf he needs to fetch flour upstairs [Td:2941-2946].
3. ***[EPIC]*** On the top floor the elf drops to a whisper by the stair rail. Nyx pulls off her Harper amulet and sets it on the table so he can see what she is [Td:2960-2965]. He gives his name as **Haronir**, confirms he is the Harpers' inside man, and tells her the cultists had already started suspecting him before her note arrived — the vigilance report she had thrown at him was about him [Td:2971-2978].
4. Haronir sketches what little he knows for her: no secret passages he has ever found, but there is a sealed cellar beneath the keep that even cultists cannot enter; access is said to be through the dining room in the **northeast tower**, through a well-watched door, and the castle's loot runs down that way [Td:3003-3011]. Beyond that, he is blind — some wings of the castle are reserved for the bullywugs and the lizardfolk alone [Td:3004-3005].
5. By this point four of the party are in cultist hands inside the keep — Mialee, Nuberroca, Sinsong and now Harley — while Nyx walks the castle unseen with a Harper ally, and Eguzkilore and Kaelthar wait out of sight of the walls for a bag that has not come [Td:2879-2881 ?ord], [Td:3011-3017].

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 43}}

\page

# Session 44 {{float:"right" 2026-04-19}}
{wide}

## 1 - Dead island
_~08:00, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The group has slept on a small half-dry island in the swamp beside the mound of carnomorph corpses they killed overnight. **Chikonaui** lies dead on the ground beside them, one hand loose, throat cut with his own blade; a half-formed wing sprouting from his back.
2. From the south, **several Metagorgers** push across the water toward the island. Behind them walks a figure bigger than Nuberroca — arms set the wrong way, ribs opened backwards, a tangle of bone and muscle with huge claws — a **Shambler**. The water around them seethes with smaller bulges — **Boneleechers** surfacing.
3. Nuberroca walks back to **Juancho**, his lizard mount killed the night before, and gathers a ragged bouquet from the swamp weeds. He lays the flowers across the lizard's head and tells him he was a brave mount who could carry his weight. Noticing a twitch, he presses one of Nyx's berries into Juancho's mouth; Sinsong lays both hands on the lizard and Juancho half-rises, slow but alive.
4. The party loads into the lizardfolk boats, leaving the corpses of **Nenetl** and Chikonaui on the island. Juancho is lifted onto one boat, **Atascaburras** swims alongside.
5. Sinsong moves between the boats laying a hand on each of them in turn — party and lizardfolk alike — his bald head shining bright; he tells them they can now walk over the water. The spell lasts for an hour but Sinsong keeps renewing it as they travel.
6. Half an hour out the carnomorphs reach the island but do not pursue — they stop over the corpses and start to feed. From a distance the party watches the creatures eat, grow and evolve into new shapes.
7. During the two hours' travel Nuberroca works on reducing the pain of his grave wounds, with the help of Nyx's berries he manages to dull the worst of them.

\column

## 2 - The castle in view
_~10:00, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The castle rises from the swamp as they close — stone, large, old, its wooden roofs mostly staved in and most of the windows either broken or boarded over. A moat rings the whole site. From the west they can distinguish three groups of buildings around it: a stockaded enclosure to the south, where giant lizards are kept, two large cane pavilions belonging to the lizardfolk in the centre outside the walls, and a cluster of bullywug-infested mud-and-filth hovels to the left.
2. Closing on the landing they realise what the patrols mean: **bullywugs and lizardfolk are both working here together**. Nobody raises an alarm as the boats pull in — nobody seems surprised at them arriving at all.
3. Sinsong and Eguzkilore pull cultist robes from the pack and shrug them on.

## 3 - At the shore

1. Sinsong steps off the boat and walks north across the open water to explore. The bullywugs and lizardfolk in sight pay the party little mind — but the dwarf walking on water turns every head on the quay.
2. Nuberroca asks **Atzi** and **Tompu** what the mixed camp means — _"how is it that there are lizardfolk here working with the frogs?"_ The two of them answer that it is just as they had already warned him: in Naerytar, lizardfolk and bullywugs coexist, and that is exactly why they wanted the bullywugs gone. Atzi and Tompu themselves will fight; the rest of the clan might not — they obey **Dralmorrer**, and above him **Rezmir**, _the dragon lady_.
3. Nuberroca presses: if the party kills the bullywugs, will Rezmir not come down on the clan? Yes — _"so we have to finish quickly and restore order before she finds out and gets angry"_. And if the party kills Rezmir instead? _"Rezmir es buena"_ — the lizardfolk will not help against her. Nuberroca argues it anyway: if she is strong and the party kills her, then the party is stronger still. The lizardfolk reply that it would make them evil.
4. Nuberroca asks for three plans with a requirement: _"uno de los planes tiene que ser violento"_. Sinsong will slip in alone, Atzi and Tompu will work the lizardfolk for sympathisers. The last plan — violence — is held in reserve.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 44}}
\page

## 4 - Failed exploration

1. Meanwhile Sinsong, briefly stopped by the lizardfolk guards after his water-walk and watched by curious bullywugs, is let go — unable to understand him, the guards move away and go back to their huts. Then the dwarf wanders alone to the south of the castle, out of sight of the rest of the party.
2. A moment later the dwarf is spotted flying above the walls and crash landing in a patch of vegetation at the gate; two bullywugs lean out between the crenellations above and crane down to peer further out.
3. From the gatehouse a bullywug sage with a two-handed staff steps out, lays a hand on the thickest bush, and starts to chant — seconds later the branches peel open and the cleric stands suddenly in daylight with no cover left.
4. The sage barks an order and bullywug guards sweep out of the gate to form up around him. Before the rest of the party can intervene, Sinsong is taken into the castle.

## 5 - Updating the plan
_~11:00, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. With Sinsong captured, the five talk through their options in the trees ringing **Castle Naerytar**. Nuberroca suggests forcing the lizardfolk to join their cause, Mialee floats trying the bullywugs instead — their chief is the one the lizardfolk want killed, so maybe the frogs are more receptive. They decide to approach the bullywugs and look for ways into the castle while Tompu and Atzi attempt to gather sympathisers.
2. Behind the plan sits the pressure: the carnomorphs they fled by boat may be pursuing them, and the next day **Snapjaw** is expected to return with reinforcements.
3. A scream cuts out of the castle — a death-shriek in a human voice.
4. Nuberroca rides Juancho up to the lizardfolk corral and dismounts to greet the keeper. A lizardfolk reaches for Juancho's reins, admiring the fine lizard, and tries to lead him off with the others.
5. Nuberroca holds the reins, pulls a cut of crocodile meat from his pack, and splits it between Juancho and the lizardfolk — "my lizard... my horse". The keeper accepts the gift and lets the lizard be. On the way back Nuberroca ties a scrap of green cloth around Juancho's neck so he can be told apart from the other mounts if the lizardfolk herd them all together.
6. Returning to the group, Nuberroca grins: *"creo que he hecho un amigo"*.

\column

## 6 - The dead bullywug

1. While Nuberroca is at the corral, Nyx wades across the river to a bullywug corpse half-sunk in the shallows and crouches to read it without touching.
2. The cut is behind the neck; the body is drained but the ground around it holds no blood — the kill happened elsewhere, and the body was dragged here from the bullywug village.
3. There is a note on the chest, in bad, irregular handwriting — "don't bury me". There are smears of blood on the paper but none on the ground where the body lies.
4. Nyx reads it as a staged suicide: the cut from behind doesn't match, and someone bothered to write a note. Factional friction inside the bullywug village — perhaps even two groups turning on each other.
5. To test the read, the heroes walk across to the bullywug compound itself — but sentries at the edge cut them off: cultists aren't allowed into the village. Mialee asks about their chief; the sentry answers that **Pharblex** isn't available and that any audience with him would be held in the breeding chambers under the castle, not here.
6. Turned back, the party loops the full perimeter of the castle. The curtain wall is complete; the moat rings the whole site; the only accessible break they find is a ragged gap on the northeast section.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 44}}
\page

## 7 - Inside the walls
_~12:00, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. ***[EPIC]*** Eguzkilore walks in wearing cultist robes, Mialee behind him with a cultist emblem over plain clothes, Nyx trailing with her Harpers pendant tucked out of sight. Nuberroca stays outside the walls — too big to disguise, Torm's fist still shining on the front of his breastplate. Inside, the stone floor of the hall is a half-and-half mire of mud and bullywug filth; a narrow trodden path kept clear runs from the gate to the inner passage, and the place reeks.
2. From a forge somewhere they can hear hammers. The hammers stop, and ten or fifteen seconds later a single short, agonised scream from a woman reaches them.
3. A pale bald-headed warrior with tattoos tracing his scalp comes out of the forge dragging the drained body of a woman with one hand, and a greatsword with the other, his own similar blade still at his hip. When he spots Eguzkilore in cultist colours, he throws the corpse at him.
4. Eguzkilore catches it and tries to simply move on, but a guard grabs him from behind: "haven't you been given an order? What are you doing?". Nyx steps in with hood half-up: she is a new recruit, no uniform yet, and Eguzkilore is showing her the castle. The lie holds.
5. The guard tells Eguzkilore "you know what to do with the body" — and sends them on. Eguzkilore shoulders it and walks out with the others close behind.

## 8 - The drained woman

1. Outside the walls they gather around the body by the river's edge. The woman's clothes are a labourer's, and her face is so dried that her age is unreadable.
2. There is a single clean puncture at the heart; the stain on her shirt around the cut is no wider than a fingertip, where it should have soaked half the fabric. Mialee reads it as the smell of dead magic — something of a necromancer. The blood went somewhere — into a vessel, into a ritual — not into the clothes.
3. Eguzkilore lights a fire with a small druidic trick and they burn the body on the river bank; Nyx says a few words over her to Yondalla. They tell any lizardfolk who ask that they are clearing carrion before the carnomorphs come for it.

\column

## 9 - Lizardfolk report
_~14:00, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Tompu and Atzi come back around two in the afternoon. One lizardfolk in three is willing to take up arms against the bullywugs — more than they had hoped — though none of them will take up arms against the cult itself.
2. **Dralmorrer** is well-liked among the lizardfolk — he teaches them the hammer — and the *lady dragon's* authority binds the clan from above. Snapjaw's name still carries weight and will help the rally.
3. Pharblex killed **Suncaller**, the lizardfolks' strongest shaman, with his own hand. He is always flanked by an escort, and he lives in the subterranean chambers beneath the castle. The castle doors are the only way the lizardfolk know of, but bullywugs disappear into the moat and never surface, so there must be another way down through the water.

## 10 - Carnomorph bait

1. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca lays out a different plan: lure the carnomorphs to the castle and let them tear through the bullywug village while the party moves on Pharblex. He meets Tompu and Atzi's protests head-on — the pavilions can be rebuilt ten kilometres further north, and the day they die in their beds they will wish they had taken this chance to rise from their ashes. The rhetoric lands: both lizardfolk agree.
2. Mialee and Nyx talk him down from the plan. Before anyone bets on carnomorphs they want to see if the water route is passable at all — if Pharblex can be reached quietly, there is no need to invite devourers in. Nuberroca floats scouting for carnomorph tracks nearby, then changes his mind and comes with them to the moat in case the crocodiles need pushing back.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 44}}
\page

## 11 - The moat crocodiles

1. After retrieving Juancho, the group moves around to the back of the castle, on the east, where Tompu says the bullywugs enter the water. The moat here is dark with castle run-off and at least five metres deep.
2. Nyx steps in to probe for the opening. A crocodile breaks surface, locks onto her leg, and drags her under — Nuberroca charges in and hauls her out, splitting the thing with his polearm as it hits land.
3. Mialee drops a scrap of ration into the water with a conjured hand and a second, larger crocodile breaches, lunges, and knocks Nuberroca flat; its jaws close on him twice in a row, and its tail lashes, leaving him bloodied in the mud. Juancho and Atascaburras shy away from the water.
4. Eguzkilore and Nyx put arrows into it while Nuberroca gets back on his feet. Atzi steps up to defend Eguzkilore and finishes a smaller crocodile with a mace, and Nuberroca drops the big one on the next swing.
5. Nuberroca drags the carcasses onto the bank for Juancho and Atascaburras to feed on and settles in to butcher the rest for meat; Mialee's conjured-hand sweep of the water shows no further movement.

## 12 - A winding tunnel
_~14:30, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Mialee drops in and feels her way along the stone — her darkvision is good enough in the pitch-black water for this — and finds the opening: a slit narrow enough that Nuberroca would have to force it, with a thin current drawing water inward. She has a conjured hand carry one end of a fifty-foot rope across to Eguzkilore on the bank, then goes back in with Nyx behind her, both hands on the line.
2. Mialee and Nyx fill their lungs with air and dive through the opening. Eguzkilore will haul on the rope if either tugs; Nuberroca stands ready to help pull.
3. First dive, fork right then left, forty feet down the passage dead-ends in slits too narrow to squeeze through — water passes, they do not. They retrieve and surface.
4. Nuberroca lashes three ropes together — a hundred and fifty feet total. Second dive, right-right-right at the next fork, another dead end. Third dive, right-right-left, the rope runs out before the tunnel does. They turn back; they have half their breath left.
5. Mialee is at her limit. Nyx goes on alone, pulling the rope with her.

\column

## 13 - Breeding chamber

1. ***[EPIC]*** Nyx takes the last of the branches — right, right, left — and keeps swimming when the rope runs out, feeling her way through a narrow squeeze that loosens as she passes it. She surfaces into a dim-lit underground lake and sees large tadpoles cutting past her in the water.
2. Only her head above the surface, she holds still — this is the breeding chamber below the castle, exactly where the lizardfolk told them Pharblex sits. She takes a breath, drops back under, and feels her way out along the rope.
3. Back at the shore she reports: the final branch opens into a lake under the castle, and the squeeze will take even Nuberroca with rope help from the others on the far side.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 44}}

\page

# Session 45 {{float:"right" 2026-05-01}}
{wide}

## 1 - At the moat
_~15:00, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The party is regrouped outside the moat around the crocodile carcasses from the earlier fight — Nuberroca still bloody from working one open with his hands [T:39, 82]. Mialee has found the tunnel-mouth that connects the moat to the castle interior; after several wrong turns she has the correct path, and the party has held short of committing to go through [T:35-36 ?ord].
2. They settle on the plan: slip through the tunnel, find and kill the bullywug leader, and leave lizardfolk sign on the body to spark a revolt between frogs and lizardfolk [T:177-178]. Nuberroca offers to fashion a necklace of crocodile teeth and leave it as a lure; freshly-pulled teeth would obviously look freshly pulled, so he rubs them dirty against his own arse to age them [T:120-128].
3. Eguzkilore has grown a step surer on his feet — ground that would mire the others passes under him clean, useful in a marsh where every step is a slog [T:147-153].

## 2 - Amidamaru returns

1. As Mialee turns to scout the tunnel, Amidamaru appears behind Nuberroca, warning the party not to do anything stupid [T:311-314].
2. He explains he was contacted a week ago by a woman he could not identify, who told him the party was in danger; he came as fast as he could, but strange creatures on the road forced him to circle wide before he could find them [T:334].
3. The scouting question is settled: Mialee and Amidamaru will slip through the tunnel first while the rest wait; the sending stone will signal the others when the way is clear [T:367-368].
4. Nyx is still visibly torn from the crocodile attacks — the same wounds Nuberroca carries [T:412-415] — and Amidamaru casts a small healing on both of them before they regroup [T:428].
5. They set a weighted guide-rope tied to a shore-stake, to steer the others through the flooded tunnel once Mialee's signal comes [T:457-462].

## 3 - Through the flooded tunnel

1. Mialee and Amidamaru submerge and swim the flooded tunnel — twenty feet across the moat pool, then ten, thirty, forty, and eighty more feet of near-straight passage, turning right at the first fork, right at the second, left at the third [T:479, 482-483].
2. They surface into near-total darkness. Amidamaru pulls out a magic-lit pebble to see, but Mialee stops him — the party has to pass unseen, there may be enemies — and he pockets it, setting Munin the owl on his shoulder to borrow its night-sight instead [T:500-503, 511, 571].
3. What little light there is comes from luminescent green fungus clinging to the rocks [T:539-540]. The air is heavy, wet, and full of the sound of running water, dripping, and constant frog-croaking on every side [T:572, 733-736].
4. Mialee scouts a chamber to their right, hugging the shadows. A tiny lagoon inside is packed with tadpoles — smaller than the giant ones the party has been fighting outside — and two bullywugs stand over them, tending the brood [T:628-634]. She calls them *"las mamás bullywugs"* [T:634].
5. She signals through the sending stone: the entrance chamber is clear, two bullywugs to the right, come through [T:675-678].

## 4 - The rest of the party comes through

1. Nyx slots into the crossing: the party goes in order — Nyx leading, Eguzkilore second, Nuberroca and Kaelthar behind — using the guide-rope through the last stretch where their breath runs short [T:700-704, 709-715].
2. Kaelthar has left his heavy armour on the outer shore — the mission is stealth and the plate would splash and clang [T:222-231 ?ord]. The rest are already soaked to the bone.
3. Nyx hands Amidamaru back one of his quivers — empty now; she says she ran through his arrows quickly in the last fight [T:753-761].
4. Amidamaru's return prompts an arrow count: Nyx has burned through her stock in the crocodile fight and only has one left [T:761-764]. Once they share out what everyone carries, each archer ends up with eighteen apiece [T:775-779]. Kaelthar remembers that the lizardfolk stripped his javelins from him before the castle, so he has nothing to throw [T:784].
5. Eguzkilore weaves a soft field over the group as they move, the water sound around Nuberroca fading unnaturally as he steps clear of the pool [T:866-868]. Anyone within his aura moves silently, though Nuberroca and Kaelthar need to stay close to keep the effect [T:838-846].

## 5 - The nursery guards

1. The party creeps toward the two bullywugs. Kaelthar takes the front without armour [T:970-971], Nyx behind him, Nuberroca groping through the near-blackness by touch, tapping Eguzkilore's shoulder to stay oriented [T:1023-1032].
2. The bullywugs turn towards them — they've heard something. They mutter to each other in low voices, weapons raised, still not quite locating the intruders [T:1039-1044].
3. Kaelthar swings first — the first cut lands hard, flame searing from the blade, and the second drives in with divine fire [T:1292-1340]; the bullywug he engaged answers with a pair of hard strikes [T:1064-1066]. Nuberroca reaches through the dark, swinging his halberd where the sound comes from, and connects for two heavy blows [T:1066-1156].
4. Mialee slides into a flanking position and looses her first shot from the shadows; it finds the first bullywug and drops it on the spot [T:1219-1239].
5. Seeing its partner dead, the surviving bullywug shrieks a sharp warning that carries into the caverns — danger, they are being attacked — and swings back at Kaelthar, its blade catching his unprotected ribs twice for a heavy wound, the edge slick with a stinging venom [T:1285, 1340-1380].
6. Nuberroca circles into its blind side and cleaves through with a savage halberd stroke, dropping the second one [T:1367-1370]; the room falls back into wet silence except for the tadpoles thrashing.
7. Kaelthar is bleeding badly, his skin already welting from the poison, and drops to one knee. He closes the worst of the wounds with a laying-on of hands, though the venom-burn stays [T:1413-1437].

## 6 - Lights in the dark

1. Mialee sees small round lights approaching from the far end of the water — motes of pale magical light drifting in slow arcs [T:1496-1500, 1569].
2. The party listens. Nuberroca picks up more splashing from deeper in the caves; the frog-croaking that has been constant since they arrived starts to thin, then stops in patches — *"este silencio me inquieta"* [T:1508-1526].
3. Amidamaru names it at once: it is a wizard's scouting light, and whoever cast it is coming to see what tripped the alarm [T:1634].
4. The party debates: retreat down the tunnel, hide in the tadpole chamber, or push forward. Amidamaru argues that a party of six can't slip past unseen — some should feign a retreat while one or two try to pass hidden [T:1586-1589 ?ord].
5. They fold back into the nursery cave. Amidamaru wedges himself into the water and pulls into his shell like a stone on the bottom [T:1667-1671]. Nuberroca stays in the light, sword and shield in hand [T:1908, 1918-1926]. Mialee presses to the wall in what little dimness she can find [T:2179-2185]. Everyone tries to go unseen — for once, hoping to blend into the shadows rather than stand out [T:1641-1644].
6. Nuberroca hears a rhythmic chant from the water past a small island [T:1685-1688, 1992], then a rush of croaking as one of the lights fixes on a frog that has spotted him and starts calling — the frog knows exactly where he is even without light [T:1726-1735].

## 7 - Acid from the mist

1. From the small island in the water a green mote of light sputters, then swells to the size of a head as it flies at the party — the wizard's throw. It bursts among them and covers the chamber in acid [T:1992-2005]. The walls give no cover; everyone but Amidamaru turns their body against it in time [T:2035, 2047]. Amidamaru, caught in his shell, takes the full lingering burn [T:2050-2054, 2343-2344].
2. The fight erupts. Waves of giant frogs start hurling themselves out of the water at Nuberroca — first one, then more, leaping impossible distances to land on him and clamp jaws [T:1842-1845 ?ord, 2528, 2622].
3. Eguzkilore and Nyx loose arrows into whatever the passing motes of light briefly reveal [T:1797-1830 ?ord, 1843-1858].
4. Nuberroca digs in and calls on the fury of his gods — the older wounds on him close visibly as he fights [T:1929-1937].
5. Kaelthar advances into the mess without armour. A wave of giant-frog bites and a bullywug champion's follow-up strike drop him in a single exchange [T:2617-2641]; Kaelthar falls to the flooded stones, unconscious.
6. Nuberroca reaches Kaelthar and tips one of Nyx's berries into his mouth [T:2754]; Kaelthar comes back to the edge of consciousness and shuffles aside on foot — a walking step is all that grace affords him in that moment [T:2799-2817].
7. A moment later Nuberroca himself vanishes in a step of grey vapour and reappears five feet under the water, holding his breath in the dark [T:2841-2853].
8. The frogs stop chasing Nuberroca and turn on Nyx — five at once, tearing at her, until one clamps onto her and swallows her down [T:2660-2668 ?ord, 2740].
9. Nyx cuts her way out from inside — she drives her shortsword through the frog's belly and crawls free [GM].
10. ***[EPIC]*** From out of the mist the frog-mage closes on Amidamaru and lands a devastating strike — a venom-slick blade at close quarters that finds the joint and dislocates his elbow [T:2988-2999, 3001]. He drops — his first grievous wound [T:2993-2995].
11. Kaelthar steps across the chamber in a flash of grey vapour and reappears beside Eguzkilore, out of the frog pack's reach [T:3255-3266].
12. Eguzkilore reaches the fallen Amidamaru and slips one of Nyx's berries into his mouth [T:3556-3565]. Amidamaru rises, barely clinging on.

## 8 - Killing the mage

1. Mialee throws a fog cloud over the fight — a twenty-foot sphere that blinds everyone inside, party and frog-mage both [T:2926, 3061-3063]. Amidamaru presses close and cuts the mage with a thunder-charged rapier strike [T:3408-3425].
2. Eguzkilore holds his bow at the edge of the mist. Mialee shouts him a bearing through it — *open to the left* — and he moves those few steps sideways until he can see the mage's silhouette [T:3049-3067].
3. ***[EPIC]*** He looses. A perfect shot through the mist finds the mage; Eguzkilore passes the credit back to Mialee — *"el mérito es de Mialee, me ha avisado"* [T:3070-3081]. The mage is left on the brink.
4. A moment later Nyx puts a second arrow through the mage — the shot finds him even across the chamber — and cuts the tendon in one leg, leaving him prone and dragging himself toward the water [T:3163-3220]. Later in the fight she finishes him with a final shot to the back; the mage sinks, jerking, into the water [T:3587-3591].
5. As the mage dies the scouting motes wink out all at once. Eguzkilore takes the cue: if the lights are gone, the toad is no longer alive [T:3594-3600].
6. ***[EPIC]*** Nyx is caught a second time by another giant frog — pulled fully into the throat as it starts to leave the chamber with her [T:3951-3963, 4181-4182]. Amidamaru and Eguzkilore break off and chase; Amidamaru's rapier bites in from one side [T:4157-4165] and Eguzkilore drops the frog with a close shot [T:4197-4207] — Nyx crawls out barely alive [T:4204].
7. The remaining bullywugs, blind in the same fog Mialee raised, blunder into each other and take swings at empty air [T:3531 ?ord, 4173]. Kaelthar, with nothing to throw, breathes fire through the mist and catches one frog outright — Nuberroca, caught at the edge of the cone, has his beard singed down the front [T:3835-3873].
8. Nuberroca hits a bullywug champion with a reckless swing [T:3690-3712], though another catches him from behind moments later for a heavy blow [T:3941-3948]; Mialee cuts into another giant frog from the shadows with paired daggers [T:4136-4147].
9. The last of the pack breaks and dives into the water. Mialee runs it down and takes it with a final shot as it tries to escape — it was the one wounded at the beginning, trying all along to survive [T:4247-4254].
10. When the noise stops the party stands amid a mess of frog carcasses and dead bullywugs. The wizard's body lies in the acid-slick where he fell; his venom-staff was broken in the fight and lost to the acid [GM]. The chamber, and the way back to the moat, is theirs for the moment.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 45}}
