# Session 46 {{float:"right" 2026-06-21}}
{wide}

## 1 - Aftermath and the short rest
_~16:20, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The GM opens the session with the in-fiction anchor: 15 Hammer 1490 DR, ~4:20 in the afternoon [T:9-12]. The party stands in the nursery cavern, the wizard's body still where Nyx dropped it, water-flow and dripping stone the only sounds [T:401-410].
2. Eguzkilore scavenges the killing ground for arrows; the DM allows eight arrows recovered between the party, split four to Eguzkilore and four to Nyx [T:88-104]. Mialee did not fire and does not draw a share [T:107-109].
3. Amidamaru claims the dead wizard's staff — but the algorithm that spawns loot has rolled the staff *broken*, not merely damaged, and only a component pouch has come through intact, empty [T:82-86 ?ord], [T:1308-1316]. Mialee salvages a bullywug rapier and mends the blade with a cantrip and focused effort [T:128-137]; the GM notes its poison charge is one-use only [T:146-148].
4. The party is *"bastante follados"* — Kaelthar and Nyx near-broken, Mialee wounded, Nuberroca still nursing yesterday's grave — and debates a short rest here or a retreat outside [T:161-186]. Amidamaru volunteers to swim back through the tunnel for Kaelthar's plate, dropped at the moat before the dive; the GM prices it at ten minutes there and back [T:214-218], [T:342-343].
5. The party settles the short rest against the seepage wall near the tunnel mouth — close enough to bolt back through if pressed [T:378-404]. Amidamaru returns with the armour ten minutes in, Kaelthar buckles back in, and the rest continues undisturbed for a further twenty [T:410-414].

## 2 - Two bullywugs in the dark
_~17:10, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Roughly fifty minutes into the rest, Amidamaru — perched on Munin at the ceiling — spots two large bullywugs coming down from the north corridor, still out of sight of the party [T:458-476]. He whispers the warning; if the two reach the corpse-strewn ground they will raise the alarm [T:471-476].
2. The party debates ambushing versus hiding; Nyx points out any sound will draw them regardless [T:477-500]. They agree to ambush from cover and creep forward, Amidamaru wading through the deeper water [T:511-534].
3. Amidamaru surfaces just enough to speak the words of a Sleep spell over the pair. Both bullywugs fail the save and go rigid, incapacitated where they stand for the round [T:562-585]. Amidamaru corrects the GM's uptime — the effect lasts to the end of their next turn, at which point they save again [T:626-629].
4. The party creeps closer while the two are frozen, but the second save comes clean on both — sixteen and twenty, no effect at all [T:626-629]. Amidamaru, undaunted, throws a Minor Illusion of a lizard-scraping to the far side of the chamber to pull their attention away from the water [T:669-696].
5. One bullywug hurls a stone at the phantom sound and the rock plunges into empty water; the other stands very still and listens [T:729-738]. Mialee lofts an Invisible Mage Hand and stirs a small chop in the water in front of the one that stayed put; the bullywug jabs at the ripples with his rapier and voices begin to sound in the corridor beyond [T:815-847].
6. Amidamaru, watching through Munin, sees the second bullywug slip back into the tunnel while the first probes the water. Behind him, more shapes are coming — *"veo que se vienen un montón de enemigos"* — and he dashes to disengage back into deep water, Munin pecking Mialee's head on the way out to hurry her along [T:895-923].

## 3 - Silent Image and the retreat

1. The party breaks toward the tunnel. Mialee double-dashes to the entry [T:928-940]; Nyx and Eguzkilore follow along the wall while Amidamaru swims [T:995-1013], [T:1141-1149].
2. Nuberroca comes online only now — Sergio's ninety-minute window has opened, his infant asleep [T:1082-1088] — and Munin pecks him and Kaelthar to hurry, Kaelthar's armour already back on him from earlier [T:1163-1167].
3. Before diving in, Mialee shapes a Silent Image on the ledge where the nursery light still glows: a lizardfolk figure crouching over the frog Nuberroca killed last session, as if handling it [T:1055-1077 ?ord]. The intent is to hand the pursuing wizard a false first target, and the GM grants the setup as generous even though moving the image would normally require an action [T:1121-1123].
4. The wizard's four Dancing Lights arc overhead, converge on the phantom lizardfolk, and a lightning bolt cracks from behind the rocks into the illusion's rocks [T:1113-1123]. The Silent Image sags away toward the stairs as planned; the bolt only chars stone [T:1240-1246].
5. Nuberroca reaches the water — *"empieza a borbotear el agua a mi alrededor"* — and drops into the tunnel, Rage already lit [T:1795-1803]. Amidamaru lights a pebble as he passes and hands another to Nuberroca to keep him from crashing the walls [T:1215-1221]. The party follows Mialee's anchored rope out; sixty feet of tunnel past its end lands them in the moat [T:1222-1229].

## 4 - Above ground: smoke on the tower
_~17:17, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The party surfaces beside Tompu and Atzi in the shallow moat outside the cave. The GM anchors: 17:17 in-fiction, 15 Hammer 1490 DR [T:1329-1330]. They post the two lizardfolk in front of the tunnel mouth in case a bullywug follows them out; nothing comes [T:1330-1380].
2. Mialee spots eyes in the water at the surface and they vanish — not a bullywug, they think, given the direction, but not something they can identify either [T:1406-1413]. Nuberroca wades in, splashing loudly to draw a response, and gets none — the surviving crocodiles were killed off before the dive [T:1428-1431].
3. Amidamaru sends Munin up a hundred feet over the courtyard to read the castle. Bullywug, lizardfolk, and human patrols move separately across the yard — no mixing, no coordination visible from the air [T:1519-1525]. The wind is *"un viento nunca lo habéis visto antes"* — new, and worth noting [T:1521].
4. A pillar of smoke is rising from the castle's entrance tower — large, controlled, not a hearth-fire [T:1566-1585]. The party works through it: Nuberroca and Amidamaru guess *carnomorfos*, Mialee lands on the likelier answer — the bullywug dead being burned [T:1587-1603].
5. Nuberroca leans on Atzi and Tompu to come inside with them — the goal is a wounded push against the bullywug rank-and-file, not the chief [T:1672-1702]. Atzi baulks: *"Pharblex es muy poderoso"* and killed his own chief, no matter how loudly Nuberroca invokes *the great Nuberroca* or reminds him they slew the crocodile Coatl between them [T:1683-1698]. Nuberroca gets his best offer — the lizardfolk will come as far as the outer chamber and break if the chief himself appears [T:1700-1702].
6. Tompu, on Nuberroca's read, is *"un poco mierdecilla"* and will only get killed inside; Nuberroca sends him back south to the initial corral to mind the mounts, Juancho marked with Nuberroca's purple scarf [T:1705-1716], [T:2071-2094]. Amidamaru hangs a backpack containing two of Nyx's berries from a nearby tree — the party's fallback stash if they come out gearless [T:1719-1735].
7. Nuberroca uses the medicine kit on Mialee's remaining lingering wound and rolls a nine — enough, just — the last-day scar clears [T:1381-1397 ?ord]. The GM tops the party's short-rest healing by ten hitpoints across the board *"por ser generosos"* [T:1401-1405].
8. The party debates waiting overnight versus going straight back in. Eguzkilore votes for a long rest under cover; Mialee argues the bullywugs will re-fortify and the party's edge will bleed out with the light [T:1481-1510]. The vote goes with Mialee — before nightfall, back in [T:1530-1540].

## 5 - The Spike Growth carpet
_~18:15, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nuberroca dives back in first, Amidamaru right behind him, to probe the tunnel [T:1795-1824]. Nuberroca palps his way along the wall in the dark for the first eighty feet without issue, then a slice opens across his arm from the wall itself — three points of damage on nothing but rock [T:1825-1836].
2. Every five feet the cut repeats. Nuberroca takes another slice and calls the retreat; Amidamaru's own light does nothing to show what is cutting them [T:1841-1856]. They reverse — Nuberroca six points down, Amidamaru three — and the GM stops the counting when the pair are back at the moat mouth [T:1880-1892].
3. Amidamaru takes ten minutes to prepare Detect Magic as a ritual, then goes back in alone with a pebble-light [T:1907-1917]. The last forty feet of the tunnel light up as active magic under the spell — Amidamaru rules it Spike Growth, invisible-to-natural cover cut into the tunnel walls at the caster's own gate [T:1940-1946], [T:2008-2044].
4. Beyond the trap, at the tunnel's inner mouth, Amidamaru sends Munin in briefly to see through his familiar's eyes: one wizard, five bullywug rank-and-file, one frog waiting in the chamber [T:1956-1988]. The chamber has gone pitch — Darkness has been laid over the entrance itself, no longer the Dancing Lights of the earlier round [T:1953-1962].
5. Amidamaru retreats back out, reports the numbers, and his emphatic tactical advice is *"lo primero que hay que hacer es abrirse, separarse, no darle lo que puede que nos tire la magia fuerte"* — spread on entry, do not clump under the wizard's area magic [T:2098-2102]. The party works the plan and files up in order: Nuberroca leads with Cloud's Jaunt in reserve for the wizard, Amidamaru second as tank, Atzi third, Mialee and the ranged behind, Kaelthar rear [T:2100-2131]. In the courtyard beyond the tunnel mouth, the party hears more distant *carnomorfo*-like sounds — the forest is not safe either [T:2046-2057].

## 6 - The wizard's death and the first line
_~18:45, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The party spills out of the tunnel into pitch black. Nuberroca cannot see the wizard through the Darkness, so the teleport plan collapses; he wades toward voices instead and drives the halberd twice into a bullywug in front of him, both swings landing [T:2182-2210]. Two Champions leap in from the flanks — one strike catches Nuberroca for four points, the second misses miserably [T:2237-2247].
2. Amidamaru comes out with the blade-song weaving around him and sprints fifty feet toward the wall along the water [T:2288-2300]. Atzi lands beside a bullywug from the water and cuts it deep on a critical strike; everyone in the pitch chamber is trading blind against blind [T:2311-2330].
3. Eguzkilore reaches high ground, finds the wizard still lit by his own Dancing Lights, and looses two arrows with advantage — both hit, and the wizard's concentration breaks on the first shot, the Darkness winking out [T:2333-2356]. Mialee reads the line as Eguzkilore does and drives a Sneak Attack shot into the wizard from cover; he does not fall [T:2358-2384].
4. ***[EPIC]*** Nyx puts two arrows down the same alley with advantage; the second drops the wizard where he stands, and the party hears the body hit the rock even in the dark [T:2404-2410].
5. Kaelthar circles to a Champion's back — the first swing goes wide, the second lands [T:2413-2422]. Two more Champions come in around him and Nuberroca as the second round opens; the paladin takes two heavy hits in succession and is left teetering [T:2585-2593].

## 7 - The halberd, the reinforcements, and Atzi

1. Nuberroca finishes his Champion with the halberd, Divine Fury flooding the strike [T:2540-2549]. In the following round, his cleave on a fresh bullywug fumbles on the wet grip — the halberd slips from his hands and sinks somewhere in the muddy water [T:2717-2733]. He falls back to the shore rather than search for it under fire; the GM rules recovery would eat his whole next turn [T:2903-2913].
2. Over the next two rounds Mialee and Eguzkilore work through two more bullywugs from the wall — a Sneak Attack shot in the fog for Mialee [T:2947-2965], a bow-shot for Eguzkilore [T:2828-2848]. Amidamaru shifts north with a Booming Blade on the rapier, then swims wider to keep Munin overhead [T:2853-2877]. The two Champions who had pressed Kaelthar earlier break off, wounded, and flee back into the deep before Kaelthar can turn on them again [T:2986-2989].
3. Torches bob into the chamber from the deeper corridors — fresh bullywugs and at least two new wizards, one carrying a torch that opens the darkness [T:2986-3020]. The first wizard drops a Vitriolic Sphere into the party's midst; Nuberroca and Eguzkilore save for half, Atzi and a friendly bullywug fail entirely, and the acid clings for a second round of burn [T:3023-3060].
4. The second wizard steps out of the mist a round later and puts another Vitriolic Sphere down on the same tiles [T:3282-3298]. Atzi cannot shed the acid this time and drops in the shallow water [T:3299-3306]. Amidamaru stabilises him; Mialee refuses to burn a Revivify diamond on a follower — *"Yo no voy a gastar un diamante en un lagarto"* — and the party moves on without spending gear to save him [T:3313-3324].
5. From the mist, a sick-green ray from the second wizard's staff lands on Nuberroca — twelve points of venom, and Nuberroca is *"a punto de morir"* [T:3325-3337]. Amidamaru wades through the acid to reach him and calls Cure Wounds down at his highest slot — a bad roll for a big spell, but Nuberroca is upright again [T:3364-3378].

## 8 - The metamorph and the withdrawal

1. ***[EPIC]*** From the water where Atzi went down, a shape rises — scales that were not scales before, tatters of Atzi's tunic still hanging off it. A metamorph, born from the lizardfolk's corpse, and the party finally understands what the *carnomorfo* rumours look like from close in [T:3540-3556]. It rolls a 22 for initiative and comes for Eguzkilore's throat with claws and an infectious bite [T:3566-3593].
2. Bullywug Champions converge on Nuberroca and Amidamaru. A Champion's second swing drops Amidamaru face-down in the water [T:3697-3713]; a second Champion's swing brings Nuberroca down beside him the next round [T:3740-3742]. Nyx and Mialee are the only two upright.
3. Nuberroca stabilises himself on a death-save crit — one point of life back and the halberd still lost somewhere behind him [T:3844-3846]. Mialee sprints across, palms one of her carried berries, and presses it into Amidamaru's mouth: he wakes with the same single breath of life the party's berries always give [T:3849-3864]. Amidamaru spends his getting-up move on a quick-recovery walk and re-lights the blade-song, clearing eighty feet on the same turn [T:3899-3917].
4. Wounds on Nuberroca glow a slow blue and start to knit on their own; the same thing happens to Amidamaru but in green light instead. Neither has cast anything to earn it, and neither has an obvious source on his sheet — *"a mí me ha dado un poquito asquito"*, Mialee says [T:3996-4008].
5. Kaelthar, still armourless from the earlier swim and out of spell slots, backs into the tunnel while the fight is still on — the paladin has nothing left to offer the front line [T:3823-3862]. The party hears bullywug voices rising in argument from the north corridor — a real disagreement, not orders — and then those voices moving deeper into the cave instead of toward the party [T:3984-3991], [T:4006-4008]. The rest of the party pulls back after him: Nuberroca hoists Mialee and carries her, Nyx finishes the last bullywug on the shore with a Hunter's Mark shot, and the group files into the tunnel one by one [T:3958-3967 ?ord], [T:4054-4069].
6. Amidamaru flings a bolt of fire at the last live frog — the frog is soaking wet and the bolt splutters out before doing much [T:4017-4028]. The party retreats down the tunnel through the disarmed spike-growth zone and back out into the moat [T:4098-4103].
7. The GM closes at 19:20 in-fiction, ten more real minutes to walk out of the tunnel. The party owes Tompu the news of Atzi's death, and Pharblex, the bullywug chief, remains untouched deeper in the cave [T:4107-4116].

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 46}}

\page

# Session 47 {{float:"right" 2026-07-12}}
{wide}

## 1 - Atzi's death and the long rest
_~19:20, 15 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The GM opens on the moat outside the cave; the party has just surfaced, and **Tompu** waits with the two mounts, looking past them for Atzi [T:75-86]. Nyx breaks the news — *"tu compañero no ha salido con vida, lo siento. Cayó antes la maldicción de esas criaturas"* — Atzi had been infected in an earlier bite and hid it [T:87-104]. Tompu, in grief, mutters *"maldito idiota"* — the lizardfolk knew the risks and should have said [T:105-107].
2. Amidamaru asks how the infection spreads and the GM answers what the party already suspected — through the bite [T:108-113]. A quick round of checks: nobody in the party was bitten in the cave; everyone is battered but nothing new is on them [T:113-125].
3. The party pulls back from the tunnel-mouth before the bullywugs can come looking. Nyx leads the search away from the moat and into the trees, and finds a small hollow between rocks with cover enough to camp [T:126-160]. Amidamaru whispers a short prayer over himself and his own wounds start to close [T:130-138].
4. Roughly an hour is spent settling; before sleeping, Nyx spends her remaining slots on Goodberries and slips one to each teammate — the party's day-berries [T:222-266]. Amidamaru takes an hour and ten minutes with his materials to call **Munin** back to him — the owl had died in the last cave [T:270-276].
5. Eguzkilore takes the first watch and, walking his loop through the trees, gathers roughly forty or fifty branches from the forest floor for arrow-shafts [T:335-346]. Partway through the watch Amidamaru catches a large controlled fire burning inside the castle's entrance tower — *"un fuego en el castillo, en la torre, bastante grande de hecho"* — and points it out to Eguzkilore; Nyx confirms it from her own watch corner [T:296-330].
6. The full rest runs ten hours plus the two spent camping, and the party wakes at ~06:15 on **16 Hammer 1490 DR**, notably fresher [T:357-362]. Mialee spends part of her guard fashioning nine improvised arrows from the branches Eguzkilore gathered, and hands them over to him [T:377-388].

## 2 - Back to the tunnel-mouth
_~06:30, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The party breaks camp and rides north — Nuberroca on Juancho, the rest on foot — for roughly half an hour back toward the cave [T:406-427]. The ground around the tunnel-mouth is churned; the crocodile corpses they left there yesterday are gone, and the disturbed earth runs to the water's edge and back [T:430-440].
2. The signs read plainly — carnomorphs have come through and eaten, or the corpses themselves have turned [T:441-451]. Amidamaru sends Munin up over the treeline but the owl reports nothing moving [T:457-461].
3. The party debates trying another entry — a breach in the wall on a different side of the castle, spotted on their first sweep and never used [T:463-499]. Amidamaru brings Munin close and sends him to peek through the breach; the owl comes back with large forms inside, three of them, though the count is not clean [T:500-521]. Eguzkilore, in a whisper, tells Amidamaru and Nuberroca that something is watching them from the underbrush [T:522-528].

## 3 - The carnomorph ambush
_~06:35, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. From the north, a rustle of leaves and a skittering of small feet on branches — the party's old acquaintances [T:540-546]. Two ratlike shapes push out of the leaves — patched flesh and mismatched bone, tendons riding wrong across joints, articulations bending where they shouldn't — and two more surface from the water at the edge [T:550-558], [T:626-631]. The party is caught by surprise; the little ones get the first turn.
2. Amidamaru steps into their path and drops into a defensive stance, drawing the pack onto himself instead of the party [T:596-605]. The small ones swing at him and none get through the tortle's guard — three attacks in succession, three misses [T:610-631]. A last shape — dog-sized this time, four bone-limbs with a long neck and a stubby snouted head, plates from birds and other creatures grafted on — pushes out of the water last and closes on Amidamaru with claws and a bite, both fumbling wide [T:642-676].
3. Nuberroca, mounted on Juancho, jabs his spear at the closest small one as it comes into reach and knocks it flat on the mud with the follow-through [T:634-641], [T:783-786]. Nyx, in the middle of the pack, opens with her short blade — the first swing goes wide, but her second bites and the little one crumples where it stood [T:690-716]. Mialee, still out at range, puts an arrow into the one closest to Amidamaru and finishes it in a single shot [T:720-733]. Eguzkilore, engaged in the melee behind, works his dagger from Amidamaru's flank and lands both cuts on another small one [T:735-752].
4. Nuberroca takes his own turn on the big one, swinging open-armed the way he trusts, and pours a wash of dark radiance through the blow — the polearm goes deep, and the butt of the shaft slips wide on the follow-up [T:753-793]. The dog-sized carnomorph closes on Nyx and sinks its teeth in for a bare scratch — the bite carries the sickness with it, but Nyx sets her jaw against the infection and shakes it off clean [T:791-807]. Another of the pack breaks off to Nuberroca and a heavier bite goes home for eight points through his fury; he saves the infection check too [T:826-846].
5. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca's second-round strike carries dark radiance again and the polearm goes deep — the wound flares first as a black orb, then blossoms into pure light before the strike is finished [T:895-916]. He follows with the polearm's butt at the last live one; Eguzkilore, holding a reaction shot, drops it clean the moment it breaks cover [T:929-956]. The five heaps of stitched flesh on the ground were once the crocodiles the party left there the day before.

## 4 - Fire, alarm, and acid from the tower
_~06:40, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Amidamaru works through the crocodile corpses one at a time with a bolt of fire — burning each so the pattern cannot repeat [T:970-987]. On the last one the flame catches broader than he means it to; a plume goes up bright enough that a bullywug at one of the castle windows sees him and starts shouting [T:988-996].
2. The party freezes rather than moves, arguing north versus south under the alarm — Eguzkilore wants a wide loop by the south, Nyx wants the tree-line north [T:998-1017]. Two or three minutes pass with nothing done [T:1200-1201].
3. A hissing green sphere arcs from the tower window and bursts on the ground at their feet, showering the whole cluster in acid [T:1050-1054], [T:1121-1124]. The PCs all twist clear and take half; **Atascaburras** and **Juancho** save badly and take the full brunt [T:1055-1075].
4. Juancho spasms and dies where he falls [T:1080-1083]. Nuberroca hauls the dead lizard up bodily and runs him into the moat, scrubbing the acid off the corpse before the second burn can eat deeper [T:1117-1130]. Nobody moves to help Atascaburras; the second lizard drops in the sun, coated head-to-tail in acid, and lies still [T:1207-1215].

## 5 - Atascaburras rekindled and the flyover
_~06:45, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. ***[EPIC]*** Where Atascaburras lies, the acid burns start to fade instead of eating deeper — the flesh knits, the scales come back new, and a moment later the acid finds fresh skin and eats it away again — a cycle that repeats before the party's eyes without anyone casting a thing [T:1160-1180], [T:1208-1219].
2. Nuberroca opens his book on beasts and works the identification keys against what he's seeing — the species he can name, and lizardfolk of the guard kind are stubborn, but *"algo extraño ha pasado, no ha sido muy natural"* [T:1220-1236]. Juancho, he notes bluntly, does not regenerate; only Atascaburras [T:1244-1250].
3. Amidamaru takes ten quiet minutes to work a magic-sensing ritual and passes the reading slowly over the pair — no active enchantment on Atascaburras, no lingering effect he can read; only the faint pull of Eguzkilore's bow among the party [T:1260-1272].
4. Nyx reminds the group of the lizardfolk pact — they said they would help if the chief were killed [T:1274-1278]. Amidamaru pushes back, tired: they have tried the cave three times, the wizards are strong, and *"por qué se reproducen tanto"* [T:1277-1285].
5. Amidamaru sends Munin a hundred feet over the castle to read the courtyard — giant lizards, lizardfolk, bullywugs, and a few cloaked human figures moving separately, no coordination visible from above [T:1296-1330]. The entry tower has the burn zone at its centre, penned in as if they use it deliberately for burning [T:1327-1329]. The southeast tower is sealed under thick, dense webs — long-abandoned by the look of them [T:1345-1354]. And the central keep-tower has an enormous horizontal cylinder resting almost on its side within it — *"un pepino cojones"* — big enough that Amidamaru points it out to the party by direction [T:1370-1378].

## 6 - Up the abandoned tower
_~06:50, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nyx asks Mialee if she carries a grappling hook and Mialee produces a full climbing kit — pitons and rope, not a grapple, but usable for a bolted ascent [T:1382-1400]. The GM reads the wall for them: the castle is ruined enough that handholds and creeping plants make an unaided climb feasible, though a bad slip from height would land on the rocks at the moat's edge [T:1404-1407], [T:1467-1470].
2. Nuberroca has another idea. He drops his armour on the ground, takes ten paces of run-up, plants a jump, and lets his Goliath step carry him partway up the tower wall in a puff of cloud [T:1476-1553]. He hangs a moment by his fingers on the stone, calms his grip, and steps again — this time to a window at fifty feet [T:1590-1594].
3. Nuberroca ties off and drops the rope from the window — then Amidamaru's message reaches him in his ear: the windows below are aligned with his, and the bullywugs in the lower rooms will spot the line the moment it swings [T:1596-1635]. Nuberroca hauls the rope back up as two lizardfolk lean half out of the window one floor down, one of them wearing a **red feather** [T:1650-1670].
4. Amidamaru works the problem: Mialee's unseen hand can reach sixty feet and can hold the rope out at an angle from the wall itself — the narrow windows are shallow enough that even half a metre of drift will clear them [T:1751-1858]. Mialee sends the hand up; Nuberroca takes the line at his end and lowers it out at the diagonal [T:1837-1858].
5. From the shadows of the room behind Nuberroca, something moves on the ceiling. He turns and sees a large spider dropping toward him — and lands the first blow himself with the polearm before it can attack, a killing critical that leaves the thing curling into a ball as it falls dead [T:1776-1793]. The room, it turns out, is not as abandoned as the webs suggested.

## 7 - The spider chamber
_~07:00, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The initiative order carries from the killed spider; two more giant spiders emerge from the corners as the party starts climbing. Nyx goes first up the rope, hand-over-hand under Nuberroca's grip [T:1862-1888]. A spider lobs a white sticky bolus down at Nuberroca and he twists just clear of it [T:1965-1979]. A second bolus catches Nyx mid-climb and holds her fast against the rope [T:1983-1988].
2. Nuberroca hammers back at the closer spider with the spear — the strike lands hard and leaves the thing sluggish, its next lunge poorly aimed [T:1989-2038]. Nyx pulls herself up the rope still webbed to it, reaches the window, and cuts herself free of the silk with her scimitar once she's inside [T:2100-2135], [T:2263-2273].
3. ***[EPIC]*** With more spiders webbing the rope and biting his exposed side, Nuberroca deliberately drops one hand from the line — takes the fourteen-point bite for it and lets fury flood the answering swing [T:2043-2074]. He holds all the party's weight one-handed while spider after spider chews at him, and answers each in turn with the polearm [T:2098-2100], [T:2255-2257].
4. Nuberroca's wounds glow blue and knit on their own again, the same slow light the party saw closing him up after the cave — no cast, no source on his sheet [T:2210-2218]. Mialee makes it up on a clean acrobatic run and clears the window [T:2151-2156]. A giant spider closes on Nyx as she pulls into the room and lands a heavy bite for twenty-one points; she drops unconscious inside the tower [T:2314-2325].
5. Mialee dashes across, disengages from the spider on her, and presses one of her own carried berries into Nyx's mouth — Nyx wakes to a single point of life and stays down on the boards [T:2427-2454]. A round later another spider is on Nyx and drops her again; Eguzkilore, only just up the rope, unloads another volley in the same breath and the last spider falls [T:2665-2686].
6. Eguzkilore uses one of his own berries on Nyx and she comes around again [T:2705-2709]. Amidamaru works down the line of the wounded — Eguzkilore, Nyx, Mialee — and where his healing lands a soft red light rises out of the wounds as they close, unlike anything on his sheet [T:2740-2755]. When he reaches Nuberroca, the last few wounds close plainly, none remaining [T:2753-2756].

## 8 - The trap door and voices below
_~07:30, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The chamber they've broken into is the tower's top floor — three storeys under them, thick webs everywhere, and a scatter of animal skeletons across the boards, three or four of them plainly bullywug [T:2717-2740], [T:2811-2821]. The bullywug bones carry no arms or armour; the spider corpses have no venom glands worth extracting either [T:2795-2803].
2. In the middle of the floor, a wooden trap-door held down by three iron nails driven through the seam [T:2804-2810], [T:2932-2934]. Amidamaru sends Munin down through the window and back into the second floor from below — the middle room there is empty and clean, no webs; central chamber, staircase, two doors, one north and one west [T:2830-2851].
3. Mialee runs the trap-door for traps and finds none — the nails are the whole of it [T:2887-2938]. Amidamaru sets Munin to picking at each nail with his beak while the party keeps quiet; the owl has little strength but the work is patient [T:2984-2997]. Nuberroca offers to descend a rope through the outer window and force the door from below; Eguzkilore takes the run instead, wanting to fetch Nuberroca's armour on the way [T:2775-2800 ?ord], [T:3016-3023].
4. Eguzkilore drops through the outer window on Nuberroca's rope, a hush pulled over his passage so his boots swallow into the wood [T:3016-3040]. From the second-floor side he twists each nail loose with his blade one at a time, catching each in his free hand so nothing rings on the boards [T:3040-3075]. The door swings free and the party files down onto the second floor [T:3080-3094].
5. Nyx puts her ear to the wood and listens through to the floor below — perhaps a dozen lizardfolk voices talking amongst themselves, and threading through them a handful of human voices, none of them in Common; a single fragment she can pick out concerns *"una espada"* [T:3094-3108]. Two doors face them from the room, one to the north and one to the west [T:3113-3116].
6. The GM closes at 07:40, 16 Hammer 1490 DR; Kaelthar is still outside in the woods with Atascaburras, and Tompu spent the night somewhere south with the other lizardfolk [T:3132-3144].

# Session 48 {{float:"right" 2026-07-26}}
{wide}

## 1 - The tower's second floor
_~07:40, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The GM opens on the tower's second floor at 07:39, 16 Hammer 1490 DR — Nyx, Eguzkilore, and Mialee alone below; Amidamaru and Nuberroca stay up on the top floor, out of the way of the quiet-hands work the three below are about to try [T:17-21], [T:159-165], [T:642-643]. The room they are in has doors leading out to a room to the north and to a room to the west; the floorboards under their feet are quieter than boards this old should be [T:352-360], [T:263-268].
2. Nyx works the doors in turn, checking each frame for a trip-line, a marker, anything to say the hinge is watched, and finds none [T:229-252], [T:317-321]. The first door is going to creak; she dabs a small amount of oil into the hinge before pulling it open [T:271-291]. The room behind is empty, its floor unmaintained enough to crick a little away from the walkway — dust on the boards, debris where it fell [T:299-336].
3. The second door has fresh grease on its hinges — the party's fingers come away marked — and opens onto a similar empty room; the walkway is quiet under boot, the perimeter debris still where it dropped [T:319-336]. Neither room has anything hidden.
4. The third door opens onto a longer chamber — a table with a dead lamp at its centre, a scrap-pile of broken armour and old weapons piled along the northwest wall, and in the southwest corner a vertical stone cylinder rising through the ceiling: the top of a chimney [T:376-393]. Nyx puts her hand to the stone — it is warm; something is being burnt below [T:390-392].
5. Working through the scrap-pile with careful hands to keep it quiet, the party finds an oiled cloth wrapped around a bundle of short-sword blades — no hilts, no crossguards, but freshly forged and in perfect condition. Three or four more bundles under it hold dagger and spear blades of the same craft [T:441-475]. Not master-work but functional — someone in the castle is running a small smithy up here [T:463-475]. Eguzkilore takes two short-sword blades and a dagger blade, Nyx pockets two dagger blades, and the rest they leave where they lie [T:498-508].

## 2 - The rope descent
_~08:10, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The party settles on a descent from the third room's window rather than a walk down through the noisy floor below [T:544-598]. Twenty-five feet to the ground; the rope threads down at a slight angle to clear the window of the floor below [T:590-594], [T:685-687]. Nyx and Mialee brace on the rope from inside the window; Eguzkilore drops first.
2. Eguzkilore goes down first, boots skimming the wall, and lands clean on the grass at the base without touching the window below [T:691-724]. Mialee then anchors the rope with an old rusty greatsword laid across the window's mullion — the blade is dull enough to hold weight, sharp enough that the rope will need watching under load [T:645-651], [T:704-716]. Nyx follows on the same rope [T:730-753].
3. Mialee ties her own end with a knot she describes as a common seaman's trick — holds under weight, comes free with one tug of the free end from below [T:749-816]. She climbs down and, once on the ground, sends her spectral hand back up to worry the anchor loose; the rope drops after her [T:812-817]. Amidamaru and Nuberroca stay above; on the GM's ruling, when they eventually come down Nuberroca will spend his heroic inspiration and step them both down in a puff of cloud [T:756-762].

## 3 - Tompu and the short rest
_~08:15, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The trio pushes through the trees to the lizardfolk camp south of the cave-mouth. Nyx spots **Tompu** among the group and whispers a small message down the line — come alone under a pretence — she needs a word [T:883-909]. Tompu comes into the trees slowly, his face rearranging as he catches up with the situation [T:895-909].
2. Nyx asks whether Pharblex is still alive. Tompu confirms it, and the news does not disappoint him — *"Pharblex es muy grande y muy fuerte. A nuestro gran líder lo mató de un solo golpe"* [T:927-929]. **Suncaller**, the lizardfolk chief, is who he means by *nuestro gran líder* [T:951-954].
3. Nyx pushes on what they need to prove they have won the fight when they do — Tompu's answer is short: *"necesitamos la prueba de que habéis acabado con él"*, the head if they can carry it, or the fish-mouth hat Pharblex wears [T:1013-1024]. Mialee asks how many warriors the lizardfolk can bring; Tompu says a few would move against the bullywugs, but *"en ningún caso contra el culto"* [T:947-950] — the cultists remain off the table.
4. Nyx asks after **Snapjaw**: has he arrived? He hasn't. He was supposed to come with warriors at daybreak — two days after the party had last agreed with him, which is today — and neither of them is late by much [T:938-945 ?ord]. Tompu is asked to wait and bring Snapjaw to the trees when he does arrive [T:1063-1078].
5. The party pulls further into cover and takes a short rest to gather Eguzkilore, whose wounds from the spider tower still linger [T:1058-1088]. The GM top-ups the rest — a generous handful of hitpoints across the board plus a rolled hit die each — and the trio comes out of the rest at strength [T:1091-1104]. Nyx passes one of her berries to Mialee for her pocket — the party's carried stock of her Goodberries [T:1113-1128]. An hour of in-fiction time crosses; Snapjaw still hasn't come [T:1131-1136].

## 4 - Back into the tunnel under cover
_~09:30, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The party votes to sneak in without waiting further — the three of them are the party's quietest, and Snapjaw might be all day about it [T:1137-1160]. Nyx casts Pass without Trace at the tunnel-mouth; the spell settles over the three of them and steals the splash from their boots [T:1183-1198], [T:1349-1352], [T:1381-1382]. Nyx says it plainly as they wade in — *"nosotros somos un comando"* [T:1383].
2. The three of them swim through the familiar tunnel — the spike-growth trap disarmed on the earlier run — and surface into the first cavern with only the eyes above the water, three heads breaking the surface like crocodiles at the shallows [T:1216-1252]. Two frog heads are visible above the pool [T:1250-1256].
3. In the pitch of the cave, Nyx's ranger-sight makes her invisible to any bullywug or lizardfolk relying on darkvision — the three of them slip past a small watcher-frog without triggering a call [T:1271-1283], [T:1477-1483]. Eguzkilore's boot slips on a natural one out of the water — a hint of splash just enough that a small bullywug turns its head, but no alarm goes up [T:1408-1449]. The party works a careful loop along the right-hand wall and moves out of the pool cavern by an eastward passage they hadn't used before [T:1515-1518].

## 5 - The insect chamber
_~09:35, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The stairs from the pool cavern climb into a chamber the party hasn't reached before — the floor and walls are honeycombed with small holes, and the loose earth on the boards is *moving* [T:1530-1550]. Mialee reads it correctly at once — a nest [T:1551-1555]. The passage forward runs the length of the chamber; there is no other path.
2. Eguzkilore leads and the swarm wakes [T:1600-1611]. Centipede-shaped things pour up his legs and bite through the leggings; the first opportunity attack goes wide, the second catches him with a mouthful of bite that stops him where he stands [T:1611-1637]. Mialee dashes across after him to pull attention off; two more swarms and their long worm-forms slap at her, one landing as a crit and a second normal — she twists half of the crit away with her rogue's reflex [T:1735-1755]. A follow-up round of swarm attacks catches Eguzkilore with a poisoned bite that leaves his hands trembling on the bow [T:1717-1765].
3. Nyx works with the party through the swarm, hiding behind Eguzkilore where the halfling's small stature lets her disappear inside his shadow, and holds her arrow for the shots that matter [T:1866-1878]. The poison in Eguzkilore's blood clears at the end of the round [T:1938-1948]. Eguzkilore breathes through the wound, a moment's second-breath discipline that closes the worst of the acid [T:1935-1955]. Mialee disengages back along the pool wall and hides in her own shadow [T:1825-1856].
4. Two full-grown bullywugs amble up the corridor to investigate the noise — one advances, one holds back, calling in the language none of the party speaks [T:1892-1908]. The insect swarm that had circled Mialee finds the advancing frog first and starts eating it in earnest [T:2019-2023]. From her hide, Mialee palms a spectral hand full of the bugs and dumps them over the bullywug's back, feeding the swarm [T:2033-2045].
5. The advancing bullywug, half-eaten and desperate, tries to spring past — bounces off Mialee in the dark and, out of turn actions, stops mid-jump [T:2249-2266]. The second, taking three arrows in the back over the round, dashes down the corridor at full stride and bounds away [T:2267-2277]. The party fires as a coordinated volley — Nyx twice, the first arrow with advantage from surprise and a burst of ranger's ambush damage on the shot, the second follow-up without advantage; Mialee's arrow into the same alley with a slow-strike that stumbles the frog mid-jump; Eguzkilore closing on the half-eaten one from behind with dagger and driving it down close to death [T:2183-2287]. Then, on a single-turn surge of effort, Eguzkilore drops the dagger, unslings the bow, and wheels for the fleeing frog eighty-five feet down the corridor — the arrow takes it clean in the throat mid-jump and the bullywug drops felled on the wet floor [T:2288-2340]. The insect swarm finishes the half-eaten one where it lay [derived:T:2019-2023+T:2249-2266].

## 6 - The pool ooze and Mialee's gems
_~09:45, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The party drags the dead bullywug out of the walkway into a small side passage; Nyx puts out both torches on the corpse so the three of them can work in their own night-vision [T:2464-2515]. The passage opens onto a stagnant pool that fills the far end; the water is not still [T:2489-2504].
2. Nyx kicks the bullywug into the pool to see what surfaces [T:2585-2589]. The water swells outward like a wet gelatin, moves toward them, and retracts — a translucent ooze taking the corpse and dropping back below the surface [T:2596-2634]. The three of them scramble back onto the path; Eguzkilore is the loudest of them on the way — *"¡Uy, cómo huele la puta!"* [T:2612-2618].
3. From the safe end of the passage, Mialee sends her invisible spectral hand back to the pool-edge — sixty feet, at the limit — and gropes for whatever the ooze had been sitting on. The hand cannot hold everything it finds; she comes back with a fistful of dark stones, sharp with facets in the shadow, half of what was there. Gems, they think, though the colours will only show under light [T:2645-2684].

## 7 - The volley from the crane ledge
_~09:50, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The path continues onward and opens onto a fifteen-foot ledge above a lower chamber; on the party's side a wooden crane and a stairway span the fall; on the chamber floor below, a rising layer of white fog and a pair of torch-bearing bullywugs with a wizard beyond them [T:2698-2775], [T:2971-2974]. The party spends a long stretch at the ledge weighing options — an ambush across the drop, a rush of the wizard, a volley from cover — and settle on the last: three arrows into the wizard from the top, all together, then break back to the ledge behind [T:2820-3040].
2. ***[EPIC]*** All three fire at once, on the same beat, with advantage from surprise [T:3043-3048]. Nyx's first arrow — the head of the shot marked with a burst of ranger's ambush damage — goes into the wizard's shoulder for near-crit damage [T:3033-3057]. Mialee's shot into the same wound follows and drives her sneak-strike through it [T:3059-3062]. Eguzkilore's arrow lands as a critical, the redraw for damage goes deeper still, and one point above what would have been enough finishes the wizard on the spot [T:3066-3083]. The wizard falls; the frogs around him haven't moved a muscle yet.
3. Eguzkilore's second shot of the volley — a critical on the roll again — wounds another bullywug but the shot uses one of the improvised arrows and doesn't finish him [T:3094-3110]. Round two: Nyx fires with advantage from the dark and drops a slow-strike from her arrow into another frog; Mialee's shot follows [T:3115-3186]. The last torch-bearer bolts for the corridor beyond; Eguzkilore's arrow at eighty-five feet finds it in the throat — a fourth critical in as many shots, a nineteen and a twenty on the two dice — the torch drops into the wet floor and sputters out [T:3226-3260]. Four arrows in a row, four criticals; both torches are gone and the chamber goes dark [T:3253-3260].

## 8 - The cultist wizard and Nyx's disguise
_~09:55, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Two hooded human heads peer out from a passage at the far end of the lower chamber — cultist wizards — and, seeing arrows in the air, pull back into cover [T:3300-3324]. Eguzkilore takes a step back to the ledge and fires; the arrow enters at the distance [T:3325-3348].
2. The nearest cultist wizard answers with a hissing green sphere lobbed onto the party's ledge — placed so Nyx sits at the edge of the burst, the others in the centre [T:3427-3456]. Mialee, Nyx, and Eguzkilore twist for the save; two make it, Eguzkilore fails and drops to the ledge unconscious, his skin still smoking under the acid [T:3455-3472]. At the top of his next turn the burn will eat another slice off him — his death-save streak will start with an automatic fail from the acid [T:3480-3484].
3. Nyx sprints across and presses one of her berries into Eguzkilore's mouth as an action — carrying it to another mouth needs the full turn, not a bonus [T:3492-3510]. His death-save had come up a three: he stabilises on the berry to a single point of life, and the acid stops eating the wound [T:3506-3510]. Mialee lays a bank of fog between the party and the wizard; the fog settles thick and white across the ledge [T:3513-3541].
4. A bullywug warrior finds Nyx through the fog by sound, comes up on her with disadvantage, and lands a hard bite that leaves her badly wounded [T:3549-3557]. Nyx is not going to outlast another exchange like that at this range.
5. Nyx casts an illusion of another skin on herself — the shape of one of the small bullywugs she had seen earlier in the corridor, a companion of the ones now lying dead, no one alive to say otherwise [T:3595-3614].
6. ***[EPIC]*** On the sapos' turn one crashes out of the fog toward Eguzkilore, unable to see the ranger through the mist [T:3638-3646]. Eguzkilore's bow was already up and readied; his reaction shot goes off with disadvantage from the close range and rolls a nineteen and a twenty on the two dice, and by the party's house rule for double-critical outcomes on advantage or disadvantage, the arrow is the killing blow the moment it leaves the string [T:3647-3673]. The bullywug takes it through the throat mid-croak and falls onto the ranger [T:3669-3673].
7. ***[EPIC]*** Nyx walks toward the wizard as if lost, then vaults from the ledge to the ground below with an acrobatic drop — advantage from surprise on the strike, her short blade lit with a second burst of ambush damage on the follow-through — a crit-and-critical that leaves the bullywug warrior heavily wounded and reeling, though not down yet [T:3720-3775].
8. Mialee follows Nyx off the ledge but the wizard sees her mid-air and side-steps her landing; she lands hard, twists an ankle on the wet floor, and her concentration on the fog fails through on the same breath — the fog thins to nothing [T:3835-3944]. She is back on her feet inside two heartbeats but bruised. In the follow-through, her sneak-strike finishes the bullywug warrior Nyx had wounded on the way down [T:3899-3911].

## 9 - Pharblex and the paralysis cliffhanger
_~10:00?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. **Pharblex** enters the chamber — considerably larger than the rest of the bullywugs, waddling in a low bow-legged rock, a fish-mouth hat pulled down over his head, and his voice grating orders in the bullywug tongue [T:4132-4145]. Two smaller bullywugs flank him.
2. Pharblex is temporarily blinded — the GM's ruling holds only for a round or two — and moves forward palpating each dead bullywug body on the floor to check for players [T:4162-4172]. He reaches Nyx, still in her illusion, and touches her shoulder. Nyx's performance to hold the impersonation comes up mid — not enough — and Pharblex hesitates, one hand still on her [T:4173-4183].
3. Nyx takes his hand as if she is guiding him toward the party's line and casts Faerie Fire around them — Pharblex, both flanking bullywugs, and the cultist wizard behind, all four in the ring [T:4186-4224]. Every one of them fails the save; the four figures around her light up in a soft dim glow that the party can see through and the frogs cannot dim [T:4210-4225].
4. Mialee lays a second bank of fog between herself and Pharblex to cover her stance [T:4256-4283]. Pharblex answers with a low bass croak that swells to a roar of thunder across Nyx's position; Nyx saves and catches half of it, and the sound carries deep into the castle above [T:4409-4432].
5. Pharblex closes on Nyx directly. His first swing is wide in the fog; the second bites hard, and a follow-up ability lands on top of it — Nyx crashes to the floor at zero, and her Faerie Fire winks out with her concentration [T:4435-4451]. Her illusion holds, since it is not on her concentration [T:4453-4463], [T:4491-4494].
6. Mialee races to Nyx from the fog and administers one of the healing potions she has been carrying since Baldur's Gate — a full pour into the mouth of an unconscious ally is a rolled draw rather than the max the potion would give her, but it is enough to bring Nyx around and back on her feet [T:4488-4557]. Nyx sits up and settles her breath [T:4560-4567].
7. ***[EPIC]*** As a follow — bonus action, on a spent heroic inspiration to let the spectral hand carry the weight — Mialee sends her invisible hand across the room with one of the ceramic spheres she took from a chest at Carnath Roadhouse and breaks it against Pharblex [T:4560-4595]. The paralysing effect closes around him; he fails both saves in succession [T:4597-4603]. Pharblex freezes mid-step, held where he stands. The session closes on him held there in the fog, the small bullywugs scrambling around him, and the three of them — Nyx healed but bloodied, Eguzkilore at one, Mialee limping — deciding what to do about a paralysed frog king [T:4604-4620].

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 48}}

# Session 49 {{float:"right" 2026-08-04}}
{wide}

## 1 - Pharblex held, and the fog goes silent
_~09:50, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The session opens where session 48 ended — **Pharblex** held mid-step by the paralysing sphere Mialee had broken against him at the close of the last fight [T:10-15], the small bullywugs that flanked him and the cultist wizard behind him already dead [T:33-35]. Nyx lies on her back where she went down and Mialee's potion brought her back to a single point of life [T:23-25], [T:38]. Mialee's second bank of fog still lies white across the ledge — nobody outside her line of sight has a picture of the field [T:26-29]. Eguzkilore stands inside the mist with a giant frog directly in front of him; at least a pair of warriors are somewhere further in [T:33-36], [T:46].
2. Mialee opens on Pharblex — at point-blank range and against the frog's held stance the sword bites deep as a crit, a rogue's cut riding through on the same swing [T:49-55]. A follow-up with the off-hand lands as well [T:64-66]; she cuts to hide behind cover before the frogs can find her [T:69-73].
3. In answer the giant frog snaps at Eguzkilore in the fog [T:74-75]; another warrior comes up on Nyx palpating in the dark and, between his blindness and hers, misses the swing wide [T:76-82]. From the south corridor shouts carry — bullywugs calling out, coming fast [T:92-96].
4. Eguzkilore draws on the giant frog in front of him — the sapo had been left at a single point of life somewhere in the earlier fight and drops on the arrow [T:104-109]. He would have stepped back to the fog but stays on the line to hear what is coming down the corridor [T:110-121].
5. Pharblex fails his end-of-turn save [T:117-128] — GM lets the whole party's fate ride on that single roll, and the paralysis holds [T:126].
6. ***[EPIC]*** Nyx puts her Hunter's Mark on Pharblex [T:163-171] and, blind through the fog, fires twice into his approximate position [T:171-176]. Both arrows find him — the first at the shoulder, the frog's held stance opening him to a crit as the arrow buries deep [T:177-181]; the second follows it in for another crit at the same range [T:184-197]. On Nyx's late catch the GM lets an ambusher's opening burst fold into the first shot [T:187-193]. Pharblex takes both without a sound; somewhere in the fog the soft glow Nyx had marked him with at the fight's opening winks out, but no one at the ledge can see through the mist to know it [T:198-199], [derived:T:198+T:340-342].
7. Mialee sees only that Pharblex is *"sigue quieto"* [T:202] and asks herself whether killing him would drop the paralysis [T:203]. She strikes again — the sword bites deep into the held frog for another crit, a rogue's follow-up cut riding through [T:216-234] — then, in the same breath and with a small step to reach one of the smaller frogs beside her, drives an off-hand cut into it and a sneak-strike behind; she hears it fall behind her as she withdraws to hide behind Pharblex himself [T:235-247].
8. The nearer warrior, homed in by the arrows, finds Nyx and lands both swings — small damage but she must hold her illusion through them [T:248-254]. She holds through the impact — then the GM realises the frog-skin illusion did not need holding in the first place, and it stays regardless [T:255-262].

## 2 - The champion in the fog
_~09:55, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. From the south passage the shouts have become the sound of a bullywug champion moving forward with warriors behind him [T:279-282]. Eguzkilore pushes three paces further into the mist and walks straight into a bullywug guerrero — the two of them touching in the dark [T:284-296]. He attacks blind; the strike lands with the dagger [T:297], and a second cut digs deeper [T:298-306]. When he wrenches the dagger out the sapo falls, the blade the only thing holding it up [T:307-309].
2. The GM tells Nyx everything around her is now dead [T:310-313]; through the fog she cannot see it, and she sets herself ready for the first fresh enemy to step clear [T:315-321]. Mialee lets the fog drop, and the mist clears from the ledge [T:323-332]; where Pharblex had stood there is only him on the floor and the corpses of his guard [T:332-341]. Eguzkilore, closest to Pharblex inside the fog, realises the frog's soft glow had gone out at some point in the exchange [T:340-342].
3. From the south passage two bullywug warriors arrive first, with a bigger sapo — a champion — coming up behind them [T:373-380], [T:456-459]. Mialee fires from cover — sneak-strike into the leading warrior [T:377-382] — warns the others down the line and re-hides [T:388-395]. The warrior tries to run through her; her turn-out swing misses [T:397-409].
4. Nyx's arrow, ready since the fog dropped, goes into the leading warrior from the darkness — a slow-strike sinks in for a solid wound [T:417-421]. Eguzkilore's first arrow catches the same warrior and drops him where he stood [T:422-429]; his second, into the champion at range and against cover, drives clean in [T:430-446]. Nyx moves her Hunter's Mark across to the champion [T:466-470], puts a slow-strike arrow into him for a thin wound [T:479-486], and a follow-up shot out of the dark finishes him [T:487-490]. The passage goes quiet; only tadpoles croaking in the birthing chamber [T:493-494].

## 3 - Pharblex's head
_~10:00, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Mialee asks whether they can carry Pharblex out whole [T:346-350]. GM notes his gear survived the fight — the leather armour is filthy and shot through with arrows and cuts, and beyond it he carried nothing but the fish-mouth hat over his face [T:352-360], [T:364-366].
2. Nyx draws her short blade, gets to it, and separates the head — grim, wet work [T:366-368], [T:495-498]. Eguzkilore keeps watch with the bow while it happens, ready for whatever might still come down the corridor [T:499-500].
3. They pull five arrows from the dead and split them — three to Nyx, two to Eguzkilore [T:504-512]. Mialee takes Pharblex's head off Nyx's hands and packs it beside the heart she keeps in her bag [T:523-528]; the GM logs the head at three kilos [T:530].
4. The three cultist wizards' bodies from the earlier volley yield nothing — no staff survived the arrows and the wet floor; only their component pouches are salvageable [T:541-551].
5. Session had opened at 09:50; between the two rounds and the decapitation and the loot-search ten minutes have crossed — the clock now reads 10:00 [T:553-555]. The cave is otherwise quiet; only frogs and running water from the birthing chamber [T:555].

## 4 - The frog-shrine and the muddy library
_~10:05, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The GM lays out the room for Nyx, who cannot see the screen from her seat — the ledge behind them where they had come down the crane, and three passages ahead: the tadpole cave to the south they already know, and two west, one climbing and one level [T:558-570]. Mialee points at the climbing passage — a soft purple glow up the stairs and what could be lamps beyond it [T:572-577].
2. Eguzkilore's judgement is to fall back down the passage the last bullywugs came from — he thinks it is the safer route, and he is badly banged up [T:579-582], [T:601]. Nyx suggests they carry the trophy out first and come back with the others [T:593-596]. Mialee, greedy for the rest of it, wants the last rooms [T:1005]; she wins the argument and they push west and up the stairs, all three in stealth [T:604-624].
3. At the top of the stairs is a low shrine — carved rocks on the walls in the shape of clumsy frogs, a soft purple glow emanating from the eye-stones [T:627-634]. Mialee reads the symbols: pieces of Ghaunadaur, the god of the oozes and slimes [T:655-658], and of Shar, goddess of shadows [T:658], mixed together as if someone had taken pieces of two religions without knowing what either was — a crooked cross and a crescent moon, side by side [T:659-662]. The eye-glows are a luminous stone, not gems [T:663-667].
4. A second chamber opens off the shrine — mud-covered floor and walls, a table with candles, and along the wall half a shelf of what were once beautifully-bound books [T:675-683]. On the table are the books the sapo had been reading with muddy hands — magic books, ruined beyond any cantrip repair [T:685-705]. Along the shelf a few volumes are still elegant and still intact [T:697-699].

## 5 - The spore-trap
_~10:15, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. In the middle of the room sits a closed chest [T:694-696]. Mialee has learned from the last chest that opened on her — she steps back to the doorway and sends Eguzkilore and Nyx into the corridor behind her while she works [T:711-724]. Her spectral hand can turn the lock but does not have the pull the lid needs [T:733-751]; she goes in in person, careful, and prises it up with a dagger [T:748-753].
2. As she opens it, six ceramic pots drop from the ceiling — a well-hidden trap [T:756-763]. Four hold; two crack on the boards and the room begins to fill with a pale spore-cloud [T:759-763]. Inside the chest are two more elegant magic books, untouched [T:754].
3. Mialee fails the save [T:765-771]. The spores take her mind; where the party stood at the door she now sees two giant bullywugs staring at her, sure with the certainty of a hallucination that the only way to survive them is to get underwater [T:778-784]. Wide pupils, yelling in a language no one at the table speaks, she drops under the table [T:793-796], then bolts for the corridor [T:817-830].
4. Nyx puts her hands out low, palms open — no threat, quiet — to keep Mialee from spooking any further [T:797-799]. Eguzkilore holds his position at the corridor mouth, watching the room and the chest [T:812-814]. Between the three of them Mialee runs at a hallucinated frog and bolts past both of them; her speed is more than theirs and she leaves them behind [T:822-830].
5. She reaches the birthing chamber pool and hides under the surface with only her eyes above the water [T:836-838]. Nyx follows at her back, arrives at the pool-edge, and pretends to look somewhere else so Mialee will hold in the water [T:838-843].

## 6 - The corpses in the water, and Mialee's return
_~10:20, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. From the pool-edge Nyx sees several bullywug warrior corpses on the rocks and floating in the shallows — none of them the party's kills [T:843-844], [T:850-853]. The wizards from the earlier fight are entirely missing from the pile, and the warriors she can see have all been killed by blunt force — no cuts, no bites [T:986-991]. It does not match how any of the party fights [T:993-994]. She keeps her distance from Mialee, going through the motions of hunting for her [T:857-861].
2. Meanwhile Eguzkilore recovers the chest — the two elegant magic books from inside, and the four intact spore-pots from the floor, all wrapped tight in his pack against water and cracking [T:867-884]. The rest of the room is only broken pot-shards and mud [T:877-879].
3. Nyx sends a whispered message across to Mialee — her voice in Mialee's head, still Nyx's own [T:910-915]. Mialee, in her hallucination, hears the frog speaking to her; Nyx narrates the small motions of her body — sitting down, opening a hand, walking a few paces at a time — as proof it is Nyx and not a bullywug [T:917-932]. Mialee: *"No eres tú, tú estás dentro del sapo"* [T:929-931]. After ten minutes the spores wear off; Mialee comes out asking for water and one of Nyx's berries [T:933-942].
4. Nyx warns her on the way back — *"la avaricia rompe el saco"* [T:1002] — Mialee's greed drove them to the chest. Mialee, half-recovered and undeterred, points at the last unexplored passage — the west-and-north one they had not walked [T:995-997], [T:1010]. Nyx follows the pull; Eguzkilore behind [T:1010-1013].

## 7 - The conjuration slab, and out
_~10:35, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The last chamber is thick with the same fog they climbed down through on the crane — dense enough that Nyx is barely visible even to those next to her; Eguzkilore, the tallest of them, is the only one whose head clears the cloud [T:1013-1017]. Mialee casts Detect Magic and picks up a conjuration aura at the room's centre [T:1021-1031], [T:1061].
2. She blows softly on the fog with a handful of air, clears a patch, and beneath it lies a slab of stone carved with a star and a ring of unknown symbols [T:1036-1043]. Roughly fifteen feet by fifteen feet, big enough that Mialee's step onto it does not trigger any mechanism she can feel [T:1044-1052], [T:1140-1142]. She reaches for the arcane knowledge she has and cannot get further than *"magia poderosa"* — powerful magic, but no more [T:1085-1106].
3. Nyx wants to sketch the pattern for Amidamaru to read later [T:1107-1108]. Between the fog to clear and the level of detail she wants, the GM warns her that a full drawing would take nearly an hour; they settle on a rough sketch in ten minutes, on the back of an old letter Nyx had carried [T:1113-1143]. Eguzkilore hangs back at the corridor mouth in cover; Mialee helps Nyx clear enough fog to get outlines down [T:1135-1144].
4. Party takes the water tunnel out, the way they had come in — all three roll high on stealth [T:1163-1177]. Between the swim and the walk they arrive at the tree-line where Amidamaru and Nuberroca were left, at 10:45 [T:1188-1192]. They stop there in cover, holding the reunion until the others are up to speed [T:1197-1204]. Session closes on Nyx's summary — they killed Pharblex and they are still alive [T:1219].

# Session 50 {{float:"right" 2026-08-23}}
{wide}

## 1 - Berries and a book at the tree line
_~10:45, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The session opens where session 49 ended — Nyx, Mialee, and Eguzkilore coming up out of the water at the tree-line about twenty paces southeast of the castle's southeast tower, mud and blood on all of them and Pharblex's head still in Mialee's bag [T:1-6], [T:37-50]. Nuberroca joins the reunion late from the mounts; his audio cuts out on first try and Mialee has to talk him through the reset before he can hear them [T:60-95].
2. Nuberroca asks Nyx whether she has any berries to spare and how soon she can grow more [T:106-113]. She grows a fresh batch to twelve [T:130-131] and shares them out — six to Nuberroca (one for himself, five for **Juancho**), two to Kaelthar, one to Mialee, three kept for herself [T:141-164]. Nuberroca wraps Juancho's five in a strip of crocodile meat and feeds them into the giant lizard's mouth; Juancho eats it whole and recovers a small measure of hurt [T:175-186]. The wound-glow the party has been reading on Juancho's flank stays lit — GM notes the lizard is still below half life [T:184-186].
3. Nyx signs off for a short rest to top off [T:191-193]. Nuberroca spends the hour with his book of Natural History, asking why the lizards are healing as fast as they are [T:206-215]. The book gives him what it can — the lizards are exceptionally resilient in extreme climates, hardened by fighting prey and each other, taking blows that would kill most creatures their size [T:231-235] — and it does not quite explain what he is watching Juancho recover from [T:236-237]. He palpates both mounts, looking for anything to tell them apart; Juancho carries scars along his flanks and Atascaburras does not, but otherwise he can find no difference between them [T:238-262].

## 2 - The pike and Snapjaw's smile
_~11:55?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The party walks toward the main gate; about ten minutes in they reach the lizardfolk village and find it in commotion — two groups shouting at each other, pushing but not yet coming to blows [T:277-282], [T:296-305]. Mialee sends **Tompu** a mind-message to come to their old meeting spot [T:297-302]; Kaelthar creeps close enough to eavesdrop through the Draconic and picks up that one side is defending the cult and the other insulting the bullywugs [T:326-352]. Nyx asks who is more numerous; those wearing the red mating-plumes are just barely the larger group [T:353-357].
2. A few minutes later Tompu arrives with **Snapjaw** — three bright red mating-plumes standing high on his crest, and no partner beside them [T:365-375]. Mialee borrows a spear from Nuberroca [T:386-425] and drives Pharblex's head down onto its point — she walks up to Snapjaw and Tompu with the pike held out and both of their eyes bulge out of their sockets [T:427-434]. Tompu starts jumping in place like a small child; Snapjaw beats his chest, then tries something no lizardfolk face is built for — a smile, teeth bared with the effort, done plainly for the party's sake — and takes the pike out of Mialee's hands [T:434-444].
3. ***[EPIC]*** Snapjaw runs the head through the village shouting in Draconic; Kaelthar catches the meaning and translates for the rest — *"está muerto, está muerto, está muerto"* [T:444-446]. The argument stops instantly [T:447]. Every lizardfolk without a red plume peels away; those with red plumes fall in behind Snapjaw [T:450-455]. Mialee, to the tribe's back: *"Pues hemos cumplido. Ahora sois vosotros los que tenéis que acabar con todos los bullywug del pueblo y del castillo"* [T:460-462]. Snapjaw points west; a runner sprints for reinforcements, and the whole troop moves out — reinforced by roughly nine lizardfolk soldiers who had ridden in with Snapjaw [T:459-478].

## 3 - The revolt and the breach
_~12:00?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. About a minute later the party watches the lizardfolk attack for themselves — an acid ball drops between the bullywug guards and kills three of them where they stand [T:481-484]; stones begin to fall from the sky onto the bullywugs as the lizardfolk casters open up, and the bullywugs pour out to meet them [T:485-489].
2. The party debates whether to fight beside the lizardfolk or slip into the castle from behind while the front line is loud [T:491-509]. Nyx and Mialee argue for the sneak — the cult's attention is elsewhere and they can go for the leaders [T:495-509]. Nuberroca is uneasy about leaving the outnumbered lizardfolk to fend for themselves [T:519-520]; Kaelthar defers, willing to swing either way [T:528-530]. They settle on the back route — the wall breach the party had scouted from the previous incursions [T:522-539].
3. Alarm drums start beating from the castle proper as they approach the breach [T:620-622]. Kaelthar's armour clangs against every stone on the way in [T:604-612]. Once through, distant combat sounds carry from further inside the castle; the party picks two words out of the Draconic drifting across the yard — *weapons* and *wings* — without understanding what they refer to [T:662-666].

## 4 - The drakes in the courtyard
_~12:05?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The drakes on this side of the wall tolerate them at the doorway but grow uneasy as they push further into the yard [T:632-634], [T:672-678]. When the party crosses some invisible threshold, one drake breaks for Mialee — the charge overshoots her at a run and passes her clean by, and her opportunity-swing of the scimitar catches its flank as it goes [T:691-714]. Another pins Nyx against the wall; she strikes back at it with her sword [T:701-702], [T:740-760].
2. Nuberroca rides Juancho into range of the drake harrying Nyx and brings the polearm down on it — the shaft cracks its skull against the flagstones, driving the beast's chin into the ground as it dies mid-lunge [T:773-825]. Kaelthar charges the third at a full run — his boot catches on the uneven stone and he goes down hard, and his scimitar snaps between two blocks of the wall where he threw his weight to catch himself [T:836-878]. The drake sees a meal at its feet and bites him where he lies [T:881-889]; Atascaburras dashes in from the wall to draw it off him [T:891-894].
3. Mialee and Nyx pick the two remaining drakes apart between them — sneak-cut and arrow, one after the other — while Nuberroca has Juancho scramble straight up the courtyard wall and back down on the far side of the melee, riding a climbing lizard over the fight [T:914-993]. Last two drakes fall between them [T:1041-1066].

## 5 - The armoury door and the man being taken alive
_~12:10?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. With the immediate ambush cleared, closer combat sounds carry from the main courtyard [T:1069]. Nyx watches from the wall: cultists moving toward the front gate, bullywugs fleeing headlong out of the smithy, and a red-plumed lizardfolk beating a bullywug to death with a mace in the yard while an armourer screams uselessly at the lizardfolk from further in [T:1076-1085], [T:1184-1187].
2. Kaelthar's scimitar is gone; Nuberroca hands him his own long-sword — a versatile blade he can hold one-handed with a shield — and lends him his shield until Kaelthar can find one of his own [T:1101-1156 ?ord]. GM rules the sword can be used un-attuned for now; attunement would need a short rest they don't have [T:1130-1132].
3. The party sees the cultists take a lizardfolk down alive rather than kill him — the lizard stops resisting the instant he is pinned [T:1205-1209]. Tompu had told them earlier that prisoners are held in the keep tower, and the party turns for that door [T:1161-1165 ?ord], [T:1195-1200].

## 6 - The Thayans in the keep
_~12:12?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nuberroca kicks the keep tower door open [T:1221-1228]. Two men in heavy armour turn to face him — faces pale and bald under drawn hoods, one word spoken in a language nobody at the table speaks, and both charge [T:1224-1229]. Nuberroca gives in to rage and brings the polearm across [T:1289-1298]; the first swing lands and the second knocks the closer man flat with the shaft [T:1263-1320 ?ord]. The divine energy he tries to layer on top seems to dissipate on impact — a resistance he was not counting on [T:1443-1447].
2. Kaelthar speaks a word that binds the prone Thayan where he lies — the man goes rigid, only his eyes moving [T:1357-1379 ?ord]. Kaelthar reaches for a second word to add a ward on himself, then realises it would break his hold on the first, and holds off [T:1382-1392]. Mialee squeezes past Nuberroca in the doorway and buries her scimitar into the paralysed Thayan — a rogue's cut riding through on the same swing — before slipping back out [T:1394-1413]. Nuberroca layers another crit into the same wounded target with the polearm; the divine energy dissipates again, but the shaft-work still bites hard [T:1421-1477].
3. The Thayan rolls free of the paralysis on his next save; the wounds he took should have felled him, but he holds on [T:1478-1482]. Kaelthar's cut finally drops him where he lies [T:1500-1516]. A second Thayan comes into the fight through a further door thirty feet in [T:1517-1523]; Nuberroca catches him with a solid swing as he crosses the threshold [T:1521-1529]. The Thayan closes on Kaelthar regardless and lands two clean strikes, and Kaelthar drops unconscious under him [T:1527-1533].

## 7 - The retreat
_~12:15, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Mialee tries to feed one of Nyx's berries into Kaelthar's mouth; the berry does not take, and the wounds do not close [T:1543-1552]. She tries to drag him and cannot — at two hundred and fifty pounds of dragonborn and plate he is more than twice her carrying limit, and she manages only three paces [T:1567-1587]. Nuberroca hauls Kaelthar up onto Juancho for the retreat [T:1644-1657], [T:1783-1786]. Kaelthar keeps failing his rolls against death through the ride — the berry Mialee had put in his mouth never takes [T:1687-1693 ?ord], [T:1852-1856].
2. As they pull back, the party sees the wounds close on one of the pursuing Thayans as he closes on Nyx [T:1710-1713 ?ord]. ***[EPIC]*** He brings the sword down at her in a full cinematic cut and she twists away in the last instant; the blade drives into the wall behind her and sticks there. He wrenches at the pommel to free it and burns his turn, and she runs [T:1707-1717], [T:1746-1758]. She and Mialee swing up onto Atascaburras and Juancho, and the party rides out into cover [T:1721-1774 ?ord], [T:1826-1832].
3. Nuberroca knocks a further Thayan prone with a lance-thrust on the way through [T:1636-1639 ?ord], [T:1809-1813]; a bullywug slips into the water and swims east, but none of them see who [T:1834-1841]. Ten minutes after the drums fall silent, the party is out in the trees again, castle in chaos behind them and Kaelthar still down [T:1869-1877]. Closing summary: lizardfolk are killing bullywugs at the entrance village, small skirmishes are burning through the courtyard, the party has opened the door to the keep tower, and the Thayans inside have driven them back [T:1873-1877].
