# Session 51 {{float:"right" 2026-08-30}}
{wide}

## 1 - The retreat resumes at the water
_~12:15, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The GM opens where session 50 left off — the party still in retreat from the keep with Kaelthar unconscious and two Thayan warriors still on their tail [T:5-11]. Eguzkilore steps back into play from the tree-line where he had been keeping watch through the whole infiltration [T:82-91]. Earlier on that watch he had come across a torn cape hooked on a bush in the forest — brooch snapped clean, as though someone had fled the castle through the undergrowth at speed and left it behind [T:91-105]. He picks it up.
2. Nyx feels something shift inside her pack [T:145-147] — she does not have time to look, but a small pale item she cannot identify shows up on her sheet [T:152-155]. Kaelthar has quietly stopped bleeding out on Juancho's back without regaining consciousness [T:267-276]; the party trade theories in the water — Mialee guesses a delayed effect from Nyx's berry, Nuberroca that the cultists' magic on the wound has worn off — and settle on neither [T:281-289].
3. The first Thayan is up and moving, and the second dashes past him toward the party [T:164-176 ?ord]. Mialee rides Atascaburras clear of them and turns for the tree-line cave-mouth she remembers from the previous incursion — right-right-left through the tunnel [T:183-186], [T:435-441]. Nuberroca weighs wading the difficult-terrain water with Juancho and Kaelthar and abandons it for the outer wall instead [T:266-269 ?ord], [T:294-303]; he scales the south wall with Juancho, carrying Kaelthar on his shoulders [T:511-513].
4. A single bullywug breaks from the reeds in headlong flight; Eguzkilore drops it in two arrow-shots from cover — a first hit that leaves the frog on its last breath, then a critical kill as it turns to run [T:374-381 ?ord]. Mialee and Nyx swim down into the underwater tunnel [T:425-431], [T:446-448]. From outside, Eguzkilore watches one of the pursuing Thayans splash out of the reeds; the cuts on his face and arm visibly close over as the blood crawls back into the wound [T:536-538]. Both Thayans cast about for the party in the water, fail to find anything, and give up the search after several passes [T:707-716], [T:817-819].

## 2 - The forest and the flying carnomorphs
_~12:18?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. A pale mid-sized figure of light forms on Nuberroca's flank as he rides along the wall [T:703-706] — the bullywug wizard on the wall had been making gestures a moment before, and the party puts the summon to him in retrospect [T:748-749]. Nuberroca lances the figure twice; the second thrust passes clean through — no purchase, no resistance, *como si no estuviese* [T:770-773]. He rides on.
2. Nuberroca picks up heavy footfalls in the forest to the south — many of them at once — and later the crack of a tree being felled [T:801-810], [T:873-881]. He turns to see what is coming. Above the trees a shape he half-recognises glides over: what looks like a Metagorger, only with wings [T:914-917]. He wheels back at once for the wall.
3. Coming around, he rides the trailing bullywug wizard down — a critical polearm-strike leaves the frog on its last breath, and a follow-up swing on his next turn drops it [T:950-956], [T:986-998] — then smears the beast's guts along the lance for a poisoned tip whenever the party next needs one [T:1004-1006]. When the flying carnomorph passes overhead, the bullywugs still chasing him stop dead in place [T:926-930 ?ord]. Juancho cannot squeeze through the tower window; Nuberroca sends the lizard back and hides against the tower turn to see what happens next [T:1226-1236].

## 3 - From the tower, Snapjaw warned
_~12:20?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nuberroca climbs to the third floor of the south-east tower with Kaelthar still lashed on his back [T:1317-1320], [T:1387-1390]. From there the whole yard opens: the entry-tower corridor is floored with dead bullywugs and lizardfolk [T:1345-1348 ?ord]; inside the inner yard some twenty lizardfolk with their giant lizards are lined against a similar count of cultists — tense but not fighting [T:1352-1358] — and in the middle of them a tall, elegantly-dressed man is trying to hold the two sides apart [T:1360-1364]. To the south, past the outer wall, he counts roughly twenty Metagorgers in the air, three ogre-sized Assemblers on the ground, and an indeterminate tide of Boneleechers beneath — perhaps two-hundred to three-hundred paces off and closing [T:1338-1343 ?ord].
2. **Snapjaw** is stepping out of the far tower into the corridor — wounded but on his feet, Pharblex's head lashed to a pike on his back — headed for the same yard [T:1369-1374], [T:1489], [T:1525-1527]. Nuberroca comes down to Snapjaw's level. *"Hemos matado a Pharblex."* — *"Lo sé"*, Snapjaw returns; *"hemos acabado con los sapos"* [T:1523-1529 ?ord].
3. Nuberroca warns him of what is coming — *"carnomorfos, muchos Coatls viniendo del sur"* [T:1531-1533]. Snapjaw gives orders in a tongue Nuberroca does not speak — one lizardfolk sprints west [T:1535-1537], another climbs to a window and reports nothing visible from ground level [T:1538-1539]. None of them can see the airborne carnomorphs at all from where they stand [T:1544-1547]; Nuberroca points up with the lance and only then do they crane, cry out in alarm and turn the line south [T:1551-1560]. Nuberroca doubles back across the roof-top toward the tunnel [T:1561-1566].

## 4 - The second Thayan wave
_~12:22?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Trying to slip into the water with the party, Eguzkilore fumbles his pack off his shoulder and it splashes down loud enough for the searching Thayan to hear at once [T:1580-1586]. Amidamaru dives after the sinking pack without a word [T:1586-1587]. Both Thayans wheel and close on Eguzkilore [T:1671-1679]; Mialee and Nyx open on them from cover in the reeds [T:1682-1717]. Nyx's marked arrow bites the first Thayan and his pace falters visibly as he charges [T:1780-1810]. Every wound the party lands on either Thayan closes over within a beat or two — as they pull the arrows loose the flesh knits under the shaft [T:1922-1934], [T:2079-2080].
2. Before that, the GM had reminded the table that a double critical is instant death for either side [T:1898-1902 ?ord]; the second Thayan then crits Eguzkilore in the melee, and only a low damage roll on the confirmation keeps him on his feet [T:1943-1963]. Eguzkilore catches a second breath and steadies [T:2094-2107]. From inside the castle a heavy signal-drum starts up, and both Thayans turn their heads a moment before shrugging it off and going back at the party [T:2044-2050 ?ord].
3. Nuberroca closes from the north on Juancho and drives the lance at the first Thayan [T:2055-2065]. ***[EPIC]*** He crits from horseback — a lance-strike straight through the shoulder, followed by a follow-up swing that lays the Thayan out on the ground [T:2168-2200]. Immediately after, the party — Eguzkilore, Nuberroca, and Nyx — feel an unexplained fortifying pulse: skin takes a red glow on Eguzkilore and Nyx and a blue one on Nuberroca, and each of them stands a beat stronger, a beat more alive, without knowing where the empowerment has come from [T:2218-2229].
4. The second Thayan pushes back at Eguzkilore [T:2230-2247]; Atascaburras dances in and cracks him clean on the head [T:2251-2255], and Nyx finishes him with an arrow and a scimitar-cut to the throat — *"cortacabezas, graba bien la espada"* [T:2311-2314], [T:2350-2356]. Nuberroca circles back to the first, hoping to keep him alive for interrogation about where the cult is moving its prizes [T:2381-2385]; but the mortal wounds on the fallen Thayan are already knitting closed and he is starting to rise [T:2388-2391]. Eguzkilore closes and takes his head off with a single stroke before he can stand, shouting down Nuberroca's protest [T:2398-2410].

## 5 - The Red Wizard's tower and the gargoyles
_~12:26?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. A pillar of fire arcs up from the courtyard — a great flame rising to the sky, a heavy column of black smoke lingering behind it [T:2439-2451]. The party guess the elegantly-dressed man in the yard has opened up on the carnomorph tide [T:2456-2465]. Nyx searches the fallen Thayan: pale skin, hairless skull, red-ink tattoos on his crown, an armour beaten past repair — but the sword survives, and hums against her palm the moment she lifts it, magical to the touch [T:2513-2528]. Between the two bodies the party recover eight arrows — six standard, two improvised — and split them between Nyx, Mialee, and Eguzkilore [T:2541-2561].
2. Nyx tries to purge the poison from Kaelthar, then feeds him one of her berries; neither takes any visible hold [T:2685-2714]. Amidamaru has no counter-magic that would help either [T:2715-2717]. They leave Kaelthar with Amidamaru and the giant lizards on the north side of the water, with orders to fall back into the trees if anything shifts [T:2683-2686 ?ord], [T:2718-2720]. Mialee rides Atascaburras up the outer wall of a nearer tower for a first look at the yard — the centre of the yard is a great burn-scar, ashes and cinders where a large heap of bodies has been fired, and only two Thayans and the elegantly-dressed man remain visible in the yard proper [T:2603-2611 ?ord], [T:2633-2641]. On her signal the rest slip back through the wall breach into the grounds [T:2721-2733].
3. The ground floor of the south-east tower holds only a stinking flooded lower chamber and the Thayans' empty dining-hall [T:2740-2751]. Nyx catches faint scratching from the floor above and the party climb the stairs [T:2751-2762]. The first upstairs room is a Red Wizard's bedroom — a caged raven starts flapping and rattling the moment Nyx crosses the threshold [T:2884-2885]; Nyx tries to calm it with a berry, and as the bird swallows the fruit its body catches a black glow, it fixes her with its eye and begins screeching *"¡intrusos! ¡intrusos!"* in perfect common — no coaxing quiets it [T:2981-2993]. Nyx sweeps a stack of loose papers from the writing-desk into her pack for later reading [T:2770-2775 ?ord].
4. Mialee, meanwhile, rides Atascaburras straight up the outer face of the tower to the third floor [T:2778-2791]. She finds a Red Wizard's alcove — star-charts scattered under her feet, a large tarnished brass tube on a stand pointing near-horizontal through a window [T:2797-2811]. She leans in to look through it — and the four decorative statues at the corners of the room uncoil and drop from their plinths as gargoyles [T:2815-2820].
5. ***[EPIC]*** Two gargoyles claw at Atascaburras through the doorway; two go for Mialee — three swings miss and only the fourth lands, and she turns half of that with a rogue's roll [T:2839-2848]. She disengages, tumbles down the stairs and warns the rest that upstairs is *"cuatro gárgolas que meten hostias como panes"* [T:2937-2951]. Nuberroca meets the first gargoyle at the polearm's reach on the landing — a critical strike that only wounds it [T:3018-3055]. On a later pass a gargoyle catches Mialee to zero and she goes down on the floor [T:3115-3120]. Nuberroca switches to the halberd, criticals another gargoyle into a heap and the halberd's cleave carries the swing into a second [T:3200-3218]. The GM cuts the session there, the second-floor bedroom still contested and the drums from the courtyard falling silent behind them [T:3225-3244].

# Session 52 {{float:"right" 2025-09-20}}
{wide}

## 1 - Gargoyles and the shouting crow
_~12:30, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The GM opens exactly where the previous session ended [T:44-48] — Nuberroca still enraged and surrounded by four gargoyles in the tower, Mialee down on the stones behind him [T:130-166]. Eguzkilore tips a healing potion into Mialee's mouth without breaking his watch; she surges back to conscious, rolls to her feet flanking a gargoyle, and cracks its wings off her first swing — the stone body clatters to the floor in pieces [T:169-181], [T:255-292]. Nyx sets her hunt-focus on a fresh gargoyle, out of arrows, and closes with the scimitar — draws blood on the stone flanks second try [T:340-380].
2. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca weathers the gargoyle rush like a boxer slipping punches — a stone claw grazes him now and then, most just pass wide [T:243-260]. On his own swing he crits a gargoyle apart with the halberd, cleaves the swing into a second, and drops a third with the reckless butt-strike on maximum damage — the remaining stone shells shatter in fragments at his feet [T:382-407].
3. Nuberroca picks up the caged raven and prises its beak open with a warning — *"¿puedes hablar?"* If it repeats the alert one more time, he says, he will break its neck himself [T:524-529]. It repeats. He breaks it [T:530]. As the crack sounds a wisp of red smoke escapes between his fingers and dissipates on the air [T:534-541]; he rips the corpse apart and throws the pieces aside just in case [T:542-543]. All arrows spent against the gargoyles are shattered on the stone — the party recovers none of them [T:399-400].

## 2 - The Red Wizard's alcove and the two-tailed shape
_~12:31?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Mialee walks the writing-desk room a second time; her feet catch soft floorboard under the great rug, she cuts the rug open with her dagger and lifts a false plank — a small chest sits beneath, unlocked [T:565-593], [T:686-690]. Nyx climbs the stair to the alcove above and finds a Red Wizard's observatory: mouldering star-charts, brittle scrolls that crumble in her hands, and a large tarnished brass tube on a stand aimed near-horizontal through an arrow-slit — the room has stood untouched under the swamp damp for a century or two [T:620-641].
2. Amidamaru, hidden with Kaelthar in the eastern tree-line, tries first to reach the party in the tower with a message — the range falls short, no sending stone in either pocket [T:591-604]. He hears the boom of a large-scale battle rolling in from the south of the castle — the roars of carnomorphs threading through the shouting — and moves north with Kaelthar to keep clear of it [T:665-679], [T:429-432 ?ord], [T:849-853].
3. Kaelthar wakes from a strange half-vision he cannot quite place: a dwarven woman had stood over him and touched his forehead, said a few words he did not follow, and a weight he had not known he was carrying lifted off him — nothing physical, no missing armour, just the sense of something quietly gone [T:832-847].
4. Mialee climbs the wall on Atascaburras and joins Nyx at the crenellations; through the brass tube she sees the far marsh, then — for a heartbeat — a shape with two tails moving in the reeds, and a moment later a large black dragon's head lifting between the plants before it slips out of the field of view [T:749-773]. The GM places the sighting to the north-east, deep in the marsh of the dead [T:770-776]. Nyx, looking the other way from the crenels, watches the inner yard's line hold — the lizardfolk and cultists on the walls barely losing ground to the carnomorphs — no frogs anywhere in that formation [T:724-741 ?ord].
5. Nuberroca sets fire to the wedged sofa on the stair and the wooden bannister behind it [T:649-655 ?ord]; the smoke rises rather than falls, buying time but not blocking pursuit [T:654-655]. He rips loose a weathered piece of merlon from the crenels and drops it into the stairwell, wedging the breach further [T:898-913]. From below the party hear the sofa explode outward under a heavy strike and a fresh Thayan warrior step through the smoke, blade drawn — but no chance to charge before the party is gone [T:806-816].

## 3 - Down the rope and Anton in the yard
_~12:31?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The tower is seventy-five feet above the outer yard; Nyx's coil measures sixty, Mialee ties on a ten-foot length she has been carrying, they lash the whole to a merlon and drop it [T:1023-1075]. As Mialee ties the knot into Nyx's rope she notices it takes itself under her hand — Nyx confirms the coil has been shifting on its own [T:1074-1078]. Eguzkilore descends first; Nyx and Nuberroca take the last stretch; Mialee waits for Atascaburras to trot up the tower face and rides him back down [T:1094-1102], [T:1082-1091]. Nyx orders the rope to untie once the last of them is down and it does; on the second command it coils itself back into her hand [T:1207-1237]. Nuberroca snatches it off her mid-pocketing — *"cuidado Nyx, una serpiente"* — and hurls it away before she takes it back [T:1243-1253].
2. On the ground Nuberroca points west across the yard: through the stable door he can see Anton — the cultist who stole the party's cart in the swamp — some ninety feet off [T:1262-1287]. *"Por si queréis eviscerarlo"* he offers [T:1281]. He grips the Pendant of Sinsong's Warrior on his neck for a locate-Sinsong pulse; the medallion sits inert in his hand — magical but not that sort of magical [T:1310-1317].
3. From a tower to the west of the yard the party hear Sinsong — screaming, then a stretch of blows on a door with someone shouting for quiet, then silence [T:1300-1311]. Mialee sights the direction and calls the tower [T:1327-1332].

## 4 - Azbara Jos on the parapet
_~12:31?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. A hooded man leans out from the top of the keep — pale skull, tattoos across the crown — and the party place him at once as **Azbara Jos**, the Red Wizard who rode with their caravan on the Trade Way [T:1393-1401], [T:1406-1411]. Azbara gives an order in an unknown tongue and one of his Thayan warriors leaps clean over the parapet, seventy-five feet down, aimed at Amidamaru [T:1411-1421], [T:1428-1441]. Amidamaru whispers a counterspell and the killing blow softens to a graze on the way down; he ducks the sword-cut clean [T:1436-1458]. The Thayan brakes his own fall half a metre off the flagstones and comes to a stop hovering there [T:1449-1458].
2. Kaelthar unfolds the draconic wings, climbs to fifteen feet, picks Azbara out through the window and orders him to throw himself from the parapet — the wizard shrugs the compulsion off without effort [T:1481-1508]. The GM gifts Kaelthar an inspiration point for the read; already carrying one, Kaelthar passes it straight to Nyx [T:1512-1521].
3. Nyx closes with the hovering Thayan and pulls a shortsword; her strike lands but shallow [T:1522-1535]. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca crashes in on the Thayan next and drives the polearm sequence home — a first hit under the ribs, a cleave, and a finishing thrust that opens him for twenty-one across the pass [T:1538-1583]. Amidamaru catches Nuberroca breaking off north across the yard before the Thayan visibly bleeds — *"nos deja vendidos"* — and Nuberroca answers he hit hard but did not see him drop [T:1585-1592].
4. From the parapet Azbara casts and a red pearl falls in an arc; the ground under Kaelthar erupts in a twenty-foot ball of fire [T:1691-1704]. Atascaburras clears the first flare; Amidamaru does not, and drops on the flagstones [T:1712-1739]. Kaelthar's draconic heritage cuts the fire he takes in half but he still eats a heavy scorching [T:1758-1764]. In the same exchange Atascaburras goes down at Mialee's feet as well [T:1767-1771].

## 5 - The cell door
_~12:31?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Mialee sprints across the yard, forces the locked cell door and finds **Sinsong** on the far side — no armour, no staff, stripped nearly to the skin, blindfolded and lashed, caked in blood and freshly cut [T:1618-1646]. She strokes the top of his head and asks who did this while he half-conscious tries to place the voice [T:1642-1658]. Mialee cuts the bindings and pulls the blindfold [T:2000-2016]; Nuberroca slaps him on the rear as he stumbles out along the wall — *"vamos Sinsong, demuestra lo que vales"* — and Sinsong pushes off it for an extra pace [T:2067-2074].

## 6 - The regenerating Thayan
_~12:31?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Azbara barks a command from above and the Thayan Nuberroca had left dead climbs back to his feet — the mortal wound closing under his plate as he rises [T:1665-1673]. He drives the two-handed sword straight through Nyx from behind — the point breaks out of her chest and a lung with it; she coughs blood and folds [T:1975-1994].
2. Kaelthar, still airborne, tries to swoop down through the melee to lift Amidamaru clear — the opportunity attack would take him to zero and he waves off, retreating instead and putting the wings to a quick self-heal by hand [T:1791-1832]. He then charges the Thayan on the next pass, catches him clean under the ribs with a lance-strike, and drops the man in a heap [T:2145-2160]; a halo of protection settles around him with a whispered word, and he backs off ten feet across the yard [T:2183-2200].
3. At the top of the next round the Thayan is on his feet again for the second time — the mortal wound knitting closed under the plate — and cuts a diagonal through the air at Kaelthar, taking him shoulder-to-hip for twenty-three damage on a two-handed critical [T:2265-2295]. The cut sinks deep enough to leave a grave wound behind, and the halo of protection guttering out under the strain [T:2290-2302]. Eguzkilore protests to the table — the man had been down and beheadable at the top of the turn — and the GM confirms the regeneration mechanic [T:2263-2270].
4. Mialee vaults up onto her wall and lays a **Fog Cloud** across the top of the keep; Azbara's line of sight into the yard buries in white [T:2308-2320]. Eguzkilore puts an arrow into the standing Thayan on the second attempt and this pass takes him down for a second time [T:2337-2344]. ***[EPIC]*** *"Corta la cabeza"* Sinsong shouts, and Mialee echoes it across the yard — Kaelthar takes the head off with one automatic stroke, tears the greatsword loose for himself, and this time the body stays down [T:2437-2465].

## 7 - Nuberroca's blue halo and the healings that don't take
_~12:31?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nuberroca falls back north dragging Amidamaru by an arm — the tortle is too heavy to lift, and Nyx, tiny in comparison, he simply stuffs into his backpack and carries [T:2515-2557]. Behind him he scatters ten pieces of crocodile flesh across the flagstones as a lure for any carnomorphs breaking through [T:2213-2237].
2. ***[EPIC]*** Halfway to cover Nuberroca braces like a weightlifter under a heavy bar and roars — a blue light rises from his eyes and from every one of his marks, and his open wounds knit themselves closed under it [T:2717-2764]. He turns and thanks Sinsong before he ends his turn; Sinsong takes the credit — *"esto es lo que pasa por adorarme"* [T:2765-2768]. Neither of them can actually name the source.
3. Sinsong's cure on Nyx flows over her and dissipates without hold [T:2422-2436]; Nyx's own berry fed to Amidamaru does nothing [T:1854-1863 ?ord]; the small floating berry that drifts out of a bush and slips between Kaelthar's lips has no effect either [T:2973-2986]. Sinsong reads the pattern as an anti-healing hex and casts something that is not, strictly, a healing spell — Atascaburras and Amidamaru come back to their feet under it, Nyx stays down [T:2837-2856].
4. Sinsong feeds Atascaburras a strip of the crocodile meat, mounts up, and as the lizard begins to move every wound on him closes in front of the party [T:2860-2876]. Sinsong then rides in a sweep through the wounded, laying his hand on each as he passes — Eguzkilore is the one who comes fully alive under it; the rest feel nothing [T:3216-3235]. Nyx wakes on her own in Nuberroca's pack, reaches into her own bag for the water skin, and stays there drinking [T:3241-3263].
5. Mialee, meanwhile, has slipped away west from the main line and worked down through the tower to a lower hall — a five-headed dragon statue stands in the middle of the room, a lit brazier in each mouth [T:2018-2023 ?ord], [T:2989-2993]. She recognises the shape as Tiamat, snuffs each of the five flames with a whisper of cantrip, and nothing at all answers the gesture [T:2989-3000]. She finds one small object in the same room that reads magical to her touch, and pockets it for later [T:3010-3018].

## 8 - Anton and the cooks
_~12:31?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Sinsong rounds on the kitchens and lets a bolt of fire fly at Anton through the doorway; the shot lands and Anton turns to see who is shooting [T:2384-2394]. Kaelthar swings around the tower toward the same kitchen; a dwarf stands over a chopping-board and Kaelthar checks his fire-breath — Sinsong reminds him it is the cook [T:2621-2646]. He casts a compulsion at Anton instead — *"sal fuera"* — and Anton shrugs it off [T:2645-2666]. Kaelthar reaches for a vengeance-vow to follow through; the GM reminds him it needs an attack action first, and he ends the turn empty-handed [T:2668-2696].
2. ***[EPIC]*** Anton cuts back at Kaelthar with the rapier — the first strike is turned aside by a fresh halo of protection that shines up around him, but the second lands for sixteen and Kaelthar buckles [T:2777-2788]. He falls badly and twists an ankle on the landing — another grave wound on top of the shoulder-to-hip cut — and drops out of the sky unconscious as his wings collapse under him [T:2785-2792]. Eguzkilore steadies him with a medicine hand a few beats later [T:3161-3169].
3. Sinsong drops a red-robed cultist among the cooks with a lulling spell — the man stops in place on his feet, gone from the fight [T:3046-3053]. Atascaburras crashes through and reaches **Haranir** — the beast breaks his neck and shakes him, and a small dragon-headed pendant drops loose from his throat as it does so [T:3405-3418]. Nuberroca opens up on a second cultist with the polearm — a hit, a cleave, and the butt takes half the man's head off [T:3268-3299].
4. One of the kitchen staff the party had spared draws a scimitar and drives it into Eguzkilore's back while his attention is on the unconscious Kaelthar — the strike lands with advantage and stops him short [T:3315-3327]. Sinsong tolls a death-bell on Haranir before Atascaburras reaches him and the red cultist shrugs it off — a single note peals across the yard and dies without effect [T:3388-3402].
5. The GM stops the session there — a single in-fiction minute crossed since the top of it, the fight in the yard still hanging on Anton and the cultists, and the great battle in the south beyond the wall still going on where the party cannot see it [T:3421-3454].
