# Session 53 {{float:"right" 2026-09-27}}
{wide}

## 1 - Amidamaru at Nyx's side
_~12:31, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The GM opens exactly where the previous session ended [T:6-14] — Nuberroca in the north-west writing room with two cultists left standing, and Nyx on the flagstones outside where Atascaburras's stray bite had dropped her a heartbeat earlier [T:101-105]. Nuberroca closes on the first cultist with the halberd, opens his throat, and the swing carries clean on into the second man's shoulder — both drop before the round ends [T:118-125]. He sweeps the papers off the table into his pack, brushing coin and trinket aside to free the sheets — the gold is not what he came for [T:134-138], [T:320-323].
2. Amidamaru drops to Nyx's flank, lays himself against her, whispers words no-one present recognises, and a soft red light spreads under his hands over the arrow wounds; Nyx breathes, and wakes conscious [T:189-200 ?ord]. Amidamaru cannot say afterwards what he did, and mid-turn Sinsong asks him — *"¿Qué has hecho para curar a Nyx? Quiero hacer lo mismo con Kaelthar"* — before the fight cuts back in and he sets the answer aside for later [T:363-370].
3. Sinsong loads first Nyx and then Kaelthar onto Atascaburras — three riders is too many, the lizard falls to half his speed under the load [T:263-291 ?ord].

## 2 - The toad-mage's bell and the acid sphere

1. The wizard-toad climbs into the room from the upper stair, chants at Amidamaru, and rings a small bell at his ear; Amidamaru's save fails badly and his eyes roll back — he pitches face-first onto the flagstones, unconscious [T:376-394]. Nuberroca, further west, sees the mage framed in the doorway from the north-east passage [T:401-412].
2. The mage then drops a vitriolic sphere on the party's clumped position at the base of the stair — an acid cloud bursts open and covers everyone in it [T:571-586]. Nuberroca, Eguzkilore, and Sinsong throw themselves clear; Mialee only clears by burning her stored inspiration; Atascaburras eats the full brunt; Kaelthar, unconscious, cannot dodge [T:598-611]. Nyx makes hers on the GM's ruling [T:606-611].
3. ***[EPIC]*** Eguzkilore breaks toward the acid-drenched Kaelthar, works his sleeve into the coating over the paladin's chest and — with a stroke of impossible cleverness — strips the acid off before the second-round burn triggers. Kaelthar's life is saved [T:706-722].
4. Atascaburras trembles in place before Sinsong reaches him and the acid wounds close of their own — no visible cause [T:733-737]. Sinsong then lays a Balm of Peace across the group [T:738-745]: the healing reaches Nyx, Atascaburras, and Eguzkilore of the acid burn, but does not touch Kaelthar and passes Nuberroca by without effect [T:766-782].

## 3 - Nuberroca's halberd and Sinsong's prayer

1. Sinsong retreats with Atascaburras — the lizard carrying Nyx and Kaelthar — clearing the wall behind Nuberroca [T:801-814]; he drops down the far side and pulls into cover in the yard on foot [T:824-843].
2. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca charges the wizard-toad down the stair, drives the halberd home so hard the point breaks out through the mage's back, and Mialee steps in from behind with her dagger to hold the body in place while Nuberroca pushes it off the shaft. The mage is dead before he hits the stones [T:864-891]. Nuberroca takes the mage's staff: the protuberances at the tip are coated in a green slime that matches the bullywug secretions [T:896-903].
3. Kaelthar's breathing fails behind them; Mialee tries medicine on the paladin and fails, then Eguzkilore tries and fails too [T:930-960]. ***[EPIC]*** Sinsong drops to his knees over Kaelthar and speaks an unrehearsed prayer to a god he cannot name — *"Dios, el que sea… Ilumínanos, por favor"* — and lays his last healing on the paladin's chest. Kaelthar wakes, on his feet [T:1119-1145].
4. Kaelthar equips the Thayan greatsword recovered from S52 in place of his longsword and takes position covering the north door [T:1160-1189]. He still carries the cursed cuts left from the regenerating Thayan [T:1168-1170].
5. Nuberroca, moments later, feeds crocodile flesh to still-unconscious Amidamaru — the tortle's starving reflex closes his beak around the meat and he chews a bite without waking [T:1352-1356].

## 4 - Atascaburras infected

1. Atascaburras suddenly turns on his rider — springs off Sinsong's line and lunges for Mialee. His eyes are bloodshot, and a red shadow behind them she has not seen before; the bite misses [T:1029-1033]. Mialee reads the signs on a close look — the bloody eyes and red shadow [T:1053-1058], and the day's refusal of food she had noticed but not connected [T:1063-1067] — and names it aloud as **carnomorph infection** [T:1069-1072].
2. Eguzkilore holds out a ration to the lizard; Atascaburras sniffs it and settles, docile again for the moment [T:1101-1112].
3. Nuberroca opens the south door onto the lizardfolk stables — empty; the mounts are all south in the fighting [T:1196-1203]. He drags Amidamaru along with him as he moves [T:1215-1218]. Mialee, meanwhile, works through a smithy on another floor and gathers fifteen crude arrows off the bench for Eguzkilore's quiver [T:1240-1253]; upstairs she spots crates carrying the same brand-marks as those the party had seen on **Namocel**'s caravan and in the cultists' house [T:1478-1484]. From the window Sinsong spots the swarm of small **bonelicher** masses moving through the eastern brush toward the castle [T:1297-1303 ?ord].
4. Nuberroca pushes through the next south door into a nest of giant centipedes; he braces behind Amidamaru's shell for cover and swings the toad-mage's staff at them — the green slime crusted on the protuberances does far more damage than the wood alone, and he crushes several to a paste in successive hits [T:1698-1732], [T:1885-1899]. Between passes the party warn him about the slime — Nuberroca concedes and pitches the staff aside, re-equipping his own halberd [T:2049-2069].

## 5 - The dragonborn's quarters

1. Mialee climbs to the recently-furnished draconic quarters higher in the tower — clean new furniture, two tall onyx dragon statues too heavy to move flanking the interior door [T:1755-1780], an armour stand carrying scale mail sized for a dragonborn, a wardrobe of elegant clothes [T:2296-2320]. On a low table she finds a smaller statuette carved from real dragon scales [T:2091-2098 ?ord]; when she nudges its base with her rapier the whole piece tips and shatters on the floor — no trap, only brittle craftsmanship [T:2115-2133]. She names the room's tenant aloud — a black-scaled dragonborn [T:2326-2331].
2. Mialee opens the north wardrobe from the doorway with her mage-hand [T:2381-2395]; the moment the door swings, an acid cloud bursts out of the wardrobe [T:2397-2399] and catches her and Kaelthar (who had followed her in) [T:2408-2417]. Whatever was inside the wardrobe dissolves with the vapour [T:2418-2421].
3. In the meantime Nuberroca reaches the kitchens carrying Amidamaru and finds two cooks kneeling beside Mialee and Kaelthar, both of them unconscious and stretched along the wall. The cooks raise their hands: a lizardman had ordered them to take the two bodies down here and had gone back upstairs alone [T:2593-2607]. Nuberroca goes through Mialee's pockets for healing potions and comes out with four common and one greater [T:2653-2665]; he pours the first potion into Kaelthar and the second into Mialee, and both come around [T:2666-2682].
4. Kaelthar tells the group he saw a dwarven woman standing over him during his unconsciousness — she had laid her hand on his chest, closed her fist, and pulled *something* out of him he had not known he was carrying. He had woken emotionally lighter, a peace inside he had not felt in a long time [T:2696-2709]. It is the second such vision in as many sessions ([S52 minutes §2.3](../session-52/minutes-52.md)).
5. **Tharn** the dwarf cook offers beer in place of a fight; Nuberroca accepts, and Tharn takes them into the bodega and pours jars for whoever will drink [T:2716-2745]. Kaelthar summons his wolf **Fenris** out of empty air in the pantry — Tharn drops the milk jar he is pouring for Mialee, panics at the wolf rising up from the stones, and bolts up the stairs [T:2749-2752]. Nuberroca calls him back on the offer of survival and Tharn agrees to come along rather than stay [T:2826-2832]. The party stocks about twenty rations from the pantry on the way through [T:2758-2774 ?ord].

## 6 - Down to the caves

1. From the top-floor stair Sinsong watches six cultists file down past him toward the yard — and a **Metagorger** answers by climbing through the window; the cultists turn and rush it, opening a window to slip past unseen [T:2996-3006]. The party regroups in the sotherly cave — old bullywug ground, walls holed by insect action, half-devoured corpses on the floor [T:3170-3176].
2. Atascaburras, still ridden by Sinsong, jerks and bites at his rider — Nuberroca catches the lizard by the neck in time and takes the worst of the strike off [T:3072-3079 ?ord]. The eyes bleed red for a heartbeat and then clear [T:3079-3091]. Sinsong tries to send him up the stair to fight; the lizard lunges at him a second time and Nuberroca has to catch him again [T:3094-3098].
3. A Metagorger comes down the stair after them; Nuberroca's halberd shears two of its front bone-legs off and it pitches forward [T:3345-3362]. Kaelthar cuts in with the Thayan greatsword and Fenris takes a second bite on the same pass [T:3316-3345 ?ord]. Mialee's arrow finishes it [T:3395-3400]. Sinsong burns the corpse to ashes with a firebolt so no second carnomorph erupts from it [T:3408-3409].
4. A second Metagorger — bathed in acid — enters from the east and starts dissolving the corpse-litter as it advances [T:3427-3445]. Nuberroca strikes it once with a spear; the strike splashes acid back onto him, his armour begins to melt, and the spear's steel dulls under the vapour [T:3438-3446]. He withdraws north [T:3448-3454].
5. Sinsong points Atascaburras at the acid-carnomorph and gives him the order to fight [T:3450-3451]; the party runs north to the far cave. From the south they hear the lizard's roars and the wet sound of gelatine — Atascaburras dies down there fighting the second carnomorph, buying them the time to escape [derived:T:3483-3486+T:3489-3491].
6. Amidamaru wakes at last as the party's flight slows past the scorched corpses of the toad-mage's guardians; he pulls to his feet and runs after them [T:3506-3509].

## 7 - Anton's paper and the Blythefield circle
_~13:00?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. In the north cave the party stops to read the papers Nuberroca lifted from the writing room. Mialee shares one — an *informe de Anton* — that names Anton as the man who murdered Carlos Namocel on the caravan, and that lays out Anton's intent to let the Thayans do the party's dirty work: *"El grupo de Anton llega, son violentos, no estrategas. Usad eso, dejad que maten a los tallanos"* [T:3586-3597]. Another paper turns the massacre at Karnaz into a story about Sinsong personally leading the killing of civilians [T:3606-3607].
2. On the north cave floor is a large magical drawing Sinsong recognises through Arcana as some kind of teleport pattern he does not know how to trigger [T:3559-3567 ?ord]. Amidamaru, coming back to himself, confirms it — teleport, needs a key word [T:3656-3664]. Nuberroca tries words at random — first *Melon*, then *Blythefield* — the second word wakes the circle [T:3667-3675].
3. ***[EPIC]*** Mialee, Nuberroca, and Sinsong vanish first; Eguzkilore, Nyx, and Amidamaru step onto the circle after them (Tharn beside Eguzkilore) and vanish too. The party appears standing on an identical circle high in snowy mountains — the humid castle air replaced with sharp cold, pine forest all round, and a long house of several storeys at the far end of a footpath across the clearing [T:3676-3702]. The GM closes on the threshold [T:3703]. The party levels up to 6 [T:3729-3730].

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 53}}

\page

Note: There was an issue with Foundry's clock (each long/short rest advanced the time and that messed up things).
The full game lasted for around 9h (1 long rest and 1 short + a bit of talking and walking)

# Session 54 {{float:"right" 2026-10-04}}
{wide}

## 1 - Arrival in the snow
_~12:40?, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The party steps off the teleport circle and the humid castle air of Naerytar is replaced in a single breath by a cold, snowbound mountain in heavy rain [T:70-74], the ground slick where snow meets water [T:173]. The GM calls the area lightly obscured [T:78-79]. Downslope through the pines, ~500m away, stands a stone-and-timber house large enough to be a hunter's lodge for wealthy clients, not a trapper's hut [T:181-187], its ground floor stone and the upper storeys timber [T:188]. The path toward it bifurcates at ~50 ft, left and right [T:189-191]. Fresh boot-tracks ~20 min old lead down [T:206].
2. The arrival circle is a near-twin of the one in Naerytar [T:175-176], with two large carved monoliths standing just in front [T:177-180]. Behind the matching flanking stones at the two path-forks sit two more circles — three in all [T:245-263]. **Kaelthar** reads the Draconic script on the stones but the words make no sense in Draconic [T:208-212]; **Amidamaru** places them as *Loross*, the tongue of an ancient magical empire [T:229-232]. From his half-translation he recognises *marsh* and *frog-croak* on the party's own arrival circle [T:235-240], *snow-lands* on one of the others [T:247-251], and *red sun* on the third [T:252]. The glyphs name destinations.
3. Only the party's own circle shows fresh tracks [T:266]. Amidamaru warns everyone to spread out — he does not want another acid sphere from the toads catching the group clumped [T:273-276] — and Nyx argues for shelter and warmth first [T:279-282]. Amidamaru proposes a warded shelter ritual: ten minutes to raise, eight hours of invisible protection [T:293-294], [T:533-534]. He steps into the trees and begins [T:338-339].

## 2 - The troll and the children
_~13:03, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Two minutes into the ritual [T:342-343], **Nyx** catches a fight to the east — two bands at it, not hunting [T:321-325] — with a weapon-noise she places as the gunpowder-like bolts the party had stripped off bandits on the road weeks earlier [T:314-317], and the cries of something being driven [T:325]. The sound moves toward the party [T:334-335].
2. **Nuberroca** climbs a tall pine to see further [T:390-391]. Through the pines and rain he picks out a hulking brute with moss-like skin dragging two small struggling forms down the path toward the lodge [T:396-399], the children clearly alive and losing [T:400-401]. Nearer, something trailing up the slope resolves into a troll, badly wounded, cut and burned, crude feather-fletched arrows still in him, dark blood on his own hide and bright red on his hands and claws [T:416-424]; Nuberroca names it a troll on sight [T:425]. ***[EPIC]*** From the pine Nyx on her near-perfect perception watches the mossy brute reach the lodge grounds and bind one of the children while he takes the other by chest and arm — and *pulls* [T:477-482], and even at that distance she hears the child's shriek as the arm comes free [T:483].
3. The troll pauses, dazed and bleeding, close enough to catch the party — but they are well-hidden under the pines and he blunders past [T:432-434], [T:459-461]. Fire-bolt-like bursting projectiles crack open on his hide from unseen hunters in the woods, searing patches that do not close while the sword-cuts seal over as he moves [T:426-428], [T:463-466]. He kneels, rises, keeps going [T:467-470]. A last bursting bolt catches his neck at the tree-line and he drops and does not rise [T:504-507]. Nyx argues furiously for the party to intervene — people are being slaughtered down there [T:485-488], [T:515-517] — but Nuberroca is at half, Eguzkilore worse, and the rest no better: no-one will go without a full night's rest first [T:489-500].
4. In the last minute of the ritual, several unseen somethings slip up through the brush and brush the group as they pass; three of the party are touched [T:567-579]. Nyx finds the rope stripped from her pack [T:584-585]; Eguzkilore the canteen cut from his [T:586]; **Nuberroca** the wrapped crocodile meat lifted from the top of his [T:595-596]. Nuberroca shrugs it off — one bite feeds him a week [T:599], [T:601]. The shelter completes a minute later and the party steps inside [T:602-605].

## 3 - The night in the warded shelter

1. Inside, dry and warm, out of the rain and out of sight of what moves outside [T:547-549], [T:605-606]. **Sinsong** steps up to the group out of the long awkward quiet and offers an unscripted apology — *"la culpa ha sido mía. Lo siento mucho, chicos… creo que esto no va de mí"* — and asks the party to let him stand with them from here on out [T:655-674]. Nyx answers with her own correction: too many of them keep peeling off alone, and when the lone one is in trouble no-one knows where to look for them [T:675-682].
2. Nyx shows Sinsong the long sword-cut across her chest — a serious wound that will not close with sleep alone [T:781-784]. The GM lays out the ways to clear it: deep healing beyond her maximum, a restoration spell, or careful field-medicine with a healer's kit [T:818-820]. **Nuberroca** opens his pack, chews a packet of herbs down into a paste, spits it into the chest-wound and packs it in by hand [T:876-878] — with Sinsong whispering guidance over his shoulder [T:851-853] and Nyx steadying him [T:826-830], his treatment lands cleanly [T:870-874]. Nyx shakes off the barbarism and allows that in her tribe the field-medicine goes the same way [T:879-881].
3. Amidamaru takes the two books Eguzkilore brought from the castle and sits down to identify them, a half-hour apiece [T:942-943], [T:973]. The first is the spellbook of a wizard named **Hothars**, written in warded and awkward script that takes the full half-hour to break open [T:947-950]. The second, by one **Keneus**, is heavily annotated with uneasy marginalia and crude illustrations — not a good man, Amidamaru judges, but a wizard all the same [T:982-987]. He pockets both and lies down to sleep [T:988-990].
4. Partway through the second book Eguzkilore picks out two pairs of eyes among the brush — small, yellow, close to the ground [T:1005-1012]. Two goblins creep up to the shelter wall, touch it, probe with a dagger, cannot find a way through [T:1024-1030] — and one carries Nyx's stolen rope coiled on his belt [T:1016-1017]. A third joins a little later [T:1038-1041], and after about ten minutes all three drift off [T:1042-1043]. From the lodge, every so often, distant screams carry up to the party in the first hour of the night [T:1047]. **Mialee** uses the quiet of her last four hours to work scavenged shafts into roughly nineteen crude arrows for Eguzkilore's quiver [T:1209-1211].

## 4 - Dawn, wounds, berries
_~21:00, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The shelter dissolves on its own clock [T:1085-1088]. The rain has eased but not stopped [T:1088-1090], and the night outside bites — bad enough that Sinsong wraps himself in a captured cultist cloak and Tharn, with nothing winter-ready, starts shivering straight away [T:1250-1253], [T:1261-1265]. In his sleep Amidamaru is thrashing and muttering: *"quítame esta maldición, la puta madre que me parió"* [T:1104-1108]. He thinks the Thayan's cut from the castle may be behind it [T:1122]. Several of the party find themselves holding reduced hit-maximums from lingering wounds [T:1117-1125].
2. **Kaelthar** itemises what he has been fighting through — internal bleeding, a twisted ankle, crushed fingers in his right hand — all left over from the Thayan brawl [T:1237], [T:1272-1275]. The GM spells out the healing avenues for a serious wound [T:1280-1294]. The party picks the internal bleeding first; Nuberroca works the Healer's Kit on Kaelthar with Nyx steadying him [T:1317-1319] and Sinsong whispering guidance [T:1324-1325], and the treatment clears cleanly [T:1327-1329]. The bleeding staunches; the ankle and the fingers still carry [T:1343-1344].
3. **Nyx** had told the group yesterday there were no berries today [T:1194], but it's clear the party is in no condition to meet the morning without them. She prepares a fresh day's worth and hands one to each of the six awake — one each is all there is [T:1227-1228], [T:1247-1249]. Sinsong, meanwhile, watches Amidamaru for arcane signs of a curse — the tortle keeps shifting in his shell and the reading never resolves [T:1181-1188 ?ord], [T:1213-1216].
4. From the clearing the party can see lights in nearly every window of the lodge below, holding steady through the dark [T:1354-1358].

## 5 - Sohey's ambush

1. The approach is detected this time before it reaches them — several small forms moving fast through the trees, and Amidamaru's keen ear places them as goblins rather than children [T:1373-1378]. Mialee hides at a tree [T:1391-1393]; Nyx goes to ground [T:1377]; Sinsong hides at Amidamaru's side [T:1388]; Eguzkilore readies the longbow [T:1383]; Kaelthar climbs into a tree [T:1395]. Nuberroca stays visible [T:1402].
2. Nuberroca swings into the dark where he thinks a goblin is and shears a branch for his trouble — he cannot see what he is swinging at [T:1467-1476]. Mialee, hidden, puts an arrow clean through the first visible goblin and drops it in one [T:1498-1502]. Eguzkilore looses at a second — but the goblin catches the arrow out of the air and *throws it back* [T:1545-1546]; Eguzkilore ducks it [T:1559]. Nyx strikes a goblin from behind Nuberroca and holds back the killing blow — her call aloud is not to kill them [T:1595-1596], [T:1611-1617]. On the goblin side a crude arrow drops **Tharn** at the shelter's edge, straight to the throat [T:1655-1657], [T:1752].
3. The leader — **Sohey** — closes under the moonlight, scimitar and short sword out [T:1794], [T:1817-1818]. He opens on Nyx with sword, scimitar, and a sudden strike from behind, and leaves her a hair from going down in a single round [T:1820-1830]; then steps around to Nuberroca, drops his blades, and ***[EPIC]*** goes to work with his fists and feet — punch, punch, a leaping kick that lands with full force, each strike biting — and names him *cultista de mierda* as he hits [T:1897-1917], [T:1913-1915]. Mialee calls it half Nuberroca's life gone in one round [T:1917]. Only a later correction restores much of it: half such punishment would have slid off Nuberroca's rage-hardened hide, and the GM had been applying the strikes raw [T:2139-2149].
4. Sohey picks up the goblin Nyx had downed and turns to escape with the body [T:2076]. Nuberroca steps through a wisp of cloud onto the space where he thinks Sohey is [T:2081-2083], but Sohey has already moved further than any mortal step should allow [T:2096-2098]. Nuberroca dashes twice through the dark trying to close [T:2101-2106] and still cannot reach him; Sohey raises an orb of darkness in one hand that smothers Sinsong's lit pebble on top of Nuberroca and blinds the melee [T:2216-2223]. Under that darkness Sohey comes at Nuberroca in another flurry while Nuberroca swings into nothing [T:2224-2232], then vanishes with the body [T:2243-2245]. Nuberroca cannot follow blind — he swings at no enemy he can see [T:2248-2250].
5. **Kaelthar** lays his hands over the arrow in Tharn's throat and closes the wound before the dwarf can bleed out; Tharn stabilises, unconscious but alive [T:1746-1754 ?ord]. Combat closes at roughly ten-to-four in the morning after three rounds that cost perhaps thirty seconds in-fiction [T:2285-2286]. One goblin dead by Mialee's hand, Sohey fled with the second [T:2351]. On his pack-check Nuberroca finds five rations also gone to the earlier theft, missed first time round [T:2275-2277].

## 6 - Tharn opens up
_~22:00, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The party claws back a short breather in the time it takes Amidamaru to finish his last hour of sleep [T:2294-2303], and Tharn wakes with the throat-wound closed and bloody but intact — asking why anyone would hit him and begging to go back to the kitchen [T:2402], [T:2408], [T:2410-2412]. Mialee tells him there is no going back [T:2413-2414].
2. Mialee presses him on the lodge, the castle, the cult. He has never seen this place [T:2429-2430]; he came up from Carnath and from Waterdeep and had been three months cooking in Naerytar when the party arrived [T:2449-2452]. He names **Rezmir** — *la señora Rezmir* — the *Wyrmspeaker*, the one who speaks for the dragons [T:2566-2568], and admits she was in the castle most of the time he was there [T:2558], [T:2570-2572]. He will not help against the cult: Tiamat is the one who will rule the world, and dragons are better and smarter than mortals and ought to govern them [T:2433-2434], [T:2619-2623]. He names the higher ranks only on prompting — **Dragonsoul** (*Almas de Dragón*) and **Dragonclaw** (*Garras de Dragón*) — the ones who would know where the caravan-gold was going [T:2756-2760].
3. On the Thayans Tharn is more useful: strange ones, not well-liked at the castle, who would sometimes take wounded lizardfolk or bullywugs away and never bring them back [T:2680-2683]. He confirms the Thayans would be where a walking-dead outbreak traced back to [T:2669-2673]. Nuberroca lays it on thick — the castle is now a plague of walking dead, their beloved leaders fled, Tharn would have been eaten and raised had the party not taken him out [T:2660-2666]. ***[EPIC]*** Then he pulls the gold cult-chain from his pack and tells Tharn the gold is only worn by the real ones, that he and Kaelthar are the deep spies, that Nyx and Mialee *also* work for the cult but have lost themselves in the role [T:2706-2709], [T:2729]; the dwarf, against every reason, is half-convinced [T:2720-2722]. Tharn warns him that the symbol of Torm on his gear will cause problems eventually, but the gold chain is impressive [T:2731-2736].
4. On the march in Amidamaru identifies the remaining items from Naerytar: the recovered dagger carries a venom-generating magic [T:2506-2508 ?ord], the coin is a divinatory piece that lets its holder nudge fate [T:2511], and the small vial of oil turns the user briefly into a ghost [T:2517-2519]. Sinsong finishes his cleansing on Amidamaru along the way [T:2797], [T:2827-2829].
5. The approach to the lodge opens with signs of killing: a fallen trunk in front of the door stuck with thrown axes, javelins, and spear-marks — some bloodstains dry, one fresh of the last few hours [T:2782-2791] — and in the pines nearby, pointed green ears nailed to the trunks [T:2792-2794].

## 7 - The hunting lodge

1. The party fans out at the lodge: Nuberroca swings wide to the stables [T:3098-3100]; Mialee fades from sight on an hour of Amidamaru's magic to scout the ground floor [T:3043-3047]; Kaelthar hides behind the fallen tree with his dire wolf beside him [T:3064-3068], [T:3253]; Nyx hides and works her way toward a window [T:2962-2964]; Amidamaru hangs back watching the back angles [T:3220-3224]. Sinsong, with no sightline, decides to stay close to Tharn [T:3186-3189].
2. Mialee at the window sees a trophy hall — eleven-point stag, two roe deer, a griffon's head and foreclaws, a copper-bronze bull-head dominating the wall — and a tapestry showing a huge white deer in the forest [T:3080-3092]. The hearth is lit; the room is empty [T:3095]. Through another window she sees kitchen staff — humans, a dwarf, a halfling — in plain working clothes, no cultist robes [T:3234-3237], [T:3292-3306]. Nyx picks up a second room, a hunter's hall with its own hunting-scene tapestry and a fire burning but no-one in it [T:3014-3017]. Nuberroca, round the back, hears gorging and grunting from the stables — something eating with no civility [T:3119-3122] — and finds kobold-sized tracks mingled with human-sized boot-prints by the walls [T:3167-3169].
3. Sinsong pulls a cultist cloak over his shoulders in case Tharn's walk-in draws attention [T:3140-3141]. Tharn — *"¿y ahora qué?"* — scrapes his boots on the stone and strolls through the lodge door, which stands open [T:3215-3218], [T:3365-3367]. A man inside glances at him, past Sinsong, past the dire wolf and Kaelthar behind the fallen tree, raises a hand to Tharn, and walks out as if nothing is unusual [T:3277-3285]. The sun comes up at six in the morning on the seventeenth of Hammer [T:3371-3376] and the session cuts there, with Sinsong moving to follow the dwarf through the open door [T:3369], [T:3376-3378].

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 54}}

\page

# Session 55 {{float:"right" 2026-10-11}}
{wide}

## 1 - Outside the lodge
_~22:00, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The party is dispersed around the hunting lodge. **Mialee** still has several minutes of Amidamaru's invisibility running on her; Nuberroca works along the back of the house with Nyx one step behind him so she can relay signals back to Amidamaru; The tortle hangs back at the tree-line with Eguzkilore, a lit pebble on him from his tinkering; Sinsong, with the dwarf **Tharn**, waits at the front step, and right behind awaits Kaelthar with his summoned dire-wolf.
2. Through the kitchen window Nuberroca catches the cooks grumbling that the lady of the house is expecting visitors and extra plates have to go out; from a cleaner line Nyx picks out **Talis the White** and a rivalry mentioned without a name. She half-recalls the rival as *Borngray*, and later Amidamaru places the man as **Dralmorrer Borngray** — "elegant and racist" elf, the lieutenant of castle Naerytar.
3. The group gathers back in the pines and agrees on a cover: they walk in as reinforcements Dralmorrer has sent for the goblin problem, Harper and Torm insignias stowed or covered, cultist cloaks over their shoulders. Amidamaru hangs a dragon-chain openly at his neck; Nuberroca remains outside with Kaelthar's wolf, wary of eyes among the trees; weapons go sheathed before the door.

\column

## 2 - The chief butler
_~22:10, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. In the gloom of the entry hall, with no-one yet come down to meet them, Mialee, still invisible, raps three times at an inner door to see who comes, and nobody comes at all. A few minutes later Sinsong decides to try the horn of the great mounted elk on the north wall — Kaelthar lifts him by the waist to reach it, he cannot work it loose, only knocks the trophy skew, and nudges it straight again with a conjured hand.
2. The door-keeper eventually comes — a human steward in formal dress — **Gastyn**. The hall is near-dark; Gastyn rounds on a kobold to light the lamps, and when the first lamp is not enough he orders every lamp lit, muttering "cursed useless things" under his breath.
3. Sinsong ignores the plan and improvises — they are the escort of the Great Tharn, the finest cook in the realm — and Gastyn, visibly puzzled but unwilling to argue, waves Tharn to the kitchen and offers the party two guest rooms and a hall to lounge in.
4. As Gastyn starts to dismiss them to their rooms, Nyx adds the real pretext: they come from Dralmorrer with business for Talis. Gastyn politely insists the cook's escort's business is not his concern, but says he will tell Talis they are here.
5. Over the course of the conversation Gastyn volunteers that the previous master was **Lord Marsten**, a hunter, and on Nyx's question admits the handover to Talis came only a few months back. The party reads him as fonder of the old master and sour on the new one.
6. Only well after the others have been settled does Nuberroca finally come in from the cold; Gastyn stops him at the threshold and asks who he is — he answers "escort of the cook's escort" and Gastyn, after a tired glance at his muddy boots, has him leave them at the step before coming in.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 55}}
\page

## 3 - The sitting room
_~22:30, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Upstairs, invisible, Mialee turns over an armoury — a rain-wet room with a hole in the roof and a rack of cheap arms — and quietly cleans it out of its valuables.
2. In the next room over she finds a vaulted hall with five tapestries: four depict chromatic dragons, each razing a different location (a desert caravan under a blue, elves gasping at a green's poison, a putrid marsh ringed by a black and a white, Waterdeep's guard-castle burning under a red), and dominating the south wall between two hearths, a gold-thread Tiamat of five heads, thirty-five by twenty feet, gem-set and worth a fortune. She leaves the tapestries untouched and comes back down.
3. Back in the sitting room, Sinsong asks Nyx to lift him up to touch the horn of the metal-faced bull head mounted on the wall; Nyx tries, but she is a halfling trying to lift a dwarf — between them they barely reach Nuberroca's waist. Nuberroca steps in instead, grabs the head and pulls it off the wall himself. Its skull turns out to be real metal, not stuffed, and the back of the wooden mount hides nothing he can find on first inspection; he re-fastens it to the wall.
4. Moments later Nuberroca takes the bull head down again and drops it onto Sinsong — *"toma"* — and the dwarf buckles to the floor under the metal weight.
5. Gastyn returns with a bar of incense requested by Amidamaru, scolds Nuberroca into putting the bull head back, and asks them to behave themselves and not burn the house down.
6. Amidamaru settles by the hearth and begins the long summon of his owl **Munin**. Shortly after, **Arlaenga** — the house maid, a mature brown-haired woman — lets herself into the sitting-room and takes a corner post "in case the guests need anything".
7. Sinsong tries his luck at Arlaenga's shoulder with a whispered proposition and earns an open-handed slap that leaves five finger-marks on the dwarf's face and a direct instruction not to come near her again. The owl finally lifts out of the hearth; Arlaenga stiffens at the bird, insists animals do not come inside, and Amidamaru lets Munin out through the window after a brief argument.
8. The tortle settles into a quiet corner of the hearth and reads the room for magic: Mialee shines as a sun bright with light; Sinsong's old neck-scar glows; Nyx's only glow is the scroll Amidamaru gave her the week before; the goodberries each of them carries are their own small lights, but the room seems empty of anything magical.
9. The Thayan sword Amidamaru has stashed in his gear reads as magical, and further inspection reveals it could bond with a wizard but, afraid of a possible curse, he decides against taking it on.

\column

## 4 - A mounted griffon
_~23:00, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Sinsong, scanning the trophy wall, snaps and recognises the mounted griffon head above as *Targutai*, the mount he lost — and from then on cannot be moved off it, repeating the name through dinner between forkfuls. Nyx and Mialee cannot talk Sinsong down. Arlaenga, over the course of the back-and-forth, lets slip that Lord Marsten had died in a hunting accident.
2. Two kobolds open the door with a kick and bring dinner in on balanced plates, Arlaenga closing the door behind them. Mialee moves to check the food for poison, but Sinsong jumps in — sobbing through the grief he casts a detection spell and finds nothing.
3. The moment Arlaenga leaves to fetch some wine from the kitchen, the party moves — on Sinsong's insistence Nuberroca lifts the griffon head off the wall for him, and as it hits the table a glass chime sounds from inside. Nuberroca reaches in through the back and pulls out two flasks; Amidamaru fakes the head's shape with a shimmer-illusion where the trophy had been.
4. Nuberroca cracks the window and pitches the real head outside, considering it useless now that the goods are out. When Arlaenga returns with two bottles of wine she sees nothing out of place.

## 5 - Baths and offerings
_~23:20, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. In the sitting-room Nuberroca leans on Arlaenga with an offer — would she take him to bed. She does not slap him — he scares her — only asks, nose wrinkled, to be given some distance from him for what is left of the evening. She slips out at once to fetch Gastyn.
2. The butler returns and, after a last round of courtesies, pulls Nuberroca aside — "blue sir, come with me" — down a narrow passage to a steaming bathing-room with piles of clean towels. Mialee follows him in and climbs into the water on his knees, what happened there only they know.
3. In the meantime Eguzkilore slips out the window, retrieves the thrown griffon head from the mud, and tries to fit it back onto its wooden plate in the sitting-room — it comes back cracked and smeared and the shimmer-illusion stays up over it the rest of the evening.
4. Sinsong, meanwhile, has asked Gastyn for a toilet as an excuse to walk out of the lodge, defecate on the ground in front of the door, and offer the pile aloud to any god listening.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 55}}
\page

## 6 - Frozen armour
_~23:50, 16 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. In the kitchen, clean after the bath, Nuberroca lingers past welcome; the cook politely asks him to leave, and he bargains — give him meat and he will go. The cook splits a raw rabbit in half, sets it over the kitchen fire, and promises it to his room in twenty minutes.
2. The chill drafts drifting through the sitting room owe to the doors Sinsong keeps leaving open on his comings and goings. In the entry hall the cleric, drawn to the two elven-armour stands, wants to try the helmet and, upon touching it, freezes solid where he stands.
3. Nuberroca carries him across to the hearth; five minutes by the fire thaw him out. On his second try at the gauntlet he freezes again, and Nuberroca drags him back a second time.
4. While the dwarf thaws, Nuberroca presses everyone to translate the runes in the spear Mialee had lifted from the upstairs armoury. Only Kaelthar can read the Draconic down the shaft — "the eyes of Tiamat shine" — and the spear begins to glow.
5. Nyx jokes about the weapon from the back of the room — "a lantern" — and Nuberroca's face reflects a barbarian deflation.

## 7 - The gargoyles stir
_~00:00, 17 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. At Sinsong's suggestion to explore the house, Nuberroca steps back into the entry hall spear in hand, while twelve bell-strokes carry down through the house from somewhere upstairs.
2. Suddenly, the two demon-statues flanking the stairs peel off their plinths and go for the barbarian. The first strike glances off him and the second lands; Mialee from the sitting-room doorway puts an arrow into the nearer of the two.
3. Sinsong, braced in the doorway, commands them to flee. One gargoyle shrugs it off; the other beats out of the hall and up to the roof. Nyx, cutting round behind the remaining one and striking from the back, scores a glancing hit.
4. Nuberroca disengages back behind Sinsong at the doorway and stores the spear where no-one can see it.
5. Upstairs a woman in white plate steps out onto the landing with her guards, raises an open palm, speaks one word in a tongue the party does not recognise, and one of the gargoyles returns to its plinth. The other at first flies up instead — raising an eyebrow on the woman's face — before dropping back to its own plinth a few heartbeats later.

\column

## 8 - Talis the White
_~00:10, 17 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The woman — half-elf, pale-skinned, white plate, a staff of command in one hand and a spear in the other — presents herself as **Talis**. Through her guards and the female cultist posted at the door, she takes the party upstairs to her chamber, where a map of the local mountain country lies open on the table and is folded away the moment they enter.
2. Talis opens with *"Glory to Tiamat"* and the party echoes it. She asks after Dralmorrer and after *Wyrmspeaker* Rezmir, and the party answers vaguely about both. Then she lays out the job: tomorrow morning, find the Nashkag goblin tribe in the surrounding country and finish them — all of them, if they can.
3. She adds that her head of security, **Trepsin**, has already killed thirty or forty of the goblins and that they keep coming; Trepsin is a massive, four-armed, foul-smelling creature, loves his job but prefers to work alone, keeper of her drake-patrols.
4. ***[EPIC]*** From a window Amidamaru watches the same brute walk up to the door — tall, long-armed, moss-skinned, moving fast — and sees him stop short on the step to shake something nasty off a foot. Sinsong's offering had been left exactly there: the brute had trodden straight through it on his way in.
5. Amidamaru makes a request — may they borrow a weapon from the armoury for Sinsong, robbed by the goblins on the road? — and **Kusphia**, the female cultist, is sent to fetch him spears, swords and daggers. The tortle then asks after the Company of the Boar, but Talis has never heard of them.
6. Sinsong oversteps about Tiamat's larger plan and gets kicked under the table by Eguzkilore; Talis reads him as new to the cult and warns the party off dreaming for Dragonwing or Dragonsoul promotions, let alone the purple of a Wyrmspeaker, before they have learned to obey.
7. Kusphia returns through the door empty-handed, slips to Talis's shoulder and murmurs something in her ear the party cannot hear.
8. ***[EPIC]*** On the way out Nyx throws an *"all for Tiamat"*, met with cold looks from the guards and Kusphia. After closing the door Nyx shares with the group that Talis seemed to dislike their replies.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 55}}
\page

## 9 - A restless night
_~00:30, 17 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The party packs itself into the southern guest room, curtains drawn, window shuttered and only a double bed for six. Amidamaru raises his warded shelter over the whole chamber, ten feet of hidden protection that will see them through the night.
2. They have slept full rests twice already this day, so no third full night will restore them further — only short breathers between the hours of pretending to sleep.
3. Across the night Amidamaru works identifying all the magical objects the party carries. Eguzkilore's cloak is able to change its colour and material, his boots warm against the cold but require him to bond with them; Nuberroca's spear carries only the light-trick they already saw; Amidamaru's Thayan sword is a necromantic blade that drains the wielder's life for extra bite on bonding.
4. In the quiet Nyx asks Sinsong about her near-death vision — a female dwarven figure who steadied her, and a thirst for beer after. The name **Sharindlar** escapes him before he catches himself and begs Nyx never to speak it again; her true name is hidden, and she is to be called the **Lady of Mercy** — the dwarven goddess of healing whose shape matches the figure in Nyx's vision. The thirst for beer, he admits, fits nothing he knows of her.
5. The hours pass with no night excursion, and dawn finds them sleepless but rested, with a mission to eradicate a tribe of goblins they have already met.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 55}}

\page

# Session 56 {{float:"right" 2026-10-25}}
{wide}

## 1 - A sleepless dawn
_~08:20, 17 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The party rouse after a night without rest; through the small hours Amidamaru has noticed doors opening quietly out in the corridor, and the shadow of feet pausing once at their own door. The tortle has felt a faint sorcerous weight settle on him as the morning begins — he recognises the shape of it but not the hand behind it.
2. Feeling the artificer's curse, Sinsong asks everyone to join hands and praise his name aloud; Amidamaru turns heel on the words and walks straight out of the room with a goodbye.
3. **Fenwick**, a lodge servant, brings in porridge, dried fruit and a small cloth bag of jerky to their sitting room. Eguzkilore sniffs for poison finding only unfamiliar spices; Nuberroca empties his bowl without inspecting it; Amidamaru pleads an upset stomach and does not eat.
4. An older hunter — **Angrath**, chief of the lodge's hunt under the late **Lord Marsten** — comes to see them off. Until the new owners took the house the goblins had never caused trouble beyond petty theft; he believes the camp lies somewhere high in the mountains to the north. He has nothing in particular against them and no interest in joining the hunt.
5. Angrath lets slip that the trolls which used to roam these woods now mostly serve the lodge's new masters; the wild ones were devoured by a white dragon who has visited the house three or four times since the new owners arrived, only to speak with the lady.
6. Mialee presses him on the dragon's schedule; Angrath has not found a pattern, only that it comes from the south-east. He names **Parnast** as the nearest town, days of travel the same way.
7. Nuberroca blurts out loud that they are cultists — cultists and adventurers, and Mialee covers for him with a cheerful *"Glory to Tiamat"*. Angrath ignores the salute and takes his leave.
8. Nuberroca calls his god's furious patronage down with a shout and a fist on the table; a blue glow runs along the old wounds at his neck and they close under it. Amidamaru adds a wash of his own healing on the goliath for good measure, the same blue light brightening along the barbarian's throat.

\column

## 2 - Loose limbs
_~08:50, 17 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. On the landing above, **Talis** appears and beckons them up. Nuberroca steps outside for fresh air first — and crumples boneless across the threshold, awake but unable to move. Eguzkilore goes down next; Mialee feels the drug catch her, mouth pasty, limbs leadening.
2. From the entry hall doorway, with **Trepsin** already striding in from the east, Amidamaru throws his voice into the brute's ear — a whispered warning of goblins to the south, telling him to run; Trepsin only snorts and keeps coming.
3. Mialee weaves a word of travel and vanishes from where she stood, reappearing on the shingles of the roof overhead. From the cold tiles she lands on she glimpses a loose nest of twigs; two large feathery shapes lie coiled there at rest — perytons, the heart devourers they have faced before. She crawls under her cloak and holds there.
4. Talis arrives, **Kusphia** at her shoulder, and sends her mercenaries to carry the slumped bodies through to the breakfast room. Trepsin has to fold himself double to pass the lintel; his old mud-prints along the hall match other prints the party had seen the day before.

## 3 - Shackled
_~09:00, 17 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. The soldiers manacle each party member into their chair; Trepsin lifts Amidamaru across as a mountain shifts rocks. The drug catches every one of them — except the tortle, saved by his ill stomach — and it pulls the lie from anyone it takes.
2. Kusphia searches each prisoner. On Nuberroca she pulls back the cloak and finds **Torm**'s gauntleted fist on his breastplate; on Kaelthar the same symbol under his armour; on Nyx she lifts out the amulet at her throat, revealing the Harpers' sigil. Amidamaru pulls his head into his shell when her hand reaches, but Kusphia will not reach inside.
3. Talis asks who they really are, and the drug does the rest. Nuberroca blurts the whole story in one breath — Greenest, the cult, the stolen hoard, the portal, the goblin that beat him on the road. Kusphia bends to Talis's ear and names them as two likely members of the Order of the Gauntlet and a Harper.
4. Amidamaru bargains for a Thayan spellbook, one of two he kept from the Red Wizard they took it from.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 56}}
\page

## 4 - Common enemy
_~09:30, 17 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Talis listens, and then names what she wants: to put right the evil in this world and see **Varram** dead — a name none of the party has ever heard — adding that if **Rezmir** should suffer in the same stroke, so much the better for them all. Amidamaru shivers at Rezmir's name and says only that he is in agreement.
2. Talis's eyes return to the Thayan book with new weight. The book changes hands for two missions: kill the Nashkag goblins to earn the cult's signs, passwords and the means to reach the **Castle in the Clouds**; and once inside, sabotage Rezmir's cargo so the shipment never reaches its destination. The party may keep whatever treasure they can lift off that cargo, and Talis will deny ever having spoken to them if they meet again after Varram is dead.
3. Amidamaru circles back to the white dragon — if he visits, will he harm them?. Talis's eyes light at the name — **Glazhael**, the great lord — and she says she would never speak on his behalf, only that so long as he bears them no grudge no harm should come to them. Pressed on when he was last there, she answers: about a tenday past, and she does not know when he will come again.
4. Above, Mialee crosses from the roof into the room where they met with Talis, and then to a bedroom she identifies as hers by the clothing and decor. She picks the lock on a chest at the foot of the bed, lifting a cache of gold and a coat of chainmail woven to shed its own pale light. When voices start up in the next room, she lowers herself from the eaves to the back garden with only a bruise and slips out to rejoin the party at the gate later.
5. Nuberroca mentions his own mission to save his village from the undead in the northern mountains, but Talis has no interest beyond the bargain already struck. Trepsin will accompany them to the goblin hunt — half escort, half watchdog, and because he enjoys the work.

\column

## 5 - A thief's request

1. Waiting for the drug to clear, Nuberroca asks for a weapon for the hunt; Talis slaps him open-handed and reclaims the lightspear from the goliath's back, calling the party out as thieves.
2. Amidamaru, quiet at Talis's shoulder, asks where the truth-drug came from. She tells him to look for it in the underworld — ask the Zhentarim — and leaves them.
3. Nuberroca sets his nature book across his knees and reads it against the troll propped at the far wall. The pages tell him trolls regenerate; that they are a bog and marsh creature these mountain woods are not; and that fire cauterises their wounds and stops the regrowth.
4. Mialee uses the long wait to make some arrows in the woods, but her loose limbs hinder her, and she manages to complete a single arrow, which she lays into her quiver with the rest.
5. After around an hour, the drug finally lifts. Trepsin, who had stood cross-armed against the wall, shakes them to their feet with an open-handed slap on Nuberroca — *"you're fine"* — then hauls Amidamaru and the goliath upright by the shoulders without ceremony and marches them out.
6. On the step the brute stops to wipe something nasty off his foot — Sinsong's offering from the night before, trodden through on his way in and still exactly where it had been left.
7. He crosses to the stable and brings out two of his drakes before guiding them to the frozen forest.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 56}}
\page

## 6 - Going north
_~10:30, 17 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Nuberroca manages to coax conversation out of the troll: he likes killing and Talis feeds him; he has been to Parnast, a huge city by his standards. The goliath offers a fist to bump, and Trepsin, at a loss, cups the goliath's hand in his lower palm instead.
2. On the way to the drake-stable Nuberroca tries to clamber onto the troll's shoulder, he is shoved off at every attempt. Undeterred, he presses a hand to the troll's arm anyway — *"you don't disgust me, I can touch you"* — Trepsin bewildered as to why anyone would want to.
3. Mialee drops down from the lodge roof to rejoin the party at the gate, the gold cache and the glowing coat of chainmail from Talis's chest hidden in her bag; on the march she tells Amidamaru about the haul, agreed between them that the armour not be worn until well away from the lodge.
4. Trepsin walks them north through the woods in silence, tolerating their presence in a mission he would gladly take alone with his drakes. Two hours of climbing bite with the cold, Mialee starts struggling as her fingers go numb. Nuberroca tucks her inside his furs to keep her moving.
5. Mialee catches the first glint of eyes watching them from the pines. She and Amidamaru whisper agreement, a word carried on the air between them, to let the goblins open the fight and side with them against the troll if the troll looks beaten. Over the next half hour whole handfuls of eyes become visible, enough to pick out the green points of a goblin's ear.
6. Amidamaru proposes that they ask the goblins to lay the ambush on the troll instead; Mialee agrees. Eguzkilore drifts aside and quietly asks Amidamaru what the plan is — there is none, the tortle admits. Trepsin notices some of the party have dropped behind, turns, counts them, and warns the rest: the goblins are close.

\column

## 7 - Prove it
_~13:00, 17 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Mialee weaves a floating arrow of light straight above Trepsin, inscribed 'goblins, attack him!' in a sign visible only from aloft. Amidamaru follows with an illusion of small goblin footprints laid in the snow nearby. Trepsin stops, bends to the prints, smells them and starts to track them.
2. Mialee signals Nuberroca to something glinting forty feet south — a sword jammed into the ground behind a tree. Nuberroca creeps close with her; Mialee reaches out with her ethereal hand and the hilt passes clean through — the sword is an illusion. *"This smells bad,"* she whispers, *"and for once it isn't you."* They hold off.
3. A horn sounds from behind the trees, and a scatter of goblins hurl their explosive flasks at Trepsin, most miss but three catch him square. Flames crawl across his bulk, he sweeps his cloak closed and the fire snuffs out as if it had never caught. Mialee calls out that the cloak shields from fire.
4. One of the spheres catches Fenrir as the dire-wolf slinks between trees, the fur burns and the wolf is severely wounded, but Kaelthar is unscathed. The red dragonborn stands too plain against the dark trunks and is targeted by several arrows that miss him by an inch. Between the pines Nuberroca's bulk shadows Mialee from an arrow meant for her; she bends under her cloak and takes only a graze.
5. ***[EPIC]*** Mialee picks out the lead archer through the trees — **Sohey**, the goblin who had felled Nuberroca the day before. She projects her voice into his ear across the woods — they are not cultists, they want this troll dead too, help them. His answer comes back on the same thread: prove it.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 56}}
\page

## 8 - The butcher falls

1. Sohey's own cocktail smothers the last flicker on the cloak, and the goblin tribe redoubles their attacks, then melts into the trees. Sohey lifts his voice above the noise — they are not cultists, kill the butcher — and every goblin arrow turns onto the troll.
2. Kaelthar rides Fenrir in at a dead run, charges Trepsin with his sword and the hit lands. Trepsin turns on him, calling him traitor, and his claws rend the dragonborn, Amidamaru burns a quick arcane twist to spoil some of the attacks, but the paladin drops unconscious at the troll's feet nonetheless.
3. Nuberroca steps across without striking and tilts a small potion to Kaelthar's lips; the paladin's eyes open. Fenrir, torn apart by the troll, vanishes with a howl.
4. Amidamaru pins Trepsin and the two drakes under a weave of slowness; the drakes are affected but Trepsin shrugs it off with a grunt. Mialee hooks a poisoned arrow into Trepsin's flank — black veins climb his skin, he snarls, pulls the shaft out, and the veins recede as fast as they came.
5. Kaelthar's own prayer to lock Trepsin in place slides off — the compulsion finds nothing in the troll to bind to. Nuberroca hammers in with his polearm — the first swing lands, and with the second he hacks one of the four arms clean off. The severed arm clamps onto his boot on its own and will not let go.
6. Through the fight Sohey weaves between the pines to Trepsin's back and keeps the pressure up — the goblin leader darts in and out with his shortsword and scimitar, hooking it under the troll's guard and splitting the butcher's attention between front and rear whenever he turns to meet the party.
7. Tagged by the elf's arrows, Trepsin lunges past the others and comes down on Eguzkilore in a flurry of strikes. One claw drives into the rock beside him and chunks fly off, deep lines left in the stone where the troll's talons marked it. Eguzkilore barely holds, then disengages back out of the troll's reach.
8. ***[EPIC]*** Eguzkilore steadies between two rocks and lets fly four shafts in a single breath; the last buries itself clean through Trepsin's throat. The troll claws for the shaft, gets it out, and sprays blood in a wide fan down his chest.
9. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca shouts and lets his cloud-step carry him clear across the woods, reappearing at Trepsin's back; the troll goes down to one knee under the elf's arrows, and the goliath drives his lance into the back of the neck, the spear comes clean through to the front.
10. From the undergrowth Sohey lifts a thumbs-up at Nuberroca.

\column

## 9 - Smoke in the pines
_~13:10, 17 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Amidamaru runs over, strips the moss cloak off the body and lights the corpse with a bolt of fire from his hand so the troll will not rise again. He examines the cloak later and finds no magic on it — just moss and filth woven over hide.
2. The two drakes do not last long. One is cut down by goblin arrows while Trepsin is still falling; the other, torn and dragging a broken leg, is finished with a single quiet shot from Mialee.
3. The severed arms scuttle about searching for prey and have to be run through and set alight; one of them scratches Eguzkilore before it stops moving. The elf walks between the pieces and sets each one burning, so none of it will knit back together.
4. Kaelthar summons Fenrir back to the world of the living. From the pines on all sides, the goblin laugh and the tribe breaks into a victory dance, jumping with their tiny arms raised.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 56}}

\page

# Session 57 {{float:"right" 2026-11-08}}
{wide}

## 1 - An odd pendant
_~13:20, 17 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Combing **Trepsin**'s cooling pyre, Mialee lifts a small object the troll had carried in a pocket — a pendant that settles in her palm with the odd familiarity of something she might have seen before. She tucks it aside for Amidamaru to identify later.
2. Mialee walks over to **Sohey** and offers the words she can for his fallen. The goblin keeps tally: thirty-six warriors lost to the butcher over the last months, counting these two. *"Tonight, after we see our friends off, we feast."*
3. The Nashkag build a smaller pyre for Trepsin's bones; one of them fits the troll's skull over his own head as a vast, crooked helmet and begins to dance. Their own two dead they carry away uncremated.
4. Sohey tells them to wait and bolts north — a speed none of them have ever seen on anything living, air pulling at their faces as he passes.
5. The other goblins drift off in every compass direction, grinning when Mialee asks where their village lies.

## 2 - Sticky rope
_~14:00, 17 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Forty minutes later Sohey staggers back from the west, soaked in sweat and barely able to pull air — a man who had fought Trepsin without ever losing his breath. He lays a cloth bundle at their feet and gives back each item one of theirs had been robbed of: Eguzkilore's waterskin, Nuberroca's rations and Nyx's magical rope.
2. The returned rope sits tacky in Nyx's palm — the halfling frowns at it, puzzled, as if she were no longer sure it were truly hers. Then she pauses over the pendant Mialee had found and notes it is close kin to the one already at her own throat.
3. From his pack he draws two items and offers one each to Mialee and Eguzkilore — Sohey has heard they are good at drawing words — a pair of spectacles and a beautiful pen, both carrying the weight of magic under the touch. Mialee takes the glasses; Eguzkilore the pen.
4. Mialee asks him for some evidence the cultists might accept that they have found their village, and one or two days of inactivity so **Talis** will believe the extermination is done. Sohey agrees and, after catching breath for a few seconds and complaining of his luck, runs again, east this time.

\column

## 3 - A painful itch

1. Eguzkilore pleads with Sinsong for a touch of healing and shows him the ache on his chest. Sinsong lays a hand there and begins the spell — as the magic reaches in, a red welt the size of a fist blooms where the cleric touches, then rapid-blooming blisters crawl across his chest faster than he can refuse to scratch.
2. Reading the condition as some kind of curse, Sinsong sits Eguzkilore down with his back turned, lays a hand on his head, and begins to weave the counter-magic. As its threads draw close a lock of hair drops and fresh blisters bloom across the elf's scalp; the cleric presses on regardless, waving off Mialee's dry question about his cosmetic intentions and admitting with a grin that he rather likes the look.
3. After a long moment Sinsong remembers the old tales: certain people's flesh simply rejects the touch of magic, convulsing or killing them outright under enough of it. The affliction's origin was never traced, it did not spread from one sufferer to the next, and the symptoms cleared on their own after some days — though some forms of healing still worked on them, despite the agony of first contact.
4. Sinsong retreats behind a tree and sends a disembodied hand onto Eguzkilore's scalp. The earlier welts on his chest and head fade on their own as the new magic lands; fresh blisters rise where the hand touches, and a few more locks of hair drop. Half-bald and stinging, Eguzkilore chases Sinsong down and hauls him up by the collar: *"¿Y ahora cómo voy a ligar?"*

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 57}}
\page

## 4 - Rum and powder
_~15:00, 17 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Sohey returns from the south with a carved wooden totem almost as large as he is balanced on one shoulder, and lays it in the snow. *"The tribe's totem. Proof you have been to our settlement. These are our gods and our ancestors. Treat it carefully."*
2. Nuberroca turns to Sohey and asks the goblin to train him in some of his techniques, offering to stay behind for a couple of days while the others report him as another combat casualty to sweeten the lie. Sohey shrugs and accepts — though Sinsong and Mialee, already laying bets on the outcome, insist the first lesson happen there and then, in front of them.
3. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca opens with his polearm in a raging charge; despite an impossible dodge, the spear grazes Sohey's cheek and draws a single bead of blood. Sohey's eyes widen — *"Oh? You're good."* Then the goblin dances: he slips the killing stroke that should have finished him, counters with Nuberroca's own spear-shaft driven into the goliath's flank, and reads him minute by minute until Sinsong calls it off with the barbarian nearly spent.
4. Sinsong gathers the party into a circle around him and heals them all without incident — Eguzkilore stays well back. The cleric then runs over to the elf and lays a hand on him anyway; the healing takes, but the rash flares across his skin again and another lock of hair drops where Sinsong touches him. Grievance doubled, Eguzkilore throws a right-hook at the dwarf's jaw in the same breath: *"That's for leaving me bald."*
5. Nuberroca honours Sohey with a bottle of rum after the fight — a good one, he says, and he hopes one day to learn from him. The goblin receives it like a bar of gold — *"Where have you got this? I haven't tasted any in years!"* — downs half in one pull and hands it back; Nuberroca presses the full bottle on him. Willing to give something in return, Sohey draws out a small sealed ceramic jar like the ones the goblins had lobbed at Trepsin at range.
6. Mialee adds a box of carved figurines from her bag in tribute for the totem; Sohey weighs it at a glance and nods. His tribe has eyes in the trees, so he will come up there himself to fight the whole party when they are ready. He asks whether they know the magical stones for sending messages — he will use one to start the friendly duel. Then he turns and heads west toward the village.
7. Rejoining the others, Nuberroca turns the duel into a lecture — the fight was not a lesson for him, he says, but a lesson for them about how badly they cramp him by standing at his elbow in combat. He then channels his god's fury and his wounds start closing with a blue glow.

\column

## 5 - Through the snow
_~16:00, 17 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. They set off back to the lodge along a faint trail. Mialee shows Nyx an area map she stole from Talis's meeting room. With the cartographer's tools from Nuberroca, Nyx takes point with a good heart.
2. The first hour reads easily; by about five the sun dips below the trees, and with it the cold creeps in and the snow begins to fall. Mialee wraps herself back into Nuberroca's furs; the southward trail still shows clearly enough for Nyx to follow.
3. The second hour is harder. Snow settles over the path and the way all but disappears; night presses in heavier, and even eyes used to the dark struggle against the blowing white. Nyx excuses her stumbles complaining that the blizzard is in her eyes; Kaelthar offers to turn back, but she waves him off and keeps her feet on what she can see.
4. By the third hour Eguzkilore suggests Kaelthar take wing and spot the lodge from above; Mialee counsels trust in Nyx instead. The halfling finds her own markers — branches she had broken on the way out — and walks them from one to the next until the lodge's cooking-smoke rises out of the pines ahead.

## 6 - Talis appears
_~19:00, 17 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. In the clearing outside the lodge the party settles their roles. Kaelthar will carry the totem forward as proof and announce the mission complete; Amidamaru hangs back well out of sight so that Trepsin's cloak stays unseen, and Mialee will not enter at all. Sinsong primes a touch of guiding magic to ride on Kaelthar when he starts talking.
2. A kobold spots them from the east and runs for the lodge; Nuberroca gives chase and finds more of them on that side of the house. Every kobold the party has seen here until now has been dressed for scrubbing pots; now they all carry steel. Through the walls, doors open and voices rise in what sound like orders.
3. Nuberroca argues for the back door and a pre-emptive knockout, but Mialee pushes for dialogue and nominates Sinsong to carry it. Before the cleric can decide how to call out, the front door opens — Talis steps out with Kushpia at her shoulder, and in the hall just behind them a pair of mercenaries wait with swords drawn. Talis asks what they are doing; Sinsong steps up: *"Talis — Nyx wanted a word with you."* Her own weapon stowed, Talis invites them in — provided they leave their arms outside.
4. Sinsong and Kaelthar step inside. Nyx will come only as far as the antechamber — she will not go farther unless Talis's people lay down their weapons too: *"No vamos a entrar aquí a que nos apuñalen."* Eguzkilore sighs and steps in beside her. Talis brings her fingers to her mouth, and whistles.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 57}}
\page

## 7 - Talis's whistle
_~19:30, 17 Hammer 1490 DR_

1. Crossbow bolts burst out of murder holes in the antechamber's east wall; one strikes Nyx deep in the ribs, another thumps into Eguzkilore. Talis raises a short wand and throws a mote of frost that swells into an ice-ball before it slams Kaelthar in the chest, cold crawling across his armour where it lands.
2. The two mercenaries — **Sorvic** and **Wessic** the Wizened, a grey-bearded old man with sixty hard years of war on him — drive forward and seize Kaelthar and Sinsong at the wrists. Sinsong lets himself be taken; Kaelthar tries to resist to no avail.
3. Nyx lifts her hand and casts a dome of silence on the far side of the hall — Talis's spoken words, and Sinsong's, both die inside it.
4. Eguzkilore's shot annihilates a kobold, leaving him nailed against the antechamber's west wall, feet off the ground. **Kushpia** cuts in at his back with her scimitar, managing only glancing hits.
5. From a woodpile in the east wing of the lodge, Mialee works arrows into Kushpia and vanishes into the shadows again. From the east a new sound carries through: the unmistakable noise of a fight outside the lodge.
6. Nuberroca charges in yelling curses and lands a halberd-blow on Kushpia, avoiding vital points to keep her breathing. Kaelthar pivots in the grapple and breathes a cone of fire into three kobolds crowding the corner — two die on the spot with soundless screams, the third scuttles away burning; his follow-up spell dies in his throat in the silence.
7. Sinsong, bound to his party by shared sigils of peace, feels Nyx about to take Talis's magical spear through the chest and appears beside her at the moment of impact; the cold takes him in her stead and she stares, uncomprehending, at the dwarf.
8. Mialee drives her short sword into Kushpia from behind and the cultist's eyes go out, scimitar bouncing uselessly off the elf's armour as she dies. Mialee then throws her dagger at Talis, but misses badly and the dagger bounces along the floorboards without a sound.
9. Nuberroca shoves Eguzkilore aside and closes on Talis: *"Talis! Drop your spear, hand it over and I'll spare your life."* He does not wait for a reply — his halberd bites deep into her flank and a shaft strike rounds it off.

\column

10. Finding herself bleeding and ringed, Talis presses the spear tighter and steps a pace outside the silence to incant. Black motes stream from her mouth and bloom into a dense swarm of wasps that fills the chamber and blots out sight.
11. The swarm catches most of them, leaving Sinsong and Eguzkilore unconscious — the scar at the back of the dwarf's neck flashing for an instant; Mialee, Nuberroca and Kaelthar grind their teeth and endure the swarm while they can.
12. Talis breaks for the door — still bleeding from Nuberroca's wounds — but the swarm she conjured does not spare her, and she falls short of the door and does not rise. The wasps turn into mist as her soul abandons her body.
13. Rushing to her friend, Mialee drops one of Nyx's berries into Eguzkilore's mouth, waking him from the swarm's bite. From the east, the sound of the fight beside the lodge is redoubling.
14. ***[EPIC]*** Nuberroca changes the hold on his halberd and hurls it like a javelin at Sorvic across the room. The polearm punches clean through visor and skull both, throwing the mercenary through the stair railings and pinning him against the north wall.
15. In the same breath the barbarian stoops and takes up Talis's spear from where it has fallen — Dragongleam, the draconic-runed weapon she had reclaimed from him that morning, now back in his hands.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{footnote Tyranny of Dragons | Session 57}}
\page

## 8 - Sinsong rises

1. The south window of the east wing bursts open in a cloud of glass shards, stone fragments and loose timber as a huge troll smashes out of the lodge and crashes onto Fenrir. A moment later Kaelthar feels the bond snap in his chest as the wolf dissipates into vapour; the troll is left stuck half-through the ruined opening, and from outside Mialee calls out: *"There's a huge troll out here!"*
2. Eguzkilore drags himself over to Sinsong, gives him one of the berries with a disgusted face, and finds a last breath in himself to get back to his feet.
3. ***[EPIC]*** Sinsong rises with a host of fey guardians. From beyond the murder holes grunts of pain carry back through the slits as the fey forms reach through to the guards on the other side. The cleric walks into the hall in the midst of the kobolds — eleven of them drop silently where he passes — Wessic, still on his feet, is left badly wounded. Sinsong throws himself behind the stairs to the second floor for cover.
4. As Nuberroca enters the main hall the two armour-suits flanking the door loose a freezing arc at him, he dodges it and finds Wessic still on his feet; a quick thrust with Dragongleam goes clean through him and the old veteran is left crushed, bleeding out on the floor.
5. In the antechamber Eguzkilore stands and shoots back through the murder-hole slits at the crossbowmen on the other side, leaving one of the guards close to death. Outside, Mialee cloaks her position in a thick mist of her own making and works another arrow into the troll still pinned at the breach.
6. As Nyx crosses into the main hall a cold blast hits her, the two gargoyle sentries peel from their pedestals, stone wings beating, and tear at her until she drops, only to fall themselves to the fey ethereal guardians.
7. A moment later Mialee, badly wounded, rushes into the hall shouting *"Troll!"* and is caught mid-word by an ice-blast in the doorway; she drops frozen on the threshold and her mist vanishes, revealing a huge troll a few steps from the entrance.

\column

## 9 - Three trolls

1. Nuberroca shoulders open the east door and comes in on the two guards readying their crossbows at the murder-hole slits. A brutal thrust with Dragongleam leaves the nearest in a heap on the floor; his comrade fires back and misses. An arrow from Eguzkilore finishes the last guard.
2. The troll crouches through the entrance into the antechamber — too big for the frame, stooped low at a doorway built for humans — and swings at Eguzkilore in the cramped space; the elf parries the stroke away with his sword. As it crosses into Sinsong's circle of fey guardians the spirits tear into it for a punishing wound on the way in.
3. On Nuberroca's left a second door explodes inward — splinters of timber spin through the tiny room and a guard's corpse crushes under the weight as a second troll forces its shoulder through the splintered frame and reaches an arm at the goliath. Nuberroca twists clear of the grab; the troll pokes its head through for another clutch and finds only air.
4. Nuberroca disengages from the grabbing arm and closes on the crouched troll in front of Eguzkilore — the armour-suits flanking the door loose a freezing arc as he crosses, but he carries through the cold and drives Dragongleam into the monster's flank in a reckless charge, following with a shaft blow, that leaves the beast bleeding profusely. Eguzkilore puts an arrow through it to finish it off, then lights a torch with a word of druid-magic and hurls it onto the body so the fire catches its hide and the troll will not rise again.
5. From the east a third troll comes up to the antechamber window, tears what is left of its frame away, and reaches through at Eguzkilore; most of the claws tear across the stonework, but one gash finds the elf.
6. Sinsong moves toward the troll in the eastern room, his fey guardians tearing at its flesh. The troll reaches for the dwarf and connects — but Kaelthar is pulled to Sinsong's side by the bond and takes the blow himself, dropping unconscious in his stead.
7. The dragonborn's sacrifice turns meaningless when a second strike from the troll drops Sinsong on the ground, the fey guardians fading away as the cleric falls. Nuberroca pours a potion down his throat and the cleric comes back to his senses a heartbeat after.
8. From the western corridor **Fenwick** the cook steps out at the head of a knot of kitchen staff, sharp blades in every hand, and calls across the ruined chamber: *"What the hell? Talis is dead? Get them!"* From the far side of the lodge, the sound of combat is clearly audible.
